śuṇḍādaṇḍena vanyānāṃ prakhaṇḍayati maṇḍanam

maheśaḥ paripapraccha praṇāmādyupacārakṛta

brahmeśassatya deveśo rudrakāraṇakāraṇaḥ

kāmikaṃ yogajaṃ cintyaṃ kāraṇaṃ cājitaṃ tathā

vijayaṃ caiva niśvāsaṃ svāyaṃbhuvamathānalam

candrajñānaṃ ca bimbaṃ ca prodgītaṃ lalitaṃ tathā

kiraṇe vātula caiva * * * * * * * * ?

sa vistaratvāt saṃmārjyai tajjātasya * *? mabho

acintyaṃ viśvasādākhyaṃ granthamaṣṭasahasrakam

niṣkriyaṃ nirmalaṃ yacca hetu dṛṣṭānta varjitam

samyagdarśana sanmārga jñānaparyāya vācakam

dāsamārgo bhavetkena kathyatāṃ bhagavanmama

rūpadhyāna vinirmuktamarūpadhyānasaṃyutam

māyāvivarjitaṃ yacca sahamārgamiti smṛtam

putramārga svabhāvo'yaṃ gurureva hi daivatam

sarvāgamoditaṃ jñānaṃ sarvaiśvaryaṃ tathaiva ca

putramārga iti prokto dāsamārgastathocyate

kaupinamambaraṃ jñeyaṃ kathaṃ prāvaraṇaṃ tathā

maṭhādīni vidheyāni naiṣṭhikā vāsakāraṇāt

taṭākaṃ kūpayantraṃ ca sthāpayeda prayatnataḥ

snapanārthaṃ jalandeyaṃ deyaṃ dehādimārjanam

sanmārgaṃ sahamārgaṃ ca putramārgamanantaram

sanmārgaṃ tatvaratnaṃ hi sarvatatvaika nāyakam

tathāpi putramārgaṃ tu gurudeva pradhānataḥ

etaccatuṣṭaye mārge sevyastanmokṣakāṃkṣiṇam

aiśvaryādiphalaṃ labdhvā punarjanma prapadyate

labdhvā gatvā prakāśitvā mokṣātmī vrajati kramāt

śivasya prītidaṃ pautraṃ dāsamārgamanugraham

anuṣṭheyaṃ sadānandin bhogamokṣasya kāraṇāt

tvayā mayā viśeṣatvād dattametadabhīṣṭadam

jñānayogakriyācaryāḥ samyagācaryate yataḥ

ācāryau dvau samākhyātau viraktaḥ prathamo bhavet

ājanmabrahmacārī ca yatiśca tadanantaram

gṛhastho vānaprasthaśca catuṣṭayamudīritam

naiṣṭhiko naiṣṭhikaścaiva gṛhasthāndīkṣayetkramāt

bhautiko liṃgaṃ yuktastu tasya jātirna vidyate

cintāsantati saṃśūnyā lokācāra vimohitam

eteṣāṃ deśikānāṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu suvrata !

kṣamāvānśāntacittaśca nigrahānugrahe mahān

vijñānadīkṣā saṃyukto gurubhaktiviśāradaḥ

ḍambha māyāvinirmuktaḥ kṣudrakarmavivarjitaḥ

rāgadveṣa vinirmuktaḥ kṛpāguṇasamanvitaḥ

adhiṣṭhāne triyopeto gamanatraya pāragaḥ

śivānubhūti saṃyuktaḥ saṃśayacchida saṃśayaḥ

jñaya kuṣṭhādirogaiśca vītaḥ paramasundaraḥ

tamovihīnaḥ śāstrajñaḥ paramārthavicintakaḥ

bhautiko'pi viśeṣeṇa dharmapatnīsamanvitaḥ

vadasvalokarakṣārthaṃ asmākaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

gurvājñālaṅghanaścāpi gurudoṣa vicārakaḥ

guruśuśrūṣaṇāhīno gurunindāsamanvitaḥ

krodhalobhajalonmeṣa paiśunyo ḍambhaveṣakaḥ

capalaḥ karako duṣṭaḥ kṣudrakarmaparigrahī

jñānayoga vinirmuktaḥ kṣayakuṣṭhādi rogavān

jñorajñātiśca durmānī bhakṣako durmukhastathā

asyaivācārya nāmatvamabhiṣekaṃ na kārayet

ahiṃsā satya saṃyuktaḥ krodhalobhavivarjitaḥ

āsanaṃ dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca pādaprakṣālanaṃ tathā

talasya mardanaṃ caiva saṃgrāmaṃ ca vivādanam

atihāsamavaṣṭambhaṃ līlāmaṃgaprasāraṇam

vyākhyātaṃ ca pratiṣṭhāṃ ca maṇḍalaṃ śiṣyasaṃgrahe

karasphoṭaṃ ca nṛttaṃ ca gāthāgānaṃ ca suptakam

dhāmacchāyāṃ gurucchāyāṃ tathaivāntena vedayet

gurorgurau tu daṣṭe tu prācāryānvandayettathā

satāṃ caiva prayacchecca jyeṣṭhatva gurukalpanāt

rātrau jale bhaye mārge pratyādeśa paro vrajet

guṇagrāme bhavetsiddhi na siddhirdoṣasaṃgrahe

bhaktiviśvā samadrohaṃ gurvājñālaṅghanasthitiḥ

kṛtajñatā dvādaśakaṃ yasya śiṣyasya vidyate

bhakti yuktaśca śiṣyaśca na śikṣayati cedguruḥ

tasmātsarvaprayatnena guṇānsarvānparīkṣayet

abdaśuśrūṣaṇānte tu guṇāguṇanirūpaṇāt

kuryādācāryakastasya vijñānāvadhikaṃ kramāt

paścāddoṣaṃ samutpannaṃ manovākkāya karmabhiḥ

vijñāpya sarvadoṣaṃ ca vandayeddeśikottamam

asaṃnidhau gurorevaṃ kartavyaṃ jyeṣṭhasannidhau

tatvārthamananādyādi jagattrāṇāstramucyate

vācya vācakatā hīnaṃ manovākkāya gocaram

niṣkalaṃ paramaṃ tatvamanantamacalaṃ dhruvam

anādhāraṃ svasaṃvedyamadhiṣṭhānaṃ parātparam

śivatatvamidaṃ proktaṃ tatvānāṃ (sthityamapatti)

* * * śānimittāyā svecchayā gṛhyate tanum

madhyamaikaṃ samastaikamityevaṃ dvividhaṃ matam

indrāyudha samākāraṃ parāṃśaktyaikamūrtimāt

svāṃguṣṭhabrahmaṇā tulyaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ śivarakṣakam

siddhyarthaṃ sādhakānāṃ tu sūkṣmarūpamidaṃ param

ūrdhvakeśaṃ ca piṃgākṣaṃ viśvarakṣākaraṃ param

gururūpamidaṃ jñeyaṃ śaivānāṃ śivarūpakam

gurudehamidaṃ proktaṃ sṛṣṭidehamihocyate

tanurekā caturvaktraṃ netrairdvādaśiryutam

bhujāṣṭaka samopetaṃ varaṇāṣṭakasaṃyutam

ṭaṃkaśūlayutaṃ pūrvaṃ khaḍgaṃ nāgaṃ tu dakṣiṇe

digambaraṃ samaṃ devaṃ caturvaktrasamāyutam

divyagandhānuliptāṃgamāsanaṃ sarvato mukham

sandhyākhyaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ proktaṃ pinākaṃ paścimaṃ bhavet

icchājñānakriyāśakti sanmesādākhya saṃbhavet

* * * * * * * * * * śaṃ cāmarānvitam

icchājñānakriyā śaktiḥ same sādākhya saṃbhavet

* * * * * * * ra jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitam

icchājñānakriyāśaktiḥ same sādākhya saṃbhavam

sarvābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ sarvamālāsamanvitam

pāśāṃkuśaṃ ca nāgaṃ ca khaṭvāṃ varadaṃ vāmake

divyagandhānuliptāṅgaṃ divyamālāvibhūṣitam

arghyamantraṃ pravakṣyāmi vividhena dvijottama !

bhayamadhyā para hayamadhyākṣarānvitam

navātmakamidaṃ mantraṃ sarvabhūteṣvavasthitam

ādau praṇava saṃpannaṃ bindunādavibhūṣitam

arcane japakāle tu śivāya saha yojayet

kṛṣṇāsyaṃ cordhvakeśaṃ ca śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret

triṇetrāruṇa saṃkāśāṃ dhikṣyaṃ * * * * * *

padmotpaladharā devī varadābhayapāṇinī

pūrvavat * * * * * * yā nalasamanvitam

śivamuddiśyarāgādi varjanaṃ tu virāgitā

* * * śakalāyuktaṃ yatrotāntastraṇīyate (?)

śivānanda svarūpatvāda viṣṇurvandyaḥ sadā budhaiḥ

sṛṣṭisthityantamokṣāya vedāgama vivṛddhaye

prāṇināmupakārāya sādākhyaṃ tathyamuttamam

nānārūpadharo devo nānāvarṇa samanvitaḥ

na yānantaṃ samāsīnaṃ nṛttako paṃcasaumyakam

jaṭāmakuṭa saṃyuktaḥ candrārdhakṛtaśekharaḥ

sūryācandramasau netre vahninetraṃ tu pāvakam

nādaṃ kālasamopetaṃ vahnimārutasaṃyutam

ekaṃ maheśamantraṃ tu sarvasiddhikaraṃ param

bhavānī śyāmavarṇābhāpyeka vaktrā dvibāhukā

sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā

paṃcabrahma ṣaḍaṃgāni śṛṇu nandiratandritaḥ

aghoraṃ paṃcamaṃ bījaṃ puruṣaṃ rudrasaṃkhyakam

sabrahmasaṃhitaḥ śāstraiḥ saṃhitā mantrakaṃ bhavet

namaskārānta mātrāste tattaddhyānamataḥ param

sadyādīnāṃ ca varṇānāṃ triṇetrāśca caturbhujāḥ

aghoraṃ raudramityuktaṃ śeṣāḥ saumyā iti smṛtāḥ

śikhā ṣaṣṭhākṣaraṃ vidyā dvādaśaṃ kavacaṃ bhavet

savisargo bhavedastraṃ sānusvāro hṛdādayaḥ

hṛdādyastrāntakairyuktā mantrāsyuste prakīrtitāḥ

ṣaḍaṅgānāṃ ca varṇāḥsyuḥ triṇetrāśca caturbhujāḥ

raktaṃ śūlaṃ tathā śvetaṃ hema kṛṣṇaṃ ca kuṅkumam

kṣetreśaṃ caiva yakṣeśān dvāreśānparivarjayet

ādau praṇavasaṃsthānaṃ hṛdaye tadanantaram

svāhāntaṃ sarvamantreṣu homakāle viśeṣataḥ

ākāraṃ ca mukhaṃ sarvaṃ varṇānāṃ ca tataḥ param

praṇavādi prayoktavyamasugaitikamantrakam

agramantramiti proktaṃ mantraṃ pāśupataṃ śṛṇu

devasya pāśupatarūpamidaṃ praśastam

asya mantraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu tvaṃ nandikeśvara

dvitīyādyaparopetaṃ paṃcavāmākṣarānvitam

kalānte naiva cāntena caturthasvarasaṃyutam

dīkṣākāle viśeṣeṇa pāśacchedena mantrakam

nādaṃ yāntasamāyuktamādau praṇava saṃyutam

pārśve ghorāstramevaṃ niyamita manasā bhāvayetsarvaśāntim

paracakra praśamane mahāvyādhiprakopame

evamevādikāleṣu aghorasya mukhāgrataḥ

vyomavyāpi mahāmantraṃ pravakṣyāmi tavādhunā

tṛtīyaṃ trisvaraṃ proktaṃ caturthaṃ ṣaṭsvaraṃ bhavet

aṣṭamāntamiti khyātaṃ saptamaṃ tu tataḥ param

aṣṭamaṃ tānta saṃyuktaṃ tṛtīya svarabhūṣitam

asya rūpaṃ pravakṣyāmi samāsācchṛṇu sattama

anupādyamidaṃ rūpaṃ vyomākhyaṃ vyomarūpiṇam

rājābhiṣeka samaye yuddhārambhe svajanmani

kṣīrasnānaṃ madhusnānaṃ mahāsnānaṃ praśasyate

duritā nāśamāyānti prārthitaṃ phalamāpnuyāt

praśāntaṃ heti hṛdayaṃ praṇavātmannamaḥ śiraḥ

phaṭkārastu stracāstra moṃ duso netramiṣyate

yātraśaṃ hṛ ca yādīnāṃ vidyārāgaṃ ca tādṛśam

aṃkurārpaṇa kāle tu śambhorāsanakalpane

ādyantaṃ bindusaṃyuktaṃ brahmabījamiti smṛtam

saukāraṃ syāttṛtīyaṃ tu pratyekaṃ bindusaṃyutam

vyākhyāna pustakavilāsitadoścatuṣkam

yāntaṃ yānta samāyuktaṃ māyābīja samanvitam

durgābījaṃ tathā taṃ tu paṃcamasvarasaṃyutam

ekānanā triṇetrā ca ḍāḍimābhā caturbhujā

padmāsana sthitā saumyā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā

rudraśaktiriyaṃ vidyā sānnidhyā sarvasiddhidā

yataścandramiti khyātaṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ sa sūrya guṇyate

vakratuṇḍāya vidmahe hastivaktrāya dhīmahi

bindunāda samāyuktaṃ bhayaṃ sarvaṃ suvarjitam

sarvaprāṇiṣu sarvāsāṃ sarvajñānamayī śubhā

vyomākṣaraṃ ca prathamaṃ bindusandhiyutaṃ bhṛguḥ

niṣkalaṃ paramaṃ tatvaṃ sarvatatvabahiṣkṛtam

na śakyaṃ tantanādāntaṃ nalāyāti tameva ca

nāstītyabhāvamāpannaṃ paranirvāṇamātmagam

asāsantamanīśañca sarvakāraṇa kāraṇam

sadāśivaṃ śivaṃ śaktimīśaṃ rudraṃ janārdanam

sarvadevamayaṃ caiva svagvīṃ? daivataṃ yajet

mantrasiddhimavāpnoti bhaktiviśvāsa saṃyutaḥ

sahasraṃ gurupūjāntanniyogādamitaḥ param

viṣṇorvai ṣaṭsahasraṃ tu ṣaṭsahastaṃ pinākinaḥ

eti mantrajapādeva sakṛtsantatakaṃ japet

evaṃ vibhaktimātreṇa divārātraṃ japaṃ kṛtam

te naivānantarūpeṇa nāgnimūlādi rakṣati

mahānadīṃ hi vā jñāsurataivānyayathā bhavet

brahmacārī gṛhī vānaprastho vā athavā yutaḥ

brahmahatyāśca medhādyaiḥ pāpapuṇyairna lipyate

dhyātā gacchantsvayaṃ tiṣṭhan āsīnaḥ svapane'pi san

kruddho vā athavā śānto yatra tatra sthito'pi vā

jñānaṃ tatkurute vācyaṃ nānyathā śāstra koṭibhiḥ

gurau santoṣite sarvaṃ tuṣyatīha carācaram

gururmātā pitā caiva bhrātā bandhujanaścasaḥ

labdhvā tu gurukāruṇyaṃ śivājñā saṃpracoditam

tasmājjanānāṃ rudrākṣa kāraṇaṃ śivabhaktimām

araṇerlakṣaṇaṃ cāpi sṛksṛvau ca * * * *

prokṣaṇīpātra cihnaṃ ca ghṛtapātrasya lakṣaṇam

snānodañca tathādhāraṃ puṣpapātrasya lakṣaṇam

dhūpaghaṇṭā dīpapātra lakṣaṇaṃ darpaṇasya tu

cala prasādacihnaṃ ca vardhanīśivakumbhayoḥ

puṇḍarīkākṣacihnaṃ ca daṇḍalakṣaṇameva ca

pavitra lakṣaṇam tadvaduṣṇīṣottarya lakṣaṇam

kaupīnalakṣaṇaṃ caiva lambakūrcasya lakṣaṇam

sthālikā lakṣaṇaṃ caiva tripādīlakṣaṇaṃ tathā

aṣṭamaṅgalacihnamābharaṇānāṃ ca lakṣaṇam

lakṣaṇaṃ yogapaṭṭasya cāsamānāṃ ca lakṣaṇam

mānāṅgulādibhedaṃ ca tattadbhedaṃ śṛṇu kramāt

parvāyāmaṃ tu yattulyaṃ mātrāṅgulamihocyate

ṣoḍaśāṅgulamāyāmaṃ tattāraṃ vasumātrakam

guṇavedāṅgulāyāmaṃ tannāhaṃ bhūtamātrakam

raṃjvāṅgulasamāyāmaṃ tasyārdhaṃ tasya cāpalam

muṣṭiraṣṭāṅgulenaiva tannāhaṃ caṇamātrakam

araṇīlakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ sruglakṣaṇamidaṃ śṛṇu

khadireṇa karañjena caturbhūruhajena vā

vaktraṃ syātkhuri sammitaṃ na dadhare tārastribhāgaiḥ kṛtaḥ

daṇḍasyārdrasabhāganāha sahite kumbhaśca nirnāhakam

bāhye vayava nimnaṃ tu yavārdhaṃ bāhya paṭṭikā

evaṃ sruglakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ sruvo lakṣaṇakaṃ śṛṇu

sa carmanirvraṇaṃ snigdhaṃ kṛmikoṭara varjitam

yaissamidbhirhutaṃ proktaṃ taireva paridhirbhavet

paridhīnāṃ tathāṃ proktaṃ prokṣaṇīlakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu

oṣṭhamekāṃgulaṃ proktaṃ pūrṇacandravadākṛtiḥ

carusthālīsamāṅgulyamoṣṭhamekāṅgulaṃ bhavet

carusthālyāstu cihnaṃ syānmṛdā tāmreṇa vā bhavet

śatāhutyā ghṛtaṃ prasthaṃ kṣaudreṇa dviguṇaṃ bhavet

tilasarṣapamudgāṃśca yavamāṣa kulutthakāḥ

kramāduttamamadhyasthā adhamaṃ ca bhavettataḥ

netra vṛkṣapalāśaṃ vā manudvayayutaṃ tu vā

adhama meveti proktaṃ snāna jalaṃ tathā

palvalo daṃ ca śailodaṃ mārgasthamaśuci sthitam

nadyāṃ vāpi taṭāke vā plave khāte hrade'pi vā

aṣṭadroṇairathādbhistu snānamuttamamucyate

snānatoyamidaṃ proktaṃ tadbhājanamatho śṛṇu

saṃgṛhya lakṣaṇopetaṃ kṛtaṃ tu caturaśrakam

snānapātramidaṃ proktaṃ puṣpapātravidhiḥ param

vetreṇaiva tu kartavyaṃ svarṇādyairuttamaṃ bhavet

paṃcāṅgulaṃ taducchrāyaṃ vistāraṃ ca tathaiva ca

pādyārghyā camanānāṃ ca pātraṃ haimādirucyate

dhūpapātrādipātraṃ syāccāpātita tanmukham

dhūpapātramidaṃ proktaṃ dhūpaghaṇṭā tataḥ param

ghaṇṭārdhaṃ śikharaṃ proktaṃ galamardhāṅgulānvitam

tasyordhvamaṃgulaṃ pīṭhaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ vṛṣaśūlabhāk

navādhikā bhavedeva dīpādhāramanantaram

paṃcavedāṅgulāntaṃ tu navamānamudīrayet

tanmadhye mukulaṃ kuryānnānā daṇḍikayānvitaḥ

tāmreṇa paittalenā'tha cāyasenāpi vā bhavet

yāvattaṇḍula saṃpattistāvattriguṇamucyate

tadardhamupadaṃśāya pātramityabhidhīyate

asya tāraṃ samaṃ tāraṃ vidhānavalayānvitam

smṛtyaṃgulaṃ tu vistāraṃ vistārārdhena vardhayet

tadardhaṃ madhyamaṃ jñeyaṃ tadardhaṃ kanyasaṃbhavet

vidyāttrīṇyaṅgulotsedhaṃ tasyārdhaṃ dvaktranāhakam

śarāvākṛti mūle tu paramatya vidhīyate

jāmbūnadamayaṃ vātha tāraṃ vā tāmrameva vā

bhāskarāṅgulavistāramutsedhaṃ caturaṅgulam

balipātraṃ samākhyātaṃ darvī lakṣaṇamucyate

tadardhaṃ pādavistāraṃ tasyārdhaṃ madhyavāhalam

darvīlakṣaṇamevaṃ syādusṇīṣasya vidhiṃ śṛṇu

ṣaḍḍhastamadhamaṃ proktaṃ bhūtabhāgairapi smṛtam

dukūlapaṭṭadevāṅgaiḥ kārpāsenaiva vā kuru

ṣaṭkarāyāmasaṃyuktaṃ kārayennandikeśvara

tṛtīyaṃ tantunā kuryāt trividhaṃ yogapaṭṭikā

adhastādavakaṃ kuryān muṣṭi kuryāttu paṃcake

īśākṣahemarūpyaiśca samyaṅmuktimavāpnuyāt

kuśagranthimayaiḥ pāpa nāśāya ca bhavetpunaḥ

mauktikaiḥ śriyamāpnoti saubhāgyaṃ sphaṭikairbhavet

ānīyanayanānanda jalādrudrākṣa saṃbhavaḥ

trivaktraṃ tu trimūrtiḥ syāccaturvaktramajo bhavet

mātarassaptavaktraṃ tu vidyeśāścāṣṭavaktrakam

navavaktraika vaktrāntaṃ phalaṃ śataguṇottaram

rekhayāṣṭaguṇaṃ vidyāt putradīpaiḥśataṃ bhavet

sphaṭikairdaśasāhasraṃ mauktikairlakṣamucyate

kuśagranthyāśca rudrākṣairanantaguṇitaṃ bhavet

aṣṭāviṃśajjapāccaiva siddhiśca dhanadhānyadā

dhanapradaṃ kīrtikaraṃ triṃśatirjayavardhanam

japamālāvidhiścaivaṃ japasthānasya lakṣaṇam

gavāṃ goṣṭhe śataguṇaṃ nadyāṃ śatasahasrakam

samudratīre devyagre girau devālayeṣu ca

jyeṣṭhāṅgulaṃ mokṣadaṃ syāttarjanyā śatrunāśanam

kaniṣṭhākarṣaṇaṃ stambha maṅgulyādijapakramaḥ

viṃśatyaṅgulavistāraṃ jaṭharaṃ samudāhṛtam

kaṇṭhe'pyardhāṅgulotsedhaṃ vistāraṃ kauśikāṅgulam

śivakumbhamiti proktameva manyāṃśca kumbhakān

kuṇḍikā kalaśasyāṅgaṃ śeṣaṃ yuktyā tu kārayet

ṣaḍviṃśaddarbha saṃyuktaṃ ṣaḍviṃśāṅgulamānayuk

ravyaṅgulasamopetaṃ saddabhīyugalena ca

darpaṇasyāpi cihnaṃ tu dvādaśāṅgulavistṛtiḥ

evaṃ tu darpaṇaṃ proktaṃ dīpamālāvidhiṃ śṛṇu

dvāre ca dvāravatkuryāt prabhākāraṃ ca pṛṣṭhake

yājñikīvṛkṣamādāya kārayellakṣaṇānvitam

vṛṣaṃ ca makaraṃ caiva svastikaṃ matsyayugmakam

khadiraṃ toraṇa vistāramucchrāya muktam

kanyasaṃ caikamātraṃ tu madhyaṃ madhyāṅgulaṃ bhavet

nalinyākāravatpuṣpaṃ karṇikāravadākṛtiḥ

suvarṇa puṣpamevaṃ syāddhemapaṭṭavidhiṃ śṛṇu

paṭṭalakṣaṇamatroktaṃ golakā lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu

tannāhasadṛśaṃ nāhaṃ adhikaṃ somamātrakam

daṇḍalakṣaṇakaṃ vakṣye kalmāṣeṇa samanvitam

kalmāṣeṇa vihīnaṃ vā tasya lakṣaṇamucyate

ubhayogranthitormadhyaṃ parva ityabhidhīyate

dviparvā rākṣasī jñeyā māṃsaśoṇitabhojinī

kāminī rasaparvā tu sarvasiddhivivardhinī

mūrtiparvā śivānāma ata ūrdhvaṃ na kārayet

tattatsidhyaṃgulenaiva tribhirastu na siddhidam

rasāṃgulaṃ dhānya siddhiḥ tannāśaṃ saptamaṃ bhavet

mūlāttu dviguṇaṃ pucchamevaṃ daṇḍasya lakṣaṇam

bhruvo'ntaraṃ tu putrasya nāsāntaṃ samayināṃ sadā

sarvadā dhāraṇīyaṃ tu vaiṇavaṃ daṇḍamuttamam

dukūlapaṭṭasaṃkalpaṃ prasthabhasma samanvitam

vṛttamūlaṃ dṛḍhaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ susūtraṃ daṇḍasaṃsthitam

antarīye paṭe vāpi dhārayetsa śivassadā

kaupīna lakṣaṇaṃ vāpi hastaṃ vā viddhi hastakam

kaupīnamevamuktaṃ tu kārpāsādyādinā bhavet

pustakasya vidhiṃ vakṣye nandīśa śṛṇu taṃ punaḥ

vedabhūtāṅgulāntaṃ tu navamānamudīrayet

tāmreṇa vātha kartavyaṃ suṣiradvaya saṃyutam

catustribhāgayormadhyaṃ madhyaṃ ca suṣiraṃ bhavet

tribhāgaikena bhāgena kartavyaṃ yuktipūrvakam

patravaddāruyugalaṃ varṇavistāraśobhitam

ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistāraṃ pīlikākṛtamadhyagam

tīkṣṇenaiva ca kartavyaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ doṣavarjitam

calaprāsādakaṃ vakṣye prāsādakaraṇaṃ tataḥ

tāmreṇa dāraveṇāpi śarāveṇāpi vā bhavet

ghṛtavāre bahiścaiva mānāṅgulavijṛmbhaṇāt

liṃgapīṭhāttatocchrāyaṃ dviguṇaṃ garbhagehataḥ

āyāmotsedhakaṃ vāpi vardhayedaṅgulena vai

ata ūrdhvaṃ na kartavyamupānādyaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

dharātalamadhiṣṭhānaṃ vasudhāramasūrakam

pādārdhavanna sūtrasya dviguṇaṃ ceti kīrtitam

śikharaṃ caraṇāyāmaṃ tasyotsedhaṃ tadardhakam

janmamekena kartavyaṃ dvābhyāṃ vai jagatī bhavet

pakṣāṃśaṃ galamityuktaṃ ūrdhvapatramathāṃśakam

adhiṣṭhānaṃ samākhyātaṃ pādagarbhamataḥ param

ekātripadibhirvāpi bhūtapaṃktirathāpi vā

vasvaśraṃ vā ṣaḍaśraṃ vā samānākṛtirucyate

prokṣayetpaṃcagavyena hṛdayānyasya ca kramāt

tālamānasya hastasya dvividhaṃ chatramucyate

sanāladaṇḍa saṃyuktaṃ muktādāmavirājitam

cāmaraṃ maṇiṣaṇḍena bhūṣitaṃ sitavālajam

ālāmye tu tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā tu kārayet

sarvamaṅgalakāryādau vījāvāpaṃ ca kārayet

sadyoṅkuraṃ vā kurvīta vidhivannandikeśvara

dine'pi ca na kartavyaṃ kṛtaṃ cetkartṛnāśanam

pālikā navavistāraṃ paramaṃ randhramātṛkam

vistārasamamutsedhaṃ tadardhaṃ pādavistaram

pādapaṭṭaṃ samākhyātaṃ prastaraṃ cādhunocyate

ūrdhva padmamathāṃśena kapotoccaṃ guṇāṃśakam

evaṃ prastaramākhyātaṃ grīvaṃ sudartikaṃ bhavet

satāraṃ kārayeddhīmān ghaṇṭāyāścākṛtiryathā

ekena mukulotsedhaṃ suvṛttaṃ caturaśrakam

vaktratārarasāṃśaikamoṣṭamityabhidhīyate

śuddhadeśe manoramye caiśānyāṃ diśi saṃsthitam

darbhaiḥ puṣpairalaṃ kṛtya pālitāravake'pi vā

sthaṇḍilopari saṃsthāpya gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

kaṣairvālukābhiśca mṛdbhistāḥ pūrayetkramāt

etaistilairmudgamāṣaiḥ śimbasarṣapa veṇujaiḥ

kṣīraiśca dhenujairebhiḥ kavacena ca vāpayet

tatkumbhaṃ candramabhyarcya gandhapuṣpādinā tathā

aṅkurārpaṇakaṃ caiva kāryādau nandikeśvara !

dīkṣāsaṃkalpamasmākaṃ maheśa vaktumarhasi

ādau kriyāvatīṃ dīkṣāṃ vakṣye sadadhikāriṇīm

tathā dīkṣā samākhyātā icchājñāna pradāyikā

dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ kāraṇaṃ sadguruḥ smṛtaḥ

gurornirīkṣaṇādeva sarvapāpātpramucyate

śivaṃ bhavati tatsarvaṃ śivadeva prasādataḥ

tasmādguruḥ śivaḥ prokto guruśśaktirmanonmanī

śivajñānamalabdhañced jñāna yātrāṃ samāśrayet

bhinnanāvāśritassadhau yathā pāraṃ na gacchati

madhulubdho yathā bhṛṃgaḥ puṣpātpuṣpaṃ vanāntare

taṃ labdhvā visṛjecchiṣyopyadhaḥ patana niścayam

adhikāreṇa saṃyuktāpyanyā niradhikāriṇī

vidhihīnā tva paṃkādi strībāleṣu vidhīyate

nitye naimittike kāmye yogyatāsti viśeṣataḥ

evaṃ caturvidhāpyeṣā kriyādīkṣā mayoditā

parīkṣya cāmalaṃ śiṣyaṃ vilokya kṛpayā guruḥ

anugrāhyāstrayaśśāstre vijñāna pralayā kalau

malakarmasamāyuktaḥ pralayā kala eva ca

teṣāṃ kriyāvatī dīkṣā dvividhā nandikeśvara

nirādhārāhyathānyeṣāmevaṃ dīkṣā mayoditā

yā kriyā kriyate sā tu nirādhāreti kīrtitā

bhaktyā śaṃbhuśca kurute yā dīkṣā sā sāvikaraṇa

vindhye ca mandarecaiva śrīgirau himaparvate

sarasvatyāṃ payasvinyāṃ kāverī kanyakā taṭe

samudragāyā nadyā vā tīre śuddhe mahāhrade

pāsāde ca maṭhe caiva śrotriyasya gṛhe sadā

maṇḍapaṃ kārayitvā tu viraktastaṃbhasaṃyutam

vedīṃ kuryādviśeṣeṇa darpaṇodarasannibhām

hastamātraṃ tadutsedhaṃ ratnimātramathāpi vā

caturaṃgulamutsedhaṃ vistāraṃ dvyaṃgulaṃ bhavet

atha hastadvihastaṃ vā vedīṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ

mekhalāyoni saṃyuktaṃ nābhiyuktaṃ manoharam

gomayālepanaṃ kuryāddarbhamālābhirāvṛtam

puṣpamālāsamākīrṇaṃ taraṃgeṇa niveṣṭayet

śvetaraktaṃ tathā śyāmaṃ pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca varṇakam

sphaṭikairjāti liṃgotthairharidāraissajāmikaiḥ

śvetaṃ syācchālipiṣṭena raktamiṣṭikayā tathā

tuṣāṃgārabhavaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ pañcacūrṇena vā punaḥ

raktavarṇamidaṃ jñeyaṃ munipatrasya sārayuk

evaṃ bhūtairyathā labdhairdeśikendra purassaraiḥ

pūrvādi paścimāntaṃ tu trayastriṃśannyaset kramāt

punarviṃśati saṃyukta sahasra padapaṃktikam

aṣṭapatrasamāyuktaṃ karṇikākesarojvalam

karṇikā pītavarṇena raktavarṇena kesarān

karṇikābījasaṃyuktaṃ śvetaraktasamanvitam

kṛṣṇena tadanujñeyaṃ vṛttaṃ śvetamiti smṛtam

uttare hemaliṅgantu śvetaliṃgantu paścime

nair-ṛtyāṃ śyāmavarṇābhāṃ vāyavyāṃ kṛṣṇavarṇikām

avaśiṣṭaṃ yadaikatrāpyupa liṃgāṃśca svastikān

tadā liṃgodbhavaṃ hetan maṇḍalaṃ parikīrtitam

dvārādhipārcanaṃ kṛtvā dehalīvighnamutsaret

bhūtaśuddhiṃ purā kṛtvā cāntaraṃge yajetpunaḥ

kṣīranīraṃ yavaṃ caiva viśeṣācca vidhīyate

tilakaṃ svāsanaṃ caivamātmanaśca viśuddhaye

vikīrya dravyamevaṃ hi lājacandana bhasmabhiḥ

astrajaptābhisaṃyuktā nastrāṃbu prokṣitāṃstathā

pratyūhadhvaṃsakānetān kṛtvā vā nandikeśvara

śivahetyā saptajaptāṃ veṇuvāthāsi rūpiṇīm

śivaṃ ca niṣkalaṃ nyasya śivohamiti bhāvayet

uttarīyaṃ tu gāyatryā sadyojātena vāsasam

bhasmanā gandhayuktena svātmānaṃ bhūṣaṇaistathā

kṣaumaiśca vastraiśśuddhaiśca vividhābharaṇādibhiḥ

tasminnīśaṃ samabhyarcya śivahastaṃ prakalpayet

īśādabhinnamātmānaṃ kartāraṃ bhāvayettadā

kuṃbhe makhaṃ pālayati vahnyādhikaraṇaṃ tathā

svasminnati gṛhīteti pañcādhāre maheśvaraḥ

nair-ṛtyāmu śiṣṭyā sthitvā jñānakṛpāṇa yuk

catuṣpathānta saṃskārairīkṣaṇādyaissusaṃyutaḥ

tatra vikṣipta vikirāt kuśakūrcānsamūhayet

maṇḍalasyeśa digbhāge sthaṇḍilaṃ caturaśrakam

brīhyardhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ proktaṃ taṇḍulārdhaṃ tilaṃ smṛtam

sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ sarvadoṣavivarjitam

śivakuṃbhaṃ kuṃbhamevamanyatkalaśameva vā

caturhastaṃ trihastaṃ vā dvihastaṃ caikahastakam

dalāṣṭakasamopetaṃ karṇikākesarojvalam

pūrvottarātridarbhaiśca pūrvādikramaśo nyaset

viṣṇukrāntiśca pañcaite pañcauṣadhamiti smṛtam

ete pañcayavāḥ proktāḥ kuṃbheṣvetānsamarpayet

kṣetre pañca mṛdogrāhyāḥ krameṇaiva tu nikṣipet

indranīlaṃ ca gomedaṃ tathā marakataṃ tviti

hemakuṃbhaṃ ca nikṣipya navavastraiśca veṣṭayet

mātuluṃga phalaiścaiva pūrvasyāṃ śobhanāstathā

vāruṇyāṃ saṃmukhī raktaiḥ pūrayantī makhālayam

maṇḍapasyottare bhāge pūjayecchivamavyayam

nirāhāraṃ ca śraddhāluṃ śiṣyamagre nidhāpya ca

gurudroha śivadroha samayadrohavartinam

gurudoṣāvalokaṃ ca svadoṣānavalokinam

śiṣyamevaṃ vidhaṃ vāpi śāsayitvā tu deśikaḥ

vajrāsano parinyasya sarvālaṃkāra saṃyutam

piṃgalādi samāyuktaṃ bindunāda samanvitam

agnimaṇḍalamadhye tu raśmijvālāsamākulam

cakṣurbhūtassadāmantraṃ pāśastobho vidhīyate

sadācāryaḥ prasannātmā tvekacittassamāhitaḥ

tasmāddakṣiṇahaste tu rakṣāsūtraṃ prakalpayet

bhautikaṃ pūrvadigvaktraṃ paścimāsyamathāpi vā

abhimantrya guruṃ pūjya savyāṃguṣṭha vivarjitam

kavace naiva tadgranthiṃ bhasmanā rakṣaṇaṃ bhavet

naiṣṭhike tvīśamantreṇa bhautike puruṣādibhiḥ

maṇḍala nyāsapūjāṃ ca tataḥ kuryāttu deśikaḥ

īśānaṃ tatra vinyasya pūrve tatpuruṣaṃ yajet

sadyaṃ paścimadigbhāge pañcabrahma nyasetkramāt

vāyavyāṃ ca śikhā proktāpyaiśānyāṃ kavacaṃ yathā

anantādyaṣṭavidyeśā naṣṭāśāsu yajetkramāt

aṃgavidyeśvarāṇāṃ ca madhye vahnyadikoṇake

vardhanīmīśadik saṃsthāṃ siṃhasthāṃ khaḍgarūpiṇīm

vāhanāyudhadīptāṃśca dikṣu śakrādiṣu sthitān

tāṃ vardhanīṃ samādāya kuṃbhāgrānugamonmukhīm

makhamaṇḍaparakṣārthaṃ mūlamantraṃ samuccaran

dhṛtvā kuṃbhe yajedetān pūjārthaṃ nandikeśvara

śauṇḍyaṃ vinyasya cādhvānaṃ vardhanyāṃ cāstramarcayet

caturbhujaṃ caturvaktraṃ prativaktraṃ trilocanam

śaktimudgaraśūlāgni hastaṃ candrārdhaśekharam

aṃguṣṭhena spṛśetkuṃbhaṃ hṛdā muṣṭyā tu vardhanīm

jvalajjvālānalaprakhyaṃ kṣamūrdhva susaṃyutam

īśānaṃ śivakuṃbhaṃ ca puruṣaṃ pūrvakuṃbhake

sadyaṃ paścimakuṃbhaṃ tu pūjayennandikeśvara

dakṣiṇe kanyakāṃ pūjya vāruṇyāṃ ca samudrikām

sarasvatīṃ ca nair-ṛtyāṃ kāberīṃ vāyukuṃbhake

āvāhana visargāntaṃ oṃ hāṃ yuktaṃ svanāmabhiḥ

oṃ yathā kṛtaṃ yatnena bhagavanmukhamandiram

oṃkārasthaṃ caturbāhuṃ vighneśaṃ nair-ṛte yajet

candaneṣu samāyuktaṃ vāribhiḥ kṛtamaṇḍale

sthaṇḍilātsapatirmadhye sa vistāraṃ ca pūjayet

mantrāṇāṃ tṛptaye caiva kuṇḍapārśvaṃ vajedguruḥ

sarvaṃ vāme ca vinyasya samiddarbhatilādikam

agnyūrdhvamukhamadhye ca śivaṃ saṃpūjyadeśikaḥ

sṛṣṭikrameṇa vinyasya śodhyā'dhvānaṃ yathāvidhi

kuṇḍamānaṃ mukhaṃ jñātvā hṛdayenāhutirbhavet

uttare puṣṭi saubhāgya vaśyākarṣaṇa śāntike

astrāñjanapadālepā gulikāstvabhilāṣaṇe

staṃbhanoccāṭanaṃ caiva māraṇaṃ dakṣiṇe bhavet

hiraṇyā kanakā raktā kṛṣṇā caiva tu suprabhā

aiśendra vahni nair-ṛtyā vāruṇīdikpatikramāt

bahurūpā madhyataḥ sthitvā dakṣiṇottaragāminī

raktāruṇasamābhāsā kṛṣṇā nīlamaṇidyutiḥ

bahurūpā śaraccandra candrakāntasamaprabhā

hiraṇyākarṣaṇe vaśye staṃbhane kanakā bhavet

suprabhā śānti puṇyādau suraktoccāṭane bhavet

abhīṣṭavaktre jihvānāṃ hore ceyaṃ vidhīyate

indubindu śikhāyuktau jihvā bījānyanukramāt

kīlālasantu puṇyāhamābhicāre viśeṣataḥ

kāraskārādanujṛṃbhiḥ samidbhiḥ kathamantrakaiḥ

anyathāna juhuyātsādhakottamaḥ

cakravartitvalābhāya padmairmuktādi saṃpade

priyaṃhuraṃ vā puṣpāṇi samāhitamanā hunet

āmrapallavahomena viṣamajvaraśāntidam

palāśakusumaiścaiva kavitvaṃ janayetkṣaṇāt

pracetasaṃ jalebhyarcya vṛṣṭikāmo viśeṣataḥ

jagatsamastaṃ meghaughaiḥ iva saṃplāvayetsadā

saptabādhāñcaśāntyarthaṃ rudraśāntiṃ tilādibhiḥ

pūrvoktavidhinā caiva prakṛtaṃ vakṣyate kramāt

plutamūlaṃ samuccārya dadyātpūrṇāhutiṃ kramāt

athavā sarvasaṃpatyai juhuyāddeśakālayoḥ

mūlamantreṇa juhuyāt pratyekaṃ tu śatadvayam

śikhāsaṃ puṭitairmantrai huṃphaḍantaiśca dīpanam

śrīkaṇṭhādyaiḥ samālabdhāṃ badhnīyātkuṇḍamaṇḍale

vāriṇā maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā dharmajñānādirāsanam

evaṃ sthālyāṃgulaiḥ puṣpaiḥ pūjayeddeśikottamaḥ

huṃkārabīja yuktāyāḥ paścimasyāṃ viśeṣataḥ

tatra sthālīṃ samāropya śuddhāṃ kṣurika vāriṇā

astrābhijaptāṃ hāṃ kṛtvā gavyaprāsādamantritam

prasṛteḥ paṃcasaṃyuktaṃ śiṣyaṃ prati gurūttamaḥ

pradhānaṃ cāgnimantreṇa pidhānaṃ kavacena tu

śivapādakadīpinyā succhinne cullimadhyataḥ

japtvā hutvā tataḥ taptvā ntakāraṇo guruḥ

sthālīṃ saṃsthāpya tāreṇa pidhāyāsyaṃ samantataḥ

śītābhighāraṇaṃ prāpya śītalo bhavati kramāt

homaṃ kuryātsudīptāgnau haviṣā nandikeśvara

sruvaṃ nidhāya pāścātye kuṇḍamaṇḍalato guruḥ

vidhūyātsruvamevaṃ ca punassaṃhitayā hunet

dhenumudrāmṛtībhūtaṃ śāntyarthaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ nayet

dikpālebhyaścaruṃ kuryātsamidājyatrayaṃ tataḥ

astramaṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ hunet

mātṛnudbhagaṇādīnāṃ svanāmāntahṛdā tataḥ

īśamadhyāpyalabdhākṣo bhāvayedekabhāvanaḥ

asmadadhvara siddhyarthaṃ adhiṣṭhātā mahādhvare

śuklamastropahāraṃ ca samasṛstrāṇu maṇḍale

bhuktyai prāgānanaṃ kṛtvā muktyai cottaravaktrakam

paścādācamanaṃ kuryān mantrakarmavidhi kramāt

utthāpyāsanataśśiṣyaṃ muktikāmaṃ viśeṣataḥ

mūrdhādi pādaparyantaṃ muktikāṃkṣī nirīkṣayet

netrāṇāṃ saṃpradānena prasanno deśikottamaḥ

antarāyavināśārthaṃ bhasmasnānaṃ vidhīyate

mūrdhādi pādaparyantaṃ pārśvadvayamatha spṛśet

sṛṣṭisaṃhārayuktyā tu snāpayedastra bhasmanā

ūrdhvaṃ nābheḥ kuśāgreṇa śodhayedastramuccaran

pāśadvaividhyanāśāya labhedastreṇa sadguruḥ

puṣpaiḥ śiṣyaṃ samārādhya netraṃ netreṇa vā hṛdā

śiṣyāṃjalipuṭe puṣpaṃ nikṣipya śvetavarṇakam

vatkaradvayamādāya deśikāgre vrajetkramāt

praṇavāsanake nyastvā varṇādhvānaṃ niveśayet

ātmānaṃ vinyasetpaścācchiṣyadehaṃ viśodhayet

nyāsaṃ kṛtvā śivaṃ dhyātvā punaścainaṃ prapūjayet

rudre ca pada tvarthaṃ śivahastaṃ ca dāpayet

tatpādasthānamālokya dadyācchiṣyasya nāma ca

brahmakṣatriyajātīnāṃ śivāntaṃ nāmadheyakam

naiṣṭhikasya viśeṣeṇa jātibhedaṃ na cintayet

ekāraśca tathokāraḥ paṃcasvaramidaṃ bhavet

yathāsaṃkhyaṃ bhavetsiddhistasmānnaivavicārayet

jñātvāyurāditritaye teṣāmekantu dāpayet

dadyānnāma ca śiṣyāṇāṃ svecchayā vā gurūttamaḥ

aṃganā ca viśeṣeṇa śaktināma ca dāpayet

vahnīśañca guruñcaiva praṇatiṃ kārayedguruḥ

āsanopari saṃsthāpya śiṣyadakṣiṇakarṇake

maheśaṃ vimukhaṃ caivaṃ sarvapāpavināśanam

piṃgalāyāṃ maheśaṃ ca śiṣyadehe vibhāvayet

śiṣyasya hṛdayaṃ gatvā mūlamantreṇa recayet

punaḥ śivāgnināpatyannāḍī sandhānamucyate

mūlena ca śataṃ hutvā śivahastaṃ sthiraṃ bhavet

uttare pūjite liṃgaṃ vilokya kusumāni vai

śarīramarthaṃ prāṇaṃ ca sadgurubhyo nivedayet

madīyājñā prasādena kartavyaṃ tritayaṃ tu tat

maṭhaṃ vṛkṣaṃ ca gotraṃ ca gocaraṃ ca tathaiva ca

maṭhamāmardakaṃ vṛkṣaṃ vaṭaṃ gotraṃ tathaiva ca

santānaṃ nandikeśākhyaṃ śrāvayecchiṣyakānprati

kṛpayā māṃ samālokya vadasva vadatāṃvara

guruṃ pādāmbujadvandvaṃ saṃcintya gurumaṇḍale

niṣkaṃpadīpavaddhyātvā śivaṃ hṛdaya paṃkaje

utthāya ca bahirgatvā daṇḍapāṇikamaṇḍaluḥ

vidhāyāvaśyakaṃ kāryaṃ yogyaṃ deśādhikaṃ kramāt

bhasmasthāne surodyāde caityavṛkṣasamīpake

anyajātisamīpe tu setau valmīkake'pi vā

nandīgomayapāṣāṇa mūtrāsthiyuta bhūtale

skandhe vā karṇamūle vā yajñasūtraṃ ca vinyaset

bhūtale tṛṇamādhāya ardhādvātha vilokayet

nāsāgra dṛṣṭisaṃyukto diśaścānavalokayan

asnāgomayapāṣāṇa dārubhirgatamārjanam

meḍhraṃ vāmena saṃgṛhya śuddhamṛtsnāṃ ca dakṣiṇe

varjayitvā mṛdassarvāḥ tisṝḥ kāmī viśeṣataḥ

sakṛddhātrī phalasamāṃ mṛdaṃ madhyajalaistataḥ

hastadvaye saptavṛttiṃ jalairmṛjyādubhau bhujau

na vinābhāva saṃśuddhiḥ samyak śuddhiritīritā

ekaṃ yāmaṃ samāyojya kṣālayecchuddhavāriṇā

pāṇiṃ gokarṇavatkṛtvā māṣamagnaṃ jalaṃ pibet

dantānāṃ śodhanaṃ paścānmanuṣyāṇāṃ viśeṣataḥ

kaniṣṭhānāhasaṃyuktaṃ dantakāṣṭhasya lakṣaṇam

śirīṣaciribilvottha karañja khadirārjunaiḥ

āmrāpāmārgakhadira plakṣacampaka saṃbhavaiḥ

vālikerotthajāśokaiḥ pāvanaiḥ patrakairapi

eraṇḍaśigruvānīravelu paiśācabhakṣaṇaiḥ

lūśāṃgulitapolohairna kuryāddantadhāvanam

mumukṣośca viśeṣeṇa nityaṃ vaidantadhāvanam

bahirantarviśuddhyarthaṃ śivejyāyogakāraṇam

ālasyādau haridrānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ ca kṣayaṃkaram

śrīkaraṃ dīrghamāyuṣyaṃ sarvavaśyapradaṃ tathā

kūpasnānaṃ na kartavyaṃ yadi nadyādyasaṃbhave

taṭāddaśaguṇaṃ nadyāṃ anantaphalamucyate

kuṇḍodaṃ ca nipātaṃ ca vāpisnānasamaṃ bhavet

sarvatīrthāni tatsthāni śivaṃ gaṃgāvidhānataḥ

hṛdayenaiva saṃgṛhya punastenaiva citrakam

śiromantreṇa tāṃ mṛtsnāṃ nidhāyāstreṇa śodhayet

prakṣālya nābhipādāntamekāntaṃ śikhayā punaḥ

sarvāṃgaṃ ca trirālabhya niruddhyākṣāṇi sarvaśaḥ

āsīta hṛdi kālāgni samamastraṃ karaṃ tadā

jāhnavyādi mahātīrtheṣvākṛṣyāṃkuśamudrayā

hṛdamuttaratastīrthaṃ ādāyānyāni vāriṇī

ekaṃ dakṣiṇabhāgastha maṃgamastrābhimaṃtritam

evaṃ bhāgatrayaṃ kuryādastrajaptaṃ tadāharet

uttarasthaṃ samādāya śivaṃ japtabhujaṃ kramāt

aṃgajaptena sarvāṃgaṃ lepayedaṃgavidyayā

catvāriṃ hṛdayādīni jalamadhye japetkramāt

śivatīrthajale magnaḥ śivaṃ vā hṛdi saṃsmaran

vauṣaḍanta ṣaḍaṃgena juhuyācca muhurmuhuḥ

ke'bhiṣiñcetkramānnityaṃ amṛtāmbu vibhāvayet

astrābhimantritaṃ toyamabhitaḥ pariṣecayet

kuṃbhe snānaṃ prakurvīta pūrvoktavidhimantrakaiḥ

tāmrācchataguṇaṃ raupyaṃ rajatāddhaimakaṃ śatam

cakravartyupacāreṇa sugandhāmalakādinā

naiṣṭhikasyāpi kāṣāyamuttamaṃ śvetameva vā

dvādaśāṃgulavistāraṃ daśāṃgulamathāpi vā

ācamya vidhivacchuddhaḥ tatvatraya samanvitaḥ

narairetādṛśaiścaiva kharoṣṭragrāmasūkaraiḥ

gurudravyāpahartā ca tattaddravyavināśibhiḥ

adīkṣitāśca sarve'pi taissaṃspṛṣṭairviśeṣataḥ

mantravicchuklasaṃparko nābherūrdhvaṃ karau vinā

vijayaṃ śrīkaraṃ puṇyaṃ sarvapāpavināśanam

devānāṃ ca viśeṣeṇa daityānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

bhasmasaṃsparśanārthāya jalasnānaṃ puroditam

bhasmasnāna vihīnaṃ cejjantuhantṛtvamāvahet

tasmātsarva prayatnena bhasmanā snānamiṣyate

bhautikasya viśeṣeṇa tripuṇṭraṃ vidhinārdrataḥ

anukalpaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca tṛtiyamupakalpakam

varjyā vṛddhā vyādhitāśca durlakṣaṇayutāstathā

sulakṣaṇāṃ ca kapilāṃ raktāṃ śuklāṃ sitottarām

pālāśapatrake vāpi hastābhyāṃ vyomni dhārayet

uparyadhastathā kuryād visṛjenmadhyamaṃ punaḥ

aghoreṇa ca saṃśoṣya śuddhadeśe dahetpunaḥ

karpūrośīra hrīberaketakaiścampakaistathā

anukalpaṃ tataścaiva śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

kuryātpūrvoktavidhinā bhavedbhasmopakalpakam

dagdhaṃ pūrvokta vidhinā bhavedbhasmopakalpakam

saṃskāre piṇḍavatkuryādakalpamabhidhīyate

cāturvidhyamidaṃ proktaṃ sarvapāpanikṛntanam

śeṣeṇa brahmamantreṇa mūrdhādyaṃgāni dhūlayet

pañcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgādyairnivṛttyādyabhimantritam

aghoraṃ tu galaṃ nābhijāntvantaṃ vāmadevakam

sadyojātena bāhūdvau mūlamantreṇa vaktrakam

tripuṇṭraṃ kārayetpaścādbrahmaviṣṇuśivātmakam

aṣṭasthāne tathā caiva pañcasthāne'pi yojayet

nāsāvaktra galeṣvevamaṃsadvayamataḥ param

nābhau guhyadvayoścaivamūrvoḥ sphig dvīdvijānunī

amṛtyaṣṭau ca vidyeśāndikpālānvasubhissaha

pratyūṣaśca prabhāsaśca ityaṣṭa vasavaḥ smṛtāḥ

vidadhyāt ṣoḍaśasthāne tripuṇṭraṃ tu samāhitaḥ

korpare maṇibandhe ca hṛdaye nābhipārśvake

śivaśaktiṃ ca sādākhyamīśaṃ vidyākhyameva ca

bāhudvaye ca hṛdaye nābhyāmūrvoryuge tathā

teṣu sthāneṣvadhipakānyathāyogaṃ śṛṇuṣva tān

śrīścaiva hṛdaye deśe tathā nābhau prajāpatiḥ

pādayośca samudrāśca tīrthāḥ pṛṣṭhe'pi cāstrakaḥ

śiraḥsthānaṃ lalāṭaṃ ca karṇadvayamataḥ param

brahmā ca ṛṣayassapta devatāśca prakīrtitāḥ

pañca sthānānyamūnyāhuḥ paṃcamūrtiśca devatāḥ

mahāpātakayuktānāṃ kṛtaghnasyāpi darśane

tasmādviśuddhadeśe'pi cāsane saṃsthitaḥ śuciḥ

paramāṇusamaṃ bhasma bhūmau na patitaṃ bhavet

sarvapāpa vinirmuktaḥ sarvaśuddhi samanvitaḥ

bahunātra kimuktena deśikekṣaṇasannidhau

tripuṇṭradhāraṇenaiva praṇaśyatyakhilānyapi

na ca yogasamādhiśca na cejyā dānameva ca

bhasmasaṃspṛṣṭahastena vāriṇā prokṣaṇaṃ param

paścātkaupīnamutsṛjya tālamudrāsamanvitaḥ

atha māhendrakaṃ snānaṃ sūryāṃśusacivaissaha

īśamantraṃ japangacched gurubhakti viśāradaḥ

puruṣaṃ kavacaṃ vāpi japansaptapadāvadhi

mūlamantreṇa manasā prāṇāyāmasamanvitaḥ

athācamanavidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

kukkuṭāsana saṃyuktaṃ pāṇiṃ gokarṇavadbhavet

saṃgṛhyamāṣamagnāmbu savyapāṇipuṭena ca

mantraśuddhiṃ purā kṛtvā cātmavidyāśivātmikā

etatprathamapānena saṃśuddhirnandikeśvara !

pūrvaṃ praṇavamuccārya hrīṃ tvayā tatvayā svadhā

śuddhavidyeśasādākhyastritatvamiti kīrtitam

punastṛtīyapānena śaktitatvasya śodhanam

adharoṣṭhau dviḥ pramṛjyaṃ namo'ntaṃ hṛdayopari

vihāya madhya saṃsthāpastisṛbhiḥ saṃspṛśeccatau

trivāraṃ vāripānena tritatvasya priyaṃ bhavet

atharvasāmayajuṣāmadharoṣṭha pramārjanāt

madhyamāṃgulatrayeṇāpi cordhvādho mārjanaṃ bhavet

mūlamantraṃ jalenaiva pādamūrdhāvapi spṛśet

aṃguṣṭhānāmikāgreṇa netradvandvaṃ tathaiva ca

tarjanyaṃguṣṭhakenaiva pṛthvībījaṃ dhruvaṃ tathā

vyomabījaṃ dhruvaṃ caiva kaniṣṭhāṃgulisaṃyutam

tāravadbhavasaṃyuktā caturaṃgula mūrtibhiḥ

dakṣahastatalenaiva hṛnmantraṃ tu samuccaran

śivamantraṃ samuccārya paṃcāṃgulamukhena ca

kṣute niṣṭhīvane kālepyācāmantu tadā bhavet

dvirācāmed viśudhyarthaṃ bhāvaśuddhisamanvitaḥ

tathaiva saṃhatya mantrān manasā tri pravartayet

ekacittaḥ prasannātmā karāṃganyāsamācaret

kareṇa dakṣiṇenaiva dakṣiṇaṃ vāmapāṇinā

astra tejonvitau hastau maṇibandhāvanusmaran

dhyāyedamṛtarūpeṇa śaktimaṇḍalamuttamam

vakāraṃ tatpuṭebhāvyaṃ tanmadhye'pi śivāsanam

evaṃ sadāśivaṃ rūpaṃ vinyasennandikeśvara

sakalīkaraṇaṃ paścāt kaniṣṭhāṃguṣṭhakāntagam

avaguṇṭhya tanuruhau punarnābhau śivāsanam

sadāśivasya saṃprāptirnābherā brahmarandhakam

hṛdaye hṛdayaṃ paścāt śiraḥ śirasi vinyaset

netreṣu netraṃ vinyasya hastayorhastameva ca

mūlena paramīkṛtvā mahāmudrā tataḥ kramāt

śā vauṣaḍantena mantreṇaiva tu kārayet

lalāṭe tīrthamākarṣed oṃ hāṃ hṛdayāya vauṣaṭ

nikṣipejjalamadhye tu mūlamantraṣaḍaṃgakaiḥ

amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kuryād vighnaughānāṃ nivāraṇam

punarācamanaṃ kuryād yathoktaṃ tadvidhānataḥ

aruṇodayamārabhya nāḍīpaṃcakasaṃyutam

sudhāmaya jalenaiva dehasaṃveṣṭanaṃ kuryāt

raktamālyāmbaradharāṃ raktagandhānulepanām

haṃsapaṃkajamadhyasthāṃ aṣṭadravyāvalokinīm

sṛgakṣamālāṃ savyaktāṃ bāhubhyāṃ dadhatīṃ parām

tripuṇṭrāvalisaṃdīptāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām

tanmadhye susthirāṃ devīṃ sarvakarmaṇi sākṣiṇīm

vainateyābjamadhyasthāṃ ekavaktrāṃ suyauvanām

bimboṣṭhīṃ cāruvadanāṃ netrayugmāṃ sa kuṇḍalām

gadābhayaṃ ca dadhatīṃ tripuṇṭrāṃkitamastakām

dviśatakoṭiyojanavistīrṇe śvetamaṇḍale

sāyāhne śivasiddhā ye raudrīṃ nīlotpala prabhām

vṛṣapaṃkajamadhyasthāṃ ekavaktrāṃ trilocanām

sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktāṃ saṃhārakramakāriṇīm

vāme śaktyābhayaṃ devīṃ sarvakarmasu sākṣiṇīm

bindumaṇḍalamadhyasthāṃ bhāvayennandikeśvara

uttamā paramā sandhyā madhyarātre viśeṣataḥ

bisatantunibhāṃ sūkṣmāṃ raktāṃ śuklāṃ sitetarām

śivabodhāparāyā tu sā sandhyā paramā bhavet

paitraṃ tu tarjanīmūle karāgre devatīrthakam

sarvaparva svaṃgulīnāmārṣatīrthaṃ viśeṣataḥ

brāhmatīrthādadhastīrthaṃ diśānāṃ nandikeśvara

trivāraṃ hṛdayaṃ japtvā ruddhādhasthakaradvaye

ṣaḍaṃgenābhi mantryaitat kavacenāva guṇṭhitam

jalaṃ prakṣipya tadadho galitena ca vāriṇā

savyahastatile nītvā śeṣitaṃ vāri tatpunaḥ

śivāyārghyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā gāyatrīṃ manasā japet

sāyamutthāpya datvārdhyamupaviśya trilocanām

ekavaktrāṃ prasannākṣīṃ paṃkajāsana saṃsthitām

tripuṇṭrāvalibhirdīptāṃ śuklayajñopavītinīm

padmābhayānvitāṃ devīṃ caturvedaphalānvitām

trisandhyaṃ śivagāyatrīṃ japedbhakti samanvitaḥ

triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvarttya mūlamantraṃ japedbudhaḥ

sādhyānāṃ mantradevānāṃ tarpayennandikeśvara

evaṃ daśadiśāṃ tṛptiṃ kārayeddakṣiṇāya ca

tatra kaṇṭhopavītī ca ṛṣīṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kramāt

atryādi pracetāntamṛṣayo devavaṃśajāḥ

ṛbhvantasanakādīnāṃ saptānāṃ munināṃ kramāt

śivādicaṇḍaparyantaṃ daśacaṇḍāṃśca tarpayet

ākāśādipṛthivyantaṃ bhūtebhyastarpaṇaṃ kuru

kuśāgraṃ tattilaiḥ sārdhaṃ tarjanīmūla tarpaṇam

pitrādyācāryaparyantaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ pitṝṃstadā

svadhāntaṃ tarpaṇaṃ kuryād yathā vidhistathaiva ca

yatra kāre samo vāmo mantramanyāmanantaram

bālabāliśa vṛddhastrīrogiṇāṃ bhogi bhūbhujām

ācamya vidhivatsavyī karāṃganyāsamārabhet

sūryātmānaṃ svayaṃ dhyātvā sādhayedarghyabhājanam

svahastaṃ vāpi tatkṛtvā ācāmaṃ śuddhabhūtale

astreṇa prokṣayettatra raktacandanavāribhiḥ

raktapuṣpaiḥ kuśairdarbhaiḥ tilasarṣapataṇḍulaiḥ

aṃgairādityamabhyarcya kavacenāvaguṇṭhanam

āsaktaṃ proddharettatra dravyamaṇḍala dṛṣṭiyuk

mūrtimūrtīśvaraṃ japtvā dāpayedardhyavāricet

śikhordhve pūjayetsamyak gurumaṇḍalamādṛtaḥ

vahiḥ kalpita bimbe ca bhānuṃ some prapūjayet

oṃ īśānyāṃ tu gaṇeśānaṃ āgneyyāṃ gurumāvahet

āgneyyāṃ diśi paryantaṃ koṇeṣu ca yathā kramam

* * raktaṃ ca hemaṃ ca haritābhaṃ yathākramam

dīttāṃ sūkṣmāṃ rucaṃ bhadrāṃ vibhūtiṃ vimalāṃ tathā

pūrvādīśānta madhye ca navaśaktīryajetkramāt

uparyarkāsanaṃ jñātvā tasyāstraṃ maṃtramarcayet

jvalaraktātitejasya maṇḍalāntarvyavasthitam

raktāṃśukaparīdhānaṃ raktacandanacarcitaḥ

hārakeyūrakaṭakaṃ mudrāyuktāṃgulaṃ śubham

sahasraraśmisaṃyuktaṃ cintayenmūrtirūpiṇam

vyāpakaṃ mūlamantreṇa kuryādāropaṇaṃ tadā

sthāpanaṃ caiva mūrtīnāṃ mudrayā bhāvayettadā

muditā tṛmudraite atra vai vihitā hi sā

khaṣolkinā tadākuryāttadā cācamanīyakam

pinākaṃ patramudrāṃ ca darśayetsādhakottamaḥ

varadāmbuja hastāni ḍāḍimī sannibhāni ca

jvalatpāvakasaṃkeśaṃ dvinetraṃ cogravihvalam

bhāskarasya svarūpaṃ ca cintayedbhakti saṃyutaḥ

nai-ṛtyāṃ vāyudigbhāge śikhāṃ varma ca pūjayet

hṛdādīnāṃ ca karṇāṃśca dhenumudrāṃ pradarśayet

uṣāṃ devīṃ dakṣabhāge pratyuṣāṃ vāmapārśvake

sarvābharaṇa saṃyuktāṃ sarvalakṣaṇa saṃyutām

oṃ hrīṃ uṣādevyai namaḥ || oṃ hrīṃ pratyuṣāyai namaḥ

budhaṃ dakṣiṇadikpātre budhāyātha namo'ntakam

maṇḍapaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kuṇḍaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ liṃgapūjanam

aṣṭavidyeśvarānbāhye śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret

akrodha bhaktiviśvāsaṃ śarvājñā pālanaṃ sadā

gurudroha śivadroha samayadroha karmakṛt

gurudoṣāvalokaṃ ca svadoṣā na valokanam

viparītaguṇaṃ śiṣyaṃ dīkṣayetsamaye punaḥ

maṇḍalādyagnipūjāntaṃ kṛtvā vai deśikottamaḥ

agnimadhye maheśasya hṛdayāmbujamadhyame

saṃtarpya pūjāṃ kuryāt tayoḥ saṃnidhikāraṇam

śiṣyasya hṛdayaṃ paścāt puṣpeṇāstreṇa tāḍayet

tatra praviśya huṃkārayuktaye cakrayo gatā

śivaṃ prāpto'pi caitanyaṃ śiṣyasya vadanāntaram

nirdhūme jvalite vahnau homaḥ sarvārthasiddhidaḥ

pradakṣiṇa śikhaḥ snigdhaḥ sugandhastejasā yutaḥ

uddīptaḥ susamāyuktaḥ śveto raktaḥ śubhapradaḥ

mīnaṃ ca śiśunirghoṣaḥ kauśikasvarasannibhaḥ

śiṣyasya pāpacihnāni pāvakena vilokayet

kṛtaghno deva nindaśca jñāninindastathaiva ca

mahāpātakināmeṣāṃ viṣṭhāgandhirbhavettadā

vālasya vardhake vahni sphuṭama sphuṭayetkṣaṇāt

pāvakasya guṇaiḥ śiṣya kalmaṣaṃ jñāpayedbhṛśam

dvirjaptvā pāpanāśāya tathā rudrāṃśaṃ bhāvayet

sīmantonnayane caiva jātakarmaṇi nāma ca

niṣkrāmaṇe tathānnasya prāśane caulakarmaṇi

vivāhe vanavāsatve yatitve nandikeśvara !

caturāśramaniṣṭheṣu tattadāśramakā vidhi

gṛhasthāraṇyavāsitvaṃ yatitvaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

śithilībhūtabandhasya śaktādurkarṣaṇaṃ tu yat

svātantryādi guṇaṃ vyaktistathā puṃsavanaṃ bhavet

śivādisadasadvastu svīkāro jananaṃ bhavet

śivatvasya praveśo hi niṣkrāmaṇamitīryate

śiva prasādotkarṣantu yattaccūḍātmanā bhavet

indriyāṇāṃ ca saṃrodho vratādeśa iti smṛtaḥ

sarveṣu nirapekṣitva sadbhāvaḥ snānamucyate

anantaśakti viśleṣo vānaprasthatvamiṣyate

śikhābāhyopavītādi saṃtyajedyadi taṃ prati

catvāriṃśattu saṃskārāḥ sārabhūtāḥ prakīrtitāḥ

saṃhāramudrayādāya gurorhṛdayapaṃkaje

śivayossamarasībhāvaṃ mūlamantreṇa kārayet

ātmānaṃ śivasadbhāvaṃ nītvā sadbhāvayogataḥ

mantreṇa recakenaiva śiṣyasya hṛdayāmbuje

śivamagniṃ ca saṃpūjya pūjayitvārbhakeṇa ca

śiṣyasya samayātsarvāt śrāvayedgurusattamaḥ

sādhakaṃ jñānavṛddhaṃ ca jyeṣṭhaṃ bhaktisamanvitam

nirmālyantu na bhuñjīta śivabhaktānna nindayet

śivāgni gurupūjā vai kartavyā jīvikārthibhiḥ

niyamena ca kartavyaṃ catuṣṭayamapi kramāt

adīkṣitasya bandhutvaṃ kṛtaghnatvāvivekatām

eteṣāṃ saptaviṃśānāṃ śrāvakāṇāṃ viśeṣataḥ

pūrvavatpañcagavyaṃ ca carusaṃbhojanaṃ bhavet

vratāṃgapoṣāssarve'pi yogyameva tadādikam

pañcabrahmatadaṃgaiśca parijapya yathoditam

labdhvā śivājñāṃ yogyāya vratānetānvadedguruḥ

samādāya hṛdādevaṃ dharecchiṣyopasaṃyutaḥ

liṃgārcanaṃ cāpi tathaiva kuryāt

yogadaṃ jñānadaṃ caiva mokṣadaṃ bhaktivardhanam

ātmārthaṃ ca parārthaṃ ca dvividhaṃ śivapūjanam

pūjāphalaṃ tu sāmānye sarvabhūtāya nirdiśet

sarvatatvādikaṃ jñeyaṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇam

tatprabhā śaktirityuktā pīṭhikā saiva nādyathā

līyate nikhilaṃ tasmin layatvālliṃgamucyate

tṛptirbhavati śākhānāṃ vṛkṣamūle jalārpaṇāt

ātmārthaṃ caraliṃgaṃ ca tadviśeṣācchṛṇuṣvatha

śailajaṃ caikabhedaṃ syād bhogamokṣa phalapradam

māṇikyaṃ ca prabālaṃ ca vaiḍūryaṃ sphāṭikaṃ tathā

māṇikyaṃ śrīpradaṃ jñeyaṃ pravālaṃ vaśyadaṃ bhavet

śatrukṣayakaraṃ vajraṃ marakataṃ puṣṭivardhanam

evaṃ ratnajamākhyātaṃ lohajaṃ punarucyate

āyasaṃ sīsakaṃ caiva trapukaṃ caiva lohajam

tāmraṃ putrapradaṃ caiva viṣamaṃ kāṃsyameva ca

sīsakaṃ rogaharaṇaṃ tripu cāyuṣyavardhanam

narmadādyāditīrtheṣu saṃgameṣu samutthitam

salliṃgaṃ piṇḍikāyuktaṃ bhuktimukti phalapradam

śaucamācamanaṃ snānaṃ pūrvoktavidhinā saha

uttarābhimukho bhūtvā rucirāsanamāsthitaḥ

karāṃganyāsakaṃ kṛtvā pūjādravyāṇi kalpayet

śrīkaraṃ jñānadaṃ caiva pūrvadvārasya pūjanam

paścimadvārapūjāyaṃ vairāgyaṃ prābhavantathā

udagdvāraṃ na kartavyaṃ pūrvadvāramathocyate

astramantreṇa saṃprokṣya pūjayed dvārapālakān

raktavarṇaṃ caturbāhuṃ ṭaṃkāṃkuśasamanvitam

dvārasyordhvottare bhāge pūjayecca sarasvatīm

tayormadhye mahālakṣmīṃ svarṇavarṇāṃ vibhūṣitām

caturbhujāṃ trinetrāṃ ca sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām

mudgaraṃ tarjanīṃ caiva śūlamakṣasrajaṃ tathā

śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśāṃ dvinetrāṃ dvibhujāṃ śivām

makarāsanamārūḍhāṃ sudhākalaśamutpalam

gaṃgāṃ ca nandinaṃ caiva śākhādakṣiṇake yajet

mudgaraṃ ca triśūlaṃ ca śūnyamudrāṃ kapālakam

oṃ hāṃ mahākālāya nama iti mantramuccaret

nīlotpalasudhāpūrṇakalaśena samanvitām

śākhāyā uttare bhāge oṃ hāṃ yamunāyai namaḥ

aṃguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ oṃ hāṃ hauṃ śivāya namontam

santyajya divyavighnāṃśca punarnārāca mudrayā

ityantarikṣa vighnānāṃ nirāsannandikeśvara !

huṃphaḍantāstramantreṇa vighnanissaraṇaṃ bhavet

dakṣiṇenaiva pādena praviśenmandiraṃ punaḥ

caturvaktraṃ caturbāhuṃ pradīptāgnisamaprabham

jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktaṃ mūrdhendukṛtaśekharam

dehalīmadhyame caiva vāstudevasya pūjanam

raktavarṇaṃ caturbāhuṃ triṇetraṃ candraśekharam

paraśuṃ ca mṛgaṃ caiva dhāriṇaṃ gaṇanāyakam

sarvābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ bhasmoddhūlitavigraham

rudrākṣālambamālāṃ ca hastamālāṃ tathaiva ca

evaṃ ca nandinaṃ pūjya yaśo devīṃ ca vāmake

sarvabhūṣaṇa saṃyuktāṃ sarvalakṣaṇasundarīm

oṃ hāṃ hrīṃ ca samuccārya yaśodevyai namaśca hi

aiśānye svagurau bhaktyā cāsanaṃ pūjayettataḥ

pañcaśuddhikrameṇaiva pūjayecchivamavyayam

dravyaśuddhistṛtīyāsyāccaturthaṃ mantraśuddhikā

trivāraṃ mūlamantreṇa japtvā saṃhāramudrayā

oṃ hiṃ sadyojāta mūrtaye nama ityapi

oṃ huṃ pūrvamuccārya aghoraṃ hṛdayāya ca

vaktrāya nama ityante oṃ homīśānamūrtaye

ṣaḍaṃgaṃ tu tataḥ kuryādoṃ hāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ

vaṣaḍantaṃ samuccārya tato oṃ haiṃ kavacāya hum

aṃgulīṣu saparyā su tadagreṣu ca vinyaset

mūlamantraṃ samuccārya mūrdhādyarghyādyasaṃyutam

śikhāgre dvādaśāṃgulye sahasradalasaṃyutam

cicchaktimātraṃ rūpaṃ ca tārakarṇikayojvalam

paṃcākṣara sa bījāḍhyaṃ yasya mukhetatpuruṣaṃ nyaset

sadyojātaṃ nyasetpādau makuṭādi kramonyaset

śikhāṃ śikhāyāṃ vinyasya kavacaṃ stana madhyataḥ

oṃkāraṃ mūrdhni vinyasya ākāraṃ hṛdi vinyaset

akārādikṣakārāntaṃ śrīkaṇṭhaṃ vinyasetkramāt

adhiśobhārabhūtiścātithīśaḥ sthāṇuko haraḥ

akrūraśca mahāsenaḥ svarāṇāṃ mūrtayo bhavet

ekarudrastathā kūrma ekadruccaturānanaḥ

ardhanārīśvaraścomākāntaścāṣāḍhadaṇḍinau

calaraṇḍo dviraṇḍaśca samahākālatāninau

śvetebhra kūlakūlī ca śivassaṃvartakaḥ smṛtaḥ

lohālohāvartulā tu dīrghakoṇā tathaiva ca

kuṇḍodaryūrdhvakeśinyau mukhī vikṛtapūrvikā

mahākālī sarasvatyau sarvasiddhiyutau tathā

bhūtaṃ mātā vidyate thā lambodarī nāgaryayau

kodarī pūtanā bhadrā kālīyoginya eva ca

kapardinī tathā bhadrājayā ca sumukheśvarī

rakṣovadhāriṇīcānyā tathaiva sa gayā ca yā

etābhiḥ śaktibhiḥ sārdhaṃ vinyasedakṣarānkramāt

ikāraṃ dakṣiṇe netre īkāraṃ vāmanetrake

ṛkāraṃ dakṣiṇe nāsi ṝkāraṃ vāmanāsike

ekāraṃ tu tathordhvoṣṭhe aikāramadhare tataḥ

aṃkāraṃ tālumūle ca aḥkāraṃ tālumadhyataḥ

kavargaṃ dakṣiṇe haste cavargaṃ vāmahastake

pavargaṃ pṛṣṭhadeśe tu hakāraṃ nābhyadhaḥsthitaḥ

tvagasṛṅmāṃsa medo'sthimajjāśuklā * * * *

kṣakāraṃ guhyake nyasya nyāsamantra samanvitaḥ

tatpuruṣāya vaktrāya aghorāya hṛdayāya vā

tāraṃ bījaṃ ca śaktiṃ ca mantrādyaikaṃ tamontikam

śāntyatītāni mūrdhādi caraṇāni nyasetkramāt

īśānasya kalāḥ pañca puruṣasya catuṣkalāḥ

aṣṭau sadyakalā jñeyā makuṭādi kramānnyaset

tatpuruṣo mahādevo tanno rudraḥ pracodayāt

tato ghoratarebhyaśca sarvataḥ śarva ityapi

vāmo jyeṣṭhaśca rudraśca kalavikaraṇastathā

manaśca unmanaścaiva vāmadevakalāḥ smṛtāḥ

tathānādibhavaścaiva bhavaskabhavadudbhavaḥ

śaśinīi cāṃgagā ceṣā marīca jvālinī tathā

nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyā śāntistathaiva ca

tamo dohākṣayā nidrā dhṛtiḥ kṣāntiḥ smṛtiḥ kṣamā

rajapakṣāpatiḥ pālyā kāmāṃsyaṃdhamanī kriyā

vāmadevakalāśaktistrayodaśa samīritāḥ

sadyaścāṣṭakalāśaktirīritā nandikeśvara

ūrdhvavaktre śaktyatītāṃ pūrvādi puruṣodbhavaḥ

aghorahṛdaye karṇe savye vāmakare tathā

vāmadevaṃ ca guhye ca tathaiva vṛṣaṇadvaye

sphiktvajye ca kaṭīdeśe pārśvayorvinyasetkramāt

nāsikāyāṃ śirodeśe bāhuyugme ca vinyaset

kevalaṃ bījamantraiśca devavaktrādiraṃgakaiḥ

antaryāgaṃ tataḥ kuryād dhyānapūjāpurassaram

tannābhikandātsaṃbhūtaṃ dalāṣṭaka samanvitam

ādhāraśaktimārabhya kṣityantaṃ cāsanaṃ bhavet

āvāhanādibalyantaṃ mantrāṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ tathā

sadāśivasvarūpe tu bhaktiyukto viśeṣataḥ

śivāgniṃ pūjayettatra baliṃ yājanavatkramāt

dvādaśāntāmṛtenaiva kartavyaṃ nandikeśvara

lalāṭadeśamadhyasthaṃ vimalaṃ cintayetsadā

prokṣayedastramantreṇa sāmānyārghyajalena ca

pātraśuddhistatastatra tattaddravyaṃ ca śodhayet

palāśapadmapatrotthaṃ dāravaṃ mṛttikāmayam

prakṣālyāstrāmbunā paścāccarmaṇādhomukhaṃ kuru

binduprasṛtapīyūṣarūpatoyena pūrayet

elālavaṃgatakkolajātikarpūrasaṃyutam

dhūpakṣīrakuśāgrāṇi puṣpadūrvākṣatāni ca

śuddhagandhena toyena hṛdayena ca pūrayet

candanāgarukarpūrakoṣṭhakuṃkuma patrakam

ṣaḍaṃgena hṛdā pūrya pūrayitvā'bhimantrayet

puṣpaṃ ca nikṣipetpātre śodhayeddhenumudrayā

dūrvāmrabilva jambūkamunipatrakapitthajam

saptacchadaṃ priyaṃguṃ ca lavaṃgaṃ nāgakesaram

kirañcāraṇḍakaṃ guñjā dīrghavṛttaṃ ca campakam

tamālaścāgnimanthaśca pāṭalo varukā tathā

bhṛṃgarājaśca krīṇīhidānavaṃ sumukhī tathā

devatālī ca mallī ca kaṃkirātaṃ tathaiva ca

hrīberaṃ granthiparṇī ca mahāpadmaṃ tathaiva ca

vyāghrāta puṣpameteṣāṃ supatraiḥ puṣpakairapi

sāyaṃ ca mallikā proktā niśīthe'pi ca mālatī

mukulaṃ patitaṃ śīrṇamāghrātaṃ jantu dūṣitam

sarveṣāṃ padmapuṣpāṇāṃ nīlotpalamidaṃ param

bhaktiḥ śraddhābhiyuktā cettasmādabhyadhikaṃ phalam

ekacittassusaṃyukto ninditaiścāpi pūjayet

puṣpābhāve'pi patraṃ syātpatrābhāve phalaṃ bhavet

pūjopakārapuṣpādi niṣiñcedarghyavāriṇā

dravyaśuddhiriyaṃ proktā mantraśuddhirathocyate

sarvamantraṃ ca tanmadhye namaskārāntamuccaret

divā pūjāṃ ca gṛhṇāti sūryassomastathā niśi

candrārkanāśanaṃ labdhvā guruvaktreṇa pūjayet

pañcagavyaṃ ca saṃkalpya snapanārthaṃ viśeṣataḥ

gomayenopalipyātha maṇḍalaṃ caturaśrakam

pañcakoṣṭhaṃ prakalpyeta madhye śivapadaṃ yajet

uttare puruṣaṃ tatvaṃ paścime kālatatvakam

supratiṣṭhaṃ ca śāntaṃ ca tejorūpaṃ yathā tathā

madhyekṣīra kuḍumbaṃ pūrva prasthāsya dadhyutaḥ

uttare prasthagomūtraṃ gomayaṃ gokulāhṛtam

kuśodakaṃ caiśadeśe pūrayecchuddhavāriṇā

dvādaśaṃ pañcakaṃ vāpi tribhirvā saṃprakalpayet

grantherupari cāgrāṇi caturaṃgulameva ca

īśānaṃ kṣīrapātre tu vāramātraṃ japetkramāt

trivāraṃ ghoramantreṇa ghṛtapātraṃ tu dakṣiṇe

sadyaḥ paścimabhāge ca vinyasetpañcavārakam

madhya pātre tu dadhyādīnmūlamantreṇa yojayet

pañcagavyaṃ ca purataḥ pūrvavatsthaṇḍilaṃ bhavet

uttare madhu saṃyojya śarkarā paścime bhavet

pañcāmṛtamidaṃ proktaṃ śivasya snapanaṃ bhavet

saṃmukhīkṛtya deveśaṃ pūjane copacārakam

kavace nāvaguṇṭhyaiva dhenumudrāṃ pradarśayet

* * * pe ca yāge ca bhojane pūjane tathā

āvaśya bandhanairvastraiḥ sadāvācaṃyamo bhavet

tārayitvā mahāghaṇṭānnirāsāyaśca lakṣaṇam

gāyatryā liṃgamabhyarcya pañcabrahmabhirarcayet

kaniṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tu puṣpaṃ saṃgṛhya saṃyutaḥ

kadācitpuṣpaśūnyaṃ tu na kuryālliṃgamūrdhani

astreṇa piṇḍitāliṃgaṃ prakṣālya ca muhurmuhuḥ

sakalīkaraṇaṃ paścāt kaniṣṭhādyaṃ gulāntakam

kūrmamudrāṃ parityajya mahāmudrāṃ prakalpayet

koṭikoṭi śivasyāstraṃ rakṣārthaṃ vighnavāraṇam

prākāraṃ kavacenaiva parighāveṣṭitaṃ bahiḥ

darśayettu mahāmudrāṃ mastakāccaraṇāvadhi

vāmena ṣoḍaśaṃ mātrāṃ akāreṇa tu pūrayet

dvātriṃśanmātrayārecyaṃ makāreṇa viśeṣataḥ

netrahastatale nyasya tarjanyā cāstrakaṃ bhavet

aṃguṣṭhādi kaniṣṭhāntamaiśānyādiśca sṛṣṭikam

na tu trayaṃ śikhāṃ caiva śirohṛdayādinā kramāt

īśānaṃ mūrdhni vinyasya * * * * * * * *

parātparataraṃ paścāt parātparamanantaram

jñānayoga vihīnaśced dīpākāraṃ vicintayet

evaṃ bimbacatuṣkāṇāṃ ūrdhve saṃhāra mudrayā

* * * * * * tmānaṃ pāṇi kaśchapamudrayā

* * * * * * * * kalinaṃ vajralāṃchitam

hāṃkāraṃ pañca * * * * * śśchedakaṃ bhavet

evaṃ bhūmaṇḍalaṃ cintya vāruṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tathā

padmadvitīyasaṃyuktaṃ śirodhāma samanvitam

āpyamevaṃ susaṃcintya praṇavaṃ pūrvamuccaran

rudrādhidaivataṃ bimbaṃ cintayennandikeśvara

bījādhi daivataṃ vāyuḥ śāntyā ca kalayānvitam

auṃkāramādau saṃjapya haiṃ huṃ phaṭ kakakāstathā

bhrūdeśādbrahmarandhrāntaṃ śuddhasphaṭikasannibham

śabda jama devassadāśiva itīritaḥ

sadāśivoparigato bindunāthastatopari

śivo brahma parandhāma paramātmā sanātanaḥ

avyaktaṃ vyaktāvyaktaṃ vācyāvācyamanaupamam

guruprasādamārgeṇa sarvaṃ sidhyati sarvadā

atha mārutarandhreṇa pṛthivīṃ śoṣayettataḥ

brahmaṇe nikhilaṃ dadyādviṣṇave vārimaṇḍalam

rudrāya dadyāttatsarvaṃ vahniṃ toyena śodhayet

pṛthivyāṃ vāyuṃ saṃśodhya vyomaśabdaguṇādikam

athavā svaśarīraṃ ca vaṭavṛkṣādikaṃ smaran

pādāṃguṣṭhadvayormadhye vicintyasuṣiraṃ tanau

hyaiṃkāraśāntiyuktena kumbhakenātha śodhayet

hrāṃkāreṇāgnibījena suṣiraṃ cintayetpunaḥ

huṃ phaṭ tataśca kākhyena tatsarvaṃ hṛdi bhāvayet

recakākhyena kartavyaṃ nabhorūpamatītayā

ekoccāreṇa kartavyaṃ nabhorūpaṃ tu dhāraṇāt

śirohyananta padmādyaiḥ saurīṃ dhārāṃ vicintayet

brahmarandhra śāntyatītakalāvadhiḥ

sāmānyārghya jalenaiva snāpayelliṃga śuddhaye

itthamātmāśrayaṃ dravyaṃ mantraliṃgaṃ viśuddhiṣu

tāmrapīṭhe ca nair-ṛtyāṃ pūjayettu gajānanam

āvāhanādikaṃ sarvaṃ svasvamantreṇa kārayet

brāhme viriñca vāruṇyāṃ āgneyyāṃ mahiṣāntakīm

praṇavaṃ pūrvamuccārya hrīṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ

samastādhāramādheyaṃ gurumantraṃ mahādbhutam

oṃ hāṃ hīṃ samuccārya dakṣiṇāmūrtaye namaḥ

oṃ hāṃ brahmeśvarāyeti namo'ntaṃ mantramuccaret

oṃ hāṃ skandāya saṃbhāṣya namaskārāntamīritam

pūrvaṃ kūrmaśivaṃ dhyātvā mūlaprakṛti vigraham

somasūryāgni tejo'yaṃ yadyattattanmayo vibhuḥ

na vyavasthā viśeṣeṇa na bhūteṣu guṇeṣu ca

svasaṃvedyasvarūpāṃ tāṃ darśanīyāṃ gurormukhāt

pradhāna kūrmamityuktaṃ śaktiḥ paryāyavācakam

prathamaṃ tāramuccārya hāṃ kūrmāya namo'ntakam

kūrmakundaprarohābhāṃ nādarūpojvalākṛtim

śaradindu pratīkāśaṃ mṛṇālasadṛśa prabham

oṃ hāṃ skandāya ca prokto namaskāramataḥ param

bimbapravālavarṇābhamaṃkuraṃ rajata prabham

śaṃkhavarṇā dalāgrāśca raktāścopadalāḥ smṛtāḥ

tanmadhye kalayeddhīmānkarṇikāṃ kanakaprabhām

anantākhyāsanaṃ vakṣye samāhitamanāḥ śṛṇu

vairāgyaṃ pītabhūtaṃ syādārogyaṃ śvetakuñjaraḥ

adharmājñānāvairāgyamanaiśvaryaṃ ca kalpayet

prathamaṃ nāgarājānamanantaṃ vāsukiṃ tathā

mahāpadmaṃ śaṃkhapālaṃ gulikaṃ navamaṃ smṛtam

phaṇapañca trayaṃ caikaṃ pucchādārabhya nāgavat

sumukhaṃ kuṇḍalopetaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam

varadābhayadaṃ hastaṃ suvṛttiṃ tadvaraṃ tataḥ

ekaikaphaṇasaṃyuktaṃ kṛtāñjalipuṭānvitam

oṃ hāṃ pūrvaṃ samuccārya nāmamantreṇa pūjayet

nāgādhipa śirodeśe kalpayennandikeśvara

avyakto niyatiḥkālaḥ kalā ceti catuṣṭayam

avyaktaḥ sarvanaijābho yatiḥ kālāñjanadyutiḥ

oṃ hāṃ caivaṃ samuccārya svasvanāmnaiva pūjayet

caturbāhuṃ triṇetraṃ ca śaṃkhacakrābhayānvitam

ūrdhvādhomukhapadmaṃ ca kalpayettasya mūrdhani

ūrdhve'pyevaṃ samuccārya namo'ntaṃ yogapīṭhake

yogāsanamidaṃ proktamaṣṭakoṇayutaṃ param

ūrdhvacchadaṃ tu saṃpūjya daśabījaṃ nyasetkramāt

īkāraṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ syādūkāraṃ ca caturthakam

vyāpiṃ ca saptamaṃ proktaṃ akṣaradvaya saṃyutam

daśabījamidaṃ proktaṃ pūrvādīśāntakaṃ nyaset

koṭikoṭikasaṃghaiśca pratyekaṃ parivāritam

daṃṣṭrākarālavadanaiḥ triṇetrairbhīmarūpibhiḥ

caturaśraṃ ca siṃhākhyaṃ padmāsanamataḥ param

śvetaṃ prāptassuvarṇābhaṃ catuṣṣaṣṭikakesaram

vāmā jyeṣṭhā ca raudrī ca kālī kalavikariṇī tathā

sarvabhūtadamanī caiva manonmanyantaśaktayaḥ

caturbhujāstriṇetrāśca ghṛtacāmarahastakāḥ

pūrvādīśāna patrāntaṃ kesarāgreṣu vinyaset

oṃ hāṃ pūrvaṃ samuccārya namo'ntaṃ svasvanāmabhiḥ

koṭyādityapratīkāśaṃ śivasūryasya maṇḍalam

pañcavaktraṃ caturbāhuṃ prativaktraṃ trilocanam

namo rudrānvitaṃ devaṃ sṛṣṭikāraṇakāraṇam

maṇḍalāntaṃ punaścoktvā tvadhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ

tathaiva vahnibījaṃ ca rudraṃ tatraiva mantrayuk

koṭicandradyutīyuktaṃ koṭicandrasuśītalam

bhasmoddhūlitasarvāṃgaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam

caturbhujaṃ śaṃkhacakraṃ namaskāra samanvitam

kirīṭa makuṭopetaṃ vidyātatva svarūpiṇam

āgneya lantasya viṣṇormūrdhni suvinyaset

rudraṃ māṇikyavarṇābhaṃ ekāsyaṃ ca trilocanam

bhasmoddhūlita sarvāṃgaṃ tripuṇṭreṇa virājitam

tatvatrayādhi pāścaiva sarvavidyāsamanvitāḥ

vimalāsanamevoktaṃ bimbatrayasamanvitam

dalaiḥ sahasraiḥ saṃkīrṇaṃ indugokṣīrasannibham

kṣityādiśuddhavidyāntaṃ tatvavyāpakamānasam

ātmatrayaṃ ca saṃpūjya praṇavākāra madhyame

tanmadhye devadeveśaṃ parameśaṃ tu bhāvayet

pañcavaktrayutaṃ teṣu prativaktraṃ trilocanam

śūlaṃ paraśukhaḍgaṃ ca vajrābhaye ca dakṣiṇe

sarvāvayasaṃpūrṇaṃ divyāmbaradharaṃ śubham

sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktamardhendukṛtaśekharam

prasannavadanopetaṃ praśāntaṃ sarvakāraṇam

evaṃ sadāśivaṃ nyasya mūrtiśaktyāntagocaram

anenaiva tu mantreṇa cidānandamayaṃ prabhum

brahmādikāraṇaṃ tyaktvā mantraṃ nītvā śivāspadam

tadbindunādasaṃyuktaṃ śivaṃ paramakāraṇam

kusumāñjalikaṃ bhāvyaṃ lakṣamūrtau praveśayet

athavā sūkṣmamūrtīśaṃ bhūtatatvaṃ sabindukam

oṃ hāṃ auṃ vidyādehāya namontamantramuccārayet

sadāsāṃ mukhyamūrtiṃ tāṃ liṃgadairghyaṃ ca pūjayet

aghoravidyāhṛdayaṃ vāmaprekṣaṇaguhyakam

brahmāntaṃ durdharaṃ devaṃ paramāṃsa virājitam

dṛkkriyecchātripañcākṣa jñānacandrakalotthitam

puruṣaṃ kuṃkumākāramaghoraṃ bhṛṃgabhāsvaram

śaṃkhakundendugokṣīrasadṛśaṃ savyavaktrakam

āvāhana kramādīnāṃ svarūpaṃ kathyate śṛṇu

āvāhanamitiproktaṃ mūrttyavacchedanena tu

brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ

dvādaśāntonmanīśakti vyāpinaṃ parameśvaram

puṣpayuktakaradvandve hṛnmantre vinyasetkramāt

baddhāñjaliṃ ca hastābhyāṃ kaniṣṭhāmūlaparvaṇoḥ

āvāhanamidaṃ proktaṃ sthāpanaṃ tadanantaram

avicchinnopanītaṃ tu * * * * * kīrtitaḥ

parameśatan dhyāyet svasvakāra pradarśanam

saṃsaktā suśritāṃguṣṭhau mudrā syātsaṃnidhāpinī

nirodhassatu vijñeyaḥ tadvatsyāpi varasya tu

niṣṭhureti ca sā proktā kāmikādi śivāgame

ayogyeṣvaprakāśāyestaṃ bhavedāvakuṇṭhanam

tata āpādasaṃsparśān mudrā syādavakuṇṭhanī

liṃgamudrāṃ namo mudrāṃ darśayennandikeśvara

pāṇibhyāmañjaliṃ kṛtvā hṛdaye tu niveśanam

anāmike tu saṃveṣṭya tarjanībhyāṃ tu madhyame

kṛtvā tu yonimudreyaṃ liṃgapīṭhe pradarśayet

ajināmaṃga saṃyuktiramṛtīkaraṇaṃ param

tarjanībhyāṃ madhyame tu kaniṣṭhābhyāmanāmike

athoktvā tu prayuktena gostanākāravadbhavet

nijāṃgairdevadevo'pi dhyātavyo nandikeśvara

śivatvaṃ nānyadhīnaṃ yat sā śikhopari saṃsthitā

sarvakāle'pi sarvatra sadāvasthā su santatam

netraṃ taditi vijñeyaṃ deveśasya śivasya tu

deveśasyāstramityuktaṃ abhedyaṃ devadānavaiḥ

jyeṣṭhāgraṃ ca tu vinyasya madhyamānāmikāntare

hṛdayākhyā bhavenmudrā saṃsaktāṃguṣṭhamuṣṭikam

ucchritāṃguṣṭhakaṃ muṣṭiṃ śiraḥ pṛṣṭhe niveśayet

baddhamuṣṭikarau kṛtvā sāritāṃguṣṭhatarjanī

bhavatkavaca mudreyaṃ sarvarakṣākarī matā

anāmikāṃ ca netrākhyā madhyamāṃguli sāraṇā

choṭikāmastramudreyaṃ sarvopadravahāriṇī

tanmudrāṃ visṛjetpaścādāpādatalalambitām

prabhākaravatī kṛtya prarocya śivamavyayam

vaktreṣvācamanaṃ dadyāt svadhānte prāṅmukhādiṣu

īśānādi sadyojātāntaṃ śivasyārghyaṃ pradarśayet

gandhaṃ ca puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ dūrvākṣatānvitaḥ

gandhaṃ lalāṭe puṣpaṃ ca mūrdhni dhūpaṃ tathaiva ca

candanaṃ dviguṇaṃ proktamīṣatkarpūrasaṃyutam

netre pradeśe dīpaṃ ca pārśve pṛṣṭhe nivedayet

pañcagavyābhiṣekaṃ ca mūlamantreṇa kārayet

madhye madhye ca dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ caiva pradāpayet

sugandhiśālipiṣṭena pīṭhaṃ devaṃ vighṛṣya ca

namontaṃ kavacenaiva nirmārjanamatho bhavet

uttiṣṭhamānaṃ saṃbhāvya oṃ hāṃ hauṃ śivāya namaḥ

mahāsnānaṃ tataḥ kāryaṃ gandhairāmalakādibhiḥ

śataṃ sāṣṭottaraṃ deyamabhiṣekaṃ samācaret

puṣpaṃ yatpūrvaṃ vinyastaṃ tadarghyeṇa visarjayet

jalaṃ deyaṃ liṃgamūrdhni śeṣaṃ vedyāṃ vinikṣipet

tāvatsnānaṃ prakartavyamabhiṣekaṃ kriyāya ca

saṃmārjya sūkṣmaśuddhena vastreṇa tadanantaram

himāmbumiśritairgandhaiḥ lepayeddeva vigrahe

nibandhayedadhomudrāṃ nopari bhrāmayetkaram

vastramābharaṇaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpadīpau dadedbudhaḥ

śirasā puṣpamabhyarcya ghaṇṭāmantreṇa tāḍayet

īśānaṃ karṇikāmadhye pūrve tatpuruṣaṃ yajet

sadyaṃ paścimadeśe tu pūjayedbrahmapañcakam

bhasma darpaṇaṃ chatraṃ ca cāmaraṃ tālavṛntakam

lakṣmīpuṣpairviśeṣeṇa maulau vāropayettadā

nīrājanaṃ prakurvīta navapātramathāpi vā

ekapiṣṭena saṃtaptān dīpānprajvālya sarvaśaḥ

astreṇa prokṣaṇaṃ proktamabhyukṣaṇamatho hṛdā

nābhisthenāgninā caiva pūrakeṇāgninā saha

vahnibimbe bahiḥkṣipya kṣipedārātrike'thavā

pratidīpaṃ tathā datvā dīpamudrāṃ pradarśayet

vāmāṃguṣṭhaṃ nirodhāya darśayannavadīpakaiḥ

uccanīcakrameṇaiva devamastakasannidhau

sugandhisitasūnāni prokṣayedastravāriṇā

nikṣipya dīpe tatrāsmai śeṣabhasma ca saṃgrahe

hṛdaye bāhudeśeṣu caiśāne koṇake bhavet

nīrājanādi tatsarvaṃ pūrvavatkārayetkramāt

pātrāṇi tāni sarvāṇi bahiḥpīṭhe vinikṣipet

agnīśāyā tu vāyūnāṃ dikṣu catvāryanukramāt

śikhā japābhā nandīśa tanutrāṇaṃ ca bhṛṃgavat

aṃbhojāsanasaṃyuktājjaṭāmakuṭadhāriṇaḥ

śaktiśūlābhayavarān śivasyābhimukhasthitān

tatkāladaṃṣṭramatyugramastraṃ cāśani sannibham

svasvamantreṇa saṃpūjya pādyādīn hṛdayāṇunā

karocartanatāmbūlaṃ mukhavāsaṃ ca darpaṇam

naivedyaṃ dāpayettasmād devadevasya pūrvake

supakvaṃ ca sugandhaṃ ca kalpayedvidhinā saha

ājyānneṣu ghṛtānneṣu nikṣipettadanantaram

pakvairapakvaistadbhūri paritaḥ parimaṇḍayet

pātrakaiḥ kṣīradadhyājya supakvānnena saṃyute

annairnānāvidhaiḥ śuddhaiḥ ghṛtadadhyādisaṃyutaiḥ

tatpriyaṃ ca prakartavyaṃ susvacchaṃ tu viśeṣataḥ

kavacīkaraṇaṃ kuryād hṛnmantreṇa nivedayet

astreṇa manunā ghaṇṭāṃ tāḍayecca kṣaṇāttadā

pānīyaṃ ca pradātavyaṃ sthalyā majjakaminduvat

susvāduśītalaṃ toyaṃ pānīyaṃ parikalpayet

śivādi parivārāṇāṃ baliṃ tatra pradāpayet

hastodvartanakāryārthaṃ paṭīraṃ dāpayetpunaḥ

astreṇa prokṣayetpaścāt tāmbūlādīnpradāpayet

tailakarpūrasaṃyuktaṃ tāmbūlamiti kīrtitam

tejiṣṭhaṃ darpaṇaṃ paścān mūlamantreṇa dāpayet

tatvatrayeṇa saṃjapya mūlamantreṇa dāpayet

pratyahaṃ vāṅmanaḥ kāyavyāpārajanitātalam

śuddhirakṣāṃ ca kurute pavitramiti kīrtitam

saṃtuṣṭaṃ parameśānaṃ tāḍayennandikeśvara

vāmahaste ca kusumaṃ nikṣipeddakṣapāṇinā

mānasopari śiṣyāṇāṃ prathamaṃ cottamottamam

tasmādbhāṣyaṃ samutsṛjya manasopāṃśu vā japet

mantritaṃ hṛdayenaiva kavacena tu veṣṭitam

śivāṃgānāṃ japaṃ cāpi daśāṃgaṃ dāpayettadā

siddhirbhavatu me yena tatprasādāttvayi sthite

vāmahaste pradātavyaṃ mūlamantrārghyavāriṇā

paścādāgatya taddatvā vijñāpya tadanantaram

bahissutaṇḍule kārye dūrvāpuṣpaṃ ca saṃyutam

tāmrapātre kṛtādhāre liṃgaṃ vinyasya deśikaḥ

pāśupatāstramūrtaye nama ityāsanamūrtiko bhavet

oṃ śrīṃ paśuṃ huṃphaṭ pāśupatāstrāya namaḥ

oṃ bhoṃ hṛdayāya huṃphaṭ oṃ śrīṃ śirase huṃphaḍantakam

oṃ huṃ netrābhyāṃ huṃphaḍiti oṃ haṃ phaḍantāya huṃphaḍantakam

annaliṃgārcamathaikaṃ vā pañcakaṃ daśakaṃ tu vā

pātrāssopādukādvandvaṃ vajrādyāyudha saṃyutam

sarvālaṃkāra saṃyuktaṃ gītanṛttakavādyakaiḥ

antaḥ praviśya prāsādaṃ saṃyojyaitānsamāhitaḥ

śuddhena tāṇḍavenaiva bhagavantaṃ pratoṣayet

gomayenopaliptena praṇavāsanake punaḥ

nandidevaṃ samāvāhya saṃpūjya tadanantaram

rūpayauvana saṃpannāṃ susnātāṃ sarvabhūṣaṇām

bhasma pradhānamālyaṃ ca datvā tāṇḍavamuttamam

brahmāṇaṃ keśavaṃ caiva rudramīśvarameva ca

lālakāraṇa devānāṃ pūjanaṃ svasvanāmabhiḥ

kuṇḍaṃ vā sthaṇḍilaṃ vāpi vahnerāyatanaṃ dvidhā

vṛttaṃ ṣaḍaśrakaṃ caiva padmamaṣṭāśrakaṃ bhavet

kuṇḍānāmaṣṭakānāṃ ca lakṣaṇaṃ cādhunocyate

kuryātpaścima pūrvaṃ tu yāvatkuṇḍasya vistaram

kuṇḍānāmapi sarveṣāmutpattiścaturaśrake

dakṣiṇottarasūtreṇa catuṣpādayutaṃ bhavet

āgneyādīśaparyantaṃ sūtrāntaṃ vinyasettadā

ardhacandrākṛtiprakhyaṃ nair-ṛte'pyevameva hi

tadagrādaindrasūtrāntaṃ trikoṇākṛtivannayet

bhaktibhāgakṛte kṣetre tyajedbhāgakṛtaṃ budhaḥ

kṣetraṃ tu pañcadhā kṛtvā pārśvayorubhayorapi

matsyadvayaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā rākṣase mārute'thavā

kuṇḍārdhaṃ tu parigrāhyaṃ kṣetrārdhaṃ tu vinikṣipet

vartayettena mānena vartulaṃ kuṇḍamuttamam

kṣetraṃ ca vyāsamānena pārśve madhyadvayaṃ dvayam

daśāṃśaṃ kṣetrabāhyaṃ tu caikāṃśaṃ vinyasedbahiḥ

madhyame karṇikāṃ kuryād vṛttaṃ kuṇḍavadācaret

tenāṃśena ca kurvīta caturaśraṃ samantataḥ

koṇe koṇe ca saṃgrāhya saṃgrāhyaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

kuṇḍasya yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ mekhalāyāśca tādṛśam

hetu bhūtagaṇocchrāya madhyāyaṃ parikalpayet

aśvattha patravadyoniṃ mekhalopari vinyaset

vyomāṃgulaṃ tadutsedhaṃ yonilakṣaṇamuttamam

padmākāra samākāraṃ kuryātkuṇḍasya madhyame

bahirekāṃgulaṃ kaṇṭhe dvyaṃgulaṃ tu tathā bhavet

caturṇāmakṣadaṃ proktaṃ viśvabhoga vivardhanam

śrīkaraṃ padmakuṇḍaṃ tu puṣṭidaṃ jayavardhanam

śatārdhahome muṣṭiḥ syāt cannavāra * * * m

lakṣahome caturhastaṃ dvyayute ṣaṭkaraṃ bhavet

dvyaṃgulaikāṃgulaṃ vāpi saṃkṣiptaistaṇḍulairbhavet

brahmā vaivasvataḥ somo rudraśśakraśca keśavaḥ

kuṇḍabāhyārcane kāle'pyarghyapātre dadenmuniḥ

tasya nābhiḥ puraskuryādupaviśyottarānanaḥ

nirīkṣaṇaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ ca khananaṃ secanaṃ tathā

goṣaṇḍaṃ śodhanaṃ caiva pūrvameva kṛtaṃ yadi

astramantreṇa kartavyaṃ varmaṇābhyukṣaṇaṃ kramāt

sekitaṃ kavace naiva kuṭṭanaṃ tu śarāṇunā

kavacenākṣapādaṃ ca madhye pīḍaṃ hṛdā bhavet

ṛtusnātāṃ viśālākṣīṃ gandhamālyānulepanām

vāgīśvarīṃ mahādevīṃ evaṃ dhyātvā vicakṣaṇaḥ

tadvadvāgīśvaraṃ devaṃ vinyasya hṛdayāṇunā

sūryakāntodbhavaṃ vahni maraṇyudbhavameva vā

mṛtpātre tāmrapātre vā nikṣipya hṛdayena tu

kravyāṃśaṃ pūrvavatsṛjya vīkṣaṇādyā viśodhayet

vinyasya vahnicaitanyaṃ pradīpakalikopamam

pimgalānāḍimārgeṇa bahirnissārya recakāt

sadyādīśānaparyantaṃ namontaṃ pūjayetkramāt

tanutrāṇāvakuṇṭhena hastenoddhṛtya deśikaḥ

bhūmiṣṭhajānuko bhūtvā vāgīśīgarbhagocare

ubhayatra tu saṃmelaṃ hṛdayāsaṃmukhaṃ kṣipet

anyavastraṃ parīdhāya śaucamācamanaṃ hṛdā

darbhasūtreṇa badhnīyādrakṣārthaṃ nandikeśvara

āhutitritayaṃ kuryād hṛnmantreṇa samāhitaḥ

vāmadevena hotavyaṃ śirasāmbukaṇānvitam

juhuyādāhutī stristriḥ śikhāyā śikhayeva tu

varmaṇā puruṣeṇaiva jātakarmayutakriyā

svarṇādibhūṣaṇaṃ devyā hṛnmantreṇa pradāpayet

astreṇa kuṇḍamātāḍya vahnissekaṃ tu varmaṇā

astreṇāstīrya tasyordhve sthāpayet paridhīnhṛdā

ādyante ghṛtasaṃsiktāṃ khaḍgenaiva viśeṣataḥ

prācyādyuttaraparyantaṃ hṛdā viṣṭarakānyajet

pīṭhasthaṃ hṛdayenaiva pūjayennandikeśvara

śivasyājñāmimāṃ teṣāṃ śrāvayeddikpatīnprati

adhomukhau tānsaṃtāḍya kramānmūlāgramadhyame

madhyaṃ madhyena saṃmārjya darbhaiḥ stribhiranukramāt

darbhau parinidhāyaitau darbhasaṃspṛṣṭadeśake

sṛci śaktiṃ sruve śaṃbhuṃ pūjayennandikeśvara

astreṇa tāpayitvāgnau svadhāmiti vicintayet

tato viṣṇumayaṃ dhṛtvā ghṛtamīśādi vinyaset

mukundāya hunedbinduṃ kuṇḍanābhau niveśayet

prādeśamātradarbhābhyāṃ aṃguṣṭhānāmikāgrakaiḥ

hṛdātmasaṃmukhaṃ kuryāt tasmātsaṃplavanaṃ bhavet

kṣiptvā cānyonyadarbheṇa nīrājanavidhirbhavet

nidhāyāgnau kṛtaṃ dagdhaṃ kuśānprādeśasaṃmitān

bhāgatrayātsruveṇaiva ghṛtamādāya homayet

oṃ hāmagnīṣomābhyāṃ svāhāntaṃ mantramuccaret

oṃ hāṃ agnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāhā

ājyapūrṇasruveṇaiva caturthīmāhutiṃ yajet

ṣaḍaṃgenābhimantryaiva rodhayeddhenumudrayā

ghṛtabījahṛdākṣepe bhavedanyājyaśodhanam

sadyovāmamaghoraṃ ca puruṣaṃ ceśameva ca

ekaikayājyahomena vābhighāramidaṃ bhavet

kramādīśānaparyantaṃ pañcabrahmabhiranvitam

oṃ hāṃ aghorāya svāhā oṃ hāṃ tatpuruṣāya svāhā

nair-ṛtyādīśākāṣṭhāntaṃ avicchinnājyadhārayā

oṃ hāṃ sadyovāmāghora tatpuruṣeśānebhyaḥ svāheti

anyathā kuṇḍamānena vaktramiṣṭaṃ smaraṃstadā

saṃpūrṇeśānavahnau ca hetinā cāhutitrayam

ekaṃ nāma dadetpaścāt śivāgniṃ svaṃ hutāśane

trividhaṃ vahnirūpaṃ tu svarūpaṃ nandikeśvara

ṣaṇṇetro mekhalāyuktaḥ pādatraya samanvitaḥ

upavītī ca maunī ca saptahastasamanvitaḥ

hiraṇyā kanakā raktā kṛṣṇājihvāśca dakṣiṇe

evaṃ jihvā samuddiṣṭā vaidike meṣavāhanaḥ

pañcavaktrayutaṃ raktaṃ saptajihvā virājitam

raktavarṇaparīdhānaṃ paṃkajo parisaṃsthitam

kanakā bahurūpā ca atiraktā tataḥ param

ūrdhvavaktre sthitāstisraḥ śeṣāḥ prāgādiṣu sthitāḥ

netra śrotrādikaṃ jñātvā vahnerhomamathācaret

nāsikāyāṃ manoduḥkhaṃ keśe dāridrya saṃbhavam

bandhanaṃ śrotra deśe syān nāsārūpasvarūpakam

yatra cojvalitā vahnijihvā tatraiva homayet

tribhiḥ paridhibhiścordhvaṃ samidvā saṃyutastu yaḥ

tricatuḥ paridhiścaiva śāstre'sminsaṃprakīrtitaḥ

vāgīśvarīṃ ca vāgīśaṃ visṛjettadanantaram

agneḥ priyārthamagraṃ vā svāhānta tilakena ca

oṃ hāṃ hūṃ agnaye svāhā | oṃ agnayehnamaiśvaraṃ tejaḥ

evaṃ śivājñāṃ saṃprārthya sāṃgopāṃgayutaṃ śivam

tato'gnihṛdayāmbhoje cāvāhana punaḥ śiraḥ

yāgāgniṃ śivayoḥ kuryānnāḍīsandhānamātmanā

ājyakṣīramadhūnāṃ ca karṣake homa ucyate

pathyaṃ mātraṃ ca bhakṣyāṇāṃ lājānāṃ muṣṭi mātrakam

patraṃ puṣpaṃ svamānena kaniṣṭhāṃgulisaṃmitāḥ

candanaṃ kuṃkumaṃ caiva karpūraṃ yakṣakardamam

nidānāmaṣṭamaṃ bhāge gugguluścā dāsthit

pañcabhirbrahmabījaistu homayettu pade sthitaḥ

pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ kuryāt sruvā pūrṇaṃ ghṛtaṃ bhavet

puṣpaṃ sṛgagramāropya paścādvāmena pāṇinā

sāvadhānordhvakāyaśca samāṃghrissaṃmukhasthitaḥ

srugagre dṛṣṭimādhāya brahmaviṣṇvādikāraṇāt

tayornīlaṃ samunnīya savyahastasamīpake

vauṣaḍantaṃ sanātīṃ ca sajapannandikeśvara

ācāmaṃ tu paṭīraṃ syāttāmbūlādīṃśca dāpayet

anena vā yathā lābhaṃ śaktyā hutvā dine dine

sādaraṃ tatkṣamasveti coktabhāvasamanvitaḥ

pūrakeṇa hṛdambhoje śraddhayā hṛdayāṇunā

prāgagraṃ sarvamādāya kuryātkuṇḍala yugmakam

darbhānāstīrya prāgagrān pūrvarātrau baliṃ kṣipet

yakṣāṇāmuttare bhāge grahānāmaiśadeśake

nāgānāṃ vāyudigbhāge nakṣatrāṇāṃ ca madhyame

āgneyyāṃ praṇavasyāpi nair-ṛte viśvadevatāḥ

oṃ hāṃ rudrebhyaḥ svāhā | oṃ hāṃ mātṛbhyaḥ svāhā

hṛdebhyaḥ svāhā | oṃ hāṃ asurabhyaḥ svāhā

oṃ hāṃ nakṣatrebhyaḥ svāhā | oṃ hāṃ rāśibhyaḥ svāhā

* * * * * * * * datvābalirathocyate

brahmaṇe cordhvamuddiśya pūjayedantarīyake

madhyamuddiśya madhye tu kṣetrapālabaliṃ kṣipet

oṃ hāṃ indrāya svāhā | oṃ hā magnaye svāhā

ye rudrā rudrakarmāṇo rudrasthāna nivāsinaḥ

mātaro rudrarūpāśca gaṇānāmadhipārśvayoḥ

sarve suprītamanasaḥ pratigṛhantvimaṃ balim

evaṃ balipradānaṃ ca kṛtvā bhakto nato bhavet

bhagavanmama pūjāṃ ca homādisakala kriyām

aṣṭadikṣu tathā paścāt saṃpūjya śivamavyayam

sarvaṃ tadastu saṃpūrṇaṃ tvatprasādānmama prabho

yasya pūjāṃ tataḥ kuryāt pūjānte nandikeśvara

vinyasya cāstramantreṇa taddeśasthāntarāyakam

nirasya sakalī kuryān nirmālyaṃ gāṃgamūlakaiḥ

astreṇa hastau saṃśodhya nyāsārcana mataḥ param

amuṃ mūrtīśvaraścaiva svasvanāmnā tu pūjayet

āvāhayettadā caṇḍaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam

oṃkāraṃ pūrvamuccārya ṣaḍaṃgānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ

oṃ parameśvarāyeti śirase nama ityapi

goptre kavacāyeti namaskāramataḥ param

oṃ sarvayogādhikṛtāti hṛdayāya namaḥ

jotīrūpāya śikhāyai vauṣaṭ | oṃ goptre kavacāya namaḥ

evaṃ karāṃga(ka) nyāsaṃ kṛtvā pūrvaṃ ṣaḍaṃgakam

kaṃ svarāgnisaṃbhūtāṃ mūrtinnirājanaprabhām

jvalajjaṭāmardhacandrāṃ keyūrakaṭakojvalām

phaṇikaṃkaṇasaṃyuktāṃ vyālayajñopavītinīm

śvetapadmāsanāsīnāṃ bhaktānāṃ bhayabhañjinīm

aṃgānāṃ tu daśāṃ caiva japitvā nandikeśvara

gobhūhiraṇyavastrāṇi maṇibhedādi bhūṣaṇam

dhvanipūrveṇa mantreṇa svāhāntena viśeṣataḥ

nirmālya bhojanaṃ tubhyaṃ pradattaṃ tu śivājñayā

nyūnādhikaṃ kṛtaṃ mohāt paripūrṇaṃ tadastu me

udvāsya mūrtiṃ mantreṇa saṃhāriṇyā ca puṣpayuk

caṇḍāya tatanirmālye bādhakādyatha saṃcayam

caṇḍadhyānasamāyuktaṃ tannirmālyaṃ kṣaṇāttyajet

nirmālyāpanayasthānaṃ prokṣayedastravāriṇā

ācamya vidhinā samyak samyagbhasmāni saṃspṛśet

samādhi yogamārabhya kuryāduktakrameṇa ca

asaṃjñakātparāttatvāt paraṃ sūkṣmamanantakam

śivaśaktimayaṃ tejo yattatvaṃ sṛṣṭikāraṇam

śivaśaktyaikyarūpe tu vārdhanārīśvaraṃ matam

sadāśivasvarūpābhūdīśopyaiśī manonmanī

śivarūpaṃ tathā raudraṃ ājñāsiddhipradaṃ śubham

ājñākāryakṛtaṃ raudraṃ śaktyarthaṃ śamanāya ca

guruṃ khaṃ śi heturvā bhayaṃ drāvayati prabhuḥ

mṛdvāsane sukhāsīno'pyekacittaḥ prasannadhīḥ

savyaṃ śvetaṃ tathā śyāmaṃ vāmaṃ rūpamidaṃ bhavet

lalāṭanayanaṃ deve cārdhekṣaṇamanūpamam

devasya bhāge ṣaṇmudrāṃ pañcamudrāṃ tu vāmake

kapālikarṇe piṃchañca palaṃ caivāpasavyake

nivedyahastamekaṃ tu sarvādhāre sudhārayet

evaṃ dhyātvā viśeṣeṇa pūjayedgandhapuṣpakaiḥ

citre vā svagirā vāpi bhāvayetpūjayetkramāt

etanmantraṃ japitvā tu śivājñāṃ labhate sadā

dīpaṃ naivedyakaṃ caiva mūlamantreṇa pūjayet

tripaṃcāśantu bījānāṃ mālayā mantritaṃ bhavet

oṃ heṃ hauṃ kṣauṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ hrauṃ īśānāya namaḥ

oṃ aiṃ hlauṃ praṃ prīṃ preṃ hreṃ hrauṃ hṛdayāya nama ityādi

pūjānte tu punarnyāsaṃ sādhako vardhayetkramāt

kalpamākarṣaṇaṃ caiva purakṣobhaṃ pratiśrayaḥ

bhūmityāgaṃ tathāveśaṃ prabhutvaṃ buddhivardhanam

dānavānasurānyakṣān kiṃkarāṃśca vidhīyate

sarvadā samarūpā ca devī devena vakṣyate

puṇyādiśāntyau ca svarṇavarṇaṃ caturbhujam

staṃbhane mohane caiva haridrāsannibhaṃ prabhum

kṛṣṇamuccāṭane proktaṃ dīrghabāhuṃ bhayānakam

kheṭaṃ khaḍgaṃ ca pāśaṃ ca dadhānaṃ bhāvayettadā

pāśāṃkuśābhayadaṃ ca kapālaṃ cordhvabāhuke

jñānena śvetavarṇābhaṃ prasannavadanaṃ prabhum

caturbhujairdadhānaṃ taṃ dhyeyaṃ vai umāsaravam

indriyāṇāṃ cale kāle sarvaṃ dṛśyaṃ ca yadbhavet

kukkuṭāsana saṃsthopi lokayettu śanaiḥ śanaiḥ

vāraṇāyogasaṃyukto'pyākāśagamanaṃ bhavet

ājñāṃ kabdhvoktamārgeṇa pūjayennandikeśvara

āśu tena japātsiddhirjapāntaṃ homatarpaṇam

bhaktiviśvāsa adroha śraddhāśānti samanvitam

haviṣya bhojanaṃ caiva daśakaṃ sādhakaścaret

mānuṣaṃ tryambakāyātha umāyai tadantaram

oṃ tu ṣaṭtryambakāyātha umāyai tadanantaram

oṃ mānuṣaṃ tryambakāya umāyai kṣamiti

pūrvoktāni ca kāryāṇi sarvametena kārayet

somavāre ca tanmūle rakṣāmantreṇa bandhayet

vīryāya śivāyeti namaskārāntamantrakam

etanmantraṃ japitvā tu paścādaṃgāravārake

haridrārocanāyuktaṃ peṣaṇaṃ peṣayet kramāt

varṣā nyātape deśe śuddhabhāṇḍe vinikṣipet

mahāpātakasaṃyuktān śivadrohayutānparān

gurumūrdhni namaskuryāt tatprasādaṃ ca sarvadā

lābhaṃ cālābhakaṃ caiva sukhaduḥkhaṃ mahādbhayam

yadeteṣāmekatamaḥ saṃsiddhirnāstināsti ca

anyatīreṇa vāsāṃsi visṛjyānyena veṣṭayet

vakravatyupacāreṇa sugandhāmalakādinā

bhairava ityuktakalāṣoḍaśakalānvitaḥ

svakriyāvāsasādīṃśca yāyādbhāvasamanvitaḥ

śivohaṃ bhāvayitvātu ghṛtakṣīrābhiṣecanam

tuṣā ca darśane caivamapakvaṃ jātipakṣakam

dṛṣṭaścedutthito bhūtvā samācamya prasannadhīḥ

durnimitta vihīnaścet samaṃ hutvā samācaret

śuddhadeśe śayānasya svapne sarva prakāśyate

tadājñā sarvakāryāṇāṃ saphalāṃśaṃ prakalpyate

rephañca dakṣabāhuṃ ca śvetaṃ ca tadanantaram

aṃ īśānta kramājjyotsnā piṇḍamekākṣaraṃ param

kṣauṃ ūṃ repheṇa hṛdayaṃ vidyā akārau śiro bhavet

netraṃ tu vahninā caiva yakāreṇāstrakaṃ bhavet

ardhadvinetrayuktaṃ ca strīvilāsaparaṃ prabhum

ekanetrāṃ praśāntāntāṃ patipremapravardhanīm

kālakaṇṭhaṃ sunāśaṃ ca daṃpatyossasamadbhutam

bhāvibhāvayutaṃ vāme sotpalaṃ pāṇipallave

dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuyugalaṃ ratnahemādibhūṣaṇaiḥ

vyālopavītahāraṃ ca dakṣiṇaṃ bhasmalepitam

karpūrāgarukastūrīlepitaṃ vāmapārśvakam

vyāghracarmoparilasadvāsukīmekhalāṃcitam

ratnakāṃcīkalāpāḍhyaṃ jagaddhātuṃ ca vāmakam

dakṣiṇaṃ cādidevasya maṇimaṃjīrasaṃyutam

devāsuranutaṃ vedairākranditamaharniśam

evaṃ dhyātvā japellakṣaṃ gurau bhaktisamanvitaḥ

ayutaṃ ca hunetpaścāt tilataṇḍulakairapi

anantādi ṣaḍutthaṃ yad āsanaṃ parikalpayet

āvāhanādi sadyāntaṃ pūrvavannandikeśvara !

aṣṭamūrtisvanāmnā ca bhaktyā puṣpaiḥ prapūjayet

raṃ vāpyātmane namaḥ | yaṃ ākāśātmane namaḥ

dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ vṛṣaṃ pūrve ca digdale

nandinaṃ dakṣiṇe pūjya gaṇeśaṃ nair-ṛte tathā

uttare cāsya devasyā daiśānye kṣetrapālakaḥ

indrādi lokapālānāṃ paṃcamāvaraṇe yajet

dugdhasiddhaiḥ samiddhegnau ṣaṭsahasradvayaṃ hunet

āmlādito'gninā nityaṃ prīyetāṃ parameśvarau

gurūṇāṃ sādhyakālena kriyate vābhiṣecanam

āyurārogyamaiśvaryaṃ vāksiddhiṃ jayadaṃ tathā

māraṇaṃ caindrakānāṃ ca paramantrāpahāriṇam

kathaṃ śaktirbhavettasya gurubhaktasya niścayam

navākṣarayutaṃ piṇḍaṃ dānādau sādhako japet

sakāra pūrvayuktaṃ cecchānti pauṣṭikavardhanam

candrabimbamidaṃ dhyātvā gurumaṇḍalakopari

tatpadābjasudhādhārāpūjitāṃ svāṃ tanuṃ smaret

grahagrastasya śirasi vahnimaṇḍalasaṃpuṭe

koṇarephādiṣaṭkaṃ ca pūrvakoṇādi bhāvayet

rephādivāmaśrutyantaṃ kālānalasamaṃ smaret

vaśyākarṣaṇayoraktaṃ stambhasaṃkṣobhaṇādiṣu

dhūmrābhaṃ mardanoccāṭe staṃbha rāṣṭrakarasya ca

bhūbījādi tathā proktaṃ anādimuktikāṃkṣiṇām

marmasthāne ca śirasi vāyumaṇḍalamadhyagam

bhuktāṃgaṃ dhyānamātreṇa tattadaṃgaṃ vinaśyati

kaṇṭhanāle ca vā śroghaṃ bhūmaṇḍalagataṃ smaret

etaddhyānaṃ ca netrādi sarvāṃgeṣu ca saṃsmaret

vaśyākarṣaṇakāryeṇa guṇāhīno guṇī bhavet

sādhyasya hṛdayāmbhoje madhye tad rubandhanam

dhyātvā tasyordhvake caiva candrabimbaṃ sudhāmayam

ākarṣātso'pi sā caiva sarvamāgacchati dhruvam

piṇḍasya māntarephasya madhye sādhakanāmayuk

agnibimbaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā tadbahiḥ koṇaṣaṭkakam

pratilomasvareṇaiva krameṇaiva praveṣṭayet

kṛtvā saṃgrahamau pi nikhanyātkarṣaṇaṃ param

tridhā vibhajya juhuyāt satkarmatva prasiddhaye

piṇḍākṣaramakāraḥ syān madhye nāmakam

ṭhakāreṇāvṛtaṃ paścāt tadbahirbhūpuraṃ bhavet

puṇyaṃ japaśśanti paścāt tasya rogo vinaśyati

ṣakāreṇa vakāreṇa vakṣoruhayugaṃ punaḥ

repheṇa vāyubījena bahudeyaṃ sayugmakam

bindupakṣasthale nyastvā dīrghacandrākṛtirbhavaḥ

vaśistobhūmidaṃ caiva stohakaṃ viṣabhūtale

bindujīvarasenaiva vilikhetsādhakottamaḥ

salohamaṃgulīkaṃ ca tāmrapātre nidhāya ca

trikoṇa saṃpuṭasyāntaḥ piṇḍākṣaramaśeṣakam

agniṃ tatra pratiṣṭhāpya saṃpūjya vidhinā punaḥ

ājyena hutvā saṃpātaṃ nikṣipedaṃgulīyate

sādhyasyāṃguliṃ nikṣipya abhīṣṭhaphalasiddhidam

likhitvāṣṭadalaṃ padmaṃ karṇikāyāṃ ṣaḍaśrakam

ṣaṭkoṇe ca ṣaḍaṃgaṃ ca pūrvādidalake punaḥ

sānusvarānlikhitvā tu bāhye vṛttatrayaṃ bhavet

tadbahiḥ kādimāntaṃ ca pañcaviṃśatibhiryutam

tadbāhye bhūpuraṃ likhya tatkoṇeṣuhakārakam

aṣṭagandhādibhiḥ pūjya sarvapuṣpaiśca bhaktiyuk

ikārāntaṃ likhetpiṇḍaṃ ṣoḍaśasvaraveṣṭitam

nakāre rephamālikhya jvalatpāvakasaprabham

tanmadhye tadbahirbhūyo bhūmaṇḍalayugaṃ nyaset

etaccakraṃ samabhyarcya gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt

kāśvānyabimbayugmasya madhye cintāmaṇiṃ likhet

koṇe pārśveṣu vaikāramukāraṃ ca kramāllikhet

tadbāhye bhūpuradvandvaṃ saṃlikhya vidhivatpunaḥ

saṃpūjyābhyarcya puṣpādyaiḥ dhārayedyantramuttaram

apavādādi doṣaghnaṃ vaśyake?tsaumyavardhanam

rakṣāmadhyagataṃ hyetat sarvarakṣākaraṃ param

mūlamantraṃ ca saṃlikhya bāhye * lopamujvalam

sāgraśākhāvṛtaścaiva japtvā saṃ * * * * *

vyāghracoravarāhāśca śatruvargavarāhakāḥ

evaṃ prathamasadyādi sa * * * * * * *

devānāṃ prārthanāsidhyai gajāsyāsurarūpiṇaḥ

gajarūpaṃ samāsthāya devyā saha

aṣṭāraṃ padmamālikhya pūrvādyaṣṭadale punaḥ

pūrvasya dalamadhye'pi kavargaṃ pañcakaṃ nyaset

rākṣase ca tavargaṃ syātpavargaṃ paścime nyaset

oṃ klīṃ gaiṃ kavacāya hum | oṃ glauṃ goṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ

ṣaḍaśraṃ karṇikāmadhye tanmadhye ca trikoṇakam

tīvrā ca jvālinī kandā * * * * * * * *

sarvaśaktipadaṃ coktā kamalāsanasannutiḥ

aṣṭaśaktiṃ ca vinyasya navamī kāmyadāyinī

devamāvāhya sarvaśrī vacanaṃ vighnanāśanam

mandārapārijātādi kalpavṛkṣaiḥ samāhitaḥ

jyotsnābālātapairyuktaṃ caturdikṣu suśītalam

sevitaṃ sarvadā vajrapravālamaṇipallavaiḥ

raṃgadā caiva mikṣucāpamanantaram

vāme padmaṃ ca pāśaṃ ca raktotpalamanūpamam

saratnaṃ hema kuṃbhaṃ ca pūritaṃ vasubhirvṛtam

kirīṭamakuṭopetaṃ netratrayavirājitam

tasya vāmorusaṃlīnāṃ jagatkāraṇakāraṇām

ardhacandrasamākāralalāṭāṃ candramaṇḍitām

savyahastena deveśaṃ śliṣyantīṃ premanirbharām

devaṃ ca vāmahastena nibiḍastanamadhyamam

ratnaughaṃ puṣkarāgreṇa sādhikā ca muhurmuhuḥ

āvāhanādibhissarvaiḥ saṃpūjya parameṣṭinam

trikoṇabāhyaṣaṭkoṇa syāntarāgre ca devayoḥ

caturbhujaṃ viśālākṣaṃ śaṃkhacakragadāmbujam

* * * * * * * * * * leṣṭhāṃ pārvatīṃ tathā

dadhānaṃ bhāvayitvā māṃ pāśāṃkuśasamujvalām

ikṣucāpaṃ ca puṣpeṣuṃ abhayaṃ varadaṃ tathā

uttare bhūmidevīṃ ca varāhaṃ viṣṇuvigraham

tasya devīṃ śukraṃ caiva kaṇṭhiśaṃkalamodbhavam

priyaṃgumūle sandīptāṃ bhāvayennandikeśvara

saṭkoṇamadhye ṣaḍvighnān sarvābhīṣṭa pradāyakān

gaṇapaṃ ratnasaṃkāśaṃ gajavaktrasamanvitam

dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ tṛtīyaṃ cāṃgasaṃyutam

svasvanāmnā ca gandhādyaiḥ pūjayennandikeśvara

catussahasra saṃyuktaṃ japasaṃkhyā kramoditā

japānte juhuyādaṣṭadravyaistattu daśāṃśataḥ

saktavo nālikeraśca kadalī phalamaṣṭakam

guruṇācābhiṣiktaśca kalaśaiśca caturvidhaiḥ

kṣīrapādapabilvaṃ ca vaṭaṃ pippalameva ca

pūjayetkalaśānsamyak gaṇeśānṣaṭ samarcayet

abhiṣiṃcedguruḥ śiṣyaṃ anugrahapurassaram

hemāṃgulīyakaiścaiva citravastraiśca vrīhibhiḥ

vibhavasyānusāreṇa deśikāya nivedayet

aṣṭadravyaiśca hutvā tu tattaddravyasamāptaye

gokṣīreṇa jñānasidhyai lakṣmyai gosarpiṣā tathā

annenānnasamṛddhiḥ syāttilairgranthasamṛddhaye

abhīṣṭakṣetramṛtsnāṃ ca saṃgṛhya kṣīramiśritam

pratipakṣakṣatriyasya nagarotsavamṛtsnubhiḥ

rājyāptirnacirādeva sidhyatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

juhuyātpatikāmī ca chinnodbhavasamidvaraiḥ

rājyānāṃ vṛddhaye padmaiḥ ta k phalairapi

samidvaraiśca juhuyāl lavaṇairaśvāyavaitasaḥ

madhuratrayasaṃyuktaiḥ juhuyānnandikeśvara

dūrvākāṇḍasya homena dīrghāyuśca bhavetsadā

chinnodbhavaissamidbhiśca pañcamṛtasamanvitaiḥ

caturthitaṃ japennityaṃ gurau bhaktisamanvitaḥ

bhogamokṣapradaṃ caiva sarvavighnanivāraṇam

sūkṣmayuktaṃ tathānāstramagnibījasamanvitam

atradīrgha samāyuktaḥ śikhā vāyurnamontakaḥ

tīvrādinavaśaktisthadevaṃ padme supūjayet

raktāṃgaṃ rāgasaṃyuktaṃ raktamālā vibhūṣitam

triṇetraṃ puṣkarasyāgre bījāpūrasamanvitam

yajñopavītasaṃyuktaṃ brahmacārisvarūpiṇam

vighnaṃ vināyakaṃ caiva vīraṃ śuramanantaram

lambodaramiti proktaṃ svasvanāmnā ca bījayuk

gaṃ vīrāya namaḥ | oṃ gaṃ śūrāya namaḥ

gaṃ ekadantāya namaḥ | gaṃ lambodarāya namaḥ

caturthaṃ lokapālaiśca kramāddevaṃ prapūjayet

indirāle ca yaśase sarvasidhyai viśeṣataḥ

satvacaṃ nālikeraṃ ca koṣṭhayuktaṃ kramāddhūnet

vāṃchitārthaṃ samabhyaiti cāṣṭadravyaiśca homayet

dinānte tarpaṇaṃ kuryād bhautiko naiṣṭhikastathā

catvāriṃśaddinātsiddhiḥ mantrasya tu viśeṣataḥ

tanmārgeṇāgatastoye tasyaisākāṃkṣitaṃ labhet

pūjāhomaṃ ca kāryaṃ ca mahāgaṇapateriva

tameva paścādvakṣyāmi śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

svakīyācārasaṃyuktaḥ vratāpī lobhavarjitaḥ

uttamaṃ tāramuccārya pañcāntakamataḥparam

tarpaṇe ca hute caiva svāhāntaṃ mantramuccaret

oṃ hāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ oṃ hīṃ śirase svāhā

oṃ hauṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ | oṃ haḥ astrāya phaṭ

aikṣavāmbhodhimadhye tu dvīpe kanakaratnake

divyadrumalatopetaṃ divyamārādhasevitam

sarvartuphalasaṃyuktaṃ panasaiścandanairapi

nālikeraiśca vividhairikṣubhirmātulaṃgakaiḥ

tanmadhye pārijātaṃ ca nānāratnasamanvitam

niśchidrapallavairyuktaṃ candrakāntasuśītalam

tasya madhye'pyanantādi śivādyāsnāpanaṃ smaret

anekaśākhāsaṃyuktaṃ phalapuṣpasamanvitam

āvāhanādi naivedyaṃ pūjayetpūrvavatkramāt

ikṣuṃ ca pṛthukaṃ caiva rambhāphalayutaṃ kramāt

caturthyārabhya vā samyak śubhavāre śubhe dine

nair-ṛtyāṃ tu gaṇeśaṃ ca vāyavyāṃ gaṇanāyakam

dalāṣṭakasya madhye tu vakratuṇḍaikavaktrakau

vikaṭaṃ vighnarājaṃ ca dhūmravarṇaṃ ca pūjayet

tadbahirlokapālāṃśca dalānte svasvanāmabhiḥ

saptapratiyutaṃ divyaṃ prathamaṃ ratnanirmitam

caturthaṃ sālatāmreṇa paṃcamaṃ sphaṭikaṃ bhavet

evaṃ tu saptabhissālaiḥ veṣṭitaṃ cintayedbahiḥ

śaṃkhaṃ śaṃkhenduvarṇābhaṃ padmayuktanibhaṃ bhavet

gaṇapadvādaśānāṃ ca tathāṃgānāṃ svarūpakam

evaṃ saṃcintya saṃpūjya japasaṃkhyādi vakṣyate

daśāṃśaṃ tarpaṇaṃ caivamabhiṣekāhutikriyā

prāṇapratiṣṭhāmantreṇa japtvā mūlaṃ tataḥ kramāt

sādhyadiśyamanobhūtvā saptarātrādvaśīkṛtam

sarvopadravanāśaśca jvaranāśaśca jāyate

kanyārthīlājakaiścaiva mayūratrayakekṣubhiḥ

nālikeraiśca juhuyāccaturthīṃ prati sādhakaḥ

ghṛtasaṃsiktahaviṣā sarvakāryaṃ ca sādhayet

ākarṣaṇādibhiścaiva saṃvādasya phalaṃ tathā

homātkavitvasaṃsiddhirbṛhaspatisamo bhavet

utpalairbandhujīvaiśca nandyāvartaiśca campakaiḥ

homenendrasamaḥ śrīmān gurubhaktiviśāradaḥ

ātmāna manusaṃcintya samyagghutvā vicakṣaṇaḥ

pātighnabadhvā tatsarvaṃ kaṇṭhaṃ tatpuṣkareṇa tu

talaprahārairabhitaḥ preryamāṇaṃ vibhāvayet

svabījayuktabhūbījaṃ anusvarayutaṃ tathā

haridrayā śarāvāsyaṃ madhye sādhyasya nāma ca

prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kṛtvā tu vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa tu

amuṣya sarvendriyāṇi vāṅmanaḥ kāyakarma ca

guruḥ śiṣyaṃ śakramataḥ kuryātprāṇapratiṣṭhikām

pītapuṣpaiḥ samabhyaryca mūlamantreṇa saṃjapan

catuṣpade tu nikhanena madhyarātrau viśeṣataḥ

sṛk gajastambhanaṃ caiva gamanaṃ krodhameva ca

sādhyasādhakayornāma likhedyoṣāracena ca

trisahasraṃ japitvā tu nikhanetsainyamārgake

ebhirdravyaṃ tu bālebhyo dadyānnityaṃ tu bhaktiyuk

navasāhasrajāpena sādhitaṃ ripumārgake

pāpiṣṭhaṃ dharmarodhiṃ ca piśunaṃ bhaṃjanaṃ tathā

uccāṭayetsvadeśāttu tadupāyamihocyate

vicūrṇya tadakṣakaṃ ca mantra saṃpuṭamālikhet
