taccūrṇamātrasaṃparkād deśāddeśāntaraṃ vrajet

sahasrajaptametena śatrudveṣe nikhānayet

nimbakākālayaṃ gṛhya dagdhvā cāṃgāravārake

tanmadhye śatrusaṃjñāṃ ca mantrasaṃpuṭake likhet

deśāddeśāntaraṃ bhūtvā duḥkhībhavati niścayam

homayeduktamārgeṇa śṛṇutvaṃ yattaducyate

tālapatre ca puttalyāṃ nidhāya tadanantaram

tīkṣṇaṃ ca tailenājyena śmaśāne śuṣkadūrdhare

mohanaṃ ca bhavettasya vismṛtiśca viśeṣataḥ

bhūrjapatre likhetsādhya vi * * tamanukramāt

tasyodare vinikṣipya bhṛṃgīsnehena lepayet

athāñjalikarīṃ caindravallīlakṣmīṃ ca bhadrikām

sahāṃ caiva samuddhṛtya pratyekaṃ tu śataṃ japet

pratyekamuktaṃ tatrāpi sarvasaṃyojanaṃ śṛṇu

lakṣmīrvasusamāṃśāsyā drumāṃgaṃ sarvabhadrikā

mayūradvyaṃśakaṃ proktaṃ vaśye dūrvā viśeṣataḥ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā pratyekaṃ tadanantaram

gulikīkṛtya saṃjapya sahasraṃ bhakti saṃyutam

bhasma saṃyuktametattu sarvavaśyakaraṃ param

mahāgaṇapapūjāyāṃ yatproktaṃ taddhi yujyate

pratyakṣī bhavati kṣipraṃ gaṇanātho viśeṣataḥ

vāgīśvarīṃ mahādevīṃ śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

tāveva varṇau dīrghaṃ ca vāri pañcāntakaṃ punaḥ

yāntau sūkṣmeṇa saṃyuktau svāhāntaṃ tu daśākṣarī

ādyantasvaraṣaṭkasya madhyamaṃ yaccatuṣṭayam

kavargaṃ ca cavargaṃ ca ṭavargaṃ ca tavargakam

evamādi ṣaḍaṃgaṃ ca hṛdayādi ṣaḍaṃgakam

akāraṃ prathamaṃ vidyāt kavargaṃ ca sa bindukam

evamādi kavargādi svarasaṃpuṭitaṃ bhavet

iṃ caṃ chaṃ jaṃ hyaṃ ñaṃ īṃ raṃ śirase svāhā

eṃ taṃ thaṃ daṃ dhaṃ naṃ aiṃ kavacāya hum

aṃ yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ haṃ laṃ kṣaṃ aḥ astrāya phaṭ

dvādaśānte vakāraḥ syāt śrotrayordevakaṃ nyaset

nikāraṃ cāsya madhye tu vinyasennandikeśvara

pūrvoktaṃ ca guṇairyuktaḥ sādhakotsāha saṃyutaḥ

nityānuṣṭhānakaṃ kṛtvā gurubhakti samanvitaḥ

dravyaśuddhiṃ ca mantrāṇāṃ tathā kṛtvā ca bhāvayuk

sugandhaiścaiva puṣpaiśca candanairindubhistathā

rocanā rasaṃyuktaiḥ sugandhaiḥ śītalairjalaiḥ

naivedyamapi tāmbūlaṃ mukhavāsaṃ ca kalpayet

pañcāsanamidaṃ dhyātvā karṇikāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ

kesare kāmarājaṃ ca vāgbhavaṃ tasya madhyame

śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśāṃ sukhāsīnāṃ śucismitām

caturbhujāṃ triṇetrāṃ ca sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutām

śuddhasphaṭikamālāṃ ca vāmahastena ghaṇṭikām

cārukuṇḍalakeyūramuktābharaṇabhūṣitām

sarvagandha susaṃliptavṛttapīnonnatastanīm

kaṭhinastanayugmāṃ ca viśālajaghanāṃ śivām

āvāhādbhaviṣyantaṃ pūjayecchivavatkramāt

vidyeśvaryāśca purato dalamadhyeṣu cāṣṭasu

smṛtiṃ ca medhāṃ prajñāṃ ca pūrvavannandikeśvara

vāyavye tu śikhāṃ yajet * * * * * * * *

savye tu dalamadhye tu pūjayenmantra rājakam

namorudrānvitāścaitā iti śakvari saṃkhyakāḥ

svasvanāmnā ca saṃpūjya svasvarūpaṃ vibhāvayet

abhiṣekaṃ hutaṃ caiva bhaktiyuktaḥ samācaret

madhuratraya saṃsiktaiḥ śvetaiśśubhaiśśītalairapi

prātaḥ snātvā samācamya nyāsādisahitaḥ kramāt

mūlamantra sahasraṃ ca japitvā tajjalaṃ pibet

hṛdayāvadhike toye sthitvā bhāskarabimbake

sahasra tritayaṃ japtvā maṇḍalāvadhi nityaśaḥ

trisvādu madhubilvotthaiḥ supatraiścā yutaṃ hunet

kṛtāñjali samiddhomāṃ madhuratrayasaṃyutām

brahmīrasena payasā ghṛtayuktaṃ supācayet

karṣakatrayamātreṇa kavitā maṇḍalādbhavet

saindhavaṃ cājamodaṃ ca vācaścaitā ca pippalīḥ

brāhmīrasena bahuśo bhāvayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ

ardhamāsa prayogena bhakṣayedardhamātrakam

tasmātkavitva saṃsiddhiravivāraṃ tu sādhake

sahasraṃ tu dinādau tu japtvā nityaṃ tu māsakam

kavitvaṃ ca yaśovaśyaṃ draviṇatvaṃ ca sidhyati

akārādikṣakārāntaṃ pañcāśadapi cauṣadhīḥ

uśīraṃ kuṣṭhakaṃ caiva parikuṃkumameva ca

gandhaṃ ca rocanāṃ caiva patraṃ tadanu pippalīm

udumbaraṃ kāmarīkāṃ vārāmajjaṃ tathaiva ca

lākṣāgnimandhasiṃhīṃ ca kuśadarbhaṃ tathaiva ca

bṛhatīṃ pāṭalīṃ citrāṃ tulasīṃ kiṇikaṃ tathā

indravallīṃ ca bhṛṃgīṃ ca viṣṇukrāntiṃ ca sūkarīm

lakṣmīṃ caiva sadābhadrāṃ śubhavāre śubhe dine

etābhirgulikāṃ kuryād bhasmasthāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

sujaptā mūlamantreṇa dhāritā sarvasiddhidā

kramaśaḥ prāśayennityaṃ ṣaṇmāsādvākpatirbhavet

nityasnānarato bhaktaḥ paradūṣaṇavarjitaḥ

anadhītāni śāstrāṇi vaktuṃ śaktiśca tasya vai

paścātsannidhimāgatya vāṃcchitārthaṃ dadeddhruvam

tvadīyāṃ mūrtimenāṃ ca pūjāṃ kartuṃ prasīdame

bhaktijñānaika nirataṃ ratnagarbhaṃ śṛṇuṣva tat

tudhyāmadhyagatā sabhyaṃ vaṭamūlanivāsine

tāraśaktinirūcco'yaṃ mantraḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadakṣaraḥ

ṣaḍbhirdvābhyāṃ tathāṣṭāṣṭa pañcatrīṇi tathā kramāt

evaṃ ṣaḍaṃgaṃ vinyasya jyeṣṭhādyaṃguliṣu nyaset

kaṭyodvayaṃ ca guhyaikaṃ pādayośca catuścatuḥ

akārādikṣakārāntamātṛkānyāsamācaret

varṇaṃ ca catvāriṃśaṃ tu navavargavirājitam

saptavyañjana vargaṃ syāt saptaṣaṭpañca varṇakam

karṇatāloṣṭhakarajeṣvekamātra pravṛttitaḥ

astikāreṇa saṃyogādekāraḥ syātpareṇa tu

kaṇṭherdhamātrakāṃ mauṣavivṛtātprāṇaghoṣavān

tālī ca ṣaṭkato yassyātkevomantoṣayoścayaḥ

sparśa bāhyatvasāmānyasvaragambhīralāghavaiḥ

prasiddhāyāṃ lipaukasyāṃ vargā ye pañca saṃmatāḥ

tālu mūrdhni ca de yuṣṭhak saṃyuktaṃ vivṛto havaiḥ

mūlādhārātparā vāṇī sūtramātrā samanvitā

hṛdayādudgatā saiva tripānmadhyamasaṃjñitā

vaktrepi vāṣṭasthāneṣu saṃparkāśca dahṛṣṭayaḥ

paśyantī cārdhasaṃparke parātvanubhavānugā

khegakṣābhūmayāvarṇā varṇāḥ saṃbhavataḥ kramāt

ākāśādi pṛthivyantaṃ ye varṇāḥ samudāyajāḥ

* * * * ilāmena gulikakṣmādikhātmavat

lipirapyeva veha kramātkramagatā bhavet

visargāntā ime proktā vyutkrame bindumastakāḥ

bhavasvarā navādhāre hādiparnamukhe tathā

rephaṃ dakṣaśirobhāge vakāraṃ vāmake tathā

kakāraṃ bāhumūle ca khakāraṃ korparāntakaḥ

vāmahaste ca vargaṃ syāṭṭavargaṃ dakṣapādake

phakāraṃ nābhideśe ca vakāraṃ pṛṣṭhapārśvake

sakāraṃ hṛdi vinyasya evaṃ bhūtalipikramaḥ

evaṃ krameṇa vinyasya pūjayennandikeśvara

bhasmasnānayuto dakṣo rudrākṣeṇopavītakaḥ

prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā bhūtaśuddhiṃ tataḥ kramāt

śrīkaṇṭhādi ca vinyasya mātṛkānyāsakaṃ punaḥ

pāśanirvāṇadīkṣāṃ ca vadabhaktānukampayā

saptāhe paṃcarātre vā tridine sadya eva vā

gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā kuṇḍavedyādi sarvaśaḥ

pūrvoktalakṣaṇairevaṃ maṇḍalasyāpi vedikā

trayastriṃśacca sūtreṇa pūrvottarasamaṃ nyaset

aṣṭapatrasamāyuktaṃ rekhāgraṃ tena lekhayet

tadbahissamavṛttaṃ tu tadbahiḥ śvetamucyate

tadbāhye caturaśraṃ tu kalpayetkalpavittamaḥ

aghoraṃ dakṣiṇe vaktre vāmadevaṃ tu cottare

āgneyyāṃ hṛdayaṃ pūjya aiśānyāṃ tu śiro yajet

netraṃ tu purato nyasya kesare tu viśeṣataḥ

saṃskāradīkṣāśiṣyāṇāṃ varjitaṃ cāṣṭakaṃ tathā

kartā brāhmasaṃyukto dvijātyādyabhimānakaiḥ

etairguṇairvihīnaścen nirvāṇasyāpya yogyakaḥ

śivāgniṃ pūrvavatpūjya dīkṣākāryaṃ samārabhet

krūrakāryeṣu sarveṣu mantrāṇāṃ dīpanaṃ mukhe

vauṣaḍvaṣaḍsamopetaiḥ sudhābījoparisthitaiḥ

huṃkāramūlamantrasya śikhāmantramudīritam

mātṛkāyāmapi ca nāsti tadupari likhyate

ekaikadhā tadāhṛtya pratyekaṃ tatkrameṇa vā

mūlamantraṃ ca tatpaścāt punaśca śikhayānvitam

oṃ haṃ phaṭ pūrvamuccārya huṃkāraṃ tadanantaram

netradīpanamevaṃ hi hauṃ saṃpuṭitaṃ tathā

pūrvavacchikhayā caiva mantrāṇāṃ dīpanaṃ bhavet

sasavyabhāgadeśasthaṃ śiṣyaṃ saṃpūjya deśikaḥ

astreṇa prokṣitaṃ varma kuṇḍāntaṃ mūlamantrakam

śiṣyaṃ taṃ śikhayābadhnyātpādāṃguṣṭhāvalambakam

narasya savyabhāge tu nāryāccāpyapasavyake

saṃhāriṇāṃ bharaṇāya sūtraṃ te naiva vinyaset

nyastvā dhyātvā hṛdābhyarcya kavacenāvakuṇṭhayet

nāryāścāpyevamevaṃ hi kuryātsarvatra yuktitaḥ

bhuvanādhvā kalādhvā ca ṣaḍadhvānaḥ kramānnyaset

oṃ hāṃ tantrādhvane namaḥ oṃ hāṃ padādhvane namaḥ

oṃ hāṃ bhuvanātmane namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ kalādhvane namaḥ

apaḥ prāptaṃ talenaiva prokṣayecchiṣkaṃ prati

huṃkāraṃ recakenaiva śiṣyasya hṛdi vinyaset

oṃ hāṃ svāheti mantreṇa paricchidyāttathā punaḥ

nāḍībhūte ca sūtre vai saṃhāriṇyā ca yojayet

kavacenāva kuṇṭhyaiva hṛdayena iti trayam

vidyādehaṃ tu vinyasya śāntyatītasya darśanam

mantrabhūtamiva dhyātvā nāḍīsūtre tu bhāvayet

anenaiva tu mantreṇa darśanāya japetkramāt

etasyāṃ bhuvanānyaṣṭau bījaṃ nāḍī ca tadvayam

hṛtāyāṃ śāntyatītāyāṃ antarbhāvyaṃ pratāḍayet

gṛhītvā saṃhāriṇyā ca nidātyātmatra mastake

dve tatvevākṣare dve ca bījanāḍīkhagadvayam

padāni dvādaśaṃ proktaṃ bhuvanaṃ daśasapta ca

kṛṣṇāyāṃ madhyataḥ smṛtvā tāḍayitvā ca pūrvavat

oṃ haiṃ haiṃ iti bījena sānnidhyāyāhutitrayam

ṣaḍvarṇamantramekaṃ ca pañcaviṃśatimekakam

jihvāyāmatiraktāyāmantarbhāvyotkramātpunaḥ

ādāya tāḍya saṃśodhya hṛtsūtre sthāṇunā yajet

caturviṃśati tatvāni pañcaviṃśati varṇayuk

lokānāṃ ca caturṇāṃ ca ṣaṣṭirguṇa catuṣṭayam

pratiṣṭhāmadhyamaṃ dhyātvā śuklāśāntānamādikam

hrīṃ lokamaṣṭottaraśataṃ ekaviṃśatpadāni vai

varṇamevaṃ ca tatvaṃ ca viṣayaṃ caiva meva hi

jihvāyā pītavarṇāyā madhye saṃbhāvya tāḍayet

tantrasannidhikāryāya juhuyādāhuti trayam

dīkṣāyāṃ tu sabījāyāṃ samayācārapāśataḥ

vāṃchitārthāya dharmācca vyatiriktaṃ tu bandhanam

anainaiva krameṇaiva kuryāttarpaṇadīpanam

dīpane tu hunettadvat huṃphaḍantaṃ śarāṇunā

tarpaṇe pāśāya huṃphaṭ | iti trayāhutiḥ

saṃmṛjya kuṃkumājyena tatra sāṃgaṃ śivaṃ yajet

praviśyānte namaskāraṃ grahāṇāṃ bandhanaṃ bhava

badhnāmi ityādi mantraiśca kalāgrahaṇa bandhanam

sarvavyāpārarodhaṃ ca puṣpaṃ prati vibhāvayet

dvisṛktvādya ca nāśāya mūlena ca śataṃ hunet

hṛdāstraṃ saṃpuṭenaiva nidhāya tadanantaram

bahirgatvā tataḥ kuryān maṇḍala tritayaṃ kramāt

krameṇa vaśyānśiṣyāṃśca prāśayecculukatrayam

madhye madhye cācamanaṃ vidadhyānnandikeśvara

aṣṭagrāsamitirvāpi darśanasparśavarjitam

hṛdā tu sakalagrāsaṃ bhājayeddeśikottamaḥ

dantakāṣṭhaṃ hṛdā datvā cakṣuṣā śodhitaṃ tadā

vidandhāmaṃ vinā sarvaṃ śodhanaṃ pātamuttamam

nānyāthādoṣamokṣāya śatamaṣṭottaraṃ hunet

samarpya sthaṇḍileśāya sarvakarmāṇi bhaktiyuk

nirmālyaśodhanaṃ kṛtvā gomayenopalepayet

lokapālāṃśca kalaśān pūjayitvā nirudhya ca

saṃkṣepeṇa krameṇaiva lokapālabaliṃ kṣipet

naiṣṭhikānbhasmaśayyāyāṃ hṛdādakṣiṇaśīrṣakān

śikhābaddhaśikhānsarvān hetinā rakṣitānpunaḥ

oṃ hili hili śūlapāṇaye svāhā taṃ cāpi paścimām

śambho triṇetrāya piṃgalāya mahātmane

oṃ hili hili nigine śūlapāṇaye namaḥ

bahirnirgatya saṃprāśya pañcagavyaṃ caruṃ guruḥ

ramaṃ dhvajaṃ chatraṃ ca gajaṃ vṛṣabhamuttamam

gaṇādyārohaṇaṃ cāpi śuklamālyānulepanam

sumālāṃ ca vadhūṃ vāpi veśyāṃ sarvāṃgalakṣaṇam

ratnahemāṃgulīyādibhūṣaṇaṃ svapnamuttamam

tailābhyaṃgaṃ maṣīlepaṃ aṃgacchedaṃ tathaiva ca

mṛdbhāṇḍanavakaṃ cāpi potrikāmahiṣādibhiḥ

bhujaṃgadarśanaṃ cāpi bādhakāścaivamādi ca

prāyaścittaṃ tu kartavyaṃ tattaddṛṣṭyānurūpataḥ

ācamya vidhivacchuddhaḥ soṣṇīṣo bhūṣaṇānvitaḥ

bhāsvaraṃ śivahastaṃ ca bhāvayennandikeśvara

indrādīnapi dikpālānpūjayitvā samāhitaḥ

pāvakaṃ pūjayetpaścād darpaṇaṃ pūrvakāvadhi

huṃkāraṃ saṃpuṭenaiva hutvā mantrasya dīpanam

śiṣyāṇāṃ ca praveśāya kabdhvājñāṃ ca bahirbhavet

saṃpātahomaṃ tannāḍīrūpamarhakarāṃgakam

sthaṇḍile tu samabhyarcya pāśaviśleṣakaṃ gurum

kapālo jaladehaśca vajradehaḥ pramardanaḥ

agnīrudro hutāśaśca piṃgalaḥ khādako hariḥ

kālamṛtyuharo dhātā vidhātā kartṛsaṃjñakaḥ

nair-ṛto māraṇo hantā krūradṛṣṭirbhayānakaḥ

mahābalo mahābāhurdaśaite vandyarākṣasāḥ

śvetaśca jayabhadraśca dīrghabāhurjalāntakaḥ

śīghro laghurvāyuvegaḥ sūkṣmastīkṣṇaḥ kṣayāntakaḥ

niśīśo rūpavāndhanyaḥ saumyadeho jaṭādharaḥ

vidyādharo jñānadharaḥ sarvajño vedapāragaḥ

sarvavidyāvidhātā ca sukhaduḥkhakarau daśa

vṛkṣo vṛṣadharo dhīro grasnaśca sarvatomukhaḥ

guruvibhurgaṇādhyakṣaḥ stridaśavanditaḥ

vīrabhadro'tha kālāgnirudro hāṭaka eva ca

rudraścāhurime rudrāḥ kaṭāhābhyantare sthitāḥ

aṣṭāviṃśatpadānāṃ tu viprato tiṣṭhati kramāt

sarvasānnidhyakara brahmaviṣṇurudrārcita aśubhā

jñānajñāna śabdaśabda sūkṣma sūkṣma śivaśśarva

evamekona viṃśatiḥ padamantramataḥ param

hakāraśca sakāraśca varṇadvayamudīritam

vāyudvayaṃ ca saṃproktaṃ prāṇāpānakramādubhau

viṣṭayaṃ bhaya tu ityukto guṇapañcakamucyate

maṇḍalaṃ pārthivaṃ pītaṃ caturaśra mataḥ param

kalāyāṃ ca nivṛttiḥ syādyonayopi caturdaśa

paśavaśca mṛgāścāpi sarvabhaṃgāḥ sarīsṛpāḥ

mānuṣyamekamanyattu devayonigataṃ śṛṇu

prādeśaṃ brāhmyamityeva devatāṣṭakayonayaḥ

prakṛtāścālayaṃ teṣāṃ bhoge buddhau ca kāraṇam

oṃ hāṃ hāṃ hāṃ samuccārya nivartteśca kalāḥ punaḥ

oṃ hāṃ svāhāṃ nivṛttikalāpāśāya huṃ phaṭ

oṃ hāṃ hlāṃ nivṛttikalāpāśāya namaḥ

kuṇḍamadhye tu saṃsthāpya cārghyaṃ datvā namo'ntayuk

anenaiva tu mantreṇa svāhāntenāhutitrayam

sānnidhyaṃ tarpaṇaṃ caiva punareva tribhistathā

oṃ hāṃ hlāṃ nivṛttikalāpāśāya namaḥ

svāhāntena punastena mantreṇoccāraṇaṃ bhavet

oṃ brahman tavādhikāreṇa sa mumukṣuṃ dīkṣayāmyaham

āvāhayettato devīṃ raktāṃ vāgīśvarīṃ tadā

pūjayettarpayeddevīṃ bhaktibhāvasamanvitaḥ

oṃ hāṃ haṃ hāṃ huṃphaḍantena cātmane nama ityapi

sphuratsphuliṃgasaṃkāśaṃ tasya caitanyavigraham

oṃ hāṃ hāṃ haḥ huṃphaḍantaṃ ca oṃ svāhityanena ca

ātmamantreṇa saṃgṛhya yojayedātmani sphuṭam

vāgīśvarasya saṃyogaṃ vāgīśvaryāśca bhāvayet

brahmādikāraṇatyāgaṃ kramānnītvā śivāspadam

sarvayonisvarūpāyā vāgīśvaryā varāṃgake

paścādvāgīśvarīgarbhe vāmayādbhavasaṃjñayā

oṃ hāṃ haṃ hāṃ ātmane namaḥ iti pūjayedbhāvasaṃyutaḥ

sarvayoniṣu śudhyarthaṃ hṛdayena tu homayet

dehimeñjana kurvīta śirasā yugapattadā

varmaṇā sarvabhāgaṃ ca mantreṇa viṣayātmanoḥ

garbhādhānādikāryeṇa pañca pañcāhutīrhunet

pāśaviśleṣaṇe caiva malaśaktitiraskṛtau

malādyantaḥsthapāśasya saptadhāstreṇa japtadhā

oṃ huṃ nivṛttikalāpāśāya huṃ phaṭ nā punaḥ

ājyapūrṇasṛvasyāgne nidhāyāgnau tu homayet

pāśāṃkuśanivṛttyarthaṃ oṃ aṃ astrāya phaḍiti

tatra brahmāṇamāvāhya pūjayettarpayettathā

śukaṃ brahmangṛhāṇeti juhuyādāhuti trayam

mantreṇānena santarpya prārthayettu viśeṣataḥ

sandhānaṃ punarāstheyaṃ śivājñā sādhayediti

kumbhakenātmamantreṇa saṃhāriṇyā ca mudrayā

pañcāṃgabindubimbaiśca bhāvayennandikeśvara

arghyapātrajalaṃ dhyātvā sudhāmayamanaupamam

pitarau tau visṛjyātha vauṣaḍanta śarāṇunā

nivṛttiśodhanaṃ hyetat pratiṣṭhāmārabhetkramāt

hrasvadīrgha prayogeṇa cānnānnādānta saṃhitām

guṇatrayamahaṃkāraṃ caturviṃśati niti

khādirāntākṣarāṇyeva pañcaviṃśatireva ca

amareśaṃ prabhāvaṃ ca naimiśaṃ puṣkaraṃ tathā

lakulīśo hariścandraḥ śrīśailo daśamo bhavet

kedāro bhairavaśceti dvitīyā * * * ritam

vimalaṃ cāṭṭahāsaṃ ca mahendro bhīmasaṃjñakaḥ

gokarṇī bhadrakarṇaśca svarṇākṣaḥ sthāṇureva ca

subāhurmantrarūpī ca viśālo jaṭilastathā

binduraścaiva ghoraśca prājāpatyo hutāśanaḥ

pataṃgaḥ piṃgalaścaiva haro vidhātra saṃjñakaḥ

saheto bhavanirvṛttau samābāhyātha pūjayet

vyāpi vyāpin rūpārūpaprathamaprathamatejastejaḥ

nā nā nā nā dhū dhū dhū dhū oṃ bhūḥ oṃ bhuvaḥ oṃ suvaḥ

mahādeva sadbhāveśvara | mahātejo yogādhipate

bījaṃ ca reca yo mantro vāmadeva śiva śikhānāḍī

rasanāvāyusaṃyuktamindriyaṃ ca tadā bhavet

abjopetārdhacandraṃ ca maṇḍalaṃ vāruṇaṃ tathā

bhuvanāmā jagatsarvaṃ bhuvānāntargataṃ smaret

oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ hrāṃ samuccārya pratiṣṭhāṃ ca kathāṃ tathā

oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ hrāṃ pratiṣṭhākalāpāśāya huṃphaṭ

mudrayā caivamākarṣed huṃphaḍantaṃ sa bījayuk

* * * mudrayā gṛhya kumbhakena hṛdānyadhaḥ

oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ pratiṣṭhā kalāpāśāya namaḥ

recakeṇa viśeṣeṇa kuṃbhe caiva vidhāpayet

anenaiva tu mantreṇa cāpayitvā vibhāvayet

sannidhānāhutiṃ caiva pūrvavatkuryādguruḥ

anenaiva tu mantreṇa viṣṇumāvāhya pūjya ca

bhāvyaṃ tvayānukūlena viṣṇuṃ vijñāpayediti

puṣpeṇa dakṣahastena śiṣyavakṣasi tāḍayet

huṃphaḍantaṃ samuccārya praviśenmantrapūrvakam

pāśayuktaṃ tu śastreṇa jyeṣṭhayāṃkuśamudrayā

pūrvoktena tu mantreṇa svāhānta sphuṭitena ca

pitroḥ saṃyogamuddhārya mudrayodbhavayānayā

gātrotpattau tathā paṃcaśirasā janmane tathā

saṃsārapāśaśastreṇa srotaśśiṣyaiḥ śivāṇunā

pāśaviśleṣaṇaṃ cāpi śirasā tadanantaram

śastreṇa pañcadhā kṛtvā malaśaktitirohake

saptāstrajaptaṃ kartavyaṃ chindyādastreṇa tattadā

pratiṣṭhāṃ ca kalāpāśāya haḥ punaḥ

oṃ hāṃ pratiṣṭhākalāya pāśāya haḥ huṃphaṭ

ājyaṃ tu pūrvavaddatvā kalāstreṇa hutaṃ bhavet

aṣṭāhutiṃ ca juhuyāt prāyaścittanivṛttaye

adhikārādikaṃ sarvaṃ sarvavannandikeśvara

gṛhāṇasvāhāntamuccārya mantramevaṃ bhavettadā

niśśeṣapāśadagdhasya paśostasya hare tvayā

tato visṛjya viṣṇuṃ ca raudrātmānaṃ viśodhayet

saṃhāramudrayātmasthaṃ vidhāyodbhavamudrayā

vahne visṛjya pitarau pūjayetkusumādibhiḥ

vidyāsandhānakāryārthaṃ pūrvoktakalayā saha

oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ hrūṃ hāṃ ca sandhāne rāgaṃ cā śuddhavidyayā

śuddhavidyā ca tatvānāṃ saptakaṃ ca kramādbhavet

ekaviṃśatpadaṃ proktaṃ tārādyaṃ nandikeśvara

mūrdhāya tatpuruṣāya vaktrāya aghora hṛdayāya

guhyātiguhya goptre anidhanāya sarvayogādya

parameśvara acetanācetana vyomāvyoma | rudrāṇāṃ

pūrvantu vāmadevaḥ syāt tattatsarvabhavodbhavaḥ

ardhaḥ praśāntanāmā ca paramākṣarasaṃjñikaḥ

adṛṣṭarūpadhārānau tathā rūpavivardhanaḥ

kalyāṇa iti vijñeyāḥ pañcaviṃśati saṃkhyayā

pūjā ca hastirjihvā ca vyānanāgau maruddvayam

śabdaḥ sparśaśca rūpañca traya eva guṇāḥ smṛtāḥ

bhāvayedbhuvanādiṃ ca vidyāmūrdhagate tadā

vidyayā hṛtpradeśe tu grahāṇāṃ karṣaṇaṃ bhavet

kāraṇaṃ rudramāvāhya śiṣyaṃ vijñāpya bhaktiyuk

caitanyaṃ pūrvavattatra mantreṇa ca viyojayet

iḍayā yojayedbharge saṃgrāhya dvādaśāṃśataḥ

bhogaṃ virāmaṃ strotaśca tatvānāṃ ca viśodhanam

pūjayettarpayeccaiva śatāhutyā ca tatra vai

mūlaśakti tirodhāne cchedane mardane tathā

prāyaścittaniṣedhārthaṃ rudrasyāvāhanaṃ tathā

rūpaṃ granthiṃ ca gulphaṃ ca rudrāya viniveśayet

śaivīmājñāṃ ca saṃśrāvya rudraṃ visṛjya kāraṇam

bhāvyaṃ tvayānukūlyena bhaktyā vijñāpanaṃ bhavet

ātmanyātmānamādhāya pāśastatra niyojayet

pūrṇāhutiṃ ca kṛtvā tu vidhānena viśeṣataḥ

vidyāsvabījametvaṃ hi repheṇa tu viśeṣataḥ

anenaiva tu mantreṇa vidyāvāca viśodhanam

etadvayaṃ ca śāntau ca vilīnaṃ bhāvayetkramāt

pakṣavarṇau ca saṃproktau bhuvanādhiparudragāḥ

prabhavakṣamayakṣudrā vimalaśśiva ityapi

dīptā ca kāraṇaṃ caiva tridaśeśvara ityapi

rudrāścāṣṭādaśatvete śāntyāśca bhuvanādhipaḥ

padānāṃ ca viśeṣeṇa praṇavādyaṃ bhavettadā

śivāya anantāya anāthāya anāśritāya

nityayogine dhyānāhārāya * * * * * *

puruṣe kavacau mantrau bīje binduyakārakau

mārutau kūrmakṛkarau tvarāgaḥ syāttathendriyaḥ

īśvarastu karaṃ proktamadhastāttu turīyakam

pūrvavattāḍanaṃ bhedaṃ praveśamapi yojanam

svasminsaṃropya saṃgṛhya kalāṃ kuṇḍe niyojayet

anukūlena bhavatā bhāvyaṃ vijñāpanādikam

ātmānaṃ vidhinā samyak svasminneva niyojayet

śirasā pañcajanārthaṃ adhikāro śikhā bhavet

mūlena srotasāṃ sidhyai sidhyai tatvahṛdā bhavet

pāśaviśleṣaṇaṃ cāpi tanutreṇa tadā bhavet

hetinā pañcajuhuyādevaṃ pāśaviyojane

bījena saṃpuṭaṃ kuryāt tathā śāntikalāntare

hauṃ hauṃ śāntikalāpāśāya huṃ phaṭ

ājyapūrṇasṛve sthāpya kalāstreṇa hutaṃ bhavet

aṣṭāhutīśca juhuyāt prāyaścittanivṛttaye

tasmai śuklaṃ ca vidhinā kurvīta tadanantaram

dagdha niśśeṣapāśasya śiśorasyeśvara tvayā

īśvaraṃ tatra visṛjed rudrātmānaṃ niyojayet

śiṣyāmātmani saṃbhāvyaṃ vidhinā yojayettadā

mūlena kuryācchiṣyasya śirasyamṛtabindukam

pūrṇāhutiṃ ca vidhinā juhuyānnandikeśvara

bījameva viśeṣaḥ syāt kalānāṃ śucihetave

pūrvavattatra kurvīta tatra varṇādikaṃ kramāt

uktau śaktiśive tatve bhuvanāṣṭakamīritam

vyomarūpamanādyaṃ ca tathānāśritamaṣṭamam

ilikā rodhikā sapta sahapañcadaśa smṛtāḥ

akārādi visargāntā bījanādahakārakau

devadattaṃ tathā caiva nāñjayamaṣṭadhaḥ param

guṇaśca śabdotītaḥ syāt turīyāditakaṃ bhavet

śāntyatītakalāsaṃsthaṃ dhyātvā tattāḍanādikam

namontaṃ huṃphaḍantena praviśyātra niyojayet

ākṛṣya pūrakeṇaiva caitanyaṃ vibhajettataḥ

pūjāmāvāhanaṃ pitrostarpaṇaṃ sannidhiṃ tadā

anenaiva tu mantreṇa caitanyaṃ vibhajettataḥ

hṛtsaṃpuṭātmabījena svāhāmākṛṣya bhāvayuk

pitrossaṃyogamudbhāvyaṃ bhavedudbhavamudrayā

pūrvoktavidhinā sarvaṃ garbhādhānādikaṃ bhavet

niṣkṛtyai ca śataṃ homaṃ vidadhyānnandikeśvara

pañcapañcāhutīrdadyān mantreṇa ca yathā purā

oṃ hāṃ śāntyatītakalāpāśāya huṃphaṭ

pūjya pūrṇe sṛ dhyātvā kalāstreṇa ca homayet

aṣṭāhutīśca juhuyāt prāyaścitta nivṛttaye

adhikāraṃ ca kuryācca pūrvoktavidhinā punaḥ

niśśeṣadagdhapāśasya śivonasya sadāśivam

pūrṇāhutiṃ ca visṛjen mūlena ca sadāśivam

saṃyojya raudryasaṃhāramudrayā gurumātmani

prathārita śirogrīvo nādoccāra samanvitaḥ

bhaktyā saṃpūjya pitarau visṛjettadanantaram

kāruṇyāttatkṣamitvā me vrajamaṃ sthānamātmanaḥ

śivāstreṇa śikhāṃ chidyācchiṣyasya caturaṃgulam

sṛksṛvakṣālanaṃ śiṣya snānamācamanaṃ guruḥ

astreṇa tāḍanaṃ kṛtvā caitanyaṃ ca niyojayet

svakīyahṛdayāmbhoje yojayennandikeśvara

sṛvaṃ ghṛtena saṃpūrya daśosyācca sṛvaṃ kramāt

prasāritaśirogrīvo nādoccārasamanvitam

maṇḍalātkalaśādagneḥ śirassvasmādanākulam

candārkanāśanaṃ jñātvā viṣuve ca bhavettadā

prāṇasya rodhaḥ prathamaṃ tatsvarūpāvalokanam

brahmanāḍyantarālasthanādaṃ tattu turīyakam

tasmiṃcchāntiṃ ca gacchettu layamicchati tatkṣaṇāt

tadgatiśśaktiviṣayamupadeśena labhyate

tatkālaviṣuvaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ tathā tītaṃ ca saptamam

pūrakaṃ kumbhakaṃ dhyātvā hyādāya mukhamādarāt

manolayāttathā muktiriti śāstrasya niścayaḥ

nābhervitastiṃ saṃyāpya hakāraṃ paritaḥ sthitam

tato'ṣṭāṃgulakarṇāntamukāraṃ viṣṇumucyate

sādākhyaṃ brahmarandhrāntaṃ nādarūpaṃ vibhāvayet

divyaṃ pipīlikā sparśaṃ tasminsaṃbhavati kṣaṇāt

nirguṇaṃ nirmale śuddhe vācyavācaka varjite

kaivalye cidghane śānte śive tu paribhāvayet

ājyadhārāṃ ca juhuyājjvālānte'pi pare śive

vauṣaḍantaṃ samuccārya pūrṇāṃ ca vidhinā hunet

ātmavatsarvajño bhava iti svāhāntaṃ mantrakaṃ bhavet

oṃ hauṃ ātman aluptaśaktibhava svāhā

itthaṃ ṣaḍguṇamātmānaṃ gṛhītvā paramākṣarāt

tīvraśaktinipātasya janitaśramaśāntaye

śivakumbhādikānsarvānvadayecca kramādguruḥ

uttarāsyaṃ ca tanmūrdhni dakṣiṇe tu vibhāvayet

deve vahnau gurussa syād bhaktināthasya vardhayā

śreyasābāhvini brūyādāśiṣya śivamādarāt

īśīkumbhādikalaśajalaistamabhiṣecayet

sarvadīkṣā ca jñānaṃ ca śarvānugrahato bhavet

nairantaryeṇa bhaktyā ca śuśrūṣā ca bhavetpunaḥ

nirvāṇa dīpitaḥ śiṣyo guru saṃsṛṣṭi yogyakaḥ

śāvasūtikajaṃ caivamāśaucaṃ ca na vidyate

tena kāraṇataścāpi pūrvoktaguṇavarjitaḥ

evamādiṣu sidhyarthaṃ tritatvakramadīkṣayet

liṃgārcanādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvavatkuruyānmukham

sarvatrāpi śivaṃ yaṣṭvā pūrvoktavidhinā tadā

agnermukhasya homaṃ ca mantrāṇāṃ dīpanaṃ punaḥ

śiṣyasya mūrdhni śaktiṃ ca saṃpūjyādāya mudrayā

kavacenmavakuṇṭhyaiva tarpaṇādi ca pūrvavat

sāmānyārghya jalenaiva prokṣayecchiṣyamastake

saṃpraviśyātha mantreṇa pāśaviśleṣaṇaṃ bhavet

oṃ hāṃ pūrvaṃ samuccārya haṃ hāṃ ātmane namaḥ

mantrāṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā śivatatvaṃ vilokayet

namontena ca mantreṇa śirassūtre tu vinyaset

mantrāṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā śivatatvaṃ vilokayet

namontena ca mantreṇa śiraḥ sūtre tu vinyaset

tarpaṇaṃ pūjanaṃ cāpi pūrvavadvidhinā bhavet

puṣpatāḍanapūjādi pūrvavatkuryāttataḥ

namontaṃ mantramuccārya pūrvavatpūjayedguruḥ

mantrāṇāṃ tṛptaye caiva juhuyādāhutitrayam

anāhatājñayormadhye sūtraṃ kāśmīravāriṇā

śivatatvāya huṃphaḍ dīpanaṃ bhavet

hṛtsaṃpuṭitabījena teṣāṃ grahaṇabandhanam

ṣaḍaṃgena hunetpaścāt pūrṇāṃ mūlena kārayet

śivakumbhasamīpe ca rakṣārthaṃ viniveṣṭayet

baliṃ ca dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca pūrvavatsthāpayenniśi

adhivāsanamityāhurdīkṣākarmāpyanusmaret

śiṣyaṃ praveśya kuṇḍasya pārśve caiva kramāttadā

śikhāyāṃ mastakālambi badhnīyācchiṣyake tadā

ṣaḍadhva parivṛttaṃ ca vyāpakenāvalokayet

gṛhītvā śiṣyacaitanyaṃ vahnimadhye vinikṣipet

paścād brahmāṇamāvāhya pūjayecchrāvayediti

tadātmani praveśaṃ ca kuryātsanmārgavāyukaḥ

gurumaṇḍalaparyantamādāyodbhavamudrayā

pūjanaṃ tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā saṃskāravidhipūrvakam

maṃtramāyādi niṣkṛtyai śivaṃ sāṃgaṃ ca pūjayet

astreṇa homayetpaścāt tataḥ saptāstra japtayā

vimṛśya vartulīkṛtya sṛgagre dhārayetkramāt

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ hutvā prāyaścittamanantaram

oṃ hāṃ brahman gandharasa rūpasparśa śabdatvāt

pūrvavacchiṣya caitanyaṃ svasminnādāya deśikaḥ

suyobhūmārghyavārīṇi śiṣya mūrdhni ca nikṣipet

śuddhena vātmatatvena vidyātatvaṃ ca bandhayet

caturthyānta ci tatvaṃ ca namontaṃ mantramuccaret

evaṃ mantraṃ samuccāryaṃ vidyātatvaṃ vimarśayet

ṣaḍaṃgaṃ vyāpakaṃ caiva kṛtvā puṣpādi tāḍanam

vaktrasūtraṃ ca saṃtāḍya śiṣyacaitanya saṃgraham

yojayet svātmaniyutaṃ dvādaśāntagataṃ nayet

utpattibhoga saṃhāranāthāya juhuyāttadā

mūlaśaktitirodhānaṃ hetinā pūrvavadbhavet

vimṛjya vartulīkṛtya juhuyātpūrvavadguruḥ

oṃ hāṃ viṣṇorhi saṃbhāvya buddhyahaṃkārarūpakam

oṃ hāṃ viṣṇo buddhyahaṃkārarūpaṃ śuklaṃ gṛhāṇa svāhā

ādāyādāya caitanyaṃ svātmanyeva viśeṣataḥ

arghyāmṛtāmbubindūni śiṣya mūrdhni vinikṣipet

vahnau nikṣipya saṃpūjya tarpayecca yathākramam

tatpāde dīkṣayāmyenaṃ rudraṃ taṃ paribhāṣya ca

śiṣyacaitanyamādāya praviśyāntastato guruḥ

udbhavena tamādāya vāgīśīgarbhanālake

mūlaṃ na kuryādaṃgaicca hetinā homamārabhet

pāśānāṃ chedanāddāha prāyaścittaṃ ca pūrvavat

oṃ hāṃ rudra mano bindusvarūpaṃ śuklakaṃ tathā

tirodhānāya pāśānāṃ śodhanāya viśeṣataḥ

rudrasya pūjanaṃ kṛtvā tasya śuklasamarpaṇam

gṛhāṇa svāhāyāntaṃ tu mantrametadudīritam

brahmanśiṣyasya caitanyaṃ niveśya kṣaṇamādarāt

amṛtopamasya bindūni śiṣyamūrdhani nikṣipet

oṃ huṃ śikhāyai huṃphaḍitimantreṇa netrajaptadhā

gomayāntaṃ niveśyaināṃ ājyapūrṇaṃ sṛci sthitam

deśikopi tataḥ snāyād bhasmanā bhāvasaṃyutaḥ

tasminyojana vistīrṇaṃ caitanyaṃ ca śiśoḥ punaḥ

brahmādikāraṇānāṃ ca tyāgamantraissamuccaran

ājyadhārāṃ ca visṛjecchiṣyātmānaṃ niyojayet

calasmatha samāyuktaḥ sarvakleśavivarjitaḥ

adīkṣitasya bandhutvaṃ kṛtaghnatvāvivekatā

etairaṣṭaguṇairhīno gurvājñāpālanādikaḥ

āgamoktaguṇairyukto gurudhyānaparāyaṇaḥ

evaṃ tritatvapakṣānte proktaṃ tatsarvamācaret

cakraṃ mantraṃ tato dhyānaṃ vakṣye'haṃ nandikeśvara

rucirāsanamādāya śodhayedastra mantrataḥ

śirordhvaṃ pūjayetpadmaṃ pūjayedgurumaṇḍalam

etanmantraṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvamantraikanāyakam

guṇārṇave śivaśrīśrīśivāya ca namontakam

sādhakasyāgrake pṛṣṭhe kalpayetpraṇavadvayam

tadbāhye vṛttamālikhya pratidikchūlakaṃ nyaset

stambhinī mohinī caiva jambhinī haṃsinī tathā

stambhine stambhinī svāhā mohine mohinī svāhā

mantrāṇyetāni coccārya caturdikṣu prapūjayet

pradakṣiṇa krameṇaiva dhyānaṃ kṛtvā viśeṣataḥ

mūlādhāre tathā rudraṃ brahmāṇaṃ hṛdayepi ca

pāvakena nibhaṃ rudramekāsyaṃ tryakṣaraṃ vibhum

paraśvadhaṃ mṛgaṃ caiva dadhānaṃ sarvabhūṣitam

brahmāṇaṃ hṛdaye dhyāyet kanakābhaṃ caturbhujam

akṣamālāṃ kuṇḍikāṃ ca dadhānaṃ ca dvihastakam

āsīnaṃ śaktisaṃyuktaṃ pūjayedvidhinā saha

caturbāhu samāyuktaṃ śaṃkhacakra samanvitam

kirīṭamakuṭopetaṃ pītāmbaradharaṃ harim

pūjānte tu yathā nyāyaṃ bhāvayejjyotiruttamam

śivasthāne sthitaṃ kṛtvā cintayedamṛtaṃ budhaḥ

rudrasthāne viśedyogī bhāvayettu viśeṣataḥ

bhakṣyabhojyādi tatsarvaṃ rudrasthāne viśeṣataḥ

ājñāsiddhimavāpnoti pāśāṃkuśa nivāraṇāt

tasmātsarva prayatnena tritatvaṃ bhāvayetsadā

sādhāraṇa guṇānāṃ ca śiṣyāṇāṃ ca vidhīyate

ekatatvaṃ kriyādīkṣā tasya vai pāśamocanam

prāyaścittārthakatve'pi deśikasyārcayā parā

agnikāryaṃ ca cārghyaṃ ca mantrāṇāṃ dīpanādikam

pūrvavatsūtrabandhaṃ ca kuryācchiṣyasya mūrdhani

raktau saṃsūtasūtāṃ vā śivatatvāgrakaṃ nyaset

garbhādhānādikaṃ kṛtvā śivamagnau tu pūjayet

mūlena pūrṇāṃ juhuyāt tatra prātopagarbhitam

śatamūlena juhuyāt sthiratvātpāśanāśane

śarīramarthaṃ prāṇaśca sadgurubhyo nivedayet

evamādi kriyādīkṣāsaṃpanno guṇasattamaḥ

yujiyoga iti dhātvarthe yajjīvaśśivayorapi

asadbhāva kṣayatvaṃ ca jñānasadbhāvabhāvakam

maṇḍalaṃ maṇḍapaṃ kuṇḍaṃ kuṃbhaṃ devārcanādikam

atyanta iva saṃprāptavāñcāyukta malolupam

mantradīpāttatṛptādi pūrvavannandikeśvara

vāgīśvarīṃ ca vāgīśaṃ pūrvavatpūjayedguruḥ

garbhādhānādi saṃskāraṃ pūrvavadvidhinā caret

iḍā ca piṃgalā caiva suṣumnā ca tathā bhavet

alambuṣā kuhūścaiva śaṃkhinī daśamī smṛtā

prāṇaśca prathamo vāyurapāno vyāna eva ca

kṛkaro devadattaśca dhanañjaya itīritaḥ

evaṃ caturdaśa proktāḥ prāṇādyā vāyavastvamī

svasvasthāne tu nāḍīnāṃ vāyūnāṃ sthāpanaṃ bhavet

sthāpayetpūjayed dhyātvā śivarūpaṃ ca bhāvayet

tāsāmapi suṣumnāyāṃ mukhyatvaṃ ca prakīrtitam

mūlādhāraṃ samārabhya brahmarandhrāvasānakam

gudaṃ meḍhrāntaraṃ caiva līnamūlamanantaram

tālumūlaṃ ca kodaṇḍadvayormadhyamanantaram

mūlādhāraṃ catuṣpatraṃ tadūrdhvaṃ ṣaḍdalaṃ bhavet

kaṇṭhaṃ ca dvādaśaṃ proktaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ tālumūlakam

ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatakaṃ varṇabhedātkramātsmaret

taruṇādityasaṃkāśaṃ bhāsvaradyutisannibham

eṣu sthāneṣu brahmādyān kāraṇāṃśca prakalpayet

kriyāśakti jñānaśaktiricchāśaktiḥ śiva param

vahnimaṇḍalakaṃ nābhau hṛdaye sūryamaṇḍalam

kriyāśaktau ca binduśca jñānaśaktau tu nādakam

tadūrdhvaṃ tu śivasthāne cānandāmṛtamuttamam

ardhyatoyena saṃprokṣya śiṣyadehe tu deśikaḥ

tadvadādhāradeśasthaṃ tattanmantramudīrayet

navadarbhāgrakoṇaiva navavāraṃ japetkramāt

bisatantunibhajyotiruccareddeśikottamaḥ

anyonyaṃ vidyududyotaṃ sūtrasūtropari sthitam

śivatantraṃ samuccārya devadevaṃ tu pūjayet

candrakhaṇḍadvayaṃ caiva mantroccāraṇadīpanam

mahāmāyāṃ ca saṃyojya bījadvayamudīritam

tasmādārabhyakodaṇḍayugmamadhyaṃ tu bhāvayet

jñānabimbācca tasmāttu candrapiṇḍāmataḥ param

śivabījaṃ śivasthāne bhāvayennandikeśvara

pūjayitvā vidhānena sūtravinyāsamācaret

cubukāntaṃ hṛdāntaṃ ca nābhyantaṃ viṣṇudeśakam

evaṃ pañcavidhaṃ sūtraṃ śiṣyasya śikhayānvitam

vākpāṇipādau pāyuśca varāṃgamiti pañcakam

vacanādānagamanaṃ visargānandapañcakam

hṛtsūtre tu manobuddhirahaṃkāratrayaṃ nyaset

vidyāṃ ca niyatiṃ kālaṃ sadadhiṣṭāna sūtrake

prokṣaṇaṃ tāḍanaṃ caiva hetinā ca viśeṣataḥ

ajapāṃ tasya caitanyaviśleṣaṃ pūrvavadbhavet

oṃ hāṃ haṃ hāṃ svātmane namaḥ

nivṛtyālokanaṃ caiva pūjanaṃ pūrvavaccaret

śiṣyacaitanyamākṛṣya svātmanyeva niyojayet

garbhādhānādi saṃskāraṃ pūrvavadvidhinā caret

vimalāsanamāvāhya pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ

tāraṃ ca śaktimupagaṃ brahmānandaikamuccaret

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ brahmaṇe śrotraṃ sohaṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ brahmaṇe ghrāṇaṃ sohaṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ brahmaṇe pāṇiṃ sohaṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ brahmaṇe pāyuṃ sohaṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ brahmaṇe śabdaṃ sohaṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ brahmaṇe rūpaṃ sohaṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ brahmaṇe gandhaṃ sohaṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ brahmaṇe ādānaṃ sohaṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ brahmaṇe visargaṃ sohaṃ svāhā

etairviṃśatibhirmantrairbrahmaśuklaṃ nivedanam

bandhāya na punaḥ stheyaṃ śivājñāṃ śrāvayediti

ājyapūrṇasrave datvā cāstramantreṇa homayet

mūlādhāre ca saṃsthāpya pūjayennandikeśvara

bhāvyaṃ tvayānukūlyena viṣṇuṃ vijñāpayediti

pūjya mantraṃ ca tāraṃ ca dviruccāryātha homayet

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ viṣṇave buddhissohaṃ svāhā

evaṃ śiṣyaṃ ca dadyāttu govindaṃ visṛjettadā

hṛdantasūtramānīya vartulīkṛtya pūrvavat

kalaṃkapañcakadvyaṃśamuktacandranibhaṃ śubham

nābhisūtreṇa caitanyamarghyatoyena pūjayet

rudramāvāhya saṃpūjya sthiratvādisamanvitam

bandhāya na punaḥ stheyaṃ śivājñāṃ śrāvayediti

cittaṃ ca rāgaṃ māyāṃ ca kalāṃ tatraiva dāpayet

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ rudrāya rāgaṃ sohaṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ rudrāya kālaṃ so'haṃ svāhā

maṇipūrakadeśe tu rudraṃ taṃ sthāpayetkramāt

suṣuptāvaṇumāvāhya pūjayeddhṛdayāṇunā

bandhāya na punaḥstheyaṃ śivājñāṃ śrāvayediti

vidyāṃ ca niyatiṃ kālaṃ pūrvavanmantrasaṃpuṭam

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ īśvarāya niyatiṃ sohaṃ svāhā

etaiśśuklapradānaṃ ca kuryādbhaktisamanvitaḥ

pūjayitvā vidhānena bhaktiyukto jitendriyaḥ

ājyapūrṇasṛgagne tu nidhāyāstreṇa homayet

kalaṃkaṃ caturaśraṃ va hīnaṃ candramasākṛtim

tāḍanādi vidhānaṃ ca saṃskārāntaṃ ca pūrvavat

śuddhamāyāṃ śuddhakalāṃ śuklaṃ dadyātsamāhitaḥ

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ hauṃ sadāśivāya śuddhamāyāṃ sohaṃ svāhā

evaṃ śuklapradānaṃ ca kartavyaṃ nandikeśvara

bandhāya na punaḥ stheyaṃ śivājñāṃ śrāvayediti

sthāpanaṃ pūjanaṃ caiva kārayedbhaktisaṃyutaḥ

sṛgagre ghṛtapūrṇe tu juhuyāddhṛdayāṇunā

śuddhatejoghanamayaṃ caitanyaṃ bhāvayetpunaḥ

praṇavācchaktibīje tu tasmānmūle tu yojayet

etanmantraṃ samuccārya mūlādhāre niyojayet

śuddhamātmānamapyasmin yojayecchiva bhāvayet

navātmarūpakaṃ mantramuccarennādasaṃyutam

kālayukta mahendraṃ ca vāruṇaṃ vahnisaṃyutam

etaṃ navākṣaraṃ piṇḍaṃ halamātrābhiyojayet

mūlādhāraṃ samārabhya dvādaśāntavidhiṃ smaret

gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ pūjya prāṇādīṃśca hi kārayet

punargandhādibhiḥ pūjya śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret

jñānena yogadīkṣāyāmadhivāso na vidyate

hemapuṣpopahāraiśca hemayajñopavītakaiḥ

vicitraratnanicayairbhūmigoratnabhaktibhiḥ

yogadīkṣāsamāyuktaḥ śivaḥ sākṣānmahītale

nābheradhastātkandasya tiryagūrdhvāgatānanāḥ

sa eva śivayogī syāt tridaśairabhivanditaḥ

yogī tu yogābhyāsena nirṇayelloka saṃcayam

trikoṇamujvalākāraṃmanujānāṃ viśeṣataḥ

tiraścāṃ dīrgharūpaṃ syād hṛdaye ca catuṣpadam

kandānnavāṃgulasthāne manuṣyāṇāṃ ca nāmataḥ

nābhideśe patantīva dvādaśāra samanvite

ūrdhvādho bhajate jīvo jāgrādikaraṇaissaha

jīvādayaśca prāṇaśca caratyanvahamūrjitaḥ

kandamadhye sthitā caiva suṣumnā nāḍiruttamā

brahmanāḍī ca tanmadhye suṣumnā citrarūpiṇī

sitaraktāsitāstredhā suṣumnā varṇitā bhavet

parito nābhicakrasya prāṇavāyusamanvitā

tāmasaścandra evoktaḥ sūryo rājasa ucyate

sarasvatī śuklavarṇā ca sitāmburuhasannibhā

yaśasvinī himendvābhā citrā viśvodarā śubhā

payasvinī payovarṇālaṃbuṣā pītavarṇikā

iḍāmṛtaṃ viṣaḥ piṃgā bāhyā rātrinibhā bhavet

iḍāyāḥ pūrvapṛṣṭhe ca gāndhārī hastipiṃgale

payasvinyāśca pārśvābhyāṃ pūṣā tadvatsarasvatī

alambuṣā tu nandīśa kaṇṭhamadhyādadhaḥ sthitā

adhaścordhve ca vijñeyā vāraṇā * * milinī

savyanāsāntagā tatra piṃgalācordhvagāminī

payasvinī tathā nandin savyakarṇāntagā bhavet

śaṃkhinī cordhvagā bhūtvā kaṇṭhāntatvātha gocarā

iḍā ca savyanāsāntaṃ savyabhāge ca saṃsthitā

kukṣau vighnodaya nāma mūrtipūrtividhāyinī

etāsāmeva nāḍīnāṃ dvisaptati sahasrakam

caturdaśa su nāḍīṣu vāyavo'pi caturdaśa

nāgaḥ kūrmaśca kṛkaro devadatto dhanañjayaḥ

evaṃ caturdaśa proktāḥ prāṇādyā vāyavastvamī

eteṣu pañca mukhyāso sa prāṇāpānayostayoḥ

āsya nāḍikayormūle niśvāsocchvāsavṛttikaḥ

tasmātprāṇasya mukhyatvaṃ apānasyāpi vartanam

śakṛnmūtrendriyāṇāṃ ca nissārastena vai bhavet

udāno mukhanetre tu kampayatyadharoṣṭhakam

samānaḥ sarvagātreṣu saṃkhyāyaiva vyavasthitaḥ

dvisaptati sahasrāṇāṃ nāḍīnāṃ madhyame caret

nāgasyodgārakaṃ kṛtyaṃ kūrmonmīlanimīlanam

dhanaṃjayo ghoṣayutaḥ prāṇahīnaṃ na muñcati

antaryāmyapyahaṃkārī prapañcaścitta saṃyutaḥ

jaṭharasthaṃ jalaṃ cānnaṃ vibhajedagninā saha

indragopakasaṃkāśaṃ tadadho'pāna eva ca

atyuṣṇajalasaṃyuktamannaṃ vyaṃjana saṃyutam

svedamūtraṃ bahirgacchet rasaṃ vīrya kramādbhavet

māṃsānmedopasaṃkrāntaṃ medaso snāyurasthike

śakṛjjātaṃ kramādevaṃ saptadhā dhāturucyate

udānaṃ padmakiñjalkasannibhāgni samaprabham

kusumbhapuṣpasadṛśo hikkābhīti pravartakaḥ

kṛkaraḥ kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ syād bhrūvikṣepaṃ ca kārayet

śveto dhanaṃjayaḥ prokto dehadhāraṇake rataḥ

sūtādidoṣa nirmukto'pyannadoṣa vivarjitaḥ

yamastu daśadhā prokto niyamaḥ procyate tathā

siddhāntaśravaṇaṃ bhavyaṃ hrī tā ca japaṃ hutam

akṛtvāvarjanādeva karmaśuddhirathocyate

śaucaṃ tu ṣaḍvidhaṃ proktaṃ tapaścāndrāyaṇādikam

kṛcchrādibhiḥ kāyadoṣaḥ kāraṇānāṃ ca śoṣaṇam

taporūpamidaṃ proktaṃ yogasādhanamuttamam

sarvadā duḥkhamāpnoti sarveṇaḥ paripūraṇaḥ

ātmānuṣṭhāna yogyeṣu pradeśeṣvavalokanam

anenaiva tu mūrtiḥ syāt prasiddhaṃ nandikeśvara

svagurorbhakti saṃyukto deśakālānurodhanāt

siddhāntaśravaṇaṃ caiva siddhāntaśravaṇaṃ bhavet

kulālahastamṛtsneva gurvājñā pālanāmataḥ

āgamoktaṃ vidhānaṃ tu kutsitaṃ karma yadbhavet

dīkṣottare ca guruṇā copadiṣṭaiśca mantrakaiḥ

kurvantaṃ kṛpayā yuktaṃ śiṣyamātrādisaṃyutaiḥ

candrārkanāśanaṃ kṛtvā bhāṣyopāṃśakamānasaiḥ

evaṃ gurumukhāllabdhvā japaṃ kurvīta tajjapaḥ

niyamo daśadhā prokto yamādyābhiśca viṃśatiḥ

mohāddānaṃ prakuryāccecchiṣyeṇa vṛjinaṃ vrajet

padmaṃ ca svastikaṃ caiva gomukhaṃ viśadaṃ tathā

padmāsanaṃ ca khaṃ puṣṭaṃ kūrmaṃ vimalameva ca

vṛttiṃ ca kaṭikaṃ caiva gajāsyaṃ ca mayūrakam

urdhvopari tu nandīśa kṛtvā pādatale śubhe

jānūrvorantarāle tu kṛtvā pādatale śubhe

savye dakṣiṇagulphāttu pṛṣṭhapārśve ca yojayet

śiropari ca pādābhyāṃ karṇapārśve ca yojayet

viṃśadantamiti proktaṃ sūcī ca tadanantaram

śarīrāṃghrīdvayaṃ cāpi vaktrāyordhvaśarī bhavet

ākāśagamanārthāya śanaiḥ kukkuṭavadbhavet

tasya pādatale nyastvā vāmapāṇiṃ ca dakṣiṇam

atyugratapasāṃ sādhyametadāsanamuttamam

korparāvadhi cākṛṣyādaṃgaṃ tatkukkuṭaṃ bhavet

jānukorparasaṃyogād vīrāsanamitīritam

savanīpārśvayoreva vṛṣaṇasyātha gulphagau

bhadrāsanaṃ bhavedetat sarvavyādhiviṣāpaham

pṛṣṭhapārśve karadvandvaṃ aṃghrivyagropa saṃsthitam

padmavaccaraṇaṃ kṛtvā pāṇibhyāmūrumadhyame

prāṇārodhanakaṃ caiva kāyāgnerati dīpanam

apāmākarṣaṇe śastaṃ mūlāgnerdīpanaṃ bhavet

pārśvayoḥ pādayugalaṃ nikṣipya jānunī ubhe

ūrdhvakāyaḥ sthitaḥ samyak gokarṇaṃ vāyubandhanam

vyāttāsyaḥ krūramīkṣeta nāsāgravyagralocanaḥ

pādadvayaṃ ca saṃyojya tasminpṛṣṭhe nidhāya ca

apānākarṣaṇaṃ kuryāt kāyāgnervardhanaṃ param

vakṣoruhaṃ ca hastena prasārya tadanantaram

pādāṃguṣṭhāvalokaṃ ca gajāvanamitīritam

āyuṣyavardhanaṃ khyātamatisāranirodhanam

hastayoḥ korparadvandvaṃ anyadeśe nidhāya ca

mayūrāsanametattu sarvapāpa praṇāśanam

aṃgānāṃ ca kramānyāhuḥ sadāśugamanaṃ bhavet

prāṇāyāmamatho vakṣye nāḍiśuddhyādi pūrvakam

śivaprakāśaṃ satataṃ kramātkaivalyakāraṇam

gaṃgāditīrthagaṃ sarvaṃ prāṇāyāmena sidhyati

tasmātsarvaprayatnena prāṇāyāmaṃ caredguruḥ

śītātapa vinirmukte mahāpātakavarjite

kṛtaghnāḥ krodhinaścaiva gurvājñā laṃghinastadā

śaucamācamanaṃ kṛtvā prātarutthāya saṃyataḥ

pratyekaṃ vividhaṃ snānaṃ yogināṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

pūrvamevaṃ prakāraṃ tu proktamanyattaducyate

āgneyaṃ bhasmanārakṣaḥ vāyavyaṃ prāṇāyāmakam

celājinakuśaiścaiva mṛdāsana kṛtaṃ bhavet

tatkarṇikāyāmāgneya dale vighneśvaraṃ yajet

samagrīvaśiraḥkāyo dantairdantānna saṃspṛśet

bindumadhyagataṃ caiva niṣyandatsudhayānvitam

agnibījaṃ tu saṃsmṛtya savyanāsāpuṭāntare

vāmanāsāpuṭenaiva pūrayetpūrvavadbudhaḥ

pañcaṣaṭsaptasaṃkhyākaiḥ kaniṣṭhaṃ madhyamottamam

prāṇāyāmaḥ samabhat nāḍīśodhana mucyate

śarīra saṃyutaṃ caiva nādābhivyakti jihvakam

yāvadetāni cihnāni saṃpaśyettāvadācaret

prāsādena tu mantreṇa praṇavena tathaiva ca

na tatra lakṣaṇaṃ vidyāt sadā sarveṣu vartate

catuṣṣaṣṭimātrakaṃ kumbhaṃ pūrṇakumbhaṃ tathā bhavet

ṣoḍaśaprāṇāyāmaṃ tu nityameva tu cābhyaset

adhamaṃ dvādaśodghātaṃ caturviṃśatimadhyamam

dhāraṇā dvādaśāyāmaṃ dhyānaṃ dvādaśadhāraṇam

recakaṃ sarvathā tyajya pūrakaṃ kumbhake nyaset

kampanaṃ caiva bhramaṇaṃ bhramaṇaṃ cāplutiṃ tathā

cittasthitimavāpnoti cittaśuddhimavāpnuyāt

utkṛṣyotkṛṣya yogena śrāntaḥ śrāntaḥ punaḥ punaḥ

sthūlasthaṃ vā trideśaṃ vā kṛtvā yogī samāhitaḥ

liṃgotthānkalaśānghorānsarvadeha gatāṃstathā

yoganidrāṃ samāśritya dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaṃ lipau svapet

svapne'pi yogayuktasya yoganidrāprabhāvataḥ

vidhānametatkurvīta satyametanmayoditam

nityaṃ ṣoḍaśa sandhyāyāṃ māsamātraṃ samabhyaset

janmāntarakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ ṣaṇmāsācca punāti saḥ

yogābhyāsācca satataṃ prāṇānāṃ saṃyamo bhavet

antarākarṣaṇaṃ vāyoḥ bāhye tatpūrvakaṃ bhavet

svedaḥ kampaśca pulaka ānandānāmanantaram

layātprāṇasya medhāpi sukhī ca bhavati kṣaṇāt

niśvocchvāsarūpasya haṃsarūpasya madhyataḥ

prāṇasya layanādeva kumbhake na bhavetpunaḥ

ubhau visṛjya yoge tu vāyordhāraṇamuttamam

sarvaṃ virecya cāpūrya paścātkuṃbhakasaṃyutaḥ

yāvatkevalasiddhiḥ syāttāvatsahitamabhyaset

na tasya durlabhaṃ kiṃcit triṣu lokeṣu vidyate

na jarā pitriṣā tasya vaijayantī na dṛśyate

eṣa prāṇajayopāyaḥ sarvamṛtyunivārakaḥ

pādyaṃguṣṭhadvayorvāpi prāṇavāyośca dhāraṇā

pavanasya jayopāyo nāsāgrālokanaṃ bhavet

nābhimadhyasthito vāyurvyādhināśāya vai bhavet

dvijacañcapuṭenaiva vāyunālambitaḥ śanaiḥ

agastyodaya kāle tu rājadantadvayāntarāt

ṛtudvayena rogāśca nāśaṃ yānti na saṃśayaḥ

amṛtaṃ yaḥ pibedyogī sarvasiddhiṃ ca gacchati

tasminnamṛtapānena sarvavyādhiharaṃ param

pārśve ca ghaṭikāmātradhāraṇānmāsamātrakam

ekamāsaṃ trisandhyāyāṃ mārutaṃ jihvayā pibet

gulmāṣṭhīlaplahādyādi gātravi * rśanāni ca

viṣajvaropamṛtyuśca te sarve yānti nāśanam

abhyāsādeva tatsarvaṃ vāyorvijayameva hi

indriyāṇi vaśīkṛtya viṣayebhyo nivartya ca

sarvaṃ sakṛtsamutsṛjya śanaiḥ kampuli mārgakam

kāraṇāgnikarābhyāṃ ca bandhayedaṃgulīyakaiḥ

mūrdhni caiva viśeṣeṇa prāṇasaṃyamanaṃ param

tena nādodbhavaścaiva śuddhasphaṭikanirmalaḥ

ādau śaṃkhadhvanirmadhye meghadhvaninirantaram

somarandhragate vāyau nityacaitanyake sthite

prāṇāyāmarataḥ śuddho recapūrakavarjitaḥ

vṛṣaṇasya dvayoreva sarvopari ca dakṣiṇam

* * * * ranugrīvaḥ śiraḥ karṇassamodaraḥ

mantrābhi praṇavenaiva nādaṃ pūrṇaṃ samuccaret

vapuṣo lāghavaṃ caiva jaṭharāgni vivardhanam

rodhayenmārutaṃ paścān manolayamupaiti vai

anenaiva tu madhyasthā vāyudhāraṇataḥ kramāt

svasthānātpatito bhūtvā prasārya phaṇabhṛttanum

brahmarandhre suṣumnāyāḥ prāṇāyāmaśca gacchati

hṛtpradeśe punaḥ karṇe bhrame vāyau ca dhārayet

sahasaiva samuccārya dhārayennandikeśvara

ātmabhāvitatāttatra gagane sūryabimbavat

prāsādena ca dīrgheṇa nāsāgre taṃ samuccaret

samuccaretpraṇavāṃśca mahāprāṇaśca madhyame

svātmānaṃ yojayettatra punarjanma na vidyate

sarvapāpātpraśamane prāṇāyāma samo nahi

śabdādiviṣayādibhyo balādāharaṇaṃ bhavet

yogyaṃ paśyati yatsarvaṃ tatsarvaṃ cātmani kramāt

svasminneva ca yaḥ paśyettadanuṣṭhānakaṃ bhavet

yogeṣu ca catuṣkeṣu śivayogaṃ pradhānakam

pratyāhārastatasteṣāṃ evaṃ proktaṃ caturvidham

prasārya caraṇāṃguṣṭhau dvitīyā gulphakau bhavet

pañcamañjānuyugalaṃ ūrvordvandvamataḥ param

mūlādhāraṃ tu navamaṃ adhiṣṭhānaṃ tataḥ param

trayodaśa viśuddhiḥ syāt tālumūlaṃ caturdaśa

lalāṭaṃ saptadaśakaṃ mūrdhācāṣṭādaśa smṛtaḥ

sārdhāṃgulaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca aṃguṣṭhadvayamānakam

jaṃghāmasyasthitermūlaṃ rudrāṃgulamiti smṛtam

* * navāṃgulaṃ corū tadūrdhvaṃ saptamaṃgulam

sārdhadvyaṃgulakaṃ proktaṃ mūlādhāraṃ hi madhyamam

meḍhrānnābhiṃ vijānīyāt punassārdhadaśāṃgulam

ṣaṣṭhāṃgulaṃ tu tanmadhyāt kaṇṭhakūpāntamānakam

nāsāmūlaṃ tu jihvāyāṃ mūlāttu caturaṃgulam

kodaṇḍadvayamadhyaṃ tu arkāṃgulamiti kramāt

lalāṭādbrahmarandhraṃ tu guṇāṃgulamitīritam

sthānātsthānātsamāhṛtya pratyāhaṃ samabhyaset

pratyāhāraṃ punarvakṣye śṛṇu tvannandikeśvara

mārutaṃ dhārayannāsā prāṇāyāmapravedanāt

lalāṭācca bhruvormadhye rodhayennandikeśvara

aṃguṣṭhāvadhikaścaiva pādayostannirodhayet

evaṃ tu pratyāhāraṃ ca kurvato'sya manīṣiṇaḥ

nāḍībhyāṃ vāyumāpūrya kuṇḍalinyāstu pārśvayoḥ

hṛdaye caiva māpūrya dhārayennandikeśvara

kiṃ punastasya rogasya nāśaṃ gacchati tatkṣaṇāt

bhruvoḥ kodaṇḍamadhyāttu stanadvandvādanantaram

bhrūmadhyāntaṃ samāropya cātmanaḥ prāṇamārutam

tāvadevaṃ prakurvīta vidhinānnandikeśvara

pañcaprakāramevaṃ tu tadidānīṃ śṛṇuṣvatha

dhāraṇeti tathā proktā nābhideśe samāśritā

athavā pṛthivī tatvaṃ pādādājānu saṃsthitam

pāyusthānaṃ samārabhya hṛdantaṃ vahnisaṃyutam

vāyutatvamiti proktaṃ tasmādūrdhvāntamambaram

adhvanāṃ ca viśeṣeṇa bhūtaśuddhiḥ kramādbhavet

pītaṃśvetaṃ tathā raktaṃ vajrasaṃnibhameva ca

tejoghanaṃ bodharūpaṃ kāraṇānāṃ ca pañcakam

brahmasthāne tu ghaṭikāpañcakaṃ dhārayetkramāt

viṣṇusthāne ca ghaṭikā paṃcakaṃ paṃcakaṃ pṛthak

anyadeśe ca kartavyaṃ tadāsyādanyathā yadā

kāmārthacendriya grāmadrāvaṃ jñāna sukhaṃ bhavet

dhyaicintāyāśca dhātvartheniścala jñānamucyate

śabdaḥ sparśaśca rūpaṃ ca rasogandhaḥṣaḍindriyam

mṛtaṃ cāmaradehatvaṃ gurutejassatārakam

paścādānandasaṃprāptirvedaśāstrāṇi vindati

yogināmupakārārthaṃ jñānināṃ tu viśeṣataḥ

aṣṭamūrtermaheśasya vīryaṃ rasamudīritam

śivavadvīryavān pūjyaḥ śivalokādi saṃsthitaiḥ

saptajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ darśanānnaśyati dhruvam

brahmahatyādibhiḥ pāpāḥ strīvadhā bālaghātakāḥ

rogī tu rogī na jñānī kālapādena bhākhilam

anyadeśenya kāle tu ta evāniṣṭahetavaḥ

vadharkṣe tasya janmarkṣe vipadādau viśeṣataḥ

yamāśleṣāgnirūpāṇi śraviṣṭhā vāruṇo maghāḥ

saurivāro'tha varjyaḥ syāt tathāhyaṃgāravārakam

śivāditrayanindāyāḥ duṣṭānāṃ sādhanaṃ param

śubhakarmāṇi sarvāṇi karturiṣṭadine nayet

aghora navalakṣe tu japecchuddhimavāpnuyāt

dṛṣṭvāpi na nameccaiva nastuvantyapramādataḥ

śivabhakti priye saktāḥ tepi cāntyanta pāpinaḥ

śivajñānaṃ saṃpūjya ye'dhīyante likhanti ca

lohena vikrayaṃ vāpi cānyajñānarato'pi vā

śivajñānakathāpekṣaṃ kṛtvācānyatprajalpanam

jñānanidrāsamaṃ sarvaṃ tadvacchuddhirvidhīyate

śuśrūṣāṃ cāpi bhaktiṃ ca viśvāsaṃ ḍambhavarjanam

śatrubhiḥ paribhūtaṃ ca tyajellobhādupekṣakaḥ

jyeṣṭhāva mānakaṃ cāpi svasāntānikadūṣaṇam

gurunindāsamaṃ sarvaṃ proktaṃ vai nandikeśvara

etaṃ sudikṣu kuryād acintyagranthasaṃkhyayā

alpabuddhiśca medhāvī cālpavīryo mahābalaḥ

tasya vai sakalaprāptirbhavediti na saṃśayaḥ

tena jānanti ye mūḍhāḥ satyaṃ pratyakṣaśaṃkanam

sarvavyādhikṣayaṃ gaccheducchrite bhaktito rase

trailokyaṃ bhuñjate so'pi bhavattasyājarāmaram

idṛśaṃ prastutaṃ tasya jāyate sādhakasya tu

gulmavyādhirujaṃ hanti grahabhūtavimardanam

timiraṃ paṭalaṃ kācaṃ mahāghoraṃ bhagandaram

jvaramaṣṭottaraśataṃ lūtāścāṣṭādaśastathā

apasmāra grahā ye ca ye grahāpakṣarākṣasāḥ

bhasmastratasya bhakṣāttu nivartanta iti grahāḥ

ajīrṇaṃ sāpyasaṃskārasādhakaṃ bhakṣayennaraḥ

kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi kalpakoṭiśatairapi

svamāṃsaṃ bhakṣitaṃ tena kṣudhayā ca prapīḍitaḥ

sabījaṃ bījahīnaṃ tu dvividhaṃ pāradaṃ bhavet

sabījasmṛtabhakṣaṃ tu hemaratnasutīkṣṇayuk

triphalaṃ bhakṣitaṃ yena kāmarūpī bhavennaraḥ

tatsarvaṃ kāñcanaṃ divyaṃ bhakṣayedvimalaṃ tataḥ

yaṃ yaṃ kāmayate bhāvaḥ hemabhasmani bhakṣite

trisaptāhe tu saṃprāpte tāmrākṣo jāyate bhṛśam

nārīsaṃge tu nandīśa śarīro kramaṇe rasaḥ

rasabhasma sadā samyag uparyupari bhakṣayet

bhautikaḥ pāpanāśārthaṃ svalpaṃ svalpaṃ ca bhakṣayet

aṇimādyaṣṭa saṃsiddhiṃ kramādeti na saṃśayaḥ

mutkālaṃ tu viśeṣeṇa pāradaṃ bhakṣayedgurum

prakṛtibhirasastutyajyate smābhiretat

ādheyī ṣaḍvidhaṃ proktaṃ ādhārādheya lakṣaṇam

maṇipūraṃ tu nābhau tu hṛdayaṃ tadanāhatam

padmādhāramiti proktaṃ ādheyaṃ tu ṣaḍakṣaram

* * ṇaṃ kāñcanaprakhyaṃ catuṣpatrābhamujvalam

caturdaśaiva sāntāni akṣarāṇi vicintayet

bhṛṃganādaṃ tataḥ paścāt parāśaktisvarūpakam

vyomākṣaraṃ tu tadbījaṃ tejasā bhāskaraprabham

balatejodharairyuktaṃ svacchavāri samaprabham

ḍhaphāntaṃ daśavarṇaughaiḥ taruṇāditya saprabhaiḥ

krodhīśabījasaṃyuktaṃ bhāvayennandikeśvara

kaṭhāntadvādaśārṇaughaiḥ sahasrakiraṇaprabhaiḥ

indrabīja sa saṃyuktaṃ tadbījaṃ rocanaprabham

śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ ṭhakārākṣarabījayuk

akṣapāṇiyutaṃ caiva repheṇa tu samujvalam

ṣaḍadhvasandhisaṃyuktaṃ sarvabodhapravartakam

ṣaḍādhāramiti proktaṃ guruvaktreṇa labhyate

ādhāraṃ prathamaṃ proktaṃ svādhiṣṭhānaṃ dvitīyakam

pañcamaṃ tu viśudhyākhyaṃ kaṇṭhamūle tu bhāvayet

ātmako daṇḍayanmadhyaṃ saptamaṃ jñāna bimbakam

tadūrdhvaṃ brahmarandhraṃ syānnavamaṃ nandikeśvara

kriyājñānākhyakācaivamicchāśaktistrimaṇḍalam

bindunādakalāyuktaṃ parānandāmṛtaṃ cayam

ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatakaṃ patrasyāpi dalaṃ bhavet

parāśakti mahāpadmaṃ tridaśairapi durlabham

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ

ādhārasya pradhānāntaṃ kuṇḍalīṃ ca śṛṇuśvatha

nīvāraśūkavartinyasyuptā cedbhujagākṛtiḥ

ādhārāṇāmaśeṣāṇāṃ bhedinī bodhinī parā

maṇḍalatrayasaṃbhedī tāravattu trivartulā

paścātsā kuṇḍalī devī pīyūṣa varṣakī śivam

mūlakuṇḍalinī devī dṛṣṭvā mantrāṇi bījakāḥ

evaṃ ca kuṇḍalī proktaṃ pyatigahvā mahodayā

akṣarādakhilaṃ jātaṃ kṣarāṇābhāvavarjanāt

akārādi kṣakārāntaṃ mātṛkākṣarato bhavet

paranādātsamudbhūtaḥ tuṭikālo ninādate

tatkālena tu saṃyukto binduśca parabindutaḥ

bījasya bālabhāve tu parāvāṇī vibhṛmbhitā

paśyantyabhikhyā triguṇitā yauvanaṃ madhyamā bhavet

tadakṣaramidaṃ nāma kalādīrghādadīrghatāḥ

icchājñānakriyābhedaiḥ sa bindustrividho bhavet

jñānabindossamudbhūtamākāraṃ sthitirūpakam

akārokāra saṃyogād oṃkārasvarṇamuttamam

akṣaratraya saṃyuktaṃ śabda brahmaravo bhavet

ekatra mathanādeva yathā pīyūṣa saṃbhavam

pañcavarṇāḥ praśasyante akārāntabhavātkramāt

kādibhāntākṣarāṇyevaṃ makārājjanitāni vai

dhvanitaśca samutpannā tathaivaṃ śādibhāntakam

laladvayasya cotpattiḥ pūrvameva samīritā

praṇavākāratassarva varṇotpattirapīṣyate

akārādikṣakārāntaṃ varṇānāṃ pṛthageva tu

svacchavarṇāruṇaśvetapītavarṇāstu bhūtaśaḥ

ketvantā varṇakastulyaṃ * * * * pravālakāḥ

lakṣayojanavistīrṇacaturmāsyoṣṭadṛgbhujaḥ

ūrjitābhāṇakṛṣṭo gambhīro valayāyudhaḥ

cakrāsanaścakranibho muktābhūṣaśca ṭaṃkadhṛk

caturaśca triṇayanaḥ kṣatriyaśca napuṃsakaḥ

* * kavo madakana ikāraḥ kūrmavāhanaḥ

divākalpassagandhaḥsyādikāro haṃsavāhanaḥ

kāṣāyagandhamākṛṣya bhūṣita tavigraham

caturaśrāsanaṃ svādu kapilāpāśavajradhṛk

caturbhujastriṇetraśca muktāhāropavītakam

khaḍgacakradharaḥ svādu mayū * * rṇakaḥ

tryarāsano nivarṇaśca dvibhujaḥ śūlavajrabhṛt

taptakāñcanasaṃkāśaḥ gandhastrivṛkā

sarvānukumbhakassarva devatānāṃ ca śāstrakṛt

sarvatomukhavadvāhuroṃkāro vṛṣavāhanaḥ

vajrapāśadharaḥ svādukāṣāyaṃ puṣpasaurabham

pāśahā śobhanākā * * * * karavāhanaḥ

jaṭāmakuṭadhṛcchūlī musalī kuliśāyudhaḥ

sahasravistaro lakṣārāmaṣaṭkoṇa * * kaḥ

indrādhidaivataṃ proktaṃ kakāro sakṣarottamaḥ

śaktitomarasaṃpannaḥ śubhamālyānulepanaḥ

pumārṇaḥ śaludhūvāgaḥ śvetavastraḥ prasannadhīḥ

padmāsanagataḥ sādhuhaṃsavāhaḥ prasannadhīḥ

vyāsa vatadāstamaśaktimān jharjharasvaraḥ

uṣṭroṣṭāṣṭakahmathapīṭho vidvāndhūmro mahābalaḥ

śūrpāsāḥ kākavāho durgandhasvarasaṃśrayaḥ

vibhṛnnayassahasrāṃśu hutabhṛt bhurka ca

jaṭāmakuṭasrakcchaṃkhapadmadhārī caturbhujaḥ

sahasrakoṭī vistīrṇaḥ cakāro ratnabhūṣaṇaḥ

śaktivajradharo muktākalpo varuṇadaivataḥ

makuṭī ca jakāraḥ syādhukāraśca tathāpi ca

ṭaṃkadhṛṅniṣṭhuro ghorasvayojanasamīritaḥ

sanomṛtarasaḥ kālo * * * * dvibhujaḥ pumān

vyālopavītīcandrā daivakāṣṭhuḥ prakīrtitaḥ

vajrapadmāyudho jambūdvīpamānassurakṣitaḥ

kaustubhāgaṃ pumānmeṣavāhaḥ susvādumandhavān

vaśyākṣakṛtsvādugandha * * * * ka saṃjñakaḥ

sahasrayojanāyāmaḥ satyavāso'dhidaivataḥ

sahasrayojanāyāmaḥ tadardhārdhāyutaḥ pumān

* * * padmapīṭhaśca madhuraḥ kinnarārcitaḥ

krūraḥ kaṭukamṛtsvādurasaṣaṇmukha eva vā

tomarī * * * * * yāmyassānugrahaścataḥ

napuṃsakaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ ūrdhvahṛtkrūravāhanaḥ

* * * * gadāgnimusalī śūladhanveyamau varaḥ

caturviṃśati vistīrṇo raudrī ghaṭimusuṇḍibhṛt

sitapavareṇa raudrā dhūrdhūrārthī ca nair-ṛtaḥ

kākavāhasvaraḥ kṛṣṇavastraścitrāsya utkacaḥ

madhuguggulutailākhya naro gī kaṭhorakaḥ

daśapu * * mājyotā madhujñānāsarojadhṛk

ṣaṭkoṭyāyāmakośena vismṛtena ca bhūṣaṇaḥ

pumānvidyutprabhassiddho gāruḍaḥ kamalāsanaḥ

daśakoṭīparīṇāhaḥ pumānugraprabhoṣṭadoḥ

gaṇeśān jāsanaḥ puṣpagandhaḥ prokto vakārakaḥ

umā bhedayo dhūma ūrdhvavaktro napuṃsakaḥ

dayātmakaḥ prabhonanda pumānambujāsanaḥ

vyālabhūmyāsuraśrīghramadyakaśśokakadvijaḥ

dūrvāvarṇo dhakārastu saṃprokte yamadaivatam

trikoṇagaśca āgneye repho mahiṣavāhanaḥ

* * * * * * aindro rukāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ

vākāro varuṇo vaśya tuṣṭi sthivarṇakārakaḥ

strīliṃgo madhurorarkaśca vāhaścaturbhujaḥ

raktāmbaradhara * * * * * * vanaḥ priyaḥ

mukhya kalpo vaśyakṛṣṭa śāntyā dṛṣṭiṃ prasā * *

pumānmayūragrīvābha śatruhāpāśavajradhṛk

iṣakastambhakūrcābhā gandhakāḥ

lakṣāyatostu vistīrṇo vaiḍūryabhūṣaṇaḥ

śvetaśvetāṃkuśasvāpi sarvavyāpī napuṃsakaḥ

sa śrīdaivatya strīkālajñobhijñaśśakārassarvataḥ

caturaśrāsanaḥ sarvākalpasrag gajavāhanaḥ

lastyādgaruḍagandharvā paripaṭyā dharārcitaḥ

parṇīgandharasākāra * * vāhasahetibhiḥ

sayogabhedādete'pi saṃprāptavāhāṃginaḥ

saṣa ullaṃghya saṃyogā madhano dvipastāṃ yathā

vaiṣamānihatānye ca yathā śakti viśeṣataḥ

saṃpado vipadaścaiva kuryātsvalya parasya vā

svarāḥ ṣoḍaśa saumyāḥ syurviśeṣeṇa ṣaḍakṣaram

kṣakāraśca vakāraśca ṭhakāraṃ ca caturthyakam

aṇoraṇutarāndhūva sūryāśośca na vidyate

ṛ ṝ ḷ ḹ ca * rṇāśca mādilākṣāntarāṇi vai

hakāraṃ vyavarṇaṃ syāt somasūryāgnirūpakam

sṛṣṭirūḍhi smṛtirmedhā kāntilakṣmīstathā svadhā

sthirā vyapālinīśāntiraiśvarī mamakā ratiḥ

tāmasī mohanī madhyā candrākṣarakrodhinī bhayāt

pītāśvetāruṇā kṛṣṇāścatasro vāyusaṃbhavāḥ

mṛtajñānā va vyāpinīstyapini vyomarūpā sadyānandā ṣoḍaśā narāḥ

suṣumnā bhogadā viśvā bodhinī dhāriṇī kṣamā

mṛtāmaranadā pūṣā puṣṭidā carati smṛtiḥ

pūrṇā pūrṇāmṛtākāmadāśvinyasasvarāṃ kalāḥ

ajarī hyamarī ghorā gambhīrā varṣaṇī tathā

subhagā va surūpī ca kalā śaktiśca ṣoḍaśā

kīrtirlakṣmīssṛtiścaiva tathaiva janarañjinī

kalāḥ ṣoḍaśa vikhyātāḥ paramānandadāyinā

jñānameva samādhiḥ syāt tadviśuddhastathocyate

etatturīyānītaṃ tu ātmano nityameva tu

samārgamadhyamaṃ jñeyaṃ bhavamārgamathocyate

mukhe jāgramiti jñeyaṃ tasmātsvapnakṛtāntukam

tasmādadhasturīyaṃ tu tasyātīyaṃ tu liṃgakam

jāgratkāle tvaco vyapti svapne vaktraṃ tathaiva ca

tadantastu tadātīta jīvavyāpta prakīrtitāḥ

śabdasparśaṃ ca rūpaṃ ca raso gandhaśca pañcamam

vacanādānagamana visargānanda pañcakam

nāgaḥ kūrmaśca kṛkaro devadatto dhanañjayaḥ

rāgamāyākalāvidyā niyatiḥ kāla eva ca

rā * * kāmatparyantaṃ ekatriṃśacca svapnake

jāgre viṣayabhuktiśca svapno na styāgatistakam

tadvaścaitanyaye yāgaḥ karaṇādinām

aṣṭādaśa suṣuptau tu vartate nandikeśvara

caitanyamātramekantu * * * taṃ mahodayam

ātmano'pi yavānbhoktuṃ prakṛtirhyativardhanam

vinākaraṇa dehānāṃ vyāparairātmanaḥ kṛtiḥ

svasthaṃ viṣayasaṃgośca khedalīnneyamātmani

namo vyāpārahīne tu cittāvidyāgamāgati

aṣṭādaśamadṛśyānāmaṣṭayodṛṣṭi dṛśyayoḥ

sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ ca susūkṣmaṃ ca ativṛttiṃ tathāntikam

suṣuptistu susūkṣmaṃ syāditi sūkṣmaṃ turīyakam

svapnaścaiva suṣuptiśca * * * tadadhiṃ ca vai

jāgredaśya pravṛttiṃ ca pratyāhāreṇa śāmyati

samādhinā turyatītaṃ turīyaṃ tu śivena tu

sadgurorupadeśena prakāśo na na saṃśayaḥ

rūpaśūnyamitijñeyaṃ samādhirabhidhīyate

ānandaṃ śivayogaṃ tu śivayogaṃ sadābhyaset

ānandaṃ jihvate jantostasmādānandamicchate

jñānādirahitaṃ buddhiścittāhaṃkāranāśanam

pāpanāśaḥ parāmuktiryattatsaṃsāranāśanam

ānandāntargatā muktiḥ tasyaiva tu na saṃśayaḥ

yogaṃ vakṣyehyarūpaṃ ca yogarūpamarūpakam

sā darśanaṃ svaśelliṃgaṃ sparśalakṣaṇamārabhet

sandhyākāle'thavā kāle sandhyāvandana pūrvakam

paścādyogaṃ samabhyasya te kāle yogameva tu

gurossamīpadeśe tu ca sadyogi samīpataḥ

sāntānikena sahito liṃgārcanayutena ca

sahāyo liṃgapūjāṃ ca kuryādasyāpi yoginaḥ

āsanaṃ tu tṛtīyaṃ tu cottarābhimukhasthitiḥ

ṣaṣṭhaṃ rudrākṣamālābhirupavītādi bhūṣaṇam

navamaṃ dāhanaṃ caiva daśamaṃ plāvanaṃ tathā

mantranyāsantu nandīśa ṣaḍaṃganyāsa pūrvakam

mūlādhāraḥ pañcadaśa ṣoḍaśaṃ brahmanāḍi vai

ekonaviṃśaddhṛdayaṃ viśuddhirviṃśatirbhavet

trayo bimbaṃ caturviṃśaṃ * * * * śivaṃ bhavet

ānandaṃ saptaviṃśatyā tadbhedaṃ ca punaḥ śṛṇu

śivākhyaṃ gururūpaṃ tu bhāvayettu prayatnataḥ

prabhayā vahate yastu sarvadoṣa nibarhaṇam

yadyacchivamayaṃ rūpaṃ śiṣyo dhyāyansamāhitaḥ

evaṃ dhyātvā samāyogī niścalena samāhitaḥ

śāntyatītāṃ kriyāśaktiṃ jñānaśaktiṃ tathaiva ca

ādyaṃ tatvaṃ ca pūrvoktaṃ paścādetāśca bhāvayet

tāmasī mohanītyādidaśavahnikalāḥ smṛtāḥ

rauravāśca dvādaśānu tāpanyādi kathāḥ smaret

tatra nāmādinavakaṃ dhyāyedvai nandikeśvara

ajāsthānena vāyukte kalāṣoḍaśakaṃ smaret

candrasthāne mṛtānādapūṣādyāścātha ṣoḍaśa

mūlādhāre ca santādyāstadūrdhve bālaṣaṭkakam

akārādi kṣakārāntaṃ kāntādeśe ca pūrvavat

svarāstatraiva bhrūmadhye candrabimbe tu ṣoḍaśam

mūlādhāre guhaṃ proktamādheyaṃ paramaṃ viduḥ

prāsādaṃ ca tadūrdhve tu saṃcintya tadanantaram

mṛtyuṃjayaṃ ca hṛdaye śivayantraṃ viśuddhike

śivabījaṃ tadūrdhve tu śaktibījaṃ tu bhāvayet

sthānaṃ prati ca yatproktaṃ tejorūpaṃ ca bhāvayet

sādheyamevaṃ proktaṃ syāt jñānadeśaṃ tathocyate

netra madhye tataścittaṃ svakīye sanniveśayet

jyotirākāśamevaṃ tu yāvadāste prayatnataḥ

paśyate ca jagatkṛtsnaṃ svakāyasyoparisthitam

jñānadeśasya cārdraṃ tu sthāṇuliṃgaṃ prakīrtitam

samastatejasāṃ tejaḥ pradāne ca pravīṇakam

* * prameya sādṛśyaṃ parandhāma mahodayam

cidghanaṃ sarvagaṃ sūkṣmaṃ ānandamacalaṃ dhruvam

sarvajanaṃ sarvakāryaṃ paraṃ parataraṃ śivam

kuṇḍalī jagadādhāra pṛthunāmasvarūpiṇī

* * traṭyakṣareṇaiva brahmarandhrāntamucyate

tattadādhārapadmāni nirbhidya brahmarandhragāḥ

karṣantīṃ ca śivaṃ tatra pūjaya * * * * līḥ

turyāta padmaṃ caitanyaṃ tatraivaṃ bhojayetkramāt

samādhirvakṣyate sūkṣmamādhārādheya va

tatparaṃ paramāṇūnāṃ parimāṇaṃ pracakṣate

ekaikaṃ tu śchaya pohe tu ścasmañcintayettadā

nirālambo matastasmin śaivaṃ tejaśca dṛśyate

krameṇaikāgra evātaḥ śaiva jyotiśca dṛśyate

ādyantaśūnyātītaṃ tat śivatejaḥ prakāśate

dhyātavyaṃ tu parāśaktyā śāntyatītā prakāśate

sthūla * * * śāmukteṣvannatasyā mahodayaḥ

anyane viṣayaṃ caitat tallaye līyate śive

ba * dyorbhāvite yoge sarvānandatvamaśnute

śaktyā viṣasya kampādikṣepa śāntau parasthitiḥ

savi * * * dā yogādvāraśānni śivo bhavet

brahmarandhrā praśāntyantaṃ dhyāyīta syācchivodayaḥ

śaktiṃ kośyatrayaṃ bhitvā khe paraḥ syāttadūrdhvataḥ

saṃvartya cakṣurekāgro yogī paśyetsvayaṃ nabhaḥ

prāṇaḥ kumbhādibhiḥ kṣiptaḥ kuryāttacchiva kāñcanam

ānande ca bhavedyogī sarvadā nandikeśvara

ākāśagamanādīni sarvajñānasamṛddhayaḥ

yogadṛṣṭyā tu sakalaṃ paśyate tu carācaram

ānandalakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

aparaṃ trividhaṃ proktaṃ mohajīvitasaṃjñakam

tārāṇāṃ putrapautrādi labdhikānandamohijā

sa tu kīrttyādisaṃjñāntaṃ laukikānandamucyate

etatkāraṇarūpaṃ tu priyāṃgaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam

triṃśat madhamaṃ jñeyaṃ śaktilakṣaṇamucyate

sarvaśobhanasaṃyuktā kṣaṇavedhakarāpakā

aparānanda saṃprāptiḥ siddhirasyaiva cocyate

ekaikaḥ pañcadhā jñeyaṃ śabdapañcakamucyate

gandharvodvāhatantrasya śravaṇaṃ ca tadudbhavam

śabdapañcakamevaṃ hi sparśapañcakamucyate

suhṛdāliṃganaṃ caiva gandhavāhasumarśanam

priyādarśanamevaṃ tu priyāṃ vīkṣya digambarām

vicitra darśanaṃ caiva sparśapañcakamīritam

rasapañcakamevaṃ syāt tattatkāle samudbhavam

puṣpagandhasudhāghrāṇaṃ ghrāṇapañcakamucyate

sadupāya samopetaḥ krīḍansarpeṇa dṛśyate

jñānayoga samāyuktaṃ tatvamindriya pañcamāḥ

evaṃ buddhyā svanandīśaparānandamathocyate

etattrayaṃ parānandaṃ mokṣakāraṇakaṃ bhavet

guroḥ priyavacaḥśrāvamupadeśaśataṃ tathā

liṃgasparśanakaṃ caiva bhūtyādi sparśanaṃ tathā

jñānadeśasya saṃspṛṣṭaṃ sparśapañcakamīritam

yogadṛṣṭyādilokaṃ ca rūpapañcaka saṃbhavam

yogāmṛtarasāsvādaṃ trividhaṃ rasamucyate

brahmendrādi surāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ janmakoṭibhiḥ

kadā kiṃcinna kasyāpi dattavānādiśa sthitam

evaṃ parātparaṃ jñeyaṃ varjanādi ca bhojanam

parātparataraṃ guhyaṃ grāhyagrāhakavarjitam

guruvaktreṇa tatsiddhirānandastu na saṃśayaḥ

daśakā ca sabhāyādi pūjanaṃ pūrvavadbhavet

prātassandhyā piṃgamāyaṃ suṣumnā madhyamo bhavet

dvādaśātma samaprakhyā bisatantumayā śivā

suṣumnāyāṃ tu tatkoṭi sadṛśīṃ bhāvayecchivām

sāyāhne cakṣuṣāyāṃ ca tilānalasamaprabhām

makāreṇa śivākhyena parā śaktyā vibhāvayet

sarvatejomayīṃ devīṃ candrārkadvaya nāśinīm

akāraṃ mūladhāraṃ syādukārāntopari nyaset

bindoruparinādaḥ syānnādasyopari śakriḍai

viṃśatiṃ ca visṛjyātha ānandāṃśca vimarśayet

rāgamāyākalāścāpi visṛjecca turīyake

* * * * paraṃ tatvamānasānāṃ layaṃ bhavet

kevalātma svarūpaṃ sa jñānarūpe tu bhāvayet

bhāvaṃ śivaṃ ca saṃbhāṣya svasti caivaṃ vibhāvayet

evaṃ dhyātvā mahāyogī tvakhaṇḍita mahodayaḥ

sarvoparāganirmuktaṃ tattu tiṣṭhati kevalam

sarvamāyāti vijñānaṃ tatturīyāya kalpate

nirmale svacchamādarśe manorājyaṃ purākṛtim

ghaṭānāṃ dvīparahitaṃ vijñānāntaṃ tu sarvagam

tāsāmidaṃ ca tadrūpaṃ paśyanviśvamayo bhavet

aikyaṃ mayādamaṃ paśyan yogī pūrṇatvamaśnute

svaśarīramivaikāgraṃ sarvātmādhyāyate śivaḥ

jagadvā yugapadyogī vimṛśaṃstatkṣaṇācchivam

cidrūpaṃ sarvatodhikyaṃ bhāvayedbhedamadyuti

manonmīlātvahaṃ ceti vimṛśantyāpya tiṣṭhati

suhittilīnāṃ tu viśvaṃ vimṛśaṃ syācchivo bhavet

tanau ca tasya bhidyeta iti dhyātvā tu yolayaḥ

adhaścakre bahiśceti parassamadhirohati

nirālambamanāḥ śīghraṃ cicchaktyā ca śivo bhavet

yogahīnā hata draṣṭuṃ kiṃ vā kartuṃ nirākṛtiḥ

anena sadṛśo yogo nāstyevaṃ nandikeśvara

yattatvaṃ tucchivaṃ vyāptibodhakaṃ mohavarjitam

śarīrabhedamātraṃ yat prāṇabhedo na kiṃcana

āṇavaṃ śaktisaṃbhūtaṃ śāmbhavaṃ trividhaṃ bhavet

anyairāṇava dīkṣā tu mandatīvrādibhedataḥ

vicitraṃ maṇḍapaṃ kṛtvā coktadravyairviśeṣataḥ

sugandhamakṣataṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpadīpaṃ caruṃ tathā

dravyaśuddhiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ

sugandhapuṣpairdhūpaiśca śiṣyamābharaṇādibhiḥ

bhūtavidyā praveṣṭavyā tāraratnena deśikaḥ

śivamantreṇa śaktyā ca japetsanmārganāyakaḥ

japitvā paramānandaṃ gurujñānarasaṃ param

kāritaṃ caiva tatraiva sarasaṃ śiṣyagocaram

pūrvameva tu saṃsādhyamābhyāṃ mantreṇa kārayet

yogāmṛtarasānmukto maṇḍalaṃ mudrayānvitam

sadānando bhaveddehe kṣudhānidrā tathā nahi

saptasopānasaṃpannaṃ sopānaguṇasaṃpadam

pañcābhijñāstṛtīyaṃ tu caturthaṃ siddhiraṣṭakam

ānandaṃ saptamaṃ jñeyaṃ sidhyamantyavacintayā

kleśādi varjitaṃ cittaṃ śāmbhavaṃ triguṇaṃ matam

svayamevedamādadhyāt sarvaṃ pradeśo dīpapañcakam

divya cakṣuśca śrotraṃ ca pañcābhijñānavanti ca

nirbījakaraṇānyeva viṣātpraharaṇaṃ tathā

yadbindunādamamaladehasaṃgamanaṃ tathā

daśapratyayamevaṃ hi aṣṭādaśabalaṃ śṛṇu

utpattiśca balaṃ tāpadharmacittaṃ tapaḥ phalam

labhate jñānabandhādi te nirdeśaguṇaṃ viduḥ

sarvasiddhimayaṃ sarvatrailokyaikya sunāyakam

niyatiścāpadandhe ca kalāguṇavatī tathā

śāntopāyamidaṃ proktaṃ śāmbhavaṃ tatra vidyate

śuddhavidyādharādvedhaḥ śāmbhavo vāyamīritam

etairetadvijānīyāt pratyayairāvaṇādikām

praṇāmaṃ kārayetpaścāt tasya nāma ca dāpayet

agnikārya kṛtaṃ vāpi nāgnikāryakṛtaṃ bhavet

śarīraprāṇakaṃ caiva gurvanujñāpurassaram

vittaṃ tu sakalaṃ tasmai gurave tu samarpayet

vācā ca karmaṇā caiva gurvarthaṃ tu caredbudhaḥ

pṛthivyā guṇagandhādi śuddhavastrādināpi ca

yāgāttadaivataṃ caiva visṛjennandikeśvara

rasasaṃjñasya tatvasya saṃjñāyuktaṃ vadāmyaham

cidghano jñānapūrṇatvāt eko sādṛśya pūrṇataḥ

sukhānanda svarūpatvāt citsaṃjñā tasya vairiṇaḥ

tasmājjagadekabījaṃ tu saṃjñitaṃ nandikeśvara

sukhaduḥkhādyahaṃbhāvaṃ karmapākaṃ vidhāya ca

nirajñānatvātkaivalyāt nirvāṇasya pradāyakam

saṃsārāmbhodhimagnānāmuttārātpālanātpatiḥ

sṛṣṭipālana saṃjñā ca tirodhānamanugraham

evamādyāṃ prasaṃkhyāyāṃ kalāstāḥ saṃprakīrtitāḥ

asyaiva cāvibhāgā ca jvālena ca havirbhujā

svecchāvigrahiṇāṃ vettau cidacidrakṣaṇāva hi

yūpavyoma tathaivecchā tasya paryāya vācakāḥ

śivasvecchānurūpārthaṃ jñānecchā śaktimādimān

jñānaśaktiḥ kriyāśaktiḥ samastaṃ yadvijṛmbhitam

kriyāśaktyulbaṇatvaṃ ca jñānaśaktyāpakarṣaṇaḥ

kriyāśaktyāpakarṣaṃ ca jñānaśaktyulbaṇaṃ tathā

bindoḥ kalāśca saṃjātaṃ sarvatatvasya dhārakam

śuddhānāṃ pañcatatvānāṃ kalāsambandhavarjanāt

vastutaśśivatatvasya tatvamantroditaṃ param

jvāla eva hutāśasya jyotsnā caiva himatviṣaḥ

jantorjanmasya mokṣāya śivaśśakti samanvitaḥ

jaḍavargasyāpyevameva sarvānugrāhakaṃ bhavet

sakale niṣkale caiva sarvatraiva samānatā

sārūpyaṃ mūrtisārūpyaṃ svecchāvigrahadhāraṇāt

etaccatuṣṭayaṃ śaktisāyujyaṃ cottamaṃ bhavet

malavāsanayā tasya vidyeśvarapadasthitiḥ

karmaṇonādibhūtatvāt bhoktavyaṃ mokṣakāraṇāt

tacchambhuḥ kṛtavānbhuktyai karaṇaṃ cāpi śaktitaḥ

anādinidhanāpyekā caivo pādāna kāraṇam

svakīyaśaktibhiśśambhurmāyāṃ vikṣobhya karmataḥ

dadyācchivo'pi śaktyā ca karaṇena viśeṣataḥ

bhūtaṃ bhavadbhaviṣyacca tridhārūpo bhavetpunaḥ

punaśca māyāniyatiṃ janayāmāsa daivatāḥ

saiva māyā punaścaiva kalāmajanayatkramāt

jñānaśaktau pratiṣṭhāpya vyañjayetkartṛśaktikām

kālanatvamidaṃ proktaṃ maṇḍūnāṃ bhogakāraṇāt

aśuddhākhyālpakā caiva paretānnandikeśvara

māyānniyati paryantaṃ svasva vyāpārakarmaṇā

kalayā puruṣāṇāṃ tu saṃbhūtā kartṛśaktikā

sukhaduḥkha moharūpeṇa guṇatraya samanvitā

śabdādi viṣayaṃ cāpi yadā grahaṇabuddhi vai

kṣīrasya khala saṃyogādadhīnāṃ ca tathā bhavet

tatvavidyaiva buddhiḥ syātkaraṇaṃ ca tathaiva ca

pralayānte tu yadvastu sarvajñādi guṇairyutam

tatrātmanāmalaṃ caiva bandhajanmatvakāraṇam

malānāṃ nāśanaṃ caiva dīkṣāyāśca bhaveddhruvam

tatastvaṃgagarbhādhānamanantaram

jātakarma tathā nāmakaraṇaṃ niṣkramaṃ tathā

vaiśvadevaṃ tathotsargaṃ pañcatvekatva saṃskṛtiḥ

aṣṭādaśa śarīrasya saṃskāroyamudīritaḥ

mānuṣyaṃ brahmayajñaṃ ca pañca yajñāḥ prakīrtitāḥ

paitramāsayujaścaitāḥ saptaite pākayajñakāḥ

āgrayaṇeṣṭikaṃ caiva cāturmāsyādanantaram

haviryajñāśca saptaite somasaṃsthastathocyate

vājapeyotirātrāṇi aptoryāmaśca saptamam

kṛtavatopyuktamārgeṇa dīkṣayā mokṣa eva hi

nāsti dīkṣā samaṃ nīlaṃ nāsti dīkṣāsamaṃ dhanam

nāsti dīkṣāsamaṃ jñānaṃ nāsti dīkṣā samā smṛtiḥ

vāsanānāśanaṃ cāpi dīkṣājñānakriyāstathā

vāsanāparipakvaṃ ca vandhyaṃ kuryācca vāruṇam

śivaliṃgārcanādeva nāśanāya na saṃśayaḥ

harṣaścaiva vināśaśca śeṣaṃ caiva manolayān

jñāne ca tālaṃ saṃyuktaṃ māyikepi catustathā

śivā bhūtaśravaṇācchivajñānaṃ pratiṣṭhitam

janitā vismṛtiścāpi kṣayameti na saṃśayaḥ

sthiratvātpuruṇavaryāṇāṃ kulayoṣitsamāśrayāt

dīkṣājātiśśivājātiḥ tulyajātirna vidyate

jaganmātā śivā proktā sarveṣāmātmanāṃ tathā

kāladairghyādasyayasya pitṛmātṛ pitāmahāḥ

paścāttṛtīyasturīyaḥ teṣāṃ cāpi sahasraśaḥ

sarvāṇyapi ca jantūni eka evāditaṃ tathā

tasmādruciracintādi nāsti vijñānasādhane

śarīraṃ tejasā hyante tathaivaṃ nandikeśvara

vyāpya vṛttiṃ karotyeva tatvavṛttiṃ tadanyathā

naivāsti kiṃcitkartavyaṃ vāsanāyukta karmasu

deśikastatva vijñānaṃ jñātvā tatvaprakāśakam

tatvādhvā bhuvanādhvā ca mantrādhvā ca tṛtīyakam

ṣaḍadhvabhiśca sarvatra vyāptameva carācaram

kālāgneḥśatarudrāntaṃ brahmāṇḍamiti viśrutam

sthāne vibhāgarahitaḥ paramāṇurihoditaḥ

līkṣāyugo yavo'pyevaṃ yavāṣṭakamathāṃgulam

tāladvayī karaṃ proktaṃ taddvayaṃ ca dhanurbhavet

krośatrayaṃ ca gavyūtiryojanaṃ taddvayaṃ bhavet

niyutaṃ prayutaṃ kāpe bṛndaṃ caiva tathārbudam

padmaṃ caiva mahāpadmaṃ śaṃkhaṃ ca tadanantaram

samudraṃ ca mahatpūrvaṃ samudrākhyamanantaram

ākāśaśca mahākāśamevaṃ parimitaṃ bhavet

brahmāṇḍasya kapālaṃ tu yojanairnyarbudairmatam

saṃsāra caṇḍate kālakoṭitejāstathā vidhaiḥ

sarvādhvāna tānaḥ yasmintejasi vartate

tasya svabhāvato jvālāḥ pravṛttā daśakoṭayaḥ

kālāgnibhuvanaṃ hyetat koṭiṣoḍaśayojanam

kālāgnibhuvanaṃ hyetat tasyordhvaṃ narakāṇi vai

yātanā * * * * yasthānāni kalpitāni hi pāpinām

rauravaṃ ca tamaścaiva śītamuṣṇaṃ ca bhāvakam

prasiddha narakāṇyete teṣāmadhipatīn śṛṇu

taptāṃgāra mahīnāha saṃtāpāśceti kīrtitāḥ

lākṣā pralepaṃmāsādannaruddhāsanayoccasāḥ

kriyamāṇāni yaṃ caiva rājānaḥ parikīrtitaḥ

tāmisrāścāyatāmisrāḥ kumbhīpākaśca rauravaḥ

eṣā puṭānāṃ narakaiḥ sārdhayojanasaṃkhyayā

tataḥ kūrmāṇḍa bhūmiḥ syāt didināvana khaṇḍikā

yopya badhyātpaṭastasmāddhṛdayaṃ cārdhamāyasam

kūṣmāṇḍa iti vikhyāto yugāntāgni samaprabhaḥ

paraśvathadharaścaiva tathā bhūtaiśca sevitaḥ

kulānāṃ caiva saptānāṃ pātālāntamudīritam

mahātala niṣaṇṇāśca krīḍante nirbharaṃ mudā

lakṣāṇi saptatiścaiva pātālānāṃ pramāṇataḥ

karkāntuko hiraṇyākṣo bṛhadgarbho balistathā

kārkoṭako'tha vālāṃgo * * * durdaśastathā

karālo bhīmanirhāpi piṃgalaśceti rākṣasāḥ

tatassauvarṇapātālaṃ daśalakṣapramāṇataḥ

nānāvikāra saṃyuktaṃ puraṃ koṭi samāvṛtam

tasyordhve navalakṣāṇi cāntaśceti kīrtitam

tataḥ koṭiśataṃ pṛthvī nānājana samāśrayāḥ

jambūśākakuśakrauñca śārṅgagomedaśca puṣkarāḥ

kṣārakṣīrodadhissarpirikṣukaṃ ca madhūdakāḥ

kṣīrodastu dvilakṣaḥ syād anyo dviguṇato'dhikam

tataḥ parastādgarbhodaḥ kaṭāhaśceti bhūtalam

pṛthvī bhāgavatā jñeyā sarvadā nandikeśvara

kṣīrābdhinā parivṛttaṃ parivṛttena tāvatā

tiraskṛtāṃ śumajjyotiḥ merussurasamāśritaḥ

nādrunāmānato lakṣaṃ mūle ca ṣoḍaśa smṛtaḥ

nemayaḥ kaṭakākārā nirgatā dīptimattarāḥ

naimitastatra kopānte lokapāla samāśrayāḥ

siddhagandharvamarutāṃ tadadhassarvasaṃsthitaḥ

prācyādiṣvindramukhyānāṃ nāma tadbharagarbhakam

siddhasādhyagarudyuktā rugmabhūramarāvatī

tejovatī yadāgneya nagarī jyotiṣāvṛtā

kālapāśapitṛvyādhi pretabhūtagaṇairvṛtā

indranīlamahānīla nīlaratnaprabhānvitā

śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkḷpta toraṇadhvajasaṃvṛtaiḥ

gaddhabāhasya nagarī sākṣādgandhavatī purā

nagarī sā prabhājālaiḥ dīpyantī paritaḥ kramāt

mahodayākhyāyakṣaiśca gandharvaiśca viśeṣataḥ

devasaṃghaiḥstutā bhāti puraiścaiva giriprabhaiḥ

anekarudra saṃyuktā nānāratnairvirājitā

nānāpakṣi samākīrṇā nānādruma vibhūṣitā

bhūtaye svarga ityetā gaditā svargaśīlakaiḥ

caturdaśa sahasraiśca yojanānāṃ pravistṛtā

yācakārāruṇo nunnairgaganaṃ hāsayanti yā

tasyāmupāsate devā munayaśca mahodayāḥ

meroḥ pañcamadigbhāge vaikuṇṭhaśikharaṃ bhavet

caturdaśasahasrāṇi vistārotsedhameva tu

tatraiva vasato viṣṇuḥ sarvadevagaṇairyutaḥ

ye sṛṣṭi sthiti saṃhārān kurvanti kramaśastathā

yakta jyotiṣkamityāhuḥ tathā paśupateḥ priyaḥ

skandanandimahākāla gaṇeśādi maheśvarāḥ

evaṃ lakṣaṇa saṃyukto rājate parvateśvaraḥ

ārdrāyutagaṇairyuktaṃ brahmādyaiśca surottamaiḥ

tatsaṃśiṣṭanavadvīpaṃ varṣāṇyasmin śṛṇuṣvatha

merordakṣiṇato nīlaḥ śveta bhṛṃgī tu yāmyataḥ

prāgāyatāssaparvāṇaḥ sāgarāḥ śatakoṭayaḥ

yāmyottarau prāk praticyairmerutastāvadañcarau

sukto na pakejāmālaṃ pratīcyāṃ gandhamādanāt

nāmnā kiṃ puruṣākhyātaṃ hāraktaṃ himavadgire

yaduttaraṃ śṛṃgayadaḥ kuruvarṣaṃ taducyate

viṣkambhākāravattatra parvatāssudṛḍhāstvamī

vedo haridrā cūrṇābho yāmyato gandhamādanaḥ

sahasrayojanocchrāyāsteṣu kalpadrumāḥ sthitāḥ

jambūphalarasodbhūtā meruparyanta nimnagāḥ

tāṃ pītvā pakṣiṇaḥ sarvā mṛgāśśākhāmṛgādayaḥ

dvīpake tu bhūjjāmbū * * * * * * * * *

prācyāṃ viṣkambhaśailasya mūle caitrarathaṃ vanam

yāmyāṃ nimnage gandharva * * * * * * * *

vaibhrājaṃ ca pule mūle sitodaśca samāvṛtaḥ

saupārśve dhṛtimān * * * * * * * hṛdayāḥ

trayodaśa sahasrāṇāṃ jambūphalarasāśinaḥ

* * * * * * * * * * tiḥ pibasārabhuk

candrabimbadyutirnīlājāsano bhadravājinī

triṃśābdaśca sahasrāyuḥ kāmavṛkṣa phalāśinaḥ

bhūtave sahasrordhvāvekadik sandhilakṣaṇau

candrabhadrākarādhipau candraraktāṃburugajanau ailāvṛtaḥ

antarbhāvaṃ kuruṣveva sānnidhyātkatamastadā

janasyaindradviṣo nityaṃ aśvato lāṃkulaṃ phalam

janasyābdasahasrāṇi nyagrodhaphalamaśnutaḥ

raukmaṃ kiṃ puruṣe plakṣa bhojanābdāyuta sthitiḥ

naveṣvavasthā bhedo'sti vivarṣeṣu kṛtādiṣu

nānājana padākīrṇaṃ jarārogabhayānvitam

teṣāṃ śatāyuḥ kḷptiśca caturyugavaśātpunaḥ

bhūrādīnāṃ samastānāṃ phalabhūḥ karmabhūryataḥ

bhūmibhuktiphalaṃ prāpya śivaṃ saṃprāpya yatnataḥ

indradvīpādayastatra śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

nāgadvīpāśca sābhyaśca gāndharvo vāruṇastathā

nānājāti samākīrṇā bhaktākhye pratikīrtitāḥ

nadānāṃ ca nadīnāṃ ca jñeyāḥ pañcaśatāni tu

himavaduttare bhāge kailāsaśikharaṃ mahat

sarvālaṃkāra saṃyuktaḥ divyavṛkṣairalaṃkṛtaḥ

aṣṭaṣaṣṭi mahākṣetraṃ vāraṇāsyādi pāvanam

mahendro malayassahyaḥ śaktimānṛkṣaparvataḥ

dvīpālaṃ kārasaṃyuktāḥ cāmīkaranibhāḥ śubhāḥ

jambūdvīpaṃ samākhyātaṃ lavaṇodadhyutaṃ śṛṇu

bhūdharā dvādaśāścaivaṃ prasiddhaṃ lavaṇodadhau

cakraścaiva tu maināko balākaṃ dakṣiṇastathā

varāhassomaketuśca nandanaṃ paścime diśi

lavaṇodadhirityuktaḥ śākadvīpaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

jyotiṣmānkrauñcadvīpetuśā malyucimāṃstathā

sapta sapta prajājātā saptasaptavibhāgaśaḥ

dvīpe tu puṣkarākhye tu vasānastu mahābalaḥ

parvataṃ valayākāraṃ mānasottaranāmataḥ

sindūrakāntimervādi vistāraṃ pañcakodadhi

tato hema mahābhūmiḥ vistāraṃ daśakoṭayaḥ

lokālokassamākhyātaḥ parvato vṛttarūpavān

lokapālāḥ sthitāstatra siddhadeva samāvṛtāḥ

lokālokaṃ samāvṛtya garbhodadhi samudrarāṭ

pārthivaṃ tu samāvṛtya tadāhaṃ koṭivistṛtam

daśadikṣu samaṃ jñeyaṃ pramāṇaṃ daśakoṭayaḥ

lakṣayojanamānena sūryalokaṃ vidhīyate

dvādaśādityaṃśumāhustrayastriṃśacca devatāḥ

meruṃ pradakṣiṇenaiva bhuvarlokamiti smṛtam

candrārdhalakṣamātreṇa sthitaṃ nakṣatramaṇḍalam

lakṣadvaye tu cāṃgāro dvilakṣe tu bṛhaspatiḥ

atriścaiva vasiṣṭhaśca pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ

upariṣṭāddhruvaḥ proktaścaika lakṣasya yojanāt

daśa pañcaiva lakṣāṇi bhūpṛṣṭhāttu dhruvāntakam

pañcāśītyardhalakṣāṇi janalokaḥ prakīrtitaḥ

dvādaśaiva tathā koṭiḥ pañcāśatkoṭayaḥ smṛtāḥ

kapālīśāhvayo buddho vajradehaḥ pramardanaḥ

indrasya balamākramya prabhuśakti samanvitāḥ

nir-ṛtyāṃ māṇavāṃ krodhahatrastamavilohitā

mahābalo mahābāhurdhūmaketussudaṃṣṭravān

vicaranti mahārudrā rākṣaseśena pūjitāḥ

śvetotha jayabhadraśca dīrghabāhurjalāntakaḥ

vāruṇaṃ balamākramya prabhuśakti samanvitāḥ

śīghro laghurvāyuvegaḥ sūkṣmastīkṣṇo bhayānakaḥ

pāvanaṃ balamākramya prabhuśaktisamanvitāḥ

vidyādharaśca sarvajño jñānadhṛgvedapāragaḥ

sukhado duṣkṛtaścaiva daśaite tu samāsataḥ

vicaranti mahādevā īśānena supūjitāḥ

bhūtaro kumbharaścaiva phaṇīndro vajradaṃṣṭrakaḥ

vicaranti mahādevā īśānena supūjitāḥ

saṃvāhaśca vivāhaśca nabholipsurvicakṣaṇaḥ

ūrdhvepi brahmaṇopyātha ākramya prabhuśaktibhiḥ

uktaṃ ca pṛthvītatvajñamāpyate tvamatha śṛṇu

vipadaṃ rudrakoṭīravimuktaṃ ca mahābalam

pavitrāṣṭakamevaṃ hi vyomāvaraṇamāśritam

chagalaṇḍadviraṇḍaṃ ca mokoṭaṃ muṇḍakeśvaram

sthānāṣṭakamidaṃ vidyāt pūrvādyaiśānamantakam

guhyādguhyāṣṭakaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ pavitraṃ cāpi dhūmrakam

evaṃ pañcāṣṭakaṃ proktaṃ kalpanābhimastu pārthivai

plakṣākāraṃ tu bhuvanaṃ bhuvaneśairniyāmitam

ata ūrdhvaṃ bhavedbuddhirbhaveddaśaguṇāvṛtā

saumyaṃ prājeśvaraṃ brāhmyaṃ devayonyaṣṭakaṃ smṛtam

sthānānyuktāni sarvāṇi sarvaiśvaryayutāni ca

akṛtaṃ ca kṛtaṃ caiva bhairavaṃ brāhmameva ca

proktaṃ yāmyaṣṭakaṃ hyevaṃ guṇāvaraṇamāśritam

teṣāṃ sthānāni divyāni kramādgacchanti yoginaḥ

gokarṇaṃ bhadrakarṇaṃ ca svarṇākṣaṃ sthāṇu rudrakam

krodheśaścaṇḍa saṃvarto jyotiḥ piṃgalasūragaḥ

mahādevāṣṭakaṃ hyevaṃ avyaktāvaraṇāsthitāḥ

rudraḥ piṃgo gaṇeśāno bhuvaneśapurassarāḥ

kāmakrodhavinirmuktāḥ parameśvarabhāvakāḥ

ṣaṭkauśikāni bhūtānāṃ viṣayāṇīndriyāṇi vai

etairyuktaistu puruṣo rāgatatve vyavasthitaḥ

vāmadevo'tha bhīmaśca ugraścaiva bhavastathā

īśvaro vāpyumābhartā giriśo'nanta eva ca

kalātatvaṃ bhavedūrdhvaṃ vidyāyāṃ tattatodhikam

daśarudrāḥ sthitā hyete vigraheśāna ca smṛtāḥ

ucchuṣṇāṃścambaraścaṇḍo mātaṃgo ghorarūpakāḥ

nivṛttyā śataguṇaṃ kālaṃ kālatatve vyavasthitāḥ

sthūlarudro paṭarudro rudro gurvalayastathā

kālordhvaśataguṇaṃ caiva māyātatvamihocyate

adhaśca saṃpuṭā vidyā dravordhvaṃ saṃpuṭā purā

daśośānastathā caiva prabalo gopatistathā

kṣemīśo brahmadhāmīśa vidyeśāstu tathaiva ca

ūrdhvamāyāpuṭasthāstu rudrāste viprakīrtitāḥ

bhāvayitvādyacotpattiṃ kurute svecchayā prabhuḥ

bhujyante tatra tatraiva bhogāḥ pramuditātmanā

māyāntaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu

tadātmāvidhayaśśuddhā saṃjñāḥ kratvādibhirgaṇaiḥ

arthapañcakanirmuktaḥ puruṣo syātmakastathā

māyādyavaniparyantaṃ jīvabhūtaśśivecchayā

ātmatatvāttu sāhasraṃ vidyātatvaṃ prakīrtitam

na śivena vinā śaktiḥ na śaktirahitaśśivaḥ

ahaṃkārastathā buddhirgaṇamavyaktameva ca

vidyākalā ca niyatiḥ kālatatvamanantaram

śuddhavidyā tadūrdhve tu aiśaṃ tattatvamiśritam

śivatatvaṃ paraṃ proktaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśattatvamuttamam

etānyuktāni tatvāni pañcatatve sthitāni tu

tejo vidyāntamevaṃ hi vāyurīśvaratatvayuk

nivṛttiḥ pṛthivījñeyā pratiṣṭhā ca jalaṃ bhavet

atītā śāntirvyometi kalāḥ pañca pratiṣṭhitāḥ

pṛthivyādyātmatatvāntaṃ ātmatatvamudīritam

tasyordhvaṃ śivatatvaṃ tu tatvatrayamudīritam

ātmaiva cātmatatvaṃ syāt sarvatatva prabhustataḥ

prakṛtiḥ puruṣaścaiva īśvaraśca tridhā bhavet

vidyātatvāditatsarvamaiśvaraṃ tatvamuttamam

kalāpañcaka saṃyuktiradhvanāṃ pañcake tava

etasminneva bhūtānāṃ varṇā lavaradā smṛtāḥ

repho hi vahnisaṃyukto yakāro vāyureva ca

varṇānāṃ kalayā sārdhaṃ varṇādhvā dhyānato bhavet

bhāvodbhāvodbhavaśśarvassarvabhūtasukhapradaḥ

astutatpūrva sthitaḥ | sākṣisākṣi | ṛtu ṛtu

sūkṣma sūkṣma | śiva śiva | oṃ namaḥ | śivāya namonamaḥ

vyāpin vyāpin | arūpārūpa | prathama prathama

anāde | nā nā nā nā | dhū dhū | oṃ bhūḥ oṃ bhuvaḥ

paramātman maheśvara maheśvara | mahādeva maheśvara

oṃ namaśśivāya sarvaprabhave īśānamūrdhāya

guhyāya sadyojātamūrtaye oṃ namonamaḥ

vidyāyāḥ padānyuktāni * * * * * * tatra vai

oṃ śivāya anantāya anāthāya | anāśritāya

śyānāhārāya

omityeka padaṃ nityaṃ śāntyatīta kalāpadam

vakṣyate tatra tatrādhvā īśānādi śivāntakam

sadyamityeva muktaṃ syātpadmabrahmaparaṃ matam

hṛdayaṃ ca śiraścaiva śikhā kavacameva ca

netraṃ vidānnivṛttyādi śāntyatītaśivāntakam

kalānāṃ vakṣyate tatra nivṛttyādyāśca pañca ca

etāḥ pañca kalāḥ proktāḥ pañcabrahmakalāḥ punaḥ

īśānasya kalāḥ pañca proktāstatputravā punaḥ

puruṣasya kalāścāpi catastra iti kīrtitaḥ

jarā caivāṣṭamī vidyā aghorasyetitāḥ smṛtāḥ

kalakāmavikaraṇā svayamī tu parikriyā

vāmadevakalāścāpi trayodaśakamīritam

sadyojāta kalāścāṣṭau aṣṭatriṃśadudīritāḥ

ṣaḍadhvabhiśca mārge'sminviśatyeva śivaṃ param

viśeṣajñāna pūrvatvāt vicitratvādvijṛmbhaṇāt

mantrahīna kriyāhīna dhyānahīnādidīkṣite

dīkṣāya tacchivatvaṃ hi kṣīyate sarvasaṃśayaḥ

atyanta bhaktisaṃyuktaṃ gurudarśanamātrataḥ

sarvadā śravaṇotkaṇṭhaṃ sarvadā śravaṇonmukham

ājñāpālana saṃyuktaṃ sukhaṃ vā duḥkhameva vā

samudrapatane caiva jāhnavyāṃ patane'pi vā

sarvasvaṃ jaladhau kṣiptaṃ iti cettena bhāvitam

bhūmau nikṣipya vittādī nanu vā jñānamārgaṇam

visarjane cettaccittaṃ tadvisarjanavallabham

tatrāvicārya dīkṣāntaṃ evamādiṣu sarvadā

dīkṣayetkṛpayā pūrṇo gurussanmārganāyakaḥ

sā dīkṣā jñānadīkṣā tu vijñānākhyā ca vai bhavet

manoramyākhavasthāsu ubhayossukhasāmyayoḥ

pañcādhāra sthitaścāpi gurudehe vyavasthitaḥ

sarvasākṣimayaṃ tattu śivaṃ paramakāraṇam

guroḥ pāṇitale caiva sthitaṃ sakalatīrthakam

devarṣibhistu saṃsevyaṃ aṃgulīparvasandhijam

hastamūle tu bhūtānāṃ tīrthamityabhidhīyate

saptatīrthaṃ ca taddhaste vidyamānaṃ vibhāvayet

tasya dakṣiṇa hastena śivamantreṇa vyāpitam

gandhaiḥ puṣpaiśca dhūpaiśca dīpaiśśiṣyaṃ ca kārayet

evaṃ vidho bhavecchiṣyo bhavedgururanantaram

ājñādhvāne ca sarpīṃṣi candramaṇḍalagāṃ sudhām

manassruk buddhisruvaṃ ca samidhaśca kalāstathā

iḍāṃ ca piṃgalāṃ cāpi somasūryaṃ ca bhāvayet

pūrvamevāgni siddhatvād vinā cāgnimukhaṃ tathā

puṣpāntaṃ mantramevaṃ syāt jñānāhutīranuttamāḥ

so haṃ hauṃ svāhā | oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ oṃ

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ oṃ hāṃ īśvaramayassohaṃ hauṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ sūryasomāgnissohaṃ hauṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ tatpuruṣābhyāṃ sohaṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ aghoravāmadevābhyāṃ sauṃ hauṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ śāntikalāyai sohaṃ hauṃ svāhā

oṃ hrī. haṃsaḥ | sadyojātavāmadevāghora tatpuruṣeśānebhyaḥ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsassohaṃ hauṃ svāhā

daśāṃśamaṃgamantreṇa homayennandikeśvara

oṃ hauṃ sohaṃ haiṃ svāhā | pratiṣṭhā kalāyai sohaṃ hauṃ svāhā

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ śāntikalāyai sohaṃ hauṃ svāhā

vāgīśvaryāstu bījaiśca homayennandikeśvara

śiṣyacaitanyamākṛṣya mūlādhāre ca yojayet

gurumaṇḍalamadhye ca sadānandā mṛtojvale

śivarūpamidaṃ dhyātvā punarmūle ca yojayet

māyādibhūmiparyantaṃ ekatriṃśacca tatvakam

vilomena ca tatsarvaṃ pratyekaṃ homamācaret

oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ bhavassohaṃ hauṃ svāhā

ityevamādihomaṃ ca manasā juhuyādguruḥ

pūrṇāhutīśśivenaiva homayecchivabhāvitāḥ

śivaśaktyātmakaṃ budhvā śiṣyadehaṃ nirīkṣayet

candrārkanāśanaṃ cāpi satataṃ yogavardhanam

santataṃ śivayāgaṃ ca nityaṃ tiṣṭhati tiṣṭhati

saṃyojya tatra saṃlīnaṃ śivaṃ śiṣyaṃ ca bhāvayet

saṃhārakramamārabhya ṣaḍadhvāno viśeṣataḥ

vācyavācakaśūnyaṃ tu manovācāmagocaram

ādyanta śūnyaṃ kaivalyamasaṃjñamanantakam

evaṃ dhyātvā mahājñānī śiṣyamāliṃgya nirbharam

ānandastasya tatraiva jāyate ca na saṃśayaḥ

saṃpadyate ca tatraiva śivarūpī viśiṣyakaḥ

hastābhyāmabhiṣekaṃ ca kuryātsanmārgadāyakaḥ

śivaṃ parātparaṃ sūkṣmamaprameyamanuttamam

parātpareṇa bhedena pāpenāśvatthadarśakam

caidyaṃ tvapara vijñānaṃ paśupāśārtha darśakam

tattattatvaṃ kathaṃ brūhi tadasaṃkarasaṃkaram

bauddhaṃ ca ārhataṃ caiva pāñcarātraṃ ca śaivakam

śāpakaṃ ca mahāyānaṃ tataḥ pratyekanāyakam

lokāyataṃ tu tatraiva mahāyānānugāmi ca

pratyekanāyake tatra sautrītikapadābhratāḥ

vaibhāṣikasthitirdvedhā pratyekaṃ śravakaṃ tathā

parametānaśaśceti sumantraratha eva ca

dvidhā mantrasthayogāccaradire * * * va ca

evaṃ mādhyamikā proktā mā * ṣamādvayastataḥ

bauddhabhedasthitaṃ jñeyaṃ mahābhedamathocyate

tribhistribhiḥ prakartavyamāśramaṃ ca caturvidham

vānaprasthaśca ityete āśramāśca udāhṛtāḥ

pañcarātraṃ bhāgavataṃ tathā vaikhānasaṃmatam

vaiśeṣikaśca sāṃkhyaśca ātma vā iti kramāt

vāmaṃ tu trividhaṃ proktamanādiḥ pūrvapaścimam

kālāmukhaṃ ca kāpālaṃ tadvatpāśupataṃ bhavet

karmāntamevaṃ jñānāntaṃ jñānamūrdhvaṃ paraṃ smṛtam

caryākriyā yogayuktaṃ jñānaṃ caiva bhavettadā

samāptakaluṣatvaṃ ca muktistatraiva cocyate

bindurnādaśca śaktiśca paraścaiva parātparam

nirāvaraṇamevaṃ tu śaivabhedastathottaraḥ

evaṃbhūta viśeṣo'sti tadviśeṣaṃ śṛṇuṣva tat

virāgamārhataṃ caiva avyaktādvismṛtaṃ param

śaivaṃ parātparaṃ jñeyaṃ sarvatatvaikanāyakam

janmāntaratapobhiśca sanmārgajñāna saṃbhavam

laṃghane narakaṃ caiva jñānacorastathaiva ca

gurvārādhananiṣṭhasya kartṛjñānaṃ sphuṭaṃ bhavet

dehayuktasya tatsiddhirdehāntepi ca tasya vai

nānāvidhairupāyaiśca vāsanānāṃ ca vardhanam

śaiva siddhāntayuktasya saṃjñāṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva tat

pūjanāpañcakaṃ vāpi karmāvaraṇa yuktake

pañcatatvaṃ guhāṃ vāpi vṛkṣaṃ ca vanameva ca

jñānācaraṇakaṃ tvaivaṃ nāsya nāma pṛthak pṛthak

maṭhaṃ brahma ca vikhyātaṃ sanmārgaṃ darśanaṃ bhavet

manobhaṃgākhyamatulaṃ siddhimantraṃ guhāṃ tathā

gocaraṃ śivamantraṃ tu santānaṃ tanmayaṃ bhavet

bodhayetsarvavijñānamanenaiva vicāraṇāt

viprādiṣu caturṣvevamabhiṣekaṃ tu ṣaṭsu ca

lokānuṣṭhāna saṃvādo jātibhedaśca nirṇayet

daśadīkṣānna jātīnāmabhiṣekaṃ na kārayet

tasmādyateśca kartavyaṃ śaivamārge viśeṣataḥ

parahaṃsayatīnāṃ ca tapassanyāsināṃ ca yat

homaṃ vinā tayorevamabhiṣekaṃ vidhīyate

śrutajñānopa saṃyuktamācāryaguṇa saṃyutam

sarve saṃpūrṇavatpūjyā maṇḍapādiśca pūrvavat

vardhanīṃ tasya vāmena vinyasecchakti saṃyutaḥ

pañcāśadauṣadhairvāpi daśapuṣpai * * na vā

viṣṇukrāntiśca pañcaite pañcauṣadhamathāpi vā

kuṃkumośīrakandaṃ ca caturjāti samanvitam

śuddhatoyena saṃpūrṇān cūtapallavaśobhitān

ikṣu sārāmbu śuddhāmbugarbhotpannānsamudrakān

pūrve śikhaṇḍinaṃ caiva śrīkaṇṭhopi ca koṇake

vāruṇyāmekanetraṃ ca sūkṣmadaṃṣṭraḥ kṛto bhavet

madhye śivasamudraṃ ca vardhanyāṃ śaktisāgaram

śivavatpūjayetsarvaṃ mūlamantrāṣṭavācakaiḥ

pañcakumbheṣu vidyeśān dvau dvau kuṃbhe tu vinyaset

madhye ca śivakumbhaṃ ca śivāmbhodhiṃ tathaiva ca

maṇḍapottarabhāge ca snānamaṇḍapamuttamam

śrīparṇādyāsane tatra vinyasyārcitamānasam

kulālodanamṛdbhasmadūrvāgomayagolakaiḥ

kṣīrodānukrameṇaiva hṛdi vidyeśamantrakaiḥ

śuddhavastreṇa saṃveṣṭya niveśya śivadakṣiṇe

uṣṇīṣaṃ makuṭaṃ chatraṃ pāṇḍaraṃ cāmarāṇi vai

rathāśvagajapatrādi karaṇīṃ kartarīṃ ghaṭīm

akṣamālākuśau cāpi sruksruvau pustakādi ca

tṛptaye mantrajālasya juhuyātpañca cāhutīḥ

śrotavyaṃ ca śivajñānaṃ śrāvayecca tadarthitam

svalpaṃ japaṃ cāgnikāryaṃ svalpamagnau ca homayet

āgamānāṃ cādhyayanaṃ sarvatraiva pulo bhavet

pūrvavanmaṇḍapādīṃśca pūjanaṃ cāgnikāryakam

śāntaṃ samantamānīya śodhayennandikeśvara

śataṃ saṃyojya kurvīta guṇaṃ tādṛśamaṣṭadhā

mahimā ceśitatvaṃ ca vaśitvaṃ cecchayā yathā

oṃ hāṃ aṇimāśakte bhavatu svāhā

evamādi caturthyantaṃ svāhā yuktena melayet

pūrvavadyojanāṃ kṛtvā pañcabhiḥ kalaśaissaha

mūlamantreṇa hotavyamaṣṭottaraśataṃ kramāt
