pūrvādidik samudrāṇāṃ kalaśairabhiṣecayet

pīyūṣavarṣabudhyā tu sādhakaṃ cābhiṣecayet

śambhordakṣiṇabhāge'pi niveśya sakalīṃ priyām

adhikāraṃ ca tatraiva dāpayennandikeśvara

recakena yathā dadyāt puṣpādi yutamañjalau

hṛtpadme purakoṇaiva sādhake sanniveśayet

dakṣiṇe maṇḍalādīnāṃ mātṛkāmbujamarcayet

gurorājñāṃ samāsādya prāṇāyāmaṃ kramātpunaḥ

yogītyaṇimādisidhyarthaṃ ghṛtasya payaso'thavā

triṇayanāgamārthyādi pāraṃparyakramānugamam

bhūbhujānānnarāṇāṃ ca vandhyādīnāṃ ca yoṣitām

saubhāgyalakṣmīvijaya kāmyadharmādi siddhidam

oṃ hṛdayāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ | oṃ śrīṃ ślīṃ śirase haṃphaṇṇamaḥ

mantraṃ sakṛtsamuccārya śarīre vinyasedguruḥ

padmamaṣṭadalairyuktaṃ kṛtvā cāstraṃ samuccaret

tārāsanasthadeveśaṃ daśabāhusamanvitam

arghyaṃ prati triṇayanaṃ jaṭāmakuṭa saṃyutam

jihvābhirlelihānaṃ taṃ sūryakoṭyayutaprabham

aṇimāmadhyamāsīnaṃ pūjayetparameśvaram

athavā japamātreṇa siddhaṃ kāryaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

bhuktimuktyośca saṃsiddhirbindunāda samanvitam

pakāraṃ nai-ṛte bhāge śrīkāraṃ vāyugocare

evaṃ vinyasya saṃpūjya vidhinā homamādiśet

madhutrayairhomakāryaṃ mantrarājāya vai bhavet

tathāvidhiṃ vidhānena purvaṃ kalaśapūraṇam

aṣṭordhvaśatamantreṇa pūjayedastrarājakam

vedaghoṣaiśca vāditraiḥ stotrairmaṃgalavācakaiḥ

deśikāya puraskṛtya dadyātprītyartha siddhaye

aghoraṃ prītidaṃ kurvatsarvaroganivāraṇam

amṛtaughamayaṃ cakraṃ sarvasāndrasukhāvaham

cakramantraṃ tato dhyānaṃ sādhanaṃ cābhiṣecanam

aṣṭapatraṃ likheccakraṃ bhūpatrādikake tathā

himāmbubhiśca saṃyuktaṃ rasadravyaiśca lekhayet

tadvṛte cāmbujadvandvaṃ kamalākarṇikāyutam

ekoddhārayutaṃ caiva cintāmaṇirivāparam

sakāraṃ ca jakāraṃ ca tasyānte repha saṃyutam

* turīyāṇi madhye'pi śaktibīja samanvitam

candrarūpaṃ calaṃ cāpi caturānana saṃyutam

sadāśivasya bījaṃ tu likucena tu saṃyutam

dalabāhye viśeṣeṇa kalāḥ ṣoḍaśa vinyaset

catratīyastṛtīye ca caturthīdala paṃktike

ṣaṣṭhe tu dāntaṃ vinyasya saptame śivabījakam

ṣoḍaśasvarayuktāni vinyasetkramaśassudhīḥ

anyaṃ tu madhyamaṃ cakraṃ śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

pūrvavatpiṇḍikāmadhye piṇḍarūpaṃ mahāmanum

jakāraṃ ca tato bāhye tadbahirvāruṇaṃ nyaset

sadhmarāṇi sa vijñāni nyasecchaktāni pūrvakam

gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā darpaṇodarasannibhe

sitakṣaumayutaśśuddho bhasmoddhūlitavigrahaḥ

nistulairmauktikairyuktaḥ śuklayajñopavītavān

karpūraistu himāmbhobhiḥ likhetpūrvokta cakrataḥ

ātmānaṃ śivaṃ ca nāhaṃ bhāvayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ

tasyordhve tu guruṃ dhyātvā tadgātrāmṛta vāribhiḥ

sasārūpyayutaṃ mantraṃ japaṃ kuryātsamāhitaḥ

śvetairnīlaiḥ śvetapuṣpaiḥ śvetataṇḍulakairapi

śivaṃ candrāvataṃ saṃ taṃ nivedyaṃ pāyasaṃ param

āyuṣkāmastulāyāṃ ca vidhā skandādi pūjayet

saṃhṛtya juhuyādagnau apamṛtyuvināśanam

vāratrayaṃ ca juhuyāt sahasraṃ ca dine dine

dinatrayaṃ ca kartavyaṃ paurṇādikamato bhavet

ekaviṃśaddinaiścāpi gaḍūcyā sitataṇḍulaiḥ

vṛṣṭikāmo'pi pūrvāyāṃ prātaḥ snātvā samāhitaḥ

paraṃ vighneśvarādīṃśca pūrvavatpūjayetpunaḥ

iti saṃkalpya manasā śivabhaktāṃśca bhojayet

tanmadhye vedikāṃ kuryād hastamātreṇa sādhakaḥ

caturdroṇai sthitaṃ padmaṃ sāṣṭapatraṃ saparṇakam

sapallavāpidhānaṃ tu dukūladvaya saṃyutam

muktādāpa parikṣiptaṃ kṣiptaṃ puṣpopaśobhitam

śvetaissugandhaiḥ puṣpaiśca pūjayettadanantaram

vahniṃ candramasaṃ dhyātvā vetasorthaissamidvaraiḥ

saptaviṃśaddināntepi mahāvṛṣṭirbhaviṣyati

vaśyakārye tu madhunā sarpiṣā jāti puṣpakaiḥ

mūlamantraṃ samuccārya sādhyanāmamataḥ param

sahasraṃ juhuyānmantraṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tu vā

chitvā dakṣiṇapādādi vāmapādāvasānakam

ekaviṃśaddinaṃ cāpi kṛtvā pūrṇādikaṃ caret

sikthena vāpi sādhyasya svarūpākarṣaṇaṃ hunet

ākarṣaṇamidaṃ proktaṃ kaulālikya mṛdāpi vā

jvaradāhādiśāntyai ca pūrvavaccakramādbhavet

pūrvādibhasasarpiśca kṣīrapāyasa saṃyutaiḥ

ekaṃ saptadinādeva jvaradāhādiśāntike

saptavāraṃ sahasraṃ ca juhuyānmahatīṃ śriyam

mahāgaṇapati proktāstīhanaṃ cāpi kārayet

sarpiṣā pāyasenaiva trisahasraṃ ca homayet

tatraiva bhavane kūpaṃ jalamabdhisudhopamam

svakīyāmṛta saṃsthānaṃ mardanaṃ viṣanāśanam

netraśruti bhrukuṭigaṇḍaśikhāśirassu

viṣādaṃ viṣadaṣṭaṃ ca pūrvamuktaṃ ca cakramam

niṣkairdvādaśakairvāpi tadardhaiśca tadardhakaiḥ

maṇḍapastha jalaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvavannandikeśvara !

pūrvoktaṃ ca kramāllikhya pūjayedbhaktisaṃyutaḥ

catuṣkoṇe śubhaistoyaissugandhaiḥ pūrayedghaṭam

brahmāṇḍarūpametattu budhvā tanmadhyagaṃ jalam

samabhyarcya sitaiḥ puṣpaiḥ homakarmādiśaktibhiḥ

vedaghoṣa samāyuktaṃ stotraghoṣa samanvitam

gurubhaktisamāyuktaḥ pūjayecchivavatkramāt

dadyādbhaktyā ca gurave namaskuryācca daṇḍavat

tatsaṃtatopavāsaśca bhaikṣaṃ tadvadayācitam

prathamaṃ bhujakte kāke tvannapānādisevanam

etallakṣaṇa saṃyuktamupavāsassatāṃ matam

ayācitaṃ tuścathā mānādareṇa samarpaṇam

anyatvayācanaṃ kṛtvā svakīya dravyabhojanam

trisaṃdhyāvandanaṃ cāpi śivaliṃgārcanādikam

tattadbhojanamityuktaṃ sarvapāpa praṇāśanam

sa dopavāsācchreṣṭhaṃ hi bhikṣāṃ tannandikeśvara

pūrvāhne devatābhuktiḥ madhyāhne bhūribhojanam

sarvavelāmatikramya naktabhojanakaṃ bhavet

bhikṣāvidhiṃ visṛjyātha bhojanatritayaṃ śṛṇu

viśuddhapākamānīyamaṃśakādi vivarjite

saptavāraṃ ca darbhaiśca prokṣayejjala bindubhiḥ

cullīhomāya śeṣārdhaṃ kalpayedvidhinā saha

nātyagninā caikamatyaṃ recakenāpi vāyunā

śivāni svamiti proktvā cullikāgnau tu homayet

oṃ hāṃ sūryāya namaḥ | iti oṃ hāṃ | oṃ hāṃ

sviṣṭakṛtaye namaḥ * * * * * * * * *

svāhāntamāhutīrhutvā kṣamayitvā visarjayet

oṃ dharmāya namaḥ | iti vāmabāhau | oṃ sara-

jalāya śrutāya varuṇāya namaḥ | omiti

grahapramadevacodite namaḥ | oṃ saṃmārjanam |

skandāya grahādhipataye namaḥ | iti madhyastaṃbhādhastāt

bhautiko naiṣṭhikaścāpi tadyogena baliṃ kṣipet

tatraiva sarvadevānāṃ ekānāṃ ca baliṃ kṣipet

evaṃ vā tatra kartavyaṃ deśikasya viśeṣataḥ

śuddhaśaivaiśca sanmārgataponiṣṭhaissudāruṇaiḥ

ekapaṃktyā tu bhuñjīta maunīcāntarmanāśśuciḥ

gurussa sādhakaścaiva putrāśca samayīkramāt

āmrapatraṃ nālikeraṃ madhūkaṃ ca tathaiva ca

kāṃsye tu bhasmanā śuddhe saptadhā patrake'thavā

pādādisajjabhallāta plakṣāśvatthārka saṃbhavaiḥ

durgandhayuktairanyaiśca niṣiddhairbhojanaṃ tu vā

ayogyaṃ bhojanaṃ tatra bhuktiśceddoṣa eva ca

snānaṃ tu bhasmanā caiva rekhāyā rakṣaṇaṃ bhavet

vāmahastena saṃspṛṣṭvā pātramanyena vā spṛśet

astramantreṇa saṃdīpte juhuyātsaghṛtāspadam

svāhāntairāhutīḥ pañca juhuyānnandikeśvara

upaprāṇāya svāhā * * * * * * * * *

bhuktvā oṃ amṛtāpidhānamasi | mantreṇa prakṣā-

grahenaiva tu pūrayet

prāṇasaṃcāraṇārthaṃ tu ekabhāgaṃ tu pūrayet

kūjanaṃ hastadattaṃ ca lavaṇaṃ parivarjayet

udumbaramalābuṃ tu śārgāṣṭhaṃ ca kumārikām

vṛntākaṃ kākujaṃ caiva chatrākaṃ bimbakaṃ tathā

varjanīyaṃ viśeṣeṇa bhojane'pyupadaṃśakaḥ

na jṛmbhaṇaṃ ca kartavyaṃ bhojane jalpanaṃ tathā

nava * * * vaktreṇa navapiṇḍena cetasā

duṣṭānta mantrakiṃcicca tyaktyā ca kṣālayetkaram

bhuṃjīta śeṣaṃ bhuktyante vāriṇā cāmṛtātmane

pādasya dakṣiṇāṃguṣṭhaṃ siñcedaṃguṣṭhavāriṇā

bhojayīta yathā lābhaṃ * * * ṇātithīnapi

ātmā tiṣṭhati tattatra tvabhiṣiñcābhijyeṣṭhayā

badhnīyānnāsikāntaṃ ca mudraghātibhidhām

brahmahatyādipāpānāṃ nāśanaṃ tapasocchrayam

patitānāṃ gṛhe vāpi gurudrohi gṛhe'pi vā

madyapāni gṛhe vāpi bhikṣāṃ naivātra kārayet

yathā madhvādadāno'pi puṣpaṃ bhṛṃgo na bādhate

mādhūkarīṃ caredbhikṣāmekānnaṃ parivarjayet

ekānnaṃ na tu bhuñjīta bṛhaspati kulādapi

pālāśakadalīcāmra panasādalake'pi vā

alābhe'lābupatraṃ vā bhikṣāpātraṃ tathaiva ca

dhautapāda * * * * * * * ṇāvakuṇṭhanam

mumukṣoryadi bhikṣāśā babhukṣuḥ śuklavastrakam

śivaṃ guhyaṃ ca bhaktyā ca dhyāyenmaunī dṛḍhavrataḥ

ātapatrakaro yāyād bhikṣārthaṃ ca gṛhānprati

kutsanaṃ catyajet * * * * cārvādi saṃkulam

tāvatkālaṃ pratīkṣeta yāvadgovatsayorgatiḥ

gacchanpunarnivṛtto na samāhūto na tatra vai

na kareṇa na nirvācā na kāṃsyenādadītatām

kleśinī rodinītyādi hastena tu parīlihet

jagatāṃ śobhanaṃ dhyātvā svāśramaṃ ca vrajettathā

pādaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā coddhūtya sakalāṃ tanum

candrāmṛtena saṃprokṣya mudrayā dhenu mudrayā

tṛtīyaṃ bhāgamādāya svātmano bhojanāya vai

sāyantanīṃ ca saṃdhyāṃ tu pūrvavadvandayetkramāt

śārdūla carma * * rte dhāparistarakaṃ bhavet

ajapāṃ śiva saṃyuktāṃ candrārkau nāśa saṃyutau

turīyaṃ vāpi sā sandhyā jñānināṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

svāpakālamidaṃ proktaṃ paścātpūrvavadācaret

mokṣārthināṃ mokṣadaṃ ca dhanārthināṃ dhanapradam

rogiṇāṃ roganāśaṃ ca bhaktihīnasya bhaktidam

sthāvaraṃ jaṃgamaṃ caiva dvividhaṃ śivapūjanam

* * * * * tatra jaṃgamanāyanamāparam

jaṃgame sarvakālaṃ ca sānnidhyaṃ kurute śivaḥ

dīkṣāyogyastu jātīnāṃ dīkṣitasya viśeṣataḥ

vāgīśvarīgarbhadehaṃ gatvā tu śivaputrakaḥ

malatrayasya nāśatvād vijñāna kalanādapi

yasya cittaṃ śive līnaṃ tasya jātyādikaṃ smaran

jñāna vairāgya bhaktiśca dīkṣitasya ca cihnakam

proktaṃ tatrāpi yatkāryaṃ śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

vedhako ghuṭikādyābhiḥ saṃjātāścānuyettadā

hematvasiddhau sarvaṃ ca vimānatvaṃ prapadyate

bhaktānāṃ pātramukhyatvaṃ śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

vedādhyāyī tṛtīyaḥ syādagnihotrī caturthakaḥ

satrayājī tu ṣaṣṭhaḥ syād vedāntī saptamaḥ smṛtaḥ

mukhya vedānta viduṣo koṭiśreṣṭhaśca matpriyaḥ

sarvathā dīkṣitānāṃ tu sadṛśo nāsti laukike

tasmātsarvaprayatnena dīkṣitānāṃ ca bhojanam

śuddhadeśe manoramye sālaṃkārayute pare

śuddhaśaivānsamānīya guruvadbhaktisaṃyutaḥ

annapānādikaṃ sarvaṃ śuddhamānīya yatnataḥ

dine dine vā māsevā vatsare vāpi bhojayet

muktiḥ prasāda saṃjñātvāttatraiva tu na saṃśayaḥ

karoti śrīrmahatyeva nātra kāryā vicāraṇā

aṣṭāviṃśacca puṣṭyarthaṃ bhaktānāṃ pūjanaṃ param

aṣṭottaraśataṃ cāpi sarvaśāntikaraṃ bhavet

śāntiṃ gacchati tadgehaṃ vakṣyehannandikeśvara

saukhyamasmākamakhilaṃ pareṣāmanyasādhakam

gurumāheśvarāṇāṃ ca svarṇadānaṃ vidhīyate

śrīrudraiḥ pāvamānībhiḥ nityaṃ liṃgaṃ prapūjayet

candanośīrakarpūraśītalairambubhiśśivam

śāntihomaṃ tu kartavyaṃ aṣṭāviṃśati bhojanam

divase naiva śāntiḥ syād bhaktiśraddhā samanvitaḥ

tasya śītajvaraṃ caiva kuṣṭhaṃ caiva bhayaṃkaram

rogabhedācca sañjātā vyādhayastatra śāntaye

aṣṭāviṃśatibhaktānāṃ bhojanaṃ sāṃgakaṃ bhavet

upānaṃ vāhanaṃ cāpi pādaroge ca dāpayet

visrabdhasya viṣādhānamabhakṣyasya va bhakṣaṇam

phlandilambaśrayañceyaḥ kṣudbādhāṃ cāpi jāyate

aṣṭottaraṃ ca paṃcāśat bhaktānāṃ bhojanaṃ bhavet

svarṇasya niṣkasaptena dānādgacchanti śāntikam

paradārāṃśca rāgācca nirīkṣeta ca śodhanāt

mahābhiṣeka pūjāṃ ca śivaliṃgaṃ vidhāya ca

upadānaṃ ca devāya bhaktiśraddhā samanvitaḥ

yathāśakti svargadātaṃ pāyasānnaṃ ca dāpayet

annāpahāriṇo nityaṃ jīrṇatvaṃ pratipadyate

aṣṭāviṃśatibhaktānāṃ bhojanācchāntimṛcchati

kanyā saṃyogaścāpi vidhavāyāstathaiva ca

tiryājñaryādibhiścaiva śarkarā madhubhogakaiḥ

mahābhiṣekamīśasya bhaktyā kuryātsamāhitaḥ

triṃśadbhaktāṃśca saṃpūjya homayennandikeśvara

mohādbhrātrīṃ ca jananīmabhigacchennarādhamaḥ

śivapūjā ca kāryā tu aghorāstreṇa homayet

dvādaśātha śataṃ tatra bhaktānāṃ bhojanaṃ bhavet

vimukhasya gurorājñāmanyāyenānya jihmakam

karoti tasya rogāśca mukhe bhavati ca dhruvam

homena śāntimāgacchet bhaktānāṃ bhojanena ca

gurudoṣaśravādeva śubhabodhaśirastathā

karṇakuntanayuktaśced vyādhirvāyuśca jāyate

stotraiśca vividhairdevaṃ aghorāstreṇa homayet

vastradānaṃ ca teṣāṃ vai dhānyapustakadānakam

kambalādyapahārāttu karmādeśca viśeṣataḥ

hiṃsane kuṣṭharogaśca jāyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ

āḍhakena ghṛtenaiva pañcagavyādibhistathā

aṣṭāviṃśati bhaktānāṃ bhojanaṃ ca dine dine

gurutejo vināśitvaṃ paraṃ śubhavināśanam

krodhāllobhāttathāmohāt rogāṇāṃ ca cikitsitā

śvekuṣṭhādibhirbādhā jāyate tatra niṣkṛtiḥ

gāyatryā ghorāstra mantreṇa ca hunettadā

śvetakuṣṭhādibhirbādhā śāntimāyāti niścayaḥ

denanindādayāluśca puraputrādisaṃpadam

parasparāyādanyasya rahasyādyāya nāsti tat

saṃpūjya parameśānaṃ vāgīśīpūjanaṃ tathā

aṣṭottara sahasrābhyāṃ homaṃ kuryāttu śāntaye

yāvatprabodhamāyāti tāvadbhojana saṃbhavet

śivasya tena tatsarvaṃ śāntimāyāti niścayaḥ

atītabhayamete * * * * vatha karmaṇaḥ

bhasma saṃsparśahīnasya dānavighnaṃ karoti yaḥ

śivapūjāratasyāpi śivabhaktasya pūjanam

triṃśadbhaktānnadānānubhojanācchāntireva ca

guruṃ ca māṃ ca mānyatvaṃ buddhiśūnyaśca pārvati

dāridryabhojanaṃ caiva pūjāṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ

aghorāstreṇa mantreṇa śatamaṣṭottaraṃ hunet

śītajvaraṃ kukṣirogaṃ sphoṭakaṃ pādahastayoḥ

homena śāntimāyānti bhaktānāṃ bhojanādapi

āgamādīṃstathāśāstrā na śucissaṃspṛśettu yaḥ

mahāntihvākhyake nāma grāhāti prabalo grahaḥ

dīrghaniśvāsanaṃ cāpi kampaṃ pulakameva ca

pūrvavalliṃgapūjā ca tasyāghoreṇa homayet

rasabhasma tu saṃsevyaṃ śāntimāśu prayacchati

taṃ vāsavagraho mānaṃ grāhātyasya mahājvaraḥ

pūrvavatpūjayitvā tu tasyāghoreṇa homayet

gurupūjāṃ prakurvīta vastrahemādibhistathā

sāhasānāṃ prayogeṇa lambanagṛhavaiśanam

kandhūrennapānādau vairasyaṃ gativibhramam

aghorāstreṇa mantreṇa aṣṭottaraśataṃ hunet

gurunindāratasyā'pi śivanindāratasya ca

dīrghakālaṃ tu vātena pittena ca tathaiva ca

pūrvavacchivamabhyarcya aghorāstreṇa homayet

triṃśadādiṣu tacchāntyaivāsanaṃ nandikeśvara

eteṣāṃ śāntipātena bhedena sakalāmayam

sarveṣāmanyakāle tu śivāditrayanindanāt

śivāditraya kāryārthaṃ aśanaṃ kṛtvā tu śocati

śopharogaśca timiraṃ kuṣṭharogaṃ tathaiva ca

śivaliṃgaṃ ca saṃpūjya śāntihomaṃ samācaret

devatānāṃ gurūṇāṃ ca brāhmaṇānāṃ tapasvinām

śāntikāle ca dattaṃ ced karṇagrahanipīḍanā

tasya śāntyai śivaṃ pūjya nīrogaśśānti pūrvavat

* * * svāmināṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ mitraṃ tasyopakāryakam

purīṣa mūtrayorbandhaṃ chardiśca bhavati dhruvam

triṃśadbhaktajanānāṃ tu bhojanācchāntireva hi

paradārābhigamane klaṃbāśo'pi ca jāyate

rasabhasma ca saṃsevyaṃ sarvapāpaviśuddhaye

śivabhaktāṃśca triṃśacca bhojayecchāntaye tadā

daridraścenmantrajapaṃ kuryātpāpaviśuddhaye

rasabhasma viśeṣeṇa vyādhirnāśamupaiti vai

yāvaccyutistāvadeva śāntikarma samācaret

sarvasaṃsādhakāṃśe ca mānuṣānnandikeśvara

liṃgārcanaṃ tu bhaktānāṃ pūjanācchāntikarmaṇā

payaḥ pānena pittaṃ ca kṣudbādhā ca nivartate

payo dadhyājyamadhubhiraikṣavairmadhupañcabhiḥ

devadevaṃ ca saṃsthāpya savediśca viśeṣataḥ

sugandha toyaiḥ saṃsnāpya śuddhavastreṇa mārjayet

vastrādyairbhūṣaṇaissarvairbhūṣayecchivamavyayam

indravarti samāyuktā dīpamālārtikaṃ dadet

nānopadaṃśa saṃyuktaṃ nānāphala samanvitam

elālavaṃgatakkolajāti karpūra varṇayuk

praṇamyādhyeṣya labdhajñairhomārthaṃ kuṇḍamāśrayet

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ hutvā pūrṇamantraṃ samāpayet

vāṃchato karmadevāya saṃprārthya praṇametpunaḥ

dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ cāpi catuṣpañcaguṇaṃ tu vā

viśeṣapūjā caivoktā pūrvoktavi * * iti

vartamāne tu nitye tu yadā naimittikaṃ bhavet

sandhyā parārdhakāle cet pūrvārdho vidyate yadi

alpakālena saṃdhyaṃ vai aparārdhe samācaret

pāyasaṃ vātha naivedyaṃ parya * * * meva vā

naimittikamahacceddhi samārabhya svakālake

vaidaiśca kalaśe devaṃ sitedavadhite kramāt

laghuṃ te sadṛśaṃ taccet kāmācāro vidhīyate

* * nalpa vibhāgena svalpāsudayanādhikaḥ

āditye gṛhyamāne tu mucyamāne niśākare

viṣuve madhyakāle ca snapanādyaṃ samācaret

ardhamekadvayaṃ cāpi yāmaṃ vā ghaṭikāmapi

ācāryaṃ pūjayetpaścād vastrahemāṃgulīyakaiḥ

ācāryāya ca taddadyāt nānyabhogāya kalpayet

vastraṃ sthaṇḍikaṃ kumbhaṃ * * * caikaladāyikam

taddhomacarunaivedya śeṣaṃ yanmaṇḍapasya ca

ācāryāya ca taddeyaṃ sanmārgakulanāyakam

niṣkāṣṭaka hānyasyamaṣṭamārdhaṃ tadardhakam

nitye tu kalpayedbhaktiṃ tamanaskoṣaṇī tathā

āyuṣyakaramaste tu ravivāre dvitīyake

saptamyāṃ ca kartavyaṃ vidhinā nandikeśvara

janmatrayavipadṛkṣe pratyare ṣaṭtathāpi vā

śatrunāśāya tadṛkṣe ṣoḍaśī guṇavardhanam

śivānandaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ vratānāmuttamottamam

na bādhā yamarājasya kiṃkarairapi vardhate

māghamāse caturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣataḥ

pūrve'hni kṛtasusnātaḥ kṛtakṛtya vidhiśśuciḥ

śivānantaṃ ca saṃjapya kartavyaṃ nandikeśvara

khaṇḍūṃ śamāsanaṃ tatra saṃnidhīkṛtya saṃyutaḥ

gandha le tu cā savyaṃ piṣṭenodvartayetpunaḥ

pavitraiḥ pañcabhiḥ kuryāpañcāṃgaistu krameṇa tu

pūrṇena cāpi piṣṭena rajasā sthūpitena ca

nālikerodakenaiva tatpaścādabhiṣecayet

śuddhena vāsasā cāpi saṃmārjyātha vidhānavit

candanāgarukarpūra kuṃkumādyaiśca bhaktitaḥ

dūrvākṣata tilairvāpi saṃyuktyā samyagarcayet

sa ghṛtenendra saṃyuktaṃ vartinājyakasaṃyutā

pūrve tu pāyasaṃ yāme dvitīye kṛsaraṃ bhavet

aśeṣairvyañjanairyuktaṃ mahāhavirnivedya ca

tāmbūla mukhavāsāni dāpayennandikeśvara

śivāgnau homaḥ kartavyaḥ kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍilepi vā

saṃpūjya tatra devāya juhuyājjvalitānale

śivāddaśāṃśatonyeṣāṃ mantrāṇāmāhutirbhavet

pūrṇāṃ kṛtvā śivenaiva niśchidra paripūraṇā

pūjayetpunareveśaṃ yāmaḥ prati punaḥ punaḥ

dakṣiṇāṃ ca tato dadyādvittaśāṭhyaṃ vivarjayet

pūjayedbhaktitaḥ śaktyā pūjāṃ samyak samāpya ca

saṃsmṛtyātha bhavetpūrṇaḥ devīṃ cāpi ramāṃ samām

tatpāpamakhilaṃ cāpi śivajñāna pradāyakam

evaṃ smṛtvā tu yatmāpaṃ kṛtaṃ tadakhilaṃ punaḥ

śivarātrisamaṃ puṇyaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati

kapilājyaṃ navaprasthaṃ kṛmikīṭādivarjitam

astreṇa nikṣipettoyaṃ vāruṇaṃ bījamuccaran

pañcāmṛtaiḥ pañcagavyaiḥ viśeṣasnapanaṃ bhavet

kṛtvā viśeṣapūjāṃ ca sthāpayecchivamavyayam

ghṛte saṃpātahomaṃ ca pavitrāropavartmanā

kavacenāvakuṇṭhyātha prātarmadhyāhnameva ca

viśeṣeṇārcayitvā tu sarvālaṃkāra saṃyutam

liṃgaṃ sarvatra cālipya pīṭhāntaṃ sarvapīṭhakam

dadyāddevaṃ bahiśśambhoḥ tāmbūlena samanvitam

pūrvavatpūjayed devaṃ guruṃ saṃpūjayettadā

ayugaṃ caramaṃ pūrve kartavyaṃ vā navācaret

sarvalokahitaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam

janmāntarakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādgalati dhruvam

tatkleśaṃ tu śivopyātha bhavetpāpaughanāśanam

tasmāddviguṇamutkarṣaṃ gurudarśanato bhavet

ādareṇa tu śuśrūṣā mokṣāya bhavati dhruvam

devasya cotsave kāle yaḥ kuryāttu pariśramaḥ

dhvajārohaṇakāle tu yathā kurvanpradakṣiṇam

dhvajārohaṇakāle tu vṛṣabhasya nivedyakam

kevalaṃ devāyatane prādakṣiṇya pravartanam

nityotsavasya kāle yaḥ pradakṣiṇamathācaret

namaskāraṃ yathā kṛtvā tathā loke supūjitaḥ

aṣṭamāṅgaṃ krameṇaiva tattatphalamavāpnuyāt

śirasā dakṣahastena dvayāṃgaṃ ca bhavedidam

śirasā pāṇipādaistu pañcāṃgaṃ daṇḍavadbhavet

karadvayena saṃyuktamaṣṭāṃgamiti kīrtitam

sālokyaṃ caiva sāmīpyaṃ sārūpyaṃ sadṛśaṃ tathā

sthitvā devālayasyāgre pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ svayam

yatra jñānakriyāyogī dinārghaṃ divasaṃ tathā

tattīrthaṃ tapaso yajñantanmantraṃ ca pavitrakam

yatra yatkriyate pāpaṃ nāśamāyāti niścayaḥ

divyagaṃgeti tāṃ vidyāt tatra snāyāttu yo naraḥ

deśikāṃghrija toyena prokṣayennandikeśvara

ācāryocchiṣṭakaṃ yastu gurorādeśatācchrutaḥ

mahāpātaka śāntistu nātra kāryā vicāraṇā

hemādi svarṇadānaṃ tu dhenūnāṃ koṭidānakam

liṃgasaṃsparśanādbhaktyā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate

tadvatsparśanatonmukto nātra kāryā vicāraṇā

śivabhaktyā ca yatsopi śivaloke mahīyate

yatkṛtaṃ śivaṃ saṃsmṛtya mokṣadvārā ca vai bhavet

ihaiva dhanavāṃcchrīmān so'nte rudrapadaṃ labhet

muktimāpnoti dāridryācchivaloke mahīyate

bhikṣāmātra pradānena tattulyaṃ śivayogine

gosaṃjñātīrthake kṣīrasnāpanena tu tatsamam

kāñcanena vimānena rudraloke mahīyate

yathā śaktyabhiṣekaṃ tu kṛta vāṃcchaṃkarasya vai

vidhyuktasnapanaṃ kuryād yo naro bhaktisaṃyutaḥ

nadyantasthaṃ jalaṃ śītaṃ gandhapuṣpavimiśritam

pūrvapakṣaṃ tu yaddravyaṃ bhojyaṃ dadyācchivāya vai

liṃge vā vṛtamāyā vā maṇḍale bhitticitrake

vidhināptajayelliṃgaṃ kāle kāle ca tat priyam

kṣaṇikātphalakā śreṣṭhā phalakāyāḥ paṭastathā

tadbimbālliṃgamutkṛṣṭaṃ sphaṭikaṃ bāṇajaṃ varam

sarvadānaphalaṃ caiva sarvayajñaphalaṃ bhavet

bandhuhā bīrahā vāpi mātṛhā pitṛhāpi vā

mitrahā vā mahāpāpayukto vā paradārikaḥ

havirdatvā tu vidhinā sakṛtkāle śivālaye

ihaiva dhanavāṃcchrīmān so'nte mokṣamavāpnuyāt

jñānayogakriyādhyānāddānaṃ dāridrya nāśanam

śaṃkhacāmaravādyāni darpaṇacchatravāsasām

paścādrudratvamāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā

yaśaḥ śrīḥ khyātyadāridryaṃ sidhyatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

yo dadyāddevadevāya tatsāmīpyamavāpnuyāt

devāya kṛtavānsadyaḥ sārūpyapadamāpnuyāt

kīrtimānpuṣṭimānloke cānte devaṃ samāpnuyāt

sarvadānaphalaṃ tattu sarvapāpapraṇāśanam

śivāya vṛkṣadānena sarvabhogapravardhanam

trisaptakulamuddhṛtya śivaloke mahīyate

bhaktyā ca labhate devaṃ punarjanma na yacchati

kurvanbahudhanāḍhyastu tadante śivamāpnuyāt

sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt

tasyaiva phalasaṃpattiṃ varṇituṃ naiva śakyate

svābhāvikī tu yā bhaktiḥ ihā mutra sukhapradā

liṃgāddhastaśataṃ sārdhaṃ śivakṣetramiti smṛtam

sa yāti śivasāmīpyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā

śivaṃ śāstraṃ viśeṣeṇa bhogamokṣau klamāllabham

tasmātsarvaprayatnena cintyasādākhyamabhyaset

liṃgādi puṣkaraṇyādi sthāpitānyapi kenacit

jīrṇoddhāre śataguṇaṃ pāpanāśātsamaṃ bhavet

sthāpitā pūjitā bhaktyā yāvattejo mahītalam

vastraṃ vā vṛṣabhaṃ vāpi turagaṃ vā vṛṣāhvayam

kaḥ pumāna tithitvena labdhvā naiṣṭhikamutsṛjet

ācāryeṇa tu bhuktena bhojitā koṭirucyate

putro vāpyayutaṃ puṇyaṃ samayaste samagraham

cārantaṇḍulīpuṣpaṃ ca dīpaṃ vastraphalādikam

romataṇḍulapuṣpāṇi dīpatantu phalāni vai

tāvadyuga sahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate

patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ yāvat tadudyānaṃ bhavettadā

śivabhakto jaṭī bhūyājjaṭāṃ prati dine dine

muṇḍī vāpi śikhīvāpi śivāyeti bhavedyadi

śivabhaktasya pūjāyai yoliṃgaṃ dāpayedyadi

tāvatkalpāyutaṃ divyaṃ śivaloke mahīyate

saṃmārjanaṃ caikadinaṃ cāndrāyaṇaphalaṃ bhavet

śatakoṭiyugaṃ divyaṃ rudraloke mahīyate

koṭyāditya samo bhūtvā śivaloke vasetsadā

kalpakoṭi sahasropadurjanaṃ vāsasañcayam

liṃgasnānena madhunā sarvapāpaṃ vyapohati

evamikṣu rasenaivavīro dharāpatirbhavet

yo liṃgaṃ phalasāreṇa snāpayet pāpanāśanam

sitena bhasmanā liṃgaṃ snāpanaṃ śivavatsadā

śivāya gurave caiva tuṭimātra pramāṇakam

devadāruṃ na meruṃ ca sajjaśrīvāsakundaram

vitsahasraguṇādhikyaṃ dhūpayedgandhasaṃyutaḥ

dviguṇaṃ mahiṣākṣābhaṃ śivatulyaphalapradam

sugandhi candanālepaṃ sakṛtkṛtvā tu liṃgakam

candanāgarutaḥ śreṣṭhaṃ tataḥ śreṣṭhaṃ kamuṣṇakam

anyāścaturguṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ dhūpaṃ gulgulumiśritam

tāmbūlaṃ tailakarpūra viphalāsaṃyutaṃ bhavet

ihaiva dhanavāṃcchrīmān paratra ca parāṃ gatim

śaṃkhatālakakāṃsyādikāhalaṃ veṇunandanam

śivatulyabalopeto vasetkalpāyutaṃ sukhī

yaḥ kuryātsarvalokeṣu śivavanmodate ciram

tacchrotāraśca vṛkṣāśca kṛmikīṭādayastathā

nṛttaṃ śivāya yaḥ kaścitkurute tatphalaṃ śṛṇu

nṛttagītādi saṃyuktaḥ śivaloke mahīyate

tatsarvaṃ śāstradānena nāthamāyādyaniścayam

jñānayogakriyācaryāḥ sarve bhaktipradhānakāḥ

madbhaktajanavātsalyaṃ pūjāyāṃ cānumodanam

matkthāśravaṇe bhaktiḥ svaranetrāṃgavikriyā

bhaktiraṣṭavidhā hyeṣā yasminmlecche'pi vartate

na me priyaścaturvedīmadbhaktaḥ śvapaco'pi yaḥ

āyāntaṃ śivabhaktaṃ vai dṛṣṭvā dadyādvimānakam

śiva * * * * * * dāsanaṃ śraddhayānvitaḥ

gaṃgāsnānasamaṃ puṇyaṃ dṛṣṭamātre na saṃśayaḥ

yaśovatīṃ samāśritya modate yugapañcakam

śivabhaktāya yo dadyān mukhavāsaṃ ca bhaktitaḥ

samyagācamanaṃ kṛtvā paścādarghya pradānakam

śivabhaktasya dīpasya pradānaṃ jñānamuttamam

vāyunā pūjyamāno'sau vāyulokaṃ sa gacchati

ūrṇaṃ prāvaraṇaṃ dadyāt śivabhaktāya śaktimān

tattadvyāghrādi carmāṇi tatvādiphalamāpnuyāt

kalpakoṭi sahasrāṇi rudraloke mahīyate

śivalokādi lokeṣu śivavanmodate ciram

athavā jñānayogādīn labdhvā mokṣamavāpnuyāt

samastāḥ pitaraste'pi śivaloke mahīyate

śivabhaktasya dānena vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnuyāt

varṣakoṭiyutaṃ divyaṃ viṣṇunā pūjito bhavet

kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi bhavetsūryeṇa pūjitaḥ

śivalokaṃ samāśritya na punargacchatīha saḥ

yugakoṭiśataṃ divyaṃ śivaloke mahīyate

sarvatīrthaphalaṃ labdhvā śivaloke mahīyate

vastrayugma sahasrasya dattasya phalamāpnuyāt

yo dadyātsakalaṃ dattaṃ śivavanmodate ciram

koṭikoṭiyutaṃ puṇyaṃ prāpnotyeva na saṃśayaḥ

rudrākṣa mālādānena śivabhaktāya yo naraḥ

śivabhaktāya yo dadyācchatra daṇḍasamanvitam

upānah śivabhaktāya dānaṃ kuryāttu yo naraḥ

upadeśaṃ vinā yasmācchiva dharmaṃ na cārayet

kṛtānupāvṛtaṃ caiva pañca tulyaphalāḥ smṛtāḥ

ālayādīni sarvāṇi rakṣakṣerṇa vihīyate

rakṣako hi jagatkartā rakṣako hi jagadguruḥ

samayī dāsamārgaśca śivabhaktiparāyaṇaḥ

śivārcakaḥ putramārgī putrasaṃskāradīkṣitaḥ

sādhakairabhiṣikta iti tasya paryāyavācakam

sanmārgītyeva nāmāni deśikasyaiva tatvataḥ

samaye yukta saṃskāraṃ sahasā sādhakottamaḥ

sa eva ca śiro bhūtvā sahasā ca viśiṣyate

ācāryaḥ prathamaḥ proktaḥ pañcāśantu sahasrakī

āgamānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ sārātsārākhyatantrakam

kevalaṃ bhaktimārge ca dānaṃ proktamihāgame

dānadharmādi pūjāsu vai vaiśiṣyamiṣyate

vaidikaṃ śaivamityevaṃ vrataṃ hi dvividhaṃ bhavet

śivadīkṣā pradānaṃ tu śaivamatyantamuttamam

viprādi daśavarṇānāṃ yogitvamupajāyate

caturāśramamevaṃ syād gocarāṇāṃ tu pañcakam

ājanma brahmacaryaṃ tu yāvadāmaraṇāntikam

yajñopavītī daṇḍī ca bhasmoddhūlita vigrahaḥ

trikālamekakālaṃ vā snānayukto jitendriyaḥ

guruśuśrūṣaṇāyukto gurvājñā pālane rataḥ

tāmbūlamañjanaṃ caiva haridrāṃ lavaṇaṃ tathā

guroḥ sthāne vasennityamanyagehe na ca svapet

puṇyakṣetraṃ ca tīrthaṃ ca dānaṃ karmādikaṃ tathā

gurvājñayā vihīnaṃ tu tapodānādi niṣphalam

kālaṃ saṃkalpya vidhinā pūrvavadbrahmacaryakam

gṛhasthācaraṇaṃ kuryād dvividhaṃ brahmacaryakam

vivāhaṃ vidhinā kṛtvā gṛhiṇyā saha ve yuk

atithyābhyāgatānāṃ tu yathāśaktiṃ ca pūjayet

santānavṛddhissarvaṃ ca rakṣasāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

dānaṃ pratigrahaścaivaṃ ṣaṭkarma hyāgamoditam

gṛhasthasya tu kartavyaṃ naiṣṭhikānāṃ tu naiṣṭhikaḥ

ātureṇa yute vāpi dīkṣitasya viśeṣataḥ

alaṃkṛtāṃga yaṣṭiḥ syādgandhairmālyādibhistathā

ārthavān ṣoḍaśādīnaṃ santānārthāya maithunam

vānaprasthāśramasthānāṃ ācāraṃ śṛṇu sāṃpratam

vane saṃvāsino nityaṃ śākamūlaphalāśinaḥ

vrataṃ bhāryāviyuktānāṃ " sṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

bhrātṝnsakhīṃśca janakaṃ jananīṃ ca tathaiva ca

agniṣṭomādikānyajñāntretāṃ caiva tathaiva ca

ḍambhagandhoktikaṃ caivamīṣaṇātrayameva ca

aṇimādyaṣṭakaṃ sarvaṃ varjayennandikeśvara

trikālasnānasaṃyuktopyekakālena vā yutaḥ

kaupīnadaṇḍasaṃyukto liṃgārcanaparāyaṇaḥ

śivānubhūti saṃyuktaḥ sarvakleśa vivarjitaḥ

sarvātmamaitrabhāvaśca sadā santuṣṭi saṃyutaḥ

abhedyo mattavānnityaṃ śivabhāvyaśca bhedikam

ceṣṭate tatvadevāya paraduḥkhena duḥkhitaḥ

mūlamantraṃ japennityaṃ śivadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ

dhyānoparati saṃyukto paścānmūlaṃ japetsadā

bhikṣārthaṃ grāma saṃveśaṃ vṛkṣamūle maṭhe'pi vā

sandhyā vandakaścāpi yatīnāṃ jñāna sandhyayā

vidvāniti tridhā proktāsteṣāṃ bhedaṃ śṛṇu kramāt

nādhyetavyaṃ na sāṃkalpaṃ na śrotavyaṃ kathañcana

na vadetsaṃskṛtāṃ bhāṣāṃ na daṇḍī cakramaṇḍalaḥ

priyāpriyaṃ ca saṃkalpya samaloṣṭhāśmakāñcanaḥ

janādbhīti samāyukto vasedbāhūpadhānakaḥ

pāṇipātre varetpārśve yācitaṃ tena jīvitam

viduṣoryadyateścāpi vartanaṃ nandikeśvara

evaṃ catuṣṭayaṃ tasya viduṣaśca viśeṣataḥ

sadācāryābhiṣekeṇa yukto vidvatsamākhyakaḥ

caturāśramiṇāṃ madhye vaiśeṣika vrataṃ śṛṇu

tathā saṃkhyena saṃkhyāsyurvra?ktiścāpi śṛṇu kramāt

samaye mo * * * * svādhyāyassatyavāditā

śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ caiva suduṣānāṃ sadā tathā

nipati * nkaṇānbhūmau bhakṣaṇārthamupetavat

abhyāsātsatataṃ granthamāmamādi mahāyaśān

nadītīre'pi velāyāṃ kuṭīṃ kṛtvā nivāsanam

ete pañca vratāḥ proktāḥ pretavetāla * * *

tapotsāhasamāyukto vetāla pratimānayuk

vānaprasthāśramī bhūmau * * * * * * * *

svayamutpanna * * śca haṃsakāste amī matāḥ

yogācāra * * * * karaṇendriya nirjitaḥ

śivajñānagataḥ paścād gūḍhācāraśca saṃvṛtaḥ

gopālā * * * paścād gonivāse nivāsanaḥ

vāyavyatīrthaṃ saṃyuktaḥ pañcendriya vinirmitaḥ

nityaṃ cāndrāyaṇe naiva sarvapāpaṃ vinaśyati

kuṭikānāṃ vrataṃ hyetad jñānayoga prakāśanam

bhikṣānnabhojanaṃ nityaṃ maṭhakākhyavrataṃ bhavet

dehaṃ ca tena saṃrakṣya ghuṭikākhyo vratī bhavet

śivadhyāna samāyukto daṇḍataṇḍaṃ ca saṃgraham

* * * * * * vāsī nityaṃ triṣavaṇistathā

sadyocittadhanairyuktaḥ kuṭicārādviśeṣataḥ

* * * * tathā śrāvyaṃ śuddhyate yāśraye tathā

haṃsavratāni sāvyuktāstasmātparama haṃsakam

yatīnāṃ parahaṃsānāṃ * * * * * hetukam

yāvajjīvaṃ vratī bhūtvā kālasaṃkalpasaṃyutaḥ

tadvrateśvarayāgaṃ tu śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā maṇḍalaṃ caturaśrakam

aindrāṃśaṃ vai padmakaṃ syāddakṣiṇaṃ cāñjanātmakam

īśānādi krameṇaiva madhya padmādipañcake

tattanmantraṃ śataṃ japtvā bhaktiyukto vrateśvaram

pañcabhiḥ kalaśaiścaiva pañcabrahmādi sādhitaiḥ

vrateśvaraṃ namaskṛtvācchrutyātaṃ śrāvayedvratam

mañjanaṃ mṛdbhavaṃ caiva gomayodbhūtaliṃgakam

saṃpūjya bhaktisaṃyukto vratastutyantakāraṇāt

kapanaṃ parcayitvā tu anyatsarvaṃ samaṃ bhavet

viśeṣaḥ kathyate yantri śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

caturāśramavṛttistu pūrvamevoditaṃ kramāt

tasmāttadvratamuktistu nāsti kaivalya kāraṇam

vānaprasthāśramaṃ vāpi gṛhasthānāṃ tato'dhikam

eteṣāmapi vinyāsaṃ yogyaṃ mukhyaṃ vidhīyate

pūrvokta pañcapadmeṣu sadyojātādi pūjayet

vratāṃgapañcakaṃ hyetat pratyekaṃ tu śataṃ hunet

upoṣitaśiroraṃgāmupanīyāṃgakāni vai

saṃmardya vidhinā caiva jalacāhetpravāhayet

anyakāmānurūpaṃ ca kulīnāṃ pariṇeṣyabhiḥ

vānaprastho gṛhastho vā brahmacaryayuto'pi vā

saṃśrayedyati vṛttaṃ tu taṃ vakṣye nandikeśvara

pādādi mūrdhaparyantaṃ divyadṛṣṭyā nirīkṣayet

jaṭinaṃ śikhinaṃ caiva muṇḍinaṃ vā vidhāya tam

mātaraṃ pitaraṃ putraṃ kalatramupavītakam

etanmantraṃ paṭhetpañcavāraṃ sadgurusannidhau

vaiṇavaṃ daṇḍamastreṇa bhūtikośaṃ śivena ca

etairmantraiśca saṃsādhya śiṣyāya pradadedguruḥ

yāvatkālācchiva jñānaṃ draḍhībhavati tatra vai

evaṃ vadettu taṃ śiṣyaṃ deśikaḥ kṛpayā yutaḥ

bodhyātha vānyaliṃgasthāstattatsamayakalpitān

śodhyodayaśca nībaddhistatvaikagurumauliṣu

māyātatve pāśupate vidyātatve mahāvratāḥ

pañcarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā sopavāsaparāyaṇāḥ

śivaṃ saṃpūjya vidhinā bhakti yuktassadā guruḥ

buddhitatvādi vṛkṣyantaṃ homayettatpṛthak pṛthak

bāhyasthitānāmeteṣāṃ nāḍīyojana saṃyutā

svātmanyeva sthitaṃ kṛtvā mūlena svāhayā hunet

astreṇāṣṭa sahasrāṇi liṃginaṃ liṃginaṃ prati

uddhṛtya buddhitatvāni vṛttāntaṃ taṃ niyojayet

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā mūlamantreṇa deśikaḥ

athavā rāgakāryostu ghṛtamekaṃ kramaṃ śṛṇu

dalāṣṭakasamopetaṃ padmaṃ saṃlikhya deśikaḥ

kavargaṃ pūrvottare caiva ca vargaṃ vāyudigdale

cavargaṃ dakṣiṇenaiva kavargaṃ vahnidigdale

pañcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgaiśca svaraiḥ ṣoḍaśakairapi

varṇavargāṣṭakānāṃ tu tarpaṇāya yajetpunaḥ

sanmārganāyakaḥ kuryāt pūrvavannandikeśvara

agnerākṛṣya * * * * * * * kramādguruḥ

paścādastrayutaṃ datvā dīkṣākarma samācaret

tattadīkṣānusāreṇa dīkṣayettaṃ viśeṣataḥ

sthaṇḍilaṃ vidhinā kṛtvā catuṣṣaṣṭi padānvitam

madhye catuṣpadenāpi brahmāṇaṃ tu samarcayet

samidājyena caruṇā vāstudevānsvamantrakaiḥ

śaṃkaraścaiva parjanyo jayantaśca mahendrakaḥ

vahniḥ pūṣā ca vitatho grahakṣatayamau tathā

dauvārikaśca sāvaḥ puṣpadantastathaiva ca

marīcissavitā tvai mitraścaiva dharādharaḥ

skandaścaivāryamā caiva jaṃbhagaḥ phalapiṃchakaḥ

paryagnikaraṇaṃ kṛtvā ṣaṭsadarbhaiḥ samāhitaḥ

vāstuhomaṃ ca kartavyaṃ pratiṣṭhādau viśeṣataḥ

śivamantrairyathā yogaṃ pīṭhaśaktiṃ śivaṃ tathā

tadyogaṃ ca pratiṣṭhādyāḥ pañcabhedā mayoditāḥ

viṣṭhare ca yathāyogaṃ nirneśāsthāpanaṃ bhavet

uttāpanāsyaliṃgasya proddhāragamanodyatāḥ

āsthāpanākhya caivaṃ tu pañcadhā tu śivaṃ śritā

pañcadhā tatra vihitā yathā yogaṃ kramādbhavet

śuklā raktā ca pītā ca kṛṣṇāpyevaṃ caturvidhā

sudhāgandhā yathā saṃkhyā brāhmaṇādyā vasundharā

uktavarṇamṛdo'lābhe varṇāvādhāramucyate

apadanta mṛdā caiva pūrayedadhikā bhavet

bhūmiṃ bhuvi sthalīṃ kṛtvā śodhayetkuṃkumaistathā

pūrvakumbhe tathaiśānaṃ * * madhye śivaṃ yajet

rakṣo grahādbahiḥ pūjyā * * * vidhinā kṣipet

kṣaudrasiktenakhardāla mukhena * * * nayet

susnāpya tāṃ bhuvaḥ paścātkuddālādyaṃ prapūjayet

vidhāya pañcaghoṣāni svastivācanakairapi

purasyathākta tatraiva kṣaṇaṃ sthitvā prasannadhīḥ

īśānaṃ tantuku * * * drukumbha paribhramāt

evaṃ kṛte tu bhūme'stu parigrahamudīritam

khanitvā'pyatha rājñātvā śa * * nvidhinoddharet

gurūbalivayatrasthastatra vai homaśalyakam

mandaścedāyasaṃ śalyaṃ * * * * * vettadā

tatkāla praśrajānāṃ tu akṣarāṇi bhavanti hi

ekāraśca tathokāraṃ pañcākāramidaṃ bhavet

pṛthvījalaṃ vahnirmarut vyoma caiva yathākramam

sūre ca gurukāvye ca bhūsute budha eva ca

evaṃ vinītvā tairevaṃ śalyādikamudāharet

dvipañca vāyasīścāpi pūrvādiṣu yathā kramam

tatpārśvasthānaśalyānāṃ jñānantacchalyacakrataḥ

śalyamuktaṃ tu sthānepi śīghramandatvakāraṇāt

āpūrya pādonakhātatvaṃ jarairmudgārakāhataiḥ

vakṣyamāṇaṃ maṇḍapaṃca yāvatsāmānyakākhyakaḥ

śilāśuddhyādikaṃ kuryātkuṇḍamaṇḍalaśuddhidam

garbhādhānādikaṃ sarvaṃ vahneścāpi tu pūrvavat

śilāprāsādaliṃgasya pādārdhādi saṃjñakāḥ

pāṣāṇānāṃ śilāṃ kuryāttadardhaśceṣṭakātha vā

aṃgināsanavajrādyaiḥ pañca vā pañcakāṃkitaiḥ

āsāṃvato mahāpadmaḥ śaṃkho makarakastathā

nandā bhadrā jayā pūrṇā ajitā cāparājitā

subhadraśca vibhadraśca sundaraṃ puṣpanandanaḥ

nidhikramā amī proktā navānāṃ tu yathākramam

kavace nādya sarvāsāṃ avakuṇṭhanamucyate

huṃphaḍantena cāstreṇa malasnānaṃ vidhīyate

paścāttu gandhatoyena svanāmāṃkita gandhataḥ

lepayeccandanenaiva astreṇacchādayecchilāḥ

hṛdayena tu śayyāyāṃ kuśatalpe niveśayet

trikhaṇḍavyāpakaṃ tatra padmavidyāśivāntakam

buddhyādi cittaparyantaṃ cittaṃ tanmantrakāṃ vidhau

svamantreṇa ca tatvāni tatveśāṃśca hṛdārcayet

tanmātrāṇi yathā saṃkhyaṃ śṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

oṃ hāṃ śivatatvādhipataye rudrāya nama ityapi

oṃ hrīṃ vidyā tatvādhipataye viṣṇave nama ityapi

oṃ hāṃ ātmatatvādhipataye brahmaṇe nama ityapi

pṛthvyagni yajamānārka jalavāyvindukhāni vai

śarvaṃ paśupatiṃ cograṃ rudraṃ bhavaṃ maheśvaram

oṃ hāṃ rathādhipataye śarvāya namaḥ * * *

lokapālā * * * nta svasvamantrairyathākramam

indrādilokapālānāṃ bījānyapi yathā kramam

namaśśivapratiṣṭhāsu cottaraṃ tatkrametpunaḥ

tanmūrti sūkṣmāḥ pañcaiva dharādyāṃścāpi pūjayet

ete vai pañcamūrtyaṃśānyastavyāstāsu pūrvavat

evaṃ mantraṃ samuccārya saṃpūjya kalaśānapi

vidhānena nirundhīta nyāso madhyaśilāḥ kramāt

ādhāraśakti kuṃbhe ca saṃpūjyantarpayetpunaḥ

kuśaiśca pūjayet paścāt pratitatvaṃ kramādguruḥ

evaṃ bhāgatraye karma gatvā gatvā samācaret

tatvādīnāṃ tu saṃnyāsaṃ hrasvadīrghaprayogataḥ

oṃ kṣṝṃ saṃ ūṃ śivatatva vidyātatvābhyāṃ namaḥ

pañcagavyādibhissiktān lokapālādhidaivatān

śilānāmapi sarvāsāṃ saṃsmaredadhidevatāḥ

sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktāḥ śilānāmadhidevatāḥ

hetinā pūrṇahomāntamāhutīnāṃ śataṃ śatam

catuṣṣaṣṭipade kṣetre padārthaistu same kṛte

dvipadāṣṣaṣṭi pādāstau tattadyārhyarcayettadā

smaredguhādi pūjāsuniśśeṣadhva nāśanam

aiśāne ca śirodeśe rākṣase padayoryutam

śukraṃ krodhātsusaṃpannaṃ svayaṃ siṃhmantumakṣamaḥ

tasmādastraṃ sthitā devāḥ pūjitāśca śubhā amī

yāmyādyādistu madhyotyā dikṣu caindrādiṣu kramāt

samastanāḍi saṃpāto mahādharmāmbujaṃ halam

trikaṭokarmanirbandhaṃ stuviśuddhapadaṃ tathā

īśāya taṇḍulaṃ sarpiḥ parjanyāya ca vāriyuk

* * * ja mahendrāyaravau dhūmraṃ vināyakaiḥ

antarikṣāya māṃsānnaṃ kūrceṣvaṣṭasu pūrvataḥ

pūrṇe jālaṃ hema * ni vitathāya nivedayet

gandhaṃ gandharvarājāya jihvāṃ bhraṃśāya pakṣiṇā

pitryaṃ nīlodanaṃ kṣīraṃ vṛkṣajaṃ dantadhāvanam

darbhāśca puṣpadantāya rudhiraṃ ca pracetase

yoṣerapyājyamannaṃ syādyāgāya ghṛtamaṇḍakāḥ

pītadhvajamamūtova rāgāya nāgakesaram

somāya pāyasaṃ sājyaṃ śālyaṃgaṃ mudgave diśet

brahmāṇaṃ ca purobhāge modakākṣipayetphalam

vahnyandhakoṇakoṣṭhepi savitre ratnapuṣpakam

tadadhaḥ koṇakoṣṭhe'pi sāvitrāśca kuśodakam

rakṣodhaḥ koṇakoṣṭhepi caindrāyānnaṃ haridrayā

vāruṇā * padāhīnamitreśa gulamodanam

tadadho rudradāsāya māṃsamārdramathottare

āpāya śiva karṇāya tadvaṃśāyai * * ttale

catuṣpādavinaṣṭāya brahmaṇe madhyadeśikaḥ

īśādi vāyuparyantaṃ koṇeśvapi yathākramam

ghṛtaṃ māṃsaṃ ca raktaistu dadhi padmaṃ vidhānake

asthinīpāparākṣasyoḥ rakta raktaphalāni ca

aryamṇo dakṣiṇeśāya apūpānkṛsarairyutān

pratīcyāṃ balipiṃchāya raktānnaṃ kusumāni ca

kuḍyādiṣvapi varteta vāstvekāśītikoṣṭhake

īśānyā pādikātasminnāpādyāścarcakoṣṭhakāḥ

īśānādiṣu raktādyāḥ pūrvavatpūjayedbahiḥ

bhūtakrūrabaliṃ datvā sulagne dyāsamārabhet

pṛthvībījena mūlena hrīṃ me ca dharaṇī śilām

lokapālāṇubhirnyasya svabheṣu nyastaśaktiṣu

śambarairmuktināthānāṃ yathā dikṣu ca madhyataḥ

pūrvāsu cājitadvāśca karyādiṣvathavā nyaset

adhvānaṃ cintayetpaścādyoga prāsādamavyayam

śilāpañcaka pakṣe'pi maunamuddiśyate yathā

padmādiṣu ca nandādyāḥ koṇeṣvagnyādiṣu kramāt

ūrṇāṃ tatvaṃ mahāvidye sarvasandohalakṣaṇam

oṃ nandinandini puṃsitvānmantrāśca sthāpayāmyaham

āyuṣkāmaṃśriyaṃ dadyād dehavāsiṣu dehinām

oṃ bhadre * * * * * lokānāṃ kurukāśyapi

oṃ bīje'tra sadā devi tiṣṭhatvaṃ sthāpitā mayā

oṃ rikte riktā doṣaghne siddhimuktiprade śive

vyoma cāyatanaṃ dhyātvā tatra tatvatrayaṃ nyaset

śilānyāsya vidhistvevaṃ proktasvatraṃ samāsataḥ

atha vakṣye viśeṣeṇa liṃgasthāpanakarmaṇi

tatsadā kriyate muktyai muktyai deva dine bhavet

karaṇādi kriye caiva jīvaśukrodaye tathā

riktāṃ ca navamīṃ ṣaṣṭhīṃ varjayitvā caturdaśīm

ārdrāśatabhiṣak caiva dhaniṣṭhā cottaratrayam

pūrvāṣāḍhāśca bhānyāhurdvijasya ca viśeṣataḥ

kṣatriyasya pratiṣṭhāyāṃ vihitāni ca tāni vai

makhā cāpi viśeṣeṇa vihitastārakākramaḥ

hastaṃ dvādaśa sopānāt kuryānmaṇḍalamagrataḥ

vedāsya * * * ndraṃ syādraudryāṃ prācyuttarepi vā

hāstikaṃ liṃga * * * maṣṭahastaṃ tu maṇḍapam

caturthaṃ liṃgamānasya dvau dvau hastena vardhanam

daśahastārkahaste vā hāstike liṃgamānake

vṛddhipādādiliṃgeṣu kuryān mānānusārataḥ

maṇḍapaṃ karamāyāmaṃ vidadhyānnandikeśvara

prathamaṃ ghanamaṃ syādhyā dvitīyaṃ ghoṣasaṃjñitam

rāmakāryastato nandin maṇḍapāḥ pañcato bhavet

aṣṭamaṃ ghargharākhyāsau dadakṣona ca maṇḍapaḥ

vistīrṇe tu yathāśobhaṃ bhāvayanyanyakā api

vedīnāṃ munibhiścāpi sāṣṭāṃgururudīritā

āsāṃ krameṇa yāvanto vardhante ravisaṃkhyayā

ekāśītipadānāṃ tu madhye'pi navake tathā

pādādikaṃ cikaṃ dadyāt tatpārśve kuṇḍamucyate

aṣṭadviśvaṣṭa kuṇḍāni navamaṃ prāgudagbhavet

athavāpyeka kuṇḍaṃ tu raudryāṃ prācyāmathāpi vā

liṃge tu hāstike kuṇḍaṃ hāstike ca bhavet tadā

navahaste liṃgake tu tryaṃśotīta karadvayam

muṣṭimātre tu kuṇḍe tu ratnimātre tathaiva ca

hastakumbhe sahasraṃ syāddvihastaṃ cāyutaṃ bhavet

aṣṭhahaste trilakṣaṃ syāt tata ūrdhvaṃ na kārayet

prācyāṃ plakṣaṃ tu yāmye tu udumbaramudīritam

śāntināma ca pūrve tu dakṣiṇe bhūti saṃjñakam

caturṇāmapi rakṣāṇāmalābhe'pyevameva vā

pañcaṣaṭsaptahastāni toraṇāni kanīyasām

caturaṃguladvayaṃ vṛddhyā vardhayennandikeśvara !

yavantavakṣare liṃgaṃ tadaṣṭārdhakaraṃ bhavet

madhyamānāṃ trayāṇāṃ ca caturaṃgulavṛddhitaḥ

aṃguladvayavṛddhyā vai madhyamārdhādi ṣaṭkaram

ucchrāyādyanta vistāraḥ ucchrāyasadṛśaṃ tu vā

pañcahastaṃ hyaśeṣāṇāṃ śeṣāṇāmaṃgulaṃ prati

* * * * * anyeṣāṃ vṛddhiraṃgulamaṃgulam

pañcamāṃśena cocchrāyaśūlaṃ pakṣāṃśa nirmitam

dvyaṃguṣṭhaṃ prathame'pyaṃśe cānyeṣāmaṃgulottaram

dvāraṃ prati viśeṣeṇa rambhāstambhaṃ ca pallavaiḥ

pūrvadvāre tu sāmbā syād bhuktimuktipradāyakam

yajamānecchayā vāpi dakṣiṇaṃ paścimaṃ tathā

kirābhā svarṇavarṇā ca patākā sphaṭikopamā

nair-ṛtyāṃ nīlavarṇābhā mādhye citrātha vāsitā

saptahastāḥ patākāstu viṃśatyaṃgulavistṛtāḥ

bhāstikaṃ cāpi vikhyātā sa * * * * * * *

saptabhirdaśabhiḥ kuryād viṃśatyāvā kapai jān

aṣṭahastāthavā jyeṣṭhā dvikarā vistareṇa vai

pīṭhaṃ tu brahmaṇo bhāgād dviguṇaṃ kṣīravṛkṣajam

atha coktasamucchrāyaṃ vistāre dviguṇaṃ tataḥ

caturaṃgula vṛddhyā vai * * * * * * * * t

trīṇi tatrāpi dṛśyante pīṭheṣvevaṃ tu lakṣaṇam

valmīkātkaraṇādantāt gośṛṃgātpadmaṣaṇḍataḥ

praśastā dvādaśa hyetā grāhyā mṛtsnā viśeṣataḥ

aśvattha jambūnyagrodha cūtodumbarakodbhavaiḥ

puṣpā * * * * * * phalāni ca viśeṣataḥ

sasyaṃ ca sarvapuṣpaṃ ca phalaṃ ca svarṇaratnakam

pañcāmṛtaṃ tathā piṣṭaṃ vajrādidravyakaṃ tathā

maṃgalārthaṃ ca karpūraṃ rocanāgarucandanam

galūcyāhi balāpāmā sahadevī śatāvarī

* * * * * * kṣāyai bhasmasnāne tu kevalam

ālepanārthamindreṇa candanaṃ snānakumbhakam

candrodayaṃ vicitraiśca vaktrairapi ca kārayet

dhautapucchadvayapadaṃ śayanīyadvayaṃ bhavet

tridvaikamāṣikādyāśca kramājjyeṣṭhādiṣu triṣu

svapnārthameka kumbhaṃ ca śāntyarthaṃ kuṇḍasaṃkhyayā

praśāntādi ghaṭāścāpi vāstulakṣmī ghaṭeśake

vastrasādbhūṣitāścāpi hiraṇya navaratnakāḥ

pravāṇa mukhayuktāśca śobhanāsyāssalakṣaṇāḥ

stambhānvāsobhirācchādya gaurasarṣapaviṣkirān

sāpidhānāścarūḥ kuṃbhīṃ darvīṃ tāmreṇa kalpitām

āsanāni viśeṣeṇa triṃśaddarbhadalairbhavet

pālāśasamidhaścāpi caturaścaturo bhavet

madhumantrāṇi sarvāṇi phalaviṃśati saṃyutam

ghṛṇādhi pañcitaṃ śuddhaṃ śuṣkaṃ * * * kālakam

gulguluṃ bilvapatraṃ ca dhūmaṃ kuṃkumabandhi ca

taṇḍulāni triśūlaṃ vā ājyaṃ gandhaṃ yavāṃstilān

pāyasaṃ ca haviḥ śuddhaṃ kalpayedvidhinā punaḥ

daśarātrī vihīnāṃ ca * * * nandikeśvara

adhamaṃ madhyamaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ guroricchāvaśādbhavet

bahumānaṃ prakurvīta śreṣṭhamadhyādhamakramāt

uktaṃ pūrvanidānaṃ tu kiṃcillakṣaṇadaṇḍavat

daṇḍasya nāhāyāmaṃ syādrasaviṃśatibhirbhavet

caturaśrāṃgulī tatsyāt taṃ vṛttaṃ dvyaṃgulaṃ khanet

ājyanirgamamārgaśca kaniṣṭhāmbhaśca vistṛtiḥ

daṇḍāṃgulaṃ dvāviṃśatibhiḥ tattaṃ caturaṃgulam

daṇḍasya mugro cāgre'pi daṇḍī kaṃkaṇavadbhavet

sūcyagraṃ pādapāḥ proktāḥ kiṃśukādyāśca yājñikāḥ

khādirāḥ kiṇibhiścaiva pippalodumbarodbhavāḥ

krameṇa saṃgrahet sarvaṃ tadalābhe yavāṃstilān

sarvaṃ gṛhopakaraṇaṃ sthālīṃ davīṃ vidhānakam

chatraṃ ca cāmaraṃ caiva tālavṛttaṃ ca darpaṇam

sindhūrabhājanaṃ caiva pūgaphalaṃ tathaiva ca

bhājanaṃ pañcanānānta yogapaṭṭaṃ ca mārjanīm

snānayogyaṃ tathā sarvamadhivāsāya cānayet

makuṭaṃ kuṇḍalaṃ caiva hārakeyūramudrikām

vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kartavyaṃ ācāryāya viśeṣataḥ

śrīmadguroḥ padadvandva pūjanāt sarvapūjanam

tatpādapādahīnāśca dakṣiṇāśca bhavettadā

paviraṃ karmaṇinnīlaṃ mattinīlaṃ tathaiva ca

vaiḍūryamaṣṭakaṃ proktaṃ ratnagarbhaṃ viśeṣataḥ

śrīkaṇṭhakaṇṭhaṃ ca tathā śārī ca śākhinī tathā

svarṇaṃ tāmramayovarga bhāramāraṃ sa kāryakam

torikāssargamākṣīkaṃ rasavarjitagairikam

godhūmaṃ ca tilaṃ caiva māṣaṃ mudgasvaraṃ tathā

athavāṣṭakalā tu pañcahañca samāharet

pasarpe tu yavāhā ca pāradaṃ svarṇameva vā

māṣakaṃ vyāptake kuryāt pratyekottarayā tadā

pūrvasaṃbhūtasaṃbhāraiḥ ācāryādīṃśca pūjayet

nityakriyāṃ ca kṛtvā tu prāṇavapyakavasvayam

śāntākhyatoraṇādīṃśca sarvaṃ saṃpūjayedguruḥ

pūrve nandimahākālau yāmye bhṛṃgi vināyakau

śākhāmūle ca tatrasthaiḥ praśāntaśikharau yadā

dhanadaśrīpradau dvau dvau kramātsaṃpūjayedguruḥ

lokagrahasvasyadvasthā nandinaṃ ca dvayaṃ dvayam

yāgamūle marāścaite tiṣṭhanti pratitoraṇam

pūrvāditi kramādvāpi cāyudhānyapi cārcayet

triśūlaṃ cakramambhojaṃ patākā ca yathā kramam

huṃphaṇṇamo'ntāstramantraṃ mantrairevaṃ samarcayet

oṃ hrūṃ hrūṃ daṇḍāya huṃphaṇṇamaḥ

kumudaḥ kumudākṣaśca puṇḍarīkākṣaśca vāmanaḥ

supratiṣṭhitanāmā ca gajānāgraṃ vibhāvayet

raktanetraṃ raktapānaṃ nir-ṛtiṃ rākṣasādhipam

anekaratnasaṃyuktaṃ makaropari saṃsthitam

viloladhvajahastaṃ ca pītaśyāmatanuṃ śubham

hayārūḍhaṃ śarva śarvaraṃrīraṃ ca kuberakam

vṛṣārūḍhaṃ tamīśānaṃ triśūlavyāladhāriṇam

trilocanaṃ jaṭācūṭa śaraccandrārdhaśekharam

taṭitkoṭi pratīkāśaṃ kūrmārūḍhamanantaram

caturvaktraṃ svarṇavarṇaṃ haṃsārūḍhamanaupamam

stambhamūle ghaṭeṣveva vedadharmādikān nyaset

vardhanīṃ pṛṣṭhanīṃ kumbhaṃ bhrāmayettaṃ śivājñayā

kumbhe sthirāsanaṃ devaṃ śastrāluṃ ca dhruvāsanam

nijayāgaṃ jagannāthaṃ rakṣa bhaktānukampayā

athavā sthaṇḍile kuṃbhe maṇḍale śivamarcayet

maṇḍalasya tu madhye vilikhedaṣṭadalāmbujam

dalasandhi tṛtīyāṃśaṃ caturthaṃ * * * * kam

padmapatradalāgrāṇi gātrāṇi dalasandhayaḥ

dalāgraṃ tu dalaṃ nyastvā prativāruṇa maṇḍalam

śivasyā sanakaṃ tatra vīthyādi sahitaṃ bhavet

śobhānudvāramānena kapālau kṛtadardhatā

* * * * dalāgrāṇi dalagātrāṇi ca kramāt

kuṇḍasaṃskārakādīni deśikādeśato bhavet

caturvedavido viprāḥ paṭheyuḥ śāntisūktakān

prācyāṃ paṭhettu ṛgvedī sarvametatsamaṃ tadā

puruṣaṃ gatimetāni sāmavedī tu dakṣinaḥ

yajurvedī brāhmaṇastu pratīcyāṃ vai samuccaret

sruvasūktaṃ tathaivāśu japedātharvaṇistathā

agneḥ prāgādikānyāgāt pūrvakuṇḍādibhiḥ kramāt

pūrṇāhutiṃ kramāddatvā pūrvavaccarukumbhakam

yajamāno'pi pūrvoktabhūṣaṇairbhūṣayecca tāḥ

padmāsane tu prāyedyāḥ praṇavāsanamadhyame

kāṣāyakaruṇā caiva snāpa * * * * tathā

gandhatoyena phaṭkārahetinā śuddhavāriṇā

pādādikaraśuddhiśca mūrtipaiḥ kārayedguruḥ

śuddhapuṣpaiḥ susaṃpūjya nayeduttara vedikām

vimṛjya vilikhettatra hemasūcyā guruḥ svayam

ācāryastriṃśatābodhā pādādardhaṃ tathāpi vā

liṃgadairghyavikārāṃśe tribhakte bhāgahīnataḥ

kṣetrāṣṭaṃ cetu cihnaṃ syānmūrdhni bhāktidvayocyate

pṛṣṭhabhāge tu sambaddhaṃ lekhāt saṃbhramaṇādbhavet

* * * * * * * saṃ mīcāna pañcalohake

kallakṣmamārgamadhunā ghṛtenāpi prapūrayet

śayyābhūṣaṇapātrādi * * * * * * * *

bhūṣaṇairvividhaireva gobhiḥ kṣīrasamṛddhibhiḥ

gāyantyo maṃgalaṃ nāryo jīvaputrāssabhrātṛkāḥ

spṛṣṭvā ca rocanāṃ datvā kāryanirmartsanādikam

santoṣayecca kartā tu vividhaiḥ svarṇadānakaiḥ

mṛtsnāgomayagomūtra bhasmabhissalilāntaram

kāṣāyavāriṇā caiva nirmasti tadanantaram

ratnaśṛṃga suvarṇādi vāribhiścābhiṣecayet

gaṃgāditīrthatoyena padadena ca vāriṇā

nirmatsalaṃ vilepaṃ ca sugandhaiścandanādibhiḥ

aghoreṇa tu nīrājya bhasmanā tilakaṃ bhavet

jalajīrakadhātrādi pūrvairapyādiṣūcitaiḥ

puruṣeṇa viśeṣeṇa samācamya padā tadā

tato rathaṃ samāropya maṇḍapaṃ purameva vā

vrajanṛṣyādibhissārdhaṃ rathamārgānusāribhiḥ

nṛttagītādibhiścaiva mālābhiśca sugandhibhiḥ

pūrvaṃ tu maṇḍapadvāraṃ tatra bhadrāsanasthitān

* * * * * paścime nyasya śilāṃ vai saṃjñatām

tatra śastraśatāllabdha nidrākumbhadhruvāsanam

paścāt skaraṃ phalaśirasā ca * * * * * *

padatāṃ ca grahādīni hṛdā dadyātsvaśaktitaḥ

tatra sthito guruḥ paścāt ṣaṭtriṃśattatvasaṃcayam

vinyasya puṣpamālābhiḥ tripuṇṭraṃ parikalpayet

* * * *ṇa vinyasye darghyaviddhyā śivātmanā

bhūvahniyajamānārka jalavāyu niśākarāt

śarvaṃ paśupatiṃ cograṃ rudraṃ bhavamatheśvaram

* * * * * * rasa pavasāśca trirātrika

kṣma * * * * * * * * * * * * * stathā

sṛṣṭi krameṇa bhogāya muktyarthaṃ viparītakam

tatra dvayaṃ nyasedvāpya sarvatra dvyasakāraṇam

draṣṭavyā mūrtipāścaite bhogino ma * * * *

kṛtasnātā * * * * candanādi * * * *

raktapañcaka saṃyuktaṃ liṃgasyottarataḥ sthitān

kṛtasnānādikāntadvā liṃgamūle śivāṃ nyaset

tārapūrvaṃ ca mūsūṃhrīṃ madhyādanyatamena ca

* * * * * * kuryād bhasmadarbhatilairyutaḥ

oṃ hrīṃ hrāṃ saḥ oṃ hāṃ bhauri rudra ityete svāhetyapi ca piṇḍikām

dhārikā dīptamatyugrā jyotsnā cetāsalotkaṭā

vāmā jyeṣṭhā kriyājñāni ceṣṭā tisro'thavā nyaset

mā mokṣā kṣayānidrā mṛtyuṃ māyābhaya tha cakratā

ātmavidyāśivākhyeṣu triṣu mūrtiṣu vinyaset

gauryādi mantrakaireva pūrvavatsaṃprapūjayet

kuṇḍasya madhye deveśaṃ mekhalāsu mahatsvaram

kalaśe sthaṇḍile * * * * * dhānamā * *

gacchanti śūnyamārgeṇa nissaranti samudgatāḥ

saṃdhyeyaṃ mūrtipaistatra cānyonyaṃ nandikeśvara

tatvaṃ tatveśvarānmūrtiṃ mūrtīśāṃśca ghṛtādibhiḥ

pūrṇāhutyā sahasraṃ ca śataṃ vā cāpi homayet

* * * tarpya juhuyāt sannidhau mūrtipānapi

śāntikumbhe jalaiścāpi prokṣayet kuśamūlataḥ

vidadhyāt sannidhānārthaṃ hṛdā rodhāvakuṇṭhanam

* * * * * saṃśodhya viṣṇuṃ śauca viśuddhaye

* * * * * * * * * * madhyāgrataḥ spṛśet

oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vaṃ oṃ oṃ oṃ vāṃ vahnimūrtaye namaḥ

pañcamūrtyā pyevaṃ sandhānaṃ hṛdayādibhiḥ

vṛddhyā vṛkṣe'pi vainaṃ * * * gaccha svayaṃ naraiḥ

hutvā yadyatkṛtaṃ karma śivaśrotre nivedayet

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya rudrāmovitkṛte

oṃ hrīṃ śāṃkarī pūraya svāhā * * * * * *

* ṇaśaktiṃ kriyāśaktiṃ piṇḍe thaiva ca vigrahet

saptarātraṃ vā pañcarātraṃ trirātrakam

adhivāsa vihīnaśced yogo niṣphalamāpnuyāt

digbaliṃ vidhinā kuryācchivakumbhaṃ ca pūjayet

* * * * * * paścāt kṛtanityavidhiḥ svayam

samabhyarcya ca dikpālānśivakumbhaṃ ca vardhanīm

gacchecchivājñayā tatra prāsādaṃ śastramuccaret

liṃgaṃ na madhye sthātavyaṃ vedaghoṣa viśaṃkayā

tasmādīśānamāśritya śilāṃ madhye niveśayet

athavānena mantreṇa śivasyāsana rūpiṇīm

śivājñayātvayā cedā sthātavyamihasantatam

vajrādīni tato cāntāni tathośīrādikauṣadhau

pūrvoktadhānyarāśīṃśca kramā ca viracayya ca

pūrvādicāṣṭagātreṣu kramādekaikaśaḥ kramāt

sarittaṭamṛdā yuktaṃ parvatāgramṛdādi vā

madhukākṣata saṃyuktaṃ añjanena samanvitam

sarvabījasuvarṇābhyāṃ samāyuktaṃ vinikṣipet

vede navasya śaktyādi śaktiparyantamāsanam

svabhūmācchādya vastreṇa kavacenāstrarakṣaṇam

śīlāsūtrajadoṣasya śāntaye mūlamantrataḥ

pratyekaṃ vāstudevānāṃ tṛṇena juhuyātkramāt

svabandhusahitaḥ kartā pāścātyo daivato'pi vā

liṃgaṃ sthāpya jalebhadrāṃ bhadrāsyai cārdhamuttamam

dvāreṇa dvārabandhe ca dvāradeśena tadvinā

dvārasparśādvisṛjyaiva dvāreṇaiva śayet

gṛhe praveśastaddvārārikairapi samīritam

kartustvantu vināśāya mataśśāntiṃ samācaret

dvārābhimukha māpyāpi pīṭhe liṃgamanantaram

prītottihyātha deśiko mūrtipaissaha

dhyātvaikyaṃ śaktiśivayośśivayogī ca lambanam

ryaṃśena brahmabhāge vā yadvātryaṃśadvayena ca

sīsakaṃbhamapīḍāyātha dīrghāntārbhassamaṃ hitaḥ

śaktyantamūlamuccārya nyaseddṛṣṭvā ca niṣkalam

tattaddigīśamantreṇa pūrṇāṃ taṃ dakṣiṇānvitam

śataṃ mūlena kartavyaṃ ardhavāreṇa vā bhavet

liṃgokta nyāsake kuryāddoṣaśāntyai viśeṣataḥ

vālukābhiśca bhūsandhiṃ pūrya yajñānu lepanāt

saṃsnāpya kalaśainyastaistadvat pañcāmṛtādibhiḥ

umāmaheśa mantrābhyāṃ kausparśe liṃ * * * *

ca kuryā * * * * * * * śaktiṃ ca pīṭhake

kuṃkumāgarukarpūraiḥ samālabhyātha bhūṣaṇaiḥ

dhūpadīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ hṛdayena phalāni ca

* * datvā japaṃ kuryād nivedya * * * * *

vṛṣabhaṃ sthāpayet paścāt pūrva * * * * hunet

hutvā śive * * * * śāntyarthaṃ pa * * prabho !

mahotsavaṃ ca dānaṃ kuryāddina catuṣṭayam

turīye ca dine caiva pūrṇayā bahurūpiṇā

sarvakuṇḍeṣu saṃpātāhutiṃ śodhitaṃ haret

nirmālyāpanayaṃ kṛtvā snāpayitvā prapūjayet

asādhāraṇaliṃgeṣu kṣamasveti visarjanam

pratiṣṭhānte ca kartavyaṃ sthirādyāhuti saptakam

nityotha sarvagaiścaiva nīrāśī tṛptayeva ca

oṃ namaḥ śivāya * * * * * * * * * *

saṃpādyaivaṃ vidhāyaiva śivakumbhavadavyayam

saṃsnāpya taddvitīyaṃ tu yajamānābhiṣecane

śivājñayā bahirgatvā jagatī bāhyataḥ śubhaiḥ

bhagapramāṇayukte ca svapīṭhe kalpitāsane

uktāni pūrvamaṃgāni brahmāṇi tvayanaṃ vinā

oṃ saṃ vāmadevāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ

oṃ ceṃ tatpuruṣāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ

namaskāreṇa santarpya nivedyaṃ ca japaṃ tathā

tatratvaṃ sannidhīgrāhyā deva * * * * * *

calaliṃge ca lohe ca bāṇaliṃge svayaṃbhuvi

advita bhāvanāyukte sthaṇḍile ca tridhāvadhi

bandhudārasutairyuktaṃ kartāraṃ sa parigraham

śivavatpūjanaṃ kuryād yajamāno'pi deśikam

gurussantoṣahīnaśced yajamāno'pi niṣphalaḥ

dakṣiṇāśca tato dadyād yathā śaktyā viśeṣataḥ

toṣayecca viśeṣeṇa yajamānaḥ svayaṃ tathā

pratimāliṃgadevīnāṃ yāvantaḥ paramāṇavaḥ

* * * * pradaṃ nityaṃ śivaliṃgapratiṣṭhitam

kṣamasva nādya tatsarvaṃ kāruṇyāmbunidhirmama

pratiṣṭhāṃ puṇyakaṃ yattu sphurannakṣatra suprabham

paścāt pāśupataṃ japtvā praṇamya śivamavyayam

sthātavyaṃ bhavatā tāvad yāvat sa * * * * *

svasvamantraiśca devāśca sthāpyāstatra viśeṣataḥ

sādhyādi devā ṛṣayaḥ nakṣatraṃ kiṃ narastathā

gaṅgā ca yamunā caiva nadī caiva sarasvatī

* * * * * * nāñca sthānaṃ tatra vyavasthitam

* * * va gaṇadādrī nāgakeśara janmanām

māyāntaṃ ravirevaṃ syādāntaraṃ śaktireva ca

arcanā * * rāntaṃ vai kūrmādyāsana pūrvakam

śivaliṃgaṃ ca kartavyaṃ anyabrahma * * * *

pañcamāṃśaṃ tu saṃ * * * * * * * * * *

āsanaṃ snānapādyādi ratnanyāsādikaṃ tathā

calaliṃge viśeṣeṇa tribhirvā pañcabhistathā

bhāgatraye dvayevāpi kartavyaṃ nandikeśvara

sphuṭikādiṃ ca saṃskāro viṣṭamantreṇa kārayet

bāṇasvayaṃbhuvādīnāṃ pīṭhasaṃskāra varjanam

paiṣṭikammṛṇmayādīnāṃ kṣaṇaliṃgaṃ tathā bhavet

mantrānātmani saṃsthāpya tajjale prakṣipet punaḥ

devīṃ saṃsnāpya śayyāyāṃ adhiropya ca pūrvavat

hṛdayānnābhi paryantaṃ nā * * * * * vadhi

ātmavidyā śivāntaṃ tu kuryādīśāna veśanam

kramāddhutvā ca pītvā ca sandhyāyāmamanantaram

paścātsaṃsthāpya deveśīṃ tasyāmeva niyojayet

kriyāśaktiṃ tato nyasyed * * * * nya rūpiṇīm

amukeśī ca nāmāṃ ca samālabhya ca pūjayet

oṃ āṃ ādhāraśaktyai namaḥ * * * * * * *

oṃ ḹṃ vairāgyāya namaḥ | oṃ ḹṃ aiśvaryāya namaḥ

oṃ ḹṃ avairāgyāya namaḥ | oṃ lūṃ anaiśvaryāya namaḥ

oṃ hāṃ padmāya namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ kesarebhyo namaḥ

oṃ hrīṃ prājñyai namaḥ | oṃ hrīṃ jñānyai namaḥ

oṃ hrīṃ jvālinyai namaḥ | oṃ hrīṃ vāmāyai namaḥ

navaśaktīrimā nyasya devī dhyānaṃ ca kārayet

caturbhujāṃ triṇetrāṃ ca sarvalakṣaṇa saṃyutām

evaṃ dhyātvā jagaddhātrīṃ gauryābha * * * * *

oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ saḥ mahāgauri rudradayite namaḥ

iti mūlamantraḥ | oṃ gāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ

oṃ gaiṃ kavacāya hum | oṃ gauṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ

* * * mantra pūrvoktaḥ subhagādyā anantaram

oṃ saiṃ kāmamālinyai namaḥ | * * * * * * *

evaṃ dhyātvā susaṃpūjya sugandhaiḥ kusumākṣataiḥ

vidyāntāsanaśarya * gaṃsa trayamatho nyaset

śuddhyādi pūrvavatkuryāt kṛtvā piṇḍaṃ ca pūrvavat

śaktyā ca sarvato mukhyāssaṃsthāpya vidhivattataḥ

* * * * myaṃ tathā * * kṣantaṃ nāma dhārayet

* * * * * * * * vakṣyehaṃ nandikeśvara

ātmārthaṃ ca parārthaṃ ca sthāpanaṃ ca vidhīyate

maṇṭapaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kuṇḍaṃ pūrvavannandikeśvara !

unmattaṃ lalitaṃ bhīmaṃ tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭraṃ namam

vikaṭaṃ bhīmasaṃjñaṃ ca dvārapārśve prakalpayet

āmodaśca pramodaśca sumukho durmukhastathā

aṣṭamūrtyadhipā hyete athavā hastivaktrakaḥ

daṃṣṭrākaralavadanau * * * skandapādakau

gajānano mahākāyaḥ pralambodara saṃyutaḥ

sandhyābhrasannibhovāpi nīlanīraruhaprabhaḥ

śyāmavarṇamayo vāpi yatheṣṭaphalayogataḥ

kirīṭa makuṭopetaṃ śuklayajñopavītavān

pīṭhasthaṃ mūṣikasthaṃ vā sihmasthaṃ vātha kalpayet

jalādhivāsanaṃ kuryāllambakūrca samanvitam

kuṇḍāni pañcakaṃ caivamathavā parikalpayet

jalādānīya vighneśaṃ snānavedyāṃ tu vinyaset

rakṣāsūtraṃ ca badhvā tu mūlamantraṃ paṭhedbudhaḥ

navaratnādikaṃ nyastvā vāsābhyāṃ kramaveṣṭanam

samidājya carūṃścaivaṃ aṣṭadravyaiśca homayet

mūlamantrāddaśāṃgānāṃ * * * * ntu vikriyā

svasvamantreṇa śāntyarthaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ kārayedguruḥ

āsīnāsanamevaṃ hi prātaḥkāle guruḥ svayam

uddhṛtaṃ vighnarājānaṃ kumbha vahnyādimarcayet

pūrṇāhutiṃ ca juhuyāt praviśedbhagavānguruḥ

pūrṇān nyastvā tataḥ paścādvighnarāja pratiṣṭhitam

sumuhūrte viśeṣeṇa mantranyāsādikaṃ caret

tattatkumbhodakaiścaiva tattaddeśe'bhiṣecayet

evaṃ ca snapanaṃ kṛtvā naivedyaṃ ca nivedayet

vastrahemāṃgulīyādyaiḥ stoṣayedguru mūrtipān

triṇetraṃ caturbhujaṃ śāntaṃ kṛtāñjali puṭasthitam

jaṭāmakuṭa saṃyuktaṃ namāśuśriṃ * * * * kham

bālenduśekharaṃ caiva kālakaṇṭhavirājitam

devīṃ ca śyāmavarṇābhāṃ sarvālaṃkārasaṃyutām

śayanāropaṇaṃ caiva pūrvavannandikeśvara

vasukumbhān nyaseddikṣu * * rāṃścāpi pūjayet

vāmabījāṃga saṃyukta devaṃ devyāśca śaktimat

prabhāte mūrtipaissārdhaṃ snāpayedvidhinā guruḥ

tataḥ prabhṛtinandīśamarcayedbhakti saṃyutaḥ

dakṣiṇatastu kartavyaṃ vāme vā śuddhabhūtale

astreṇa prokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā pātraṃ lakṣaṇa saṃyutam

randhrāṃgulaṃ samārabhya vyomāṃgulavivardhanāt

suvṛttaṃ cāsanopetaṃ karṇikādalarājitam

ekadvitricatuṣpañcaṣaṭ * * * * kā bhavet

ardhatripādamātraṃ vā dvimātraṃ karṇikocchrayam

kuḍumbadvayamārabhya kuḍambādhyā vidhi

annaliṃgaṃ trikālepi prātarmadhyāhnakeṣu ca

pradoṣekṣataliṃgaṃ syāt tatra pāśu * * * *

tridhā pāśupato jñeyo miśra śāntograbhedataḥ

vidyunmālānibhaśceto jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitaḥ

varadābhayapāṇirvā sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutaḥ

yojayet saumyamūrtiḥ syādratnagarbhaṃ viśeṣataḥ

śūlamūlābhaye dakṣe śūlāgravaradānvitaḥ

triśūlāstrakaraṃ vauṣaṭ tridhā raudrīṃ smaranyajet

dakṣe pāśaṃ mṛgaṃ vāme tvanyathā ca nigadyate

dadhaddvidhā samākhyātaṃ miśraṃ pāśupataṃ tridhā

athavā pratimākāro vidheyastatpramāṇataḥ

annaliṃga pramāṇe tu mūlāgne samavistare

sthilīkāmānadaṇḍena sahitaṃ vā samarcayet

pādukātryaṃgule tasmādaṃgulādaṃgulairghanāt

dairghyānusārataḥ kuryādvistārosyārdha ucchrayaḥ

tayostu vṛṣabhaḥ pūjyaḥ snānānto vā prakīrtitaḥ

navāṃgaliṃga saṃyuktamatha nityotsavaṃ nayet

dvābhyāṃ tribhiścaturbhirvā pūrvāhne ca pradoṣake

raṃge vā śibikāyāṃ vā paricārakamūrdhasu

chatracāmara saṃyuktaṃ nānādhvajasamāyutam

ādyaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryād bhṛṃgiṇī tālasaṃyutam

mātṝṇāṃ candratālaṃ syā vighneśaṃ dharakī bhavet

taṭaprabhāraṃ durgāyāṃ viṣamaścinta caṇḍike

dvitīyabhramaṇaṃ hyetan mahāpīṭhapradakṣiṇam

athavā balipīṭhe tu brahmatālasamanvitam

paiśāce jvalamevaṃ syācchāvarī tālasaṃyutam

dakṣiṇe bhṛṃginī tālaṃ kauśikena samanvitam

evaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā tṛtīyaṃ dhāma saṃviśet

athavā maṇṭapādau tu pīṭhasaṃsthānamatkramāt

evaṃ liṃgātsamāstitya liṃgadakṣe niveśayet

anyāsāmapi devīnāṃ devānāṃ mūlabimbavat

saṃpādyaṃ tena nityaṃ tu nityotsavamathācaret

pādukādvitayaṃ pūjyaṃ nityotsavavidhi kramāt

maṇṭapaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kuṇḍaṃ pūrvavadvidhinā bhavet

hāstikaṃ bhadrapīṭhaṃ tu kalpayellakṣaṇānvitam

punaścatāṃ namaskāraṃ sarve vai praṇavādikāḥ

śikhāśca kavacaṃ tatra astramantraistu madhyagaiḥ

tāraṃ hṛdaya saṃyuktaṃ bhagavate tathaiva ca

avāntareṇa rūpeṇa dhyeyamūrtissamīritā

maṇṭapādiṣu saṃskāro digīśāvāhamācaret

atha lakṣmī punaḥ sthānaṃ devasya ca yathākramam

niveśya mūrtirūpaṃ tu śayyāyāṃ tadanantaram

khaḍgādīnāṃ ca vinyāsaṃ mantrī kuryācca tadyathā

dakṣiṇau vāmapādau dhyādakṣamājāṃghriṣu kramāt

pītaśśuklāruṇaḥśveta vidyutpīta sitāruṇāḥ

śikhāyāṃ khaḍgavinyāsaṃ lalāṭe jagavameva ca

lagaṃ ca musalaṃ caiva bāhyetyekodanantaram

namaskāreṇa mantreṇa nābhī jaṭhara pṛṣṭhayoḥ

guruṃ śaṃkhaṃ ca śi * * * * * * * * * *

tārapūrvaṃ śvaṃ ḍhaṃ pāṣaṃ gadāyai ca namoṇunā

* * * * * * * * * * pādeṣu nikṣipet

viṣṇuṃ ca pṛṣṭhavaṃśe tu tathavaṃ madhusūdanam

śrīdharaṃ jaṃghanaiñcaiva hṛṣīkeśamanantaram

* * * * * kṣāmantrairakṣaṇādi svasaṃjñayā

namontaraṃ hṛdayādyaiśca oṃ hrīmityādikāstathā

śiro'bhidhāyitomantra prasiddhairnandikeśvara

śikhāṃ varmakramādastramoṃ cakrīti paṭhantikam

gāyatrīṃ cāpi sāvitrīṃ praṇavādi namo'ntakam

saptārṇaṃ piṇḍamākhyaṃ ca mantro'yaṃ sarvasiddhidaḥ

gāyatrīṃ cāpi sāvitrīṃ netrayorvāmadakṣayoḥ

oṃ kṣaṃ piṃgalākṣarūpāya namaḥ

tanmadhye ca punardeva yaṃ padmāṣṭadalaṃ bhavet

savye dale śikhāṃ cāpi paścime kavacaṃ nyaset

aiśe dale ca sāvitrīṃ rākṣasaṃ netramantrakam

atha dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ cakre vai dvāradeśake

dāmodarāntamantre ca pūrvādiṣu yathā kramam

gadāṃ ca cakraṃ śaṃkhaṃ ca padmaṃ ca halameva ca

gandhaiḥ puṣpaiśca naivedyairvastrālaṃkaraṇādibhiḥ

nyāse tu pañcamūrttyante mūlamantro bhavedyathā

oṃ hāṃ rīṃ raṃ aṃ tathā | oṃ aṃ oṃ yugmakapūrvakā

nārāyaṇa kramādetā namo'ntāśca caturthiyuk

aṃgānāṃ cāpi vinyāso yathā pūrvaṃ prapūjanam

mūlamantra sahasraṃ ca tadardhaṃ deśiko japet

yāgadoṣa nivṛttyarthaṃ balidānaṃ ca dāpayet

lakṣyo namovanmūrttyā na tu oṃ bhaṃ phaṃ dhruvānirā

pañcasaṃkhyānta mantraiśca tanmadhye ca vyavasthitaḥ

huṃphaḍastrāvasānena samudāyena vā kvacit

vyāpinīṃ prakṛtiṃ tasya lakṣmī mantreṇa pīṭhikām

pūrvavinyastaratnāṃ ca tatra ca dvādaśākṣaram

śaṃkhaṃ cakraṃ gadāṃ khaḍgaṃ bhasmaṃ ca vanamālinam

namo'ntavyāpanāntaṃ tu mūrttyā yugmaśanīstathā

puruṣāntaṃ bhavedīśaṃ vibhāvyaiva mataḥ param

bhaktyā saṃpūjya vidhivatteṣāṃ pūrvavadācaret

dhārayennāma devasya viṣṇoḥ sthāpanamuttamam

vidyā ca dvividhā proktā saṃskṛtyanyātmikā bhavet

bhaktoktasthānake vāpi sarvatīrthāntarālake

pūrvokta pustakaṃ vāpi athavā lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu

pañcaviṃśati mātrāntaṃ bhavet pustakadairghyakam

pramāṇaṃ ca viśālaṃ syāt pakṣavahniyutaṃ tu vā

tālaṃ vā bhūrjapatraṃ vā padādau svarṇapatrake

maṣyā vā dhātunā vāpi likhet tūlikayāpi vā

śubhavāre sunakṣatre sulagne ca prasannadhīḥ

adīkṣitasakāśe ca nādhyetavyaṃ viśeṣataḥ

lekhayedviśadairvarṇairjapa luktāyataṃ śubham

śivaṃ sugandhairabhyarcya mātṛkāṃ śivarūpiṇīm

gomayālipta bhūbhāge puṣpavraja suśobhite

śuddhaśaiva samāyukto likhedvā pāṭhadhāraṇam

nyūnātirikta vā * * * * * * pramādibhiḥ

alpajñāna samopetairācāryāśodhitairapi

* * * raviruddhasya svasiddhānta virodhinam

ityevamādibhirdoṣaiḥ upetasya kvacitkvacit

śivata * * * * dyaḥ sa vidyā parameśvaraḥ

śivavidyānusāreṇa vidyādānaṃ taducyate

saṃskṛta prabhavaiśśabdaiḥ viśuddhaissaṃskṛtairapi

pradeśavartibhirvākyairbodhayeddeśikottamaḥ

* * * guṇamaiśvaryaṃ vidyādānasya tatphalam

ghoraṃ ca narakaṃ yāti śivajñānasya nāśakaḥ

pratyahaṃ lekhayedvidyān yathāśakti subuddhimān

tāvadyugasahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate

mātṛkasvadharma * * svārthaṃ svargaṃ sametya ca

api ślokaṃ tadardhaṃ vā śivajñānasya vākyakam

śṛṇuyādeka cittastu tadarthamapi dhārayet

śivajñānābhiyuktasya bhojanācchādanaṃ mahat

vaśyennakāra * * ye'tha tadeva phalamaśnute

sa rājā vartate rāṣṭraṃ sa nṛpo'pyajito bhavet

purohitatve sarveṣāṃ rājakāryetyadarśane

tasmāt siddhāntaśaivena śuddhaśaiva vidāraṇāt

evaṃ vidyā prabhāvatvāt pāvanaṃ ca śṛṇuṣva vai

vidyāśālā viśālāsyātprāsāde vātha maṇḍape

vidyāpīṭhasya vistāraḥ saptāṃguli viśālataḥ

caturaścastamo pāsyād āyāmāgraṃ tu vā bhavet

prokṣayet pañcagavyena śivajñānākhyavidyatām

āsanādyaṃ vidheyaṃ ca mūrtimūrtīśa saṃyutāḥ

madhye tu śivakuṃbhaṃ tu pārśvayormakaradvayam

mantranyāsaṃ guruḥ kṛtvā madhye tu paramaṃ śiva

viprakṣatriya viṭcchūdrāstvantarālāstriyo'pi vā

kṛtvā teṣāṃ pratikṛtiṃ pratiṣṭhāpya samācaret

kaumārarauravādyaiśca yena mārgeṇa dīkṣitāḥ

sarveṣāmapi śaivānāṃ sthāpanaṃ dvividhaṃ matam

agrahāre purādau vā nadītīre vane'pi vā

sthāne'nyatra ca kartavyaṃ svapradhānasya mandiram

parivāra samopetaṃ svapradhānaṃ ca kārayet

parivāravihīnaṃ tu kalpitaṃ tvaṃgamiṣyate

ekaviṃśati hastāntaṃ vimānavyāsa iṣyate

prākāraṃ gopurāṃgaṇya mālādīnāṃ nirīkṣaṇam

mūrdheṣṭakāvidhānaṃ ca pūrvavatsamudīritam

vighneśaguhanandyādi pratimākṛtimeva vā

mṛdbhirvā kārayeddhīmān sarvamā * * * * *

liṃgaṃ vā coktamārgeṇa pratimāṃ vāpi kārayet

sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ sarvamānopaśobhitam

tasminprati kṛtiṃ kuryādgatarudrānukūlataḥ

aṣṭatālena mānena pratikṛtyāṃ vidhīyate

āsanaṃ mānavistāraṃ sarvalakṣaṇa saṃyutam

kṣatriyaścedvidhātavyaṃ sarvabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam

striyaḥ śvetadukūlaiśca bhūṣaṇairbhūṣayetpunaḥ

pratimokta prakāreṇa madhūcchiṣṭādikaṃ caret

netrasyonmīlanaṃ cāpi vidhinā vidhinā kārayedguruḥ

sthaṇḍilopari pīṭhaṃ ca praṇavaṃ kalpayedguruḥ

suvarṇa vasubhārābhyāṃ kṛṣṇamaṇḍalamālikhet

netramantraṃ samuccārya kriyāmenāṃ samācaret

ghṛtena madhunā vāpi dvayaṃ saṃpūrya darśayet

anantādhāradharmādyāmathodyāsanakalpanam

jalādhivāsitaṃ bimbaṃ snānavedyāṃ nyasetpunaḥ

vastrairābharaṇairmālyaiḥ bhūṣayetpratimāṃ punaḥ

pūrvaśīrṣaṃ nyasedbimbaṃ tasyottare kumbhaṃ ca kārayet

vyomākṣaraiśca saṃyuktaiḥ pañcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgakaiḥ

tattadbījena kartavyaṃ pañcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgakam

strīpratiṣṭhā svamantrāścedvardhanyāṃ tu prakīrtitam

parivāraghaṭe pūjā svaṣṭau vā vasavo matāḥ

pūrvādyaṣṭaghaṭe vāpi puruṣe lokapālakān

ātmavidyāśivākhyaṃ tu tatvatrayamudīritam

krameṇādhipatitvena pūjayennandikeśvara

nivṛttyādyā mūrtipāḥsyuraṣṭamūrtau viśeṣataḥ

akārādikṣakārāntaṃ mātṛkānyāsamācaret

tenaivāṃgantu vinyasya gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

kuṇḍasaṃskārakaṃ * * * * ṇāṃ jīvatarpaṇam

brahmo dubarakāśvatthavaṭāḥ pūrvādiṣu kramāt

pradhānaṃ brahma vṛkṣaṃ syācchataṃ vārdhaṃ tadardhakam

āhutitritayaṃ kuryāt pratyekaṃ nandikeśvara

śāntitoyena mūrdhādi pādāntaṃ prokṣayettadā

deśiko mūrtipaiḥsnātvā prabhāte nityasaṃyutaḥ

adhyetṛbhiśca daivajñaiḥ śilpibhissahitairyutaḥ

pūrṇāntamagnihomaṃ ca kṛtvotthāpyātha bimbakam

ratnanyāsādisaṃyuktaṃ snānaśvabhre niveśayet

mātṛkānyāsakaṃ kṛtvā jīvanyāsaṃ samācaret

vastrādyābharaṇaiścaiva malaṃkṛtvātha deśikaḥ

śivabhaktāṃśca saṃpūjya yathā vibhavavistaram

sanmārgamātmako nityamātma mantraṣaḍaṃgakaiḥ

balyantaṃ vā samabhyarcya bhaktyā sanmārganāyakaḥ

mūlamantramitikhyātaṃ svāhāntaṃ cāgnikāryake

pratimāsotsavaṃ kuryādvibhavasyānusārataḥ

dhvaje'pi vṛṣabhaṃ kuryācchivavaccotsavaṃ bhavet

pūrvotsavaṃ ca kartavyaṃ vaśināṃ nandikeśvara

tasya śrīrvijayo tārcciratne sāyujyamāpnuyāt

dvārāṃgāni jayādyaiśca saṃskṛtyāyatanaṃ nyaset

tatvatrayaṃ kramānnyasya sannirodhyātha homayet

vāstudvārādayo yaṣṭvā tatraivānantamantrataḥ

kāntisiddhārtharuddhiśca yavavṛddhisahānunā

te ca nāragvadho dūrvā sāndaghnaśca yo dalaḥ

dvāramuttarataḥ kiñcid āśritaṃ sanniveśayet

śivamākāśadeśe tu vyāpakaṃ sarvapṛdgalam

dvārāśritāṃśca nandyādīn hṛtsaṃyuktaiḥ svanāmabhiḥ

astreṇa doṣamokṣārthaṃ juhuyādāhuteśśatam

nikṣipet kuṃbhagarbhe ca nyasya tantrānnivāsikam

kṣmyāntaṃ ca śekharaṃ tatvaṃ prāntaṃ tatra niveśayet

tatrantadadhipāścāpi praṇavādyaiḥ svanāmabhiḥ

vidyeśān prerakācchambhuṃ prerakaśca svayaṃ varaiḥ

svarṇādyādbhavapuruṣaṃ * * * puruṣāntaram

śulanāsā samāptau tu pūrvavedyāśca madhyataḥ

pūrṇādyekatamaṃ pūtaṃ pañcagavyena saṃyutam

gandhaliptaṃ savastraṃ ca gandhapuṣpapradhūpitam

pūrakeṇa samādāya sakalīkṛtavigrahaḥ

bodhāyanātmavāṃcchambhoreka ketuṃ tato guruḥ

samāropya ca śayyāyāṃ dhyātvā rudramupāntime

homaṃ tu sannidhānāya prokṣaṇaṃ sparśanaṃ japam

prakṛtyantaṃ vidhāyaiva kumbhe tānviniveśayet

pratiṣṭhā ca tatoddiṣṭā tathedānīmihocyate

paiṣṭike dārujaṃ śūlaṃ śailaje dhāmni śailajam

sa ca triśūlayuktastu grahaśūlāni homataḥ

śukrairgrahairasairvāpi hastairdaṇḍastu saṃmataḥ

citrojvalaṃ ca saṃghāto yaddvijaṃghārdhato bhavet

pīṭhasya veṣṭakaṃ śukraiḥ * * * * * * * *

rālajo vaṃśajo yadvā yadvāsundarivallabhaḥ

ayamāropyamāṇastu rūṇamāyāti vā yadi

pūrvavacchāntihomaṃ ca bahurūpeṇa vā bhavet

vidhāya rūpakaṃ daṇḍaṃ sthāpayedastramantrataḥ

prāsādasthāpanaṃ kuryān mṛtkaṣāyādi vāribhiḥ

liṃgavaccūliṃkā nyāse vijñānārcākriyāṃ tathā

daṇḍaṃ tathātma tatvaṃ ca vidyātatvamataḥ param

dhvaje yatpratibhāgantu pīṭhaṃ taistaistu mantrakaiḥ

astrayāgavidhānena tatsarvamupadarśitam

jaṃghāyoditadūrdhve tu tritatva * * * * *

anantaṃ kālarudraṃ ca dhyāyettaccaraṇāmbujaiḥ

bhuvanairlokapālaiśca śatarudrādibhirvṛtam

jala tejo'nilavyoma pañcāṣkasamanvitam

yonyaṣṭaka samāyuktaṃ nyāsāvadhi guṇatrayam

mañjirīvedikāyāṃ vidyādhika catuṣṭayam

tatkalaśeśvaraṃ bimbaṃ vidyeśvaramanantaram

icchājñānakriyāṃ cāpi daṇḍe nādyaṃ ca vinyaset

jaghanyāṃ dhāmasandhāya liṃgapiṇḍikayā yathā

brahmaghoṣaistūrya ghoṣaiḥ vedamaṃgalagītibhiḥ

svamantraiśca samutthāpya vinyasecchaktipakaje

yajamāno dhvaje lagne suhṛdbhirbandhubhissaha

dhyāyanguruḥ paśupatiṃ sthiramastrādhipairyutam

nyūnāti doṣaśāntyarthaṃ hutvā datvā ca tatphalam

pratimāliṃgavedīnāṃ yāvantaḥ paramāṇavaḥ

tatkoṭi guṇitaṃ puṇyaṃ prāsāde dhvajaropaṇāt

āṣāḍhādi caturdaśyāṃ atha śrāvaṇabhādrayoḥ

triṣu māseṣu caiteṣu mukhyamadhyādhama kramāt

kṣīrāmbudhau harissākṣā suptaye trimāsike

trividhaṃ tadvijānīyān naimittikamidaṃ param

naimittikamidaṃ nāma pavitrakamiti sphuṭam

pavitramanyakāle'pi kalpayedanvahaṃ prati

arke ca śūlinītā ca caṇḍikāyāṃ yamaṃ tathā

samārabhya pratipadi yāvatpañcaśataṃ kramāt

kalau yuge ca kārpāsa tantunā ca pavitrakam

sūtraṃ śubhaṃ ca ghautaṃ ca śoṣitaṃ tantu kalpayet

sūtraṃ mauñjyādi saṃbhūtaṃ yogyaṃ tatra pavitrake

tadbahistantubhiścaiva kārayettu pavitrakam

itthaṃ trīṇi tadardhena viṃśatyā vātha saptadhā

tulyagranthyantaraiścaiva kartavyaṃ śatapañcakaiḥ

athavāliṃgavistāramānāni caturaṃgulaiḥ

gaṃgāvatārakaṃ cāpi kuryātkiṃkiṇi kānvitam

aghoreṇa tu śuddhirvai vaktramantreṇa pūjayet

niśāgairikarājaiśca rañjayettu pavitrakam

yathā śobhamantarālameka dvicaturaṃgulam

jāyānyā vijayā ṣaṣṭhī ajitā ca sadāśivā

adhikāvā yathā śobhaṃ kartavyā granthayaḥ śubhāḥ

ratnaje ca yathā yogaṃ pavitraṃ kārayedguruḥ

eteṣāṃ devatānāṃ ca pavitraṃ kārayedguruḥ

ekaikadvāradikpāla kalaśādiṣu vinyaset

daśabhirdaśabhigranthiraṣṭāviṃśatito bhavet

gra * * * namapyeṣāṃ granthivistārasaṃyutam

raktaiḥ kṣaumaiśca puṣpaiśca bhūṣayenmakhamandiram

ra * * marcayet tatra sūtrite bhūpradeśake

prokṣya dvārāṇi cāstreṇa kramātpūrvādito'rcayet

oṃ hāṃ pratiṣṭhākalā dvārāya namaḥ

tathaiva dvāraśākhāsu dve dveṣu ca yathā kramam

* * * * * ṛṣabhaṃ skandaṃ deśaissaha * *

kaṃkataṃ kuṃkumaṃ tailaṃ añjanaṃ darpaṇaṃ tathā

āsanaṃ pāduke pātraṃ nāgapaṭṭātapatrakam

sājyaṃ caruṃ ca pūrvaṃsyā sāntaṃ gandhādikaṃ nare

samādāya pavitrāṇi prokṣitānyarghya vāriṇā

kṛṣṇā jinādinācchādya smaran saṃvatsarātmakam

sveti heti prayogeṇa maṃtrasaṃhitayā punaḥ

yāgadharmavṛṣasthāna prāsādānala saṃśrayam

pūjitāyātha sūryāya datvā gandhapavitrakam

dadyādgandha pavitraṃ tu nandyādi dvārapālake

śāstrebhyo lokapālebhyaḥ pavitrāṇi svanāmabhiḥ

abhyarcya vidhivad * * * * śikāṃghripadāmbujam

pañcāśadauṣadhīliptaṃ naromānavivarjitam

ekagra * * * * ḍhya caturvargaphalodayam

amantriṇāpataṃ hyeti deśikopi yajedyathā

prabhavātmatrayaṃ mitvā tvadicchāvyāptikārakam

sarvathā sarvadā śambho namaste'stu prasīdame

mantraistrailokya pālaiśca sahitaḥ parivārakaiḥ

niyamaṃ ca kariṣyāmi parameśa tavājñayā

mūlaṃ śivāntamuccārya tacchivāya nivedayet

tṛtīyāṃśca carorhutvā tadatīta śivāgnaye

kṣetrapālebhyaḥ svāheti

atha dadyāttu vāyavyaṃ kṣetrapālabaliṃ guruḥ

pūrṇāhutiṃ vyāhṛtiṃ ca hutvā rundhati pāvakam

oṃ hāṃ agnīṣomābhyāṃ svāhā | oṃ hāṃ agnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāhā

devyāstu sthaṇḍilebhyarcya vahnau kṛtadanantaram

astramantreṇa saṃśuddhaṃ śivamantreṇa * * *

oṃ hāṃ nandine namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ mahākālāya namaḥ

oṃ hāṃ vṛṣabhāya namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ devyai namaḥ

ityevaṃ kramato'bhyarcya dvārapālādikān nyaset

bhūtaśuddhirviśeṣārghyaṃ cośīraṃ prokṣaṇādikaḥ

kusumairakṣatairdūrvā candanādyairmṛdādibhiḥ

śivohamiti sarvatra jñānamagre prajānatā

āsādya rākṣasīṃ kāṣṭhāṃ prakṣipeduttarāyaṇe

caturṣvathāntya saṃskārairlakṣaṇādyaissu saṃskṛtaiḥ

vardhanīṃ cāsanārthāya tānatraiva prakalpayet

pūrvādyabhimukhaṃ kumbhaṃ sarvadhānyopari sthitam

śivamantraissamabhyarcya vardhanyā mantramarcayet

kumbhañcānu gāṃsamyagvardhanīṃ ca tathaiva ca

mūlamantraṃ samuccārya avicchinnāmbudhārayā

ghaṭaṃ pūrvaṃ samāropya vardhanīṃ tasya pārśvataḥ

tārāsavastha vardhanyāṃ āsanaṃ ca tadā dvayoḥ

jñānakhaḍgaṃ ghaṭe nyasya mūlāṇuñca japettadā

lambodaraṃ tu nair-ṛtyāṃ pūjayettu caturbhujam

gurumaiśānake bhāge sthāpayettadanantaram

vidhivat sthāpayet paścād gandhatoyaistathāmbubhiḥ

kuṇḍasaṃskārapūrvaṃ tu śivāgniṃ paripūjayet

devavahnyātmabhedena tridhā darvyā vibhājayet

pūrvoktadantakāṣṭhaṃ ca dadyātkṣīratarūdbhavam

dātavyaṃ rūpiṇā bhasma dakṣiṇe śikhayā'thavā

puṣpamīśānake kuryād āgneyyāṃdiśi rocanam

sarvāṇi homadravyāṇi sadyo jāte kuśaissaha

kaṃkataṃ kuṃkumaṃ tailaṃ añjanaṃ darpaṇaṃ tathā

āsanaṃ pāduke pātraṃ nāgapaṭṭātapatrakam

sājyaṃ caruṃ ca pūrvaṃsyāsānna gandhādikaṃ nare

samādāya pavitrāṇi prokṣitānyarghyavāriṇā

kṛṣṇājinādinācchādya smaransaṃvatsarātmikam

sveti heti prayogena mantrasaṃhitayā punaḥ

yāgadhāma vṛṣasthāna prāsādānala saṃśrayam

pūjitāyātha sūryāya datvā gandhapavitrakam

dadyāngandhapavitraṃ tu nandyādi dvārapālake

sāstrebhyo lokapālebhyaḥ pavitrāṇi svanāmabhiḥ

abhyarcya vighnarājaṃ ca deśikāṃghri padāmbujam

pañcāśadauṣadhīliptamānonmāna vivarjitam

ekagranthyalpa sūtrāḍhyaṃ caturvargaphalodayam

āmantriṇāpadaṃ hyetad deśiko'pi yajedyathā

prabhavāmantrayāmitvā tvadicchā vyāptikārikā

sarvathā sarvadā śambho namastestu prasīdame

mantraistrailokya pālaiśca sahitaḥ parivārakaiḥ

niyamaṃ pañca kariṣyāmi parameśa tavājñayā

mūlaṃ śivāntamuccāryaṃ tacchivāya nivedayet

tṛtīyāṃśaṃ carorhutvā tadatīta śivānnayet

pūrvādi digvāsibhyo digīśamātṛgaṇarudrakṣetrapālebhyaḥ svāheti

atha dadyāttu vāyasyaṃ kṣetrapālabaliṃ kuru

pūrṇāhutiṃ vyāhṛtiṃ ca hutvārundhita pāvakam

oṃ hāṃ agnīṣomābhyāṃ svāhā | oṃ hāṃ agnaye sviṣṭa-

kuryādāhutibhiścaiva caturbhyaṃ nandikeśvara

nāḍīsandhānavidhinā yojayennandikeśvara

hṛdayākhyena mantreṇa pavitrāṇyadhiropayet

aṃgamantrairviśeṣeṇa japaṃ kuryātsamāhitaḥ

puṣpadhūpādibhiścaiva saṃpūjya vidhinā tadā

bhaktyā padāntikaṃ gatvā gurordadyātpavitrakam

pañcagavyaṃ caruṃ dantadhāvanaṃ ca vinikṣipet

svapadena smarannīśaṃ mumukṣurdarbhasaṃstare

kevalaṃ bhasmaśayyāyāṃ sopavāsassamāhitaḥ

utthāya prātarācamya kṛtaprātassamāhitaḥ

pavitrāṇi samādāya cāvisarjita daivatam

devaṃ visṛjya nirmālyamapanīya ca tadbahiḥ

śivakumbhe ca vardhanyāṃ dvāradikpālavahniṣu

kuryātpūjāṃ savistārāṃ naimittika vidhānvitām

hutvā cāṣṭottaraṃ paścācchataṃ pūrṇāhutiṃ punaḥ

dvārapālādi dikpālakumbhavardhanikāsu ca

dadyāt pavitramātmārthaṃ gaṇāya gurupaṃkaje

smarankalātmakaṃ devaṃ prabrūyāditi śaṃkaram

kliṣṭaṃ kṛtaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ dhṛtaṃ guptaṃ ca yatkṛtam

sarvātmā vāyunā śambho tvatsūtreṇa tvadicchayā

ātmatatve prakṛtyante pālite padmayoninā

vidyātatve ca vidyānte pālite viṣṇukāraṇe

śivānte śivatatvetha rudrakāraṇapālite

sarvakāraṇa bālyeṣu sarvatatveṣu suvratam

mumukṣūṇāṃ pavitraṃ tu cātmavidyāśive kramāt

svāhāntaṃ vā namo'ntaṃ vā mantraireṣāmudīrayet

oṃ hāṃ ātmatatvādhipataye śivāya svāhā

oṃ hāṃ śivatatvādhipataye śivāya svāhā

oṃ hāṃ sarvatatvādhipataye śivāya svāhā

bhaktyā puṣpakaradvandvaṃ gurave vinivedayet

antaṃ vāreṇa bhūtānāṃ dṛṣṭvātaḥ parameśvaraḥ

mantrahīnaṃ kriyāhīnaṃ vṛttahīnaṃ ca yatkṛtam

utkṛtaṃ kṛtihīnaṃ ca tatpurātha maheśvara !

tvayā pavitritaṃ sarvaṃ jagat sthāvarajaṃgamam

ekībhavatu tatsarvaṃ tatvajñārtha sūtragranthitam

praṇamyāmūlacittena santuṣṭaṃ bhāvayecchivam

saptāhaṃ pañcarātraṃ vā tryahamekāhameva vā

praṇamya kṣamayitveśaṃ gatvā kuṇḍāntikaṃ prati

samabhyarcya samāropya puṣpadhūpākṣatādibhiḥ

śivaṃ stutvā praviśyāntarvanditvā taṃ kṣamāpayet

pūrṇāhutiṃ śanairhutvā patisthaṃ pitṛśecchivam

sudityebhyo'tha datvā vai cāhutīnāṃ catuṣṭayam

pavitraṃ ca tato nyasyet samyak siddhāntapustake

oṃ hāṃ suvaḥ svāhā oṃ hāṃ bhūrbhuvassuvaḥ svāhā

oṃ hāṃ agnaye svāhā oṃ hāṃ somāya svāhā

evaṃ kramādāhutibhiḥ juhuyāttadanantaram

āyānte deśike teṣāṃ sadyaḥ prīṇāti śaṃkaraḥ

gurusaṃtoṣaṇānnānyad vidyate nandikeśvara !

ardhavakṣādikānsarvān bhojayet tadanantaram

deveśocitadānena prīyatāṃ me sadāśivaḥ

pūjayedaṣṭa puṣpeṇa bhaktyā taṃ ca visarjayet

samāropya pavitrāṇi praṇamyāgnau śivaṃ yajet

śivāya muktikāmastu kuryātkarmasamarpaṇam

bhuktikāmastu karmedamastu me nātha bandhanaḥ

mantrapūtaṃ hṛdi nyasya visṛjet pāvakaṃ punaḥ

sāpekṣaṃ ca śive yojya kṣamasveti visarjayet

yadyasmiṃścaṇḍa pūjāṃ ca kuryāddatvā pavitrakam

caṇḍeśaṃ sthaṇḍile vāpi pīṭhe vā pūrvavadyajet

tadastu paripūrṇaṃ me caturādhi śivājñayā

alaṃ kūpe'thavā toye tannirmālyaṃ vinikṣipet

viśodhya śuddhadehassan snāpayitvā śivaṃ vrajet

anena vidhinā kuryāllabhate vāṃchitaṃ phalam

sarve'surāstena jātā dānavāśca mahābalāḥ

tāntu nūnamanuprāpyamadbhogāya bhaviṣyati

te yāsyanti paraṃ sthānaṃ damanatvat prasādataḥ

caitramāse tu teṣāṃ te dattaṃ puṇyaphalaṃ mayā

śodhayitvā tamastreṇa pūjayetsaṃhitāṇubhiḥ

īśaprasādājjñāto'si mamārthe sannidhībhava

itthaṃ damanamāmantrya saṃrakṣya bhavataṃ vrajet

mṛtsnā saṃpūrite pātre samāropyātha pūjayet

sāyaṃtane tu kāle vai vidadhyādadhi vāsanam

yathā vidhi samabhyarcya sūryaśaṃkarapāvakān

hṛdaye nātha sadyaṃ ca mantreṇādāya tatpunaḥ

dakṣiṇe bhasma pātraṃ ca rūpeṇa śikhayāthavā

gāyatryā bilamūlena aiśānyāṃ gandhasaṃyutam

vijñāpayecca deveśaṃ damanārohaṇaṃ prati

kartavyaṃ tu yathā lābhaṃ tāmasarvaṃ tavājñayā

saṃyojya mūlamantreṇa japaṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ

pavitrakavidhānena kavacenāvakuṇṭhayet

nirāhāro'thavā cāpi havirbhuñjīta deśikaḥ

prātaḥ snātvā jagannāthaṃ aṣṭapuṣpaṃ visarjayet

pañcāṃgaiḥ pūjayeddevaṃ damanairapi khaṇḍitam

śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ pañcavaktraṃ sadāśivam

ātmavidyā śivaistatvaiḥ mūlādyairīśvarāntike

oṃ hāṃ ātmatatveśvarāya śivāya namaḥ

oṃ hāṃ śivatatveśvarāya śivāya namaḥ

oṃ hauṃ māheśvarāya makhaṃ pūrayaśūlapāṇaye namaḥ

bhaktyā vijñāpayedīśaṃ vāṃchitārtha phalapradam

sarvaṃ tadastu saṃpūrṇaṃ sarvaṃ damanakarmakam

brāhmaṇādīnviśeṣeṇa tuṣṭaye pūjayet tadā

caitramāsa kṛtā pūjā aphalāya bhavettadā

īśvaro'tiprasannobhūddamanasya prasādataḥ

liṃgaṃ bhūtyāśrayaṃ bhūyājjīrṇoddhāraṃ vinābhavet

jīrṇadaṇḍamanirmūlaṃ lakṣaṇena vihīnitam

saṃpuṭaṃ vyaṃgaliṃgaṃ ca nadītīrāntaraṃ punaḥ

pīḍitaṃ caiva vṛṣabhaṃ cāliṃ ca calitaṃ tathā

evaṃ vidhiṃ ca saṃjñāpya nirvāṇaṃ ca bhavedyadi

vidhyuktakarmaṇā tatra saṃsthāpyaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

śāntena sthāpitaṃ liṃgaṃ sahasreṇa tu pālayet

pūjayā rahitaṃ yadvā tatsandhirapi dusthiram

sthāpitaṃ yadi saṃmohālliṃgaṃ nimnāditāṃ vrajet

parityaktāṃ śilāyogaṃ nirvāṇaṃ ca sa lakṣaṇam

vidhinā calitaṃ tattat prāguktaphalasiddhaye

dvāratoraṇa pūjāṃ ca dvārapūjādi pūrvavat

vāstudevaṃ samabhyarcya dikpālānāṃ baliṃ kṣipet

devaṃ saṃsthāpayetpaścālliṃgaṃ duṣṭamidaṃ vibho

tasmādastravidhānena samadhiṣṭaṃ maheśvara !

madhvājya kṣīradūrvā ca mūlenāṣṭottaraṃ śatam

oṃ vyāpakeśvarāyeti nutyantaṃ śivamarcanam

oṃ vyāpakeśvarāya śirase namaḥ

oṃ vyāpakeśvarāya kavacāya hum

oṃ vyāpakeśvarāya astrāya phaṭ

astramantreṇa tantrasthaṃ satvaṃ saṃśrāvayedguruḥ

liṃgaṃ tyaktvā śivājñābhiryatreṣṭaṃ tatra gacchati

pāśupatāstramantreṇa juhuyāttu sahasrakam

kāśaissaṃmārjayetpaścāddatvārthaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

mūrtimūrtīśvarā liṃgapiṇḍikāṃstu viśeṣataḥ

khaṭvāṃgaṃ tu tato nītvā śivamastu gṛṇañjanaiḥ

dikpālatarpaṇaṃ kuryād vāstuśuddhyai śataṃ śatam

liṃgaṃ tanmātratastatra vidhivatsthāpayedguruḥ

jīrṇaṃ vāpyathavā bhagnaṃ dhināpi na cālayet

khaḍgamantragaṇā * * kārayedanya mandiram

taddravyaṃ śreṣṭhadravyaṃ vā tatkāryaṃ tatpramāṇakam

nocedanarthakaṃ tattu vidhinā tatsamācaret

maṇṭapaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kuṇḍaṃ pūrvavatkārayedguruḥ

uttareṣvekatame caiva maṭhādīni prakalpayet

annapānīyasaṃyuktaṃ kevalaṃ vā maṭhaṃ matam

pūrvoktavāstunā ye tu pādāste saṃprakīrtitāḥ

rātrau vātha divā vāpi gṛhadevāla yādiṣu

svagṛhe tvanuśaivaṃ tu mahāyāntaraśailakau

vastrayugmāvṛtaṃ skandhe śivakumbhaṃ dvijaśśivaḥ

ātmatatvamadhobhāge brahmāsāvadhi vinyaset

tadūrdhve śivatatvaṃ syācchuddhavidyāvasānakam

tatvopasthāpanaṃ śuddhiṃ prokṣaṇaṃ sparśanaṃ japam

evaṃ sarvatra vidhivadvidhānaṃ pūrvatāṃ vrajet

mandiraṃ sthāpayetpaścāt phalādyaiḥ śubhavastubhiḥ

śivakumbhaṃ ca saṃsthāpya lepayedgomayena tat

patākā toraṇādīṃśca kuryāttatra viśeṣataḥ

yajamānassulagne tu snātaśca kṛtamaṃgalaḥ

brāhmaṇānāṃ ca dānaṃ ca daivajñāya viśeṣataḥ

pūjayetpūrvataḥ kumbhānvāstu dvikaramaṇṭape

bhedena ca yathā saṃkhyānnivṛttyādikalāṃ nyaset

vya * pānsvasvasaṃjñābhiḥ pūjayed dhyāna pūrvakam

nivṛttyādi kalāścāpi dvārapālānsvamantrakaiḥ

madhurāmrakapitthaiśca bījāpūraphalaistathā

saṃsthāpya snāpayetkumbhajalairupari cālayam

kīrtyarthaṃ kīrtayennāma kartṛnāmapurassaram

evaṃ maṭhapratiṣṭhā ca kāryā vai nandikeśvara

vāstudevaṃ samabhyarcya caturaśre viśeṣataḥ

īśāna pitṛvāyūnāṃ diśamārabhya ca kramāt

āpa * karakoṣṭhasthā marīcyā dvādaśānugāḥ

vāstuṃ yajetpratiṣṭhāyāṃ vāruṇyāṃ maṇṭape śubhe

mūrttyaṃgayuktatāreṇa tannāmnā ca samarcayet

hāṭakeśaṃ baliṃ śāntibalināthamaṭhedayam

pūrvapatrakramādīśe bhrukuṭīścaramaṇṭapaḥ

yajñamaṇṭapabāhye tu pūjayeccarakīmukhān

tatrānantamahāpadmau takṣakaṃ gulikaṃ tathā

kṛtvā pūjāṃ nyasetpaścātpūrvādikramatastataḥ

matsyākṣīṃ ca sabhāṃ caiva sṛṣṭyākhyāṃ cāpi tatra vai

arghyāmbu pañcagavyaṃ ca puṣkariṇyāṃ parikṣipet

huṃ āṃ anantanāgāya nāgādhipataye namaḥ

kṣurikāyāvatāryātha kṣipedvāmena vāriṇā

yajamānāṃstarestasyāṃ guruḥ pāśupataṃ japet

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā rūpiṇīṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

deveśaṃ vāmadevākhyaṃ caruntebhyo'vasādhayet

guravai gauriyaṃ deyā vastrasaṃbhūṣaṇādibhiḥ

vāstudevaṃ samabhyarcya maṇḍalairapi saṃyute

īśādīśāntakoṇeṣu sārdhadvipadanāyakāḥ

āpādyāḥ padayugmaṃ vā bhavatyeva viśeṣataḥ

carakyādyā bahiḥpūjyā skandādyā api pūjyavat

pūrvakādyāśca rudraśca svabhūpadme yajetkramāt

pūrvodita vidhānena raupyanāgāṣṭakāvṛtam

prokṣayedambunā śāntyai yajamānaṃ tato guruḥ

bhojanādi tato dadyādyathā sāmarthyamarthinām

kṣepaṇaṃ bhavanaṃ yacca savyāsavyena saṃmatam

juhuyāt sarpiṣā caiva tilaissamyak samāhitaḥ

snapanaṃ pūjanaṃ nyāsaṃ svena svenāṇunā caret

eteṣāṃ maraṇāntasya vidhirantyeṣṭirucyate

ārdraṃ vaṃśādibhi * * * manopyatra śobhite

svargasopānayukte ca śavamāropayettataḥ

saṃskandhaiśastramantraṃ ca japayuktaiśca dhārayet

dakṣine pretaśirasaṃ vidhāyaiva viśeṣataḥ

sarvālaṃkāra saṃyuktaṃ sarvamālā suśobhitam

ṣaḍaṃgulocchritaṃ vāyau tanmṛdā sthaṇḍilaṃ bhavet

vistīrṇākārayuktāḍhyāṃ aṣṭāṃgulavihīnakam

guruḥ snātaḥ kṛtanyāso gṛhītāstrādyahastakam

apasavyakṛta nyāso hetimantreṇa rakṣitaḥ

tadambu * * * kṣetre pañcaviṃśatikoṣṭhake

pṛthivyādi yathārūpaṃ pañcavarṇairvibhūṣitam

brahmāṇaṃ pañcakoṣṭhe ca vinyasenmadhyakoṣṭhake

agnikoṇe catuṣkepi rudraṃ jvalana saṃyutam

taccatuṣke tathaiśānyāṃ gaganena sadāśivam

svanāmnā ca namontena svāhāntena baliṃ dadet

iti bhūmau ca vinyasya brahmāṇaṃ vidhinā yajet

vāri viṣṇau tu saṃpūjya govindaṃ nandikeśvara

oṃ hāṃ īśvarāyanama iti vāyau ceśaṃ samarcayet

sadāśivetyādi mantraiśca aiśānyāṃ vardhanīṃ nyaset

aṣṭakumbhāni cintyā ca paritaḥ sthāpayedguruḥ

datvaiśvaryādipīṭhaṃ ca sthaṇḍilābjagamadhyagam

vikīryatilabhasmādi ceśaṃ sāṃgaṃ prapūjayet

īśaṃ saṃpūjya hutvāgnau hunetpūrvāvasānikam

astramantreṇa saṃjaptvā raktasūtreṇa veṣṭayet

indhanaiścitimāpūrya sakuśaiḥ kusumaistilaiḥ

pretaṃ mṛdgomayāmbhobhiḥ saṃsnāpyoddhūlya cānayet

athātra dhāraṇāśuddhe gandhapuṣpādipūjite

oṃ huṃ hāṃ hāṃ hāṃ huṃ amukātmane namaḥ

nābhāvadhaśśarīre ca śāntyatītādikāḥ kalāḥ

caitanya sannidhānāya śataṃ mūlena homayet

agnau saṃpūjya saṃyojya śivādyāvāhanādikam

śivātmānaṃ ca tatrasthadīkṣāvatsarvamācaret

niṣkṛtiśca tirodhānaṃ paśoḥ pāśavimocanam

kāraṇāvāhanaṃ tadvad adhikāra samarpaṇam

yojanādbhāvanānītaśive caitanyamavyaye

citāvṛttānamāropya vidadhītasadindhanaiḥ

śiraḥ pradeśe jvalite cottarāsyo hunetpunaḥ

tvamagnerdakṣiṇaḥ kāle kālenaivaṃ śataṃ hutam

anenaiva tu mantreṇa datvā pūrṇābhavāntikam

vardhanīṃ śastrajaptāṃ tu vāmāṃse sanniveśayet

vimuñcannikṣipettatra yata eva samuddhṛtā

snātvā prāstīrya saṃdarbhānapasavyaṃ vidhāya ca

oṃ hāṃ śuddhātman sadāśivo bhava svāhā

pitṛtarpaṇametaistu mantraiḥ kuryātsamāhitaḥ

dviguṇaṃ skandhadātārastato yāsyaniyāśrayam

prathame'hni tṛtīye vā kuryātsaṃcayanaṃ citeḥ

ādāya tatra saṃsthāpya cityā kāṣṭhatrayaṃ dadet

niśśabdaṃ sthāpayettasya vidhinā nandikeśvara

nidhāya vidhinā khātvā sthāpayennandikeśvara !

dīkṣāhīnasya maraṇe śivadīkṣāyutasya ca

śastrairmṛgairgajaiścaiva vāribhirviṣabhakṣaṇaiḥ

sarpadaṃśanakaiścaiva rajjubandhanamāraṇaiḥ

avidyamāna dehānāṃ tathā vikaladehinām

apasmāragrahādīnāṃ darśanānmaraṇādapi

ṣaṭtriṃśadathavā kuryātpratimāṃ tu salakṣaṇām

iṣṭamānena kartavyaṃ tasyāṃ karma yathākramam

kiṃtu sarpirmadhukṣīradhārābhistāṃ ca secayet

saṃsthāpya karavīrotthamukulainyā samācaret

athavā vyoma saṃsthena gomayena ca bhasmanā

bhūrjapatre kṛtaṃ nāma likhitvā hṛdaye nyaset

śuddhāṃ ca piṃgalāṃ cāpi suṣumnāṃ tatra bhāvayet

dharmādharmau ca karmāṇi sarvāṇyapi ca vinyaset

oṃ haṃ hāṃ huṃ anena mantreṇa kṛṣyāṇa vāmāyeyaṃ

etattarpaṇabandhāya svasvanāmnā ca homayet

paraṃ śivaṃ śiro deśe śaktiṃ binduṃ ca vaktrake
