kaṇṭhe sadāśivaṃ vidyādbāhusandhiṣu vinyaset

māyāṃ dakṣiṇapārśve tu kālatatvaṃ ca vinyaset

tatraiva śuddhavidyāṃ ca pu * * gastathocyate

sūkṣmāṇi pṛṣṭhadeśe ca guṇatraya samanvitam

trikassandhiṣu buddhiḥ syānmeḍhre'haṃkārakaṃ nyaset

citte cāgnimadhobhāge varuṇaṃ tatra vinyaset

pañcāṣṭakānāṃ rudrāṇāṃ nyāsaṃ tasmādadhomahi

gulphāte cāpi lokādau brahmaviṣṇupitāmahān

kule tu narakānsarvān vinyasyāṃguṣṭhamūrdhani

paraśśivādikālāgni tatvānyapi ca vinyaset

pūrṇāhutyā śivaskantamātmānaṃ tu paraśśivam

pūrṇāṃ hutvā ca juhuyād agnau saṃpūrṇamānasaḥ

bhadre tripadanakṣatre bhṛgvaṃgāra bṛhaspatau

tatra vai doṣanāśāya punardahanamācaret

lokāgnimudarāgnau ca samāropaṇataḥ purāḥ

khanane'pyapadaṃ cāpi dairdhyaṃ vedakaraṃ bhavet

agnikāryaṃ vinā sarvaṃ karma pūrvavadācaret

khanane dahane caiva daṇḍakaupīna saṃyutam

tanmūrtiṃ vāpi pīṭhaṃ vā bhitticitramathāpi vā

āvāhanādibalyantaṃ dīpāntaṃ vāpi pūrvavat

āśaucānte dine prāpte ekoddiṣṭaṃ vidhīyate

tathaikahetikānāntu kartavyaṃ śrāddhamuttamam

daśāhe dvādaśāhe ca viprakṣatriya śuddhi vai

pitṛvatsarvajātīnāṃ dīkṣitānāṃ ca śuddhi vai

ekoddiṣṭaṃ viśeṣeṇa caikamarghyaṃ pavitrakam

śrāddhapakṣeṣu tithiṣu śrāddhaṃ sarvāsu sādhayet

ekanetraika rudraśca trimūrtiṣviti bhautike

śivottamādyananteśātpitrādi tritayaṃ kramāt

ṣaḍdaivataṃ gṛhasthānāṃ liṃgināṃ tu tridaivatam

śivaśrāddhavihīnaścet piśācatvaṃ sa vindati

ṛkṣāṇi trīṇi caikaṃ ca pretabhūtassa tiṣṭhati

īśassadāśivaśśāntaḥ śivaśrāddhe tapasvinaḥ

nirvāṇa dīkṣitānāṃ tu rudrāṃśānāmatonyathā

skandacaṇḍagaṇādhīśā gaṇāstriṣu yathākramam

sarvatra pañcabhāve'pi puruṣadvayamucyate

parvaśeṣeṣu cāṣṭamyāṃ mātṛpakṣavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ

āhvāyayettatonyasmindine sāyāhnake'pi vā

kṛtanityavidhiśśuddhabhāvo vai * * * * *

dakṣiṇottara saṃsthānaṃ bhasmamātraṃ karāntakam

pitṛdevaṃ dakṣiṇataḥ paścima saṃsthitam

śvetapuṣpaiḥ kramāttatra caturaśra kramādyaśet

nimittārthe tilaissārdhaṃ ruṇa * * * dāpayet

dakṣiṇe vāmadevena pādau prakṣālayenmṛdā

vāmena śuddheṣvāsaneṣu caitānnaivaṃ niveśayet

dravyamarghyāmbunā prokṣya prārthayettānsamāhitaḥ

dvābhyā * * * jāntena savyenāsanayoḥ kuśān

āvāhayedyathānāmnaḥ pañcāpi tadanantaram

sānnidhyārdhyamaghoreṇa sapuṣpāmbu kuśākṣataiḥ

yavaiḥ pādādimūrdhāntaṃ bahurūpeṇa pūjayet

gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ ca vāmena rūpiṇā dhūpadīpakam

anuktamanyacchrāddhāṃgaṃ hṛdayenābhimantrayet

pitṛsthānamasaṃtyaktvā nyubjapātramadho nyaset

kodravaṃ lavaṇaṃ sarvaṃ tathā jambūphalāni ca

bījapūrakaṣāyāṇi marīciṃ pippalīmapi

varjyaṃ ca lohitaṃ sarvaṃ hitvā kuṃkumameva ca

kulasthaṃ pādasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ śuṣkaṃ paryuṣitaṃ ca yat

hastadattāni tu snehāllavaṇavyañjanāni ca

niraṃguṣṭhaṃ ca yacchrāddhaṃ bahirjānu ca yaddhutam

antarjānu viśeṣeṇa tathaivācamanaṃ bhavet

madhye vahni saparyā vai kartavyā nandikeśvara !

oṃ somāya pitṛmate * * * * * * * *

āṃ kavyavāhanāya svadhā namaḥ * * * * *

pāteṣu hutaśeṣānnamādadīta naraṃ japan

aghoreṇa ca śudhyarthaṃ puṃsā dadyācca saṃspṛśat

vāmaṃ mṛtyuñjayaṃ vāpi parito bhrāmayejjapan

vācaṃyamāḥ prabhuñjīraṃsteṣu dhvajamitīritāḥ

bhaktyāthitīṃśca saṃpūjya tatkālenāpi cālayet

tadannaṃ pratinedbhūmau dadyādāpassakṛtsakṛt

ajātena ca saṃdarbhān vāmena vikirettilān

darbhaiḥ parikramāddadyādīśādyairmantrakaiḥ kramāt

pūjayedgandhapuṣpādyaiḥ praṇamennandikeśvara !

pūrvamācamanaṃ tribhyo dvābhyāṃ dadyādanantaram

sarvānpradakṣiṇīkṛtya praṇamya visṛjetpunaḥ

brāhmaṇāyātha vā toye vahnau go vṛṣabhe tathā

tattanmanuṣyāḥ pitaro daityāḥ śrāddhe kṛte yadi

śivaśrāddhe kṛte devāḥ pitaraśca viśeṣataḥ

paścālliṃga pratiṣṭhāṃ vā pratiṣṭhāṃ vāpi paitṛkīm

prāsādagopurādīni pūrvavattatra kārayet

arcanādiviśeṣāstu pūrvavannandikeśvara !

vidyātatvākhyaliṃgaṃ vai māyāśaktiśca pīṭhikā

puruṣottamaṃ tu vāme tu savye brahmāṇameva ca

viṣṇave ca viśeṣeṇa sthitikāraṇameva ca

brahmātalliṃgabudhyā tu jaṭhare tu carācaram

puruṣāghoravāmeśasadyamantrakramāttadā

sṛṣṭau tu prathame devā manuṣya paśupakṣiṇaḥ

brāhmaṇākhyāḥ samākhyātāḥ kavayaścāpyanekaśaḥ

turyāḥ śūdrāśca varṇāḥsyuḥ evaṃ jātāścaturvidhāḥ

ye jātāḥ śirasobhāgaṃ pūrvaṃ bāhudvitīyakam

sṛṣṭirūpaṃ brahmarūpaṃ tasmādbrahmāṃgajātayaḥ

ūrvośca jāto vaiśyaḥ syātpādayoḥ śūdra eva ca

kṣatriyaṃ rakṣaṇārthāya bhūtisaṃrakṣaṇāya ca

dhanarakṣābhimānena vaiśyasaṃjñatvajātikam

navakhaṇḍeṣu varṣeṣu bhārate varṣa eva ca

tasmāttatkalpanāmātraṃ jātibhedamitikramam

adhyayana madhyāpanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā

prāṇināmastra śastreṇa rakṣāṃ kuryāttu sarvadā

nṛpāṇāṃ vṛttirityuktā teṣāṃ mātsaryasaṃbhave

tena vardhanakaṃ kuryād dānārthaṃ dhanakaṃkataḥ

evamādi vibhedena vṛttirvai jagato bhavet

tasmādutkarṣaṇāśīte tvadato nāsti bhedakam

dvijena kanyāyāṃ jātā * * * * * * cyate

atharva proktamārgeṇa śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ bhavet

āyurvedamathavaṃ ca jñātvā gurumukhena tu

senāpatyaṃ camūnāṃ ca kuryādādiva saṃyutam

dvijasya vaiśyakanyāyāṃ jāto'mbaṣṭheti viśrutaḥ

brāhmaṇādi caturṇāṃ tu rogānhastena saṃspṛśet

śalyakriyā ca kartavyā pītakaṃ calanaṃ tathā

kulaśāstroktamārgeṇa bhadrakālīṃ samarcayet

tathā mardakavṛttiśca mudrābhāgyaṃ tathaiva ca

gṛhe gṛhe'pi * * * ṭehi dehīti yācayet

nṛpasya vaiśyakanyāyāṃ jāto māhiṣa ucyate

nṛpasyāntaḥ pure rakṣā * * * * pajīvanaḥ

viprakṣatriyaviṭchūdraśuśrūṣāṃ kārayettadā

vaiśyasya śūdrakanyāyāṃ jātaḥ kāravikaḥ smṛtaḥ

brahmāṇamarcayennityaṃ gṛhamadhye viśeṣataḥ

savarṇādadha utpannāḥ pañcaite cānulomakāḥ

kulasambandhajātatvā nnayogyāḥ paśavastathā

pāpabhedāttu saṃjātaṃ pratilomādyanekakaḥ

vyākhyā vipra syāt tantudhāraṇa mātrakam

bījadravyāṇi sarvāṇi sarjasāraṃ ca gulgulu

anyāni cauṣadhādīni kraya vikraya jīvanam

golakākhyo bhavedevaṃ vṛttiḥ pūrvavadeva hi

taṃ jyotiṣaṃ caiva bhūtatantro'rthaniścayaḥ

jñātvā sarvaṃ ca tenaiva jīvanaṃ nandikeśvara !

śāntastāraṃ daṇḍapālahemakeśyarcanaṃ tathā

nābheruddhūlanaṃ teṣāṃ bhaktiritīritā

vane vyāla mṛgādīnāṃ saṃgrahādupajīvanam

śaṃkhānāṃ vikrayaṃ kuryācchaṃkhānāṃ valayakrayī

vaiśyasya cauryācchūdrāyāṃ jātaḥ kaṭakaro bhavet

śūdrasya cauryācchūdrāyāṃ jāto mālavakaḥ smṛtaḥ

eṣa vai śūdrakuṇḍaḥ syādgolako vṛttirucyate

caturdaśa ca saṃproktā anulomakramāttadā

pratilomaṃ karīpaścācchṛṇutvaṃ nandikeśvara

pūjitaṃ pratilomeṣu sa sūto nāma nāmataḥ

viṣṇutantroktamārgeṇa pūjayecca janārdanam

nṛpasya bhojanārthāya kārayetpācanakriyām

sarveṣāṃ kīrtanaṃ kṛtvā vandanaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

vṛttivastraṃ ca kasyaṃ ca krayavikrayaṇādbhavet

somaṃ caiva surāṃ caiva saṃgṛhya kraya vikrayam

śūdrasya vaiśyakanyāyāṃ jātaḥ sthapati rucyate

ratnakāryāṇi sarvāṇi rugṇādyābharaṇaṃ tathā

pātrāṇi vividhādīni ābhāsaṃ saudhakāryakam

lakṣaṇena kṛtaṃ sarvaṃ teṣāṃ yadupajīvanam

brāhmaṇyāṃ brāhmaṇasyaiva svagotre caiva jāyate

grāmasya kośamātre tu vāsastasya bhavetpunaḥ

śmaśānavastraṃ saṃgṛhya tasyācchādanakaṃ bhavet

gocarmakhaṇḍaiśśayyā ca mahiṣasyājine'pi vā

gadādharo grāmaśuddhiṃ kuryātpretāgrayātrake

caṇḍālavādyaṃ saṃgṛhya bhakṣaṇaṃ ca bhavettataḥ

so'pi caṇḍāla ityuktaḥ tasyācāraḥ puroktavat

pūrvacaṇḍālavṛttiḥ syājjīvitaṃ ca tathaiva ca

spṛsassahavarṇaistu * * * * taṃ ca vādyakam

śūdrasya vaiśyāyāṃ saṃjātaḥ śvakikākhyāta iṣyate

kṣāreṇa lavaṇenaiva vikrayo jīvanaṃ matam

brāhmaṇānkṣatriyājvaiśyānśūdrānvaiśyavisantihi

sātyakarṇaka ityukte vyabhicāravidhānavit

śatrūṇāṃ vaśyakāryaṃ ca mantra tantra kriyādibhiḥ

auṣadhāni ca mantrāṇi gṛhyaṃ * * dīkāṃścaret

viṣṇuṃ saṃpūjayennityaṃ kaṭavṛttistu jīvanam

kṣatriyasyaiva kāryāṇi jīvanaṃ tasya cocyate

tannāma khalla ityuktaṃ corarūpeṇa keśavaḥ

tadaiva kṣatriyoyogāj jāto malla itīritaḥ

tena jīvanamityuktaṃ sa praviśya dine dine

vaiśyāyāṃ tu tato jāto maitrakākhya itīritaḥ

tathaiva vṛṣalīyogāj jāto bhāgavataḥ smṛtaḥ

prātyavargassamākhyātaḥ saṃkarātsaṃkaraṃ śṛṇu

rājñastu kuñjarāṇāṃ tu pālanaṃ vṛttirucyate

ajago mahiṣāṇāṃ tu pālanaṃ jīvanaṃ bhavet

tatkarmakāra ityuktaḥ teṣāṃ nāvikavṛttikā

tantuvāyakriyāṃ kuryāt tena jīvanamucyate

devānāmutsave yajñe vidhinā citrakādikān

sthapatyambaṣṭha saṃyogājjāto vaiṇava ucyate

vaiṇava sthapatīyogād āpiṇḍika itīritaḥ

bāhyarakṣā tu kartavyā tenaivāsya tu jīvanam

ārdravaṃ kartṛvidhinā kṛtvā matkrayavikrayam

māṃsavikrayaṇaṃ kuryāt tena jīvanamucyate

tasya nāvikavṛttiḥ syādyāvadājīvameva tu

ghaṇṭātāḍanakaṃ kuryāt prātareva mahādhvanim

ambaṣṭhasya tu viprāyāṃ jāto nāvika ucyate

ambaṣṭhasya nṛpāyāṃ tu jāto nāvika ucyate

viprāyāṃ mahiṣasyaiva jāto vaiṇava ucyate

brāhmaṇādi nṛpāṇāṃ tu vāsanaṃ śayanādikam

nṛpāyāṃ cūcukasyaiva jāto vaivatsyabandhakaḥ

vaidehakasya viprāyāṃ jātako rajakaḥ smṛtaḥ

āyogavasya viprāyāṃ jātastāmraka ucyate

nṛpāyāṃ yogakasyaiva janito bandhakaḥ smṛtaḥ

vṛṣalyo mamathasyaiva jātaḥ kukkuṭa ucyate

ambaṣṭhāyāṃ videhasya jāto baila itīritaḥ

indrajālādilīlāśvakuryāttenopajīvanam

vane vyālamṛgādīni kandamūlānyanekaśaḥ

caṇḍālasya tu viprāyāṃ jātaḥ syapava ucyate

pūrvāhne grāmamāsādya aparāhne niveśanam

carmaṇā pādarakṣādi kṛtvā tatkraya vikrayam

sūcinābandhayedvastrānyathā yuktopajīvanam

naṣṭadravyāṇi sarvāṇi yatnātsaṃgṛhyate tataḥ

jātibhedamidaṃ proktaṃ kramāttannandikeśvara !

uttamānāṃ ca madhyāstu pañcapañcādhimāmanuḥ

catustriṃśacca ye martyāḥ kulasambandhinastathā

hīnavṛttiyutāneva dīkṣāyāṃ varjayedguruḥ

tripuṇṭrāṃ kitadehāścarudrākṣamaṇidhāriṇaḥ

liṃginaṃ ca sadā bhaktyā dūrataḥ praṇamādikāḥ

yathocitaṃ tathā kuryāttena svargamavāpnuyāt

dīkṣito vidhinoliṃgī naiṣṭhiko'pi viśeṣataḥ

bhautikasya viśeṣeṇa lokācāra samanvayāt

jananī prādurbhāva iti tadbhede jātibhedakaḥ

evaṃ caturvidhaṃ proktaṃ jātibhedaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

tṛṇagulmalatādyāśca udbhidaśca prakīrtitāḥ

striyādyāśca pipīlyādyā svedajāśca prakīrtitāḥ

nakrā grāhāśca te sarve cāṇḍajāśceti kīrtitāḥ

jarāyujāśca mūlaṃ vai cauṣadhyā jāyate bhṛśam

vāyunā vyūhamānāsā vyomni jīvatvamāgatā

ucchranātārdradurbhaṃ ca mūlabhāgamiyātkramāt

parṇanālaṃ tataḥ kāṇḍaṃ kāṇḍokta prasavaṃ punaḥ

taṇḍulānāṃ tu pākāttu triyantyauṣadhajātayaḥ

phalapākāvasānāntāstathā cauṣadhijātayaḥ

* * * * * saṃyogād aneka svādutāṃ gatim

madhurāmre ca lavaṇaṃ tiktaṃ kaṭukaṣāyakam

āsādyāmāśayaṃ prāpya prāṇādi sthāpitaṃ kramāt

annāṃśaṃ bhujalāṃśantu toyāso vahnirūrjitaḥ

agninātvārthyatetoyaṃ tenānnasyāpi pācanam

kiṭṭaṃ dvādaśamārgeṇa vahniraṃgātpravartate

malāśayāḥ kaphasvedau viṇmūtrā dvādaśa smṛtāḥ

prāṇavāyuśca nāḍīnāṃ mukhe sūkṣmarasaṃ kṣipet

nāḍīnāṃ madhyame prāṇaśarīraḥ śleṣmaṇo rasaḥ

rasatastvakca rudhiraṃ māṃsaṃ raktāttu jāyate

majjā tu jāyate paścācchuklo'pyata tridhā bhavet

prājāpatya * * * nai ṛtukāle viśeṣataḥ

śuktānnarāntastho jīvaḥ karaṇasaṃyutaḥ

ekāhāmiśritaṃ tattu kalākhyaṃgaṃ ca gacchati

saptarātrovimādbhūtvā budbudātpeśikāṃ vrajet

paṃcaviṃśadinātpaścād bījāṃkurasamudbhavaḥ

śiro grīvā ca skandhaśca pṛṣṭhavaṃśastathoparam

māsadvayātkrameṇaiva bhavatyevāṃganiṣkṛtiḥ

aṃgulyaśca caturmāsājjaṭharāṇā * * śakam

guhyaṃ ca dantapaṃktiśca jāyate ca na khāṃkurāḥ

pāyurmeḍhramupasthaṃ ca nābhirvaṃśāsthireva ca

aṃgapratyaṃga saṃpūrṇaśirobhāga samanvitaḥ

navame māsi saṃprāpte saṃpūrṇajñāna vāruṇaḥ

nāntarālena saṃvyāptapoṣajā yāti nityayaḥ

jaraścābhaṃ mṛtaścābhaḥ janmajanma punaḥ punaḥ

ekākī tena dahyehaṃ gatāste phalabhoginaḥ

aśubhakṣayakartāraṃparaṃ mukti pradāyakam

yena yena prakāreṇa garbhavāso na jāyate

garbhasthaścintayannityaṃ yadi vā nirgato bhavet

mokṣaṃ vicintayañjīvī karmapāśānugaḥ

jīvopi tadvanmahatāveṣṭitaśca jarāyuṇā

garbhādikenacchannāṃgavyākulaḥ pumān

athāpya praścite garbhaḥ kumbhe tu jaṭharāgninā

duḥkhaṃ yajjāyate tasya tadgarbheṣu guṇaṃ bhavet

agnighoraṃ mahāduḥkhaṃ bahulaṃ dehināmidam

garbhātkoṭiguṇaṃ duḥkhaṃ yoniyantra prapīḍanāt

tilaṣatpīḍyamāṇasya yantreṇaiva samantataḥ

mahadduḥkhaṃ bhavatyeva jāyamānasya tasya vai

tadvannissāramāpanno vivaśo'dhaḥ patatyasau

raktamāṃsamṛdā liptaṃ viṇmūtradravyabhājanam

vadanaika mahādvāraṃ gavākṣāṣṭakabhūṣitam

sirāsveda pravāhaṃ ca śleṣma padmapariplutam

kāmakrodhendhanākrāntaṃ vyasanaiścāpi marditam

saṃkaṭenāviviktena yonimārgeṇa nirgatam

pradhānāsthīni vai trāṇi śatānyadhikamaṣṭa vai

dvisaptati sahasrāṇāṃ nāḍībhirabhito vṛtam

medaḥ prasthadvayaṃ proktaṃ dehināṃ nandikeśvara !

iti dehaṃ * * * * hyeva syānnityamātmanaḥ

śulaśoṇitasaṃyogād deha utpadyate yataḥ

śodhyamāno'pi dehoyaṃ aśuciḥ syādvi niścayaḥ

aśuddhitvaṃ kṣaṇādyāti kimanyadaśucirbhavet

sravanti pūti gandhāni kimanyadaśucirbhavet

candanāgarukarpūrakuṃkumādyanulepitam

sarvadā śodhyamānopi śuddhiṃ naivādhi gacchati

nānyadvirāgahetuḥ syāccharīrādiha vidyate

bhāvaśuddhirviśuddhirvai śuddhaye kathitaṃ mayā

gaṃgātīrthaiśca mṛdbhāraimṛjyate parvatopamaiḥ

śuddhatīrtha sahasraistu snāto bhāvavihīnakaḥ

duṣṭātmāvahni yoge'pi na śuddhaḥ kutracidbhavet

śaucaṃ paraṃ bhāvaśuddhipramāṇaṃ sarvakarmasu

mānavā bhidyate buddhirabhinneṣveva vastuṣu

patistadeva dhyāyanvai kāmārdra hṛdayo bhavet

abhāvena paraṃ tasmādbhāvassarvasya hetuka

dāhaśuddhyā ca sarvaṃ ca labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ

avinārāgaviṇmūtralepagandhaviśośadhanam

tvaṅmātra sāranissāraṃ rambhāstambhanibhaṃ param

saṃsārasāgaraṃ ghoraṃ karotyeva na saṃśayaḥ

ajñātaśeṣāṃ puṃsāṃ tu nānākarmavaśātpunaḥ

saṃkṣayadyoni mārgeṇa nirgacchannavaśo bhavet

smṛtibhraṃśaśca jāyeta pūrvakarmavaśāttadā

lobhena rakto mūḍhaśca svamātmānaṃ na vi

netrayukto'pi sadvastu nekṣate ca kadācana

saṃsāre kliśyate lobha mohakrodha vaśānugaḥ

vaktuṃ kartuṃ na śaknoti svececchannapi satkriyām

bālye tu vahumiścaiva bālarogairviśeṣataḥ

mohānmūtrapurīṣādīn bhakṣayedbālyabhāvataḥ

akṣarādhyayanādyaiśca duḥkhaṃ syādguruśāsanāt

īrṣyayā ca mahadduḥkhaṃ mohāttasyaiva jāyate

nidrāṃ na vindate rātrau kāmāgni paritāpitaḥ

yoṣitsaktasya dehasya puṃso ye śukla bindavaḥ

kaṇḍūyanāgnitāpena yadbhavetstiṣu tadviṣu

tādṛśaṃ saukhyamevaiti nānyastrīṣu na vidyate

tadvatstrīṣvapi tadviddhi na saukhyaṃ paramārthataḥ

kāntāsutadvajjñātavyaṃ anyaiḥ kalpitamanyathā

sukhaṃ nāsti vicārosti yadi tasya viśeṣataḥ

jarayā yauvanaṃ kaṣṭaṃ kvasaukhyamanupadravam

anyonyaṃ yauvane prativardhanaṃ strīnaraṃ prati

jarayā śithilībhūtaṃ svamātmānaṃ nirīkṣayet

putramitrakalatrādibandhubhirjarayā vṛtaḥ

na jīryetātha dharmārtha kāmamokṣaphalaṃ punaḥ

vyādhīnāmākaraṃ dehaṃ vātapittakaphātmakam

śarīresminmṛtyuśataṃ śatamekena saṃyutam

āgantavo bhaiṣajādyaiḥ śāntiṃ gacchanti netare

vividhā mṛtayaścāpi śastrasarpaviṣāṇibhiḥ

kālamṛtyuvaśaṃ jantuṃ na kaścidapi rakṣati

rasāyanavarairyogaiḥ kālaṃ daivatvamāpnuyāt

nāsti mṛtyusamaṃ duḥkhaṃ nāsti mṛtyusamaṃ bhayam

satputramitrabhāryādirājyaiśvaryādikānapi

pra * * * * * * dhya pyaśaniṣaṣṭireva vā

madhye rātriṃ mṛtistulyā sarveṣāṃ prāṇināmayam

vārdhakye jarayā grasto madhye vyādhiśatairapi

dehajaiśśatrubhissarvaiḥ sarvadā bādhyate naraḥ

* * sārdhaiśca madhyehni tṛptau kāmena nidrayā

vyādhyādi bādhayo ghoraṃ maraṇaṃ ca sa gacchati

anyonya mātṛpitṛtva pitṛtvajñātiśabdakaiḥ

karmaṇāṃ sāhacaryācca ṛṇamātrādi saṃbhavaḥ

dharmādyayepi yogaṃ ca dehena ca samāpnuyāt

mṛtyuduḥkhaṃ ca yadbhūtaṃ tadvacchataguṇādhikam

sarpāsyagatamaṇḍūka iva krandanmuhurmuhuḥ

sevito'pi mukhenaiva śuṣyetāti samucchvasan

* * * * * * * * nāgajālena paḍyate

śuṣkandhapipa toyāvamānasya mṛtaḥ kasya bhaviṣyati

mūtraviṣṭhānuliptāṃgo mriyate mṛtyunā punaḥ

manasvinaḥ prārthanāṃ tu maraṇādadhikaṃ bhavet

nityavyādhistu kṣudvyādhi * * * * * midam

takṣavṛ vidhiratyanta balasaṃhārakāraṇam

vyādhyauṣadhamidaṃ jñeyaṃ paramārthena paṇḍitaiḥ

saukhyaṃ tatra na vidyeta tamasācchāditātmanaḥ

gajāśvasevā vāṇijya gorakṣaṇapariśramaiḥ

* * rthedannarogādikaṣṭaṃ tat kiṃkaromyaham

kaṇṭhādibhirgṛhaṃ bhagnaṃ pārthivādiśatairnṛpaiḥ

kanyānāṃ kīdṛśāṃ caiva parācintābhibhūtavān

tasya jñānaṃ śrutiśśīlaṃ guṇāśca vinayantathā

pārthivatve kutassaukhyaṃ kīdṛśaṃ tatsukhaṃ bhavet

tatraikārthābhilāṣitvaṃ śatrutvamiti kīrtitam

savaira ṣyo nṛpatiḥ na kaścitsāmagītale

meghāḥ prabhañjanenaiva vāyuvīryaṃ ca parvataiḥ

āditya pādairambhāṃsi śoṣaṃ gacchanti nityaśaḥ

viriñco'pya paraissārdhaṃ parārdhadvayakālataḥ

sarvātiśayavīryaṃ ca nāsti saṃsāramaṇḍale

evaṃ viditvā sarvaṃ vai cā vijñānaṃ vivarjayet

sarvavitkopi nāstyeva muktordhvāpi na kutracit

sarvatra sarvadā nāsti prabhāva sadṛśaḥ punaḥ

asurainirjitā devā asurā amarairjitāḥ

prasthamātrānnasaṃtṛpto vasanadvaya saṃyutaḥ

saptabhūmayute gehe khaṭvāyāmekadeśake

rājñotimātramānaṃ vai keśāyābharaṇaṃ bhavet

gītaṃ sarvapralapitaṃ nṛttamunmatta ceṣṭitam

pārthivānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ anyonyavijigīṣayā

saukhyaṃ kutopi hi svarge vilokyātiśayastriyam

kṣayātpuṇyaphalānāṃ tu narāṇāṃ patanaṃ bhavet

chinnamūlastatkuryāttadvadavaśyaṃ patati kṣitau

svasvānubhava madhye ca puṇyakarmaphalakṣayāt

vāṅmanaḥ kāyikaiścāpi ghoraiśca vividhairapi

kuṭhārādyaiśca śastraiśca kledanaistāḍanairapi

bhedanaṃ mardanaṃ caiva karibhiścānyadehibhiḥ

bahukṣātraṃ ca sarpāṇāṃ * * * khañcadāruṇam

kīṭādīnāṃ ca maraṇamakasmāttu dine dine

śvāpadānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ varṣaśītātapādbhayam

svakīya yūthaviraho navānayanabandhanam

ityevamādibhirduḥkhai vyāptaṃ yasmāccarācaram

skandhāttatskandhayedbhāraṃ viśramangacchate yathā

nānāyonisahasraistu puṇyakarmavaśādbhavet

śiraśchinnasya vajrasya punassandhitameva ca

mārtāṇḍabhānoḥ kuṣṭhitvaṃ jalarogaṃ pracetasaḥ

bhagasyandi kṣayarogī kṣayarogitvameva ca

brahmaṇaśca śiraśchedaḥ sarveṣāmapi vidyate

parārdhadvayakālānte brahmaṇo bhavet

krodhenā jayā devī yogasthāpyaśapatprabhuḥ

duḥkhāni ca samastāni sthitānyeva na saṃśayaḥ

nirvedādglānimāpanno vairāgyaṃ ca samāpnuyāt

duḥkhādvijanmanaḥ puṃsāṃ tallavānmokṣameva ca

caturdaśa ca janma syād devatvāccāṣṭadhā yutaḥ

sthāvaraṃ caivameteṣu caturdaśasu tatra vai

paiśācaṃ rākṣasaṃ hitvā devayoniṣu jāyate

īṣatpuṇyaiśca vā pāpaiḥ svamāyātiśayena ca

rāgadveṣairmṛgatvaṃ ca jāyate nandikeśvara

vai gākāḥsañjātāḥ sarpādikṛmikīṭakāḥ

eteṣāṃ sarvajātīnāṃ dharmādharmādayordhvataḥ

paśvādīnāṃ ca janmatvaṃ saṃprāptamṛtimānnaraḥ

pṛthivīgandhatanmātrā na samāpadyate tale

rasagandhasvarūpāṇi sparśaṃ māruti gacchati

pañcatanmātrakāścāśu manastatvaṃ prapadyate

saptaitāni tvahaṃkāre vyāpnotītyaṣṭakāni vai

asya dehasya madhye ca puruṣaḥ śabdatatvayuk

śarīraṃ sukṛtaiścaiva duṣkṛtaiśca tathaiva ca

evaṃ maraṇamākhyātamutpattistadanantaram

ākāśādvāyurāpanno vāyorvahniḥ sṛjatyasau

udbhidānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ svedajānāṃ tathaiva ca

jarāyujaścāṇḍajaścopi vauṣaḍjānāti māpnuyāt

rasācchroṇitamutpannaṃ śoṇitānmāṃsa saṃbhavaḥ

snāyvāṃ majjā tu saṃjātā tanmajjācchukla saṃbhavaḥ

śuklena sārdhaṃ mātuśca yonau raktaṃ patatyasau

punaśca janma saṃprāpya punaḥ pañcatvamṛcchati

kālaṃ jñātvā tribhedaistu mṛtyuṃ jayati sarvataḥ

āyurādau parīkṣeta tato vijñānamāpnuyāt

tasmātsarvaprayatnena jñātvā kālaṃ hi yogavit

kāle vāyussamākhyātaḥ kālaḥ sarvajña ucyate

sarvadevamayaḥ kālo jñātvā kālaṃ samabhyaset

cararāśaya ityuktā vāmanāsāpuṭe caret

dakṣiṇe nāsikādvāre caretsamyak sthirākhyakāḥ

asvabhāva svabhāvatvādubhayatrāpi vai caret

ahanyahani cābhyāsānmṛtyuṃ jayati paṇḍitaḥ

iḍācandrakalājñeyā piṃgalā sūryasaṃbhavā

ghaṭikāḥ pañca pañcaiva vāmadakṣiṇameva vā

suṣumnā vahate miśraṃ nāḍyāṃ caiva pṛthak pṛthak

ṣaṣṭighaṭikamahorātraṃ jīvasaṃkhyāṃ karoti yaḥ

ahorātreṇa cāreṇa śvāsacāraṇameva tu

tadvajjihvā nirālambe nāsāgre dvādaśāṃgule

caturaṃgulahāniḥ syāttadāyurhānirucyate

prāṇāyāma vihīnaścen maraṇaṃ tu vikāraṇāt

ayanadvayakāle tu prātaḥkāle parīkṣayet

tataḥ savyāpasavyena yāvadastamito raviḥ

pakṣamityeva nāḍīnāṃ kramādastaṃ śṛṇuṣva tat

triṃśattu vedasaṃkhyāvai pañcāśaddve ca ṣaṣṭikam

dinārdhaṃ ca dinaṃ caiva dvidinaṃ tridinaṃ tathā

bhānusaṃkhyā daśābdaṃsyādaṣṭamaṃ parikīrtitam

dīpanaṃ ca mahāgī syād daśāhne vartitaṃ yadi

ṣaḍviṃśadinalopaṃsyāsaptaviṃśaddine punaḥ

triṃśadekatriṃśattriṃśaddinaistathaiva ca

catuṣṭayaṃ ca tridinaṃ dvidinaṃ dinamātrakam

pādhvavahnirataścātho daṇḍaspṛkacetharā bhavet

kumbhāho tu catuṣṭisyāllalāṭe cāñjalirbhavet

pratipadādi saṃgamya pūrvapakṣoparisthitā

chāyā na viṣṭhā tatraiva maraṇaṃ bhavati dhruvam

trimāsānmaraṇaṃ gacchenniścayaṃ nandikeśvara

iṣṭasaṃkramakāle tu sūryabimbanirīkṣaṇāt

kālamanākulena manasā yogīśvaro lokayet

patitaścetsaptadinaṃ śūnyātsaptadinaṃ bhavet

anekabhedamāsānāṃ nakṣatra patanādyaham

diddhūmāstekamāsaṃ ca maṇḍalāniṃ ca caikataḥ

pratyādityaprabhāśca pratyādityadvayaṃ tathā

bhūmidhūmāddahāhaṃ ca bhūmighoṣaistrayaṃ tathā

śironūnādekavarṣaṃ sarvanyūnā tadardhataḥ

kubje naraścaturviṃśat śironāsā tadardhakam

dīpadhūmātsapañcamye sahadīpārdhapakṣataḥ

ardhaṃ ca navamaṃ caiva mṛtyupā * śrayaṃ tathā

tastrasthānehnimīśāśca catvāri maraṇaṃ bhavet

galasthānamima * * * dinairmaraṇaṃ bhavet

śrotrapāśve tu kṛṣṇaścet pañcāśaddivasānmṛtiḥ

dante kṛṣṇaṃ ca dṛśyeta māsānmṛtyumavāpnuyāt

tanmūlaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ syādaṣṭaviṃśatyahānmṛtiḥ

kadācitkaṇḍūya * * budbudākāra śartanāt

triṃśaddināttu maraṇaṃ kathitaṃ nandikeśvara

padamadhye ca khinnaṃ ce * * * mṛtyumāpnuyāt

pāde viṣaṃ ca kaṇḍūścet ṣaḍvārānmaraṇaṃ bhavet

mukhaṃ śobhāvihīnaṃ cet * * * mṛtimṛcchati

rekhādivarṣametaddhi dṛṣṭikarṇadviviṃśatiḥ

etatkālamahājñānaṃ śrutvaitadu * * * kaḥ

śabdastoyanidhau bhīmamapiśvāsasya nedhanaḥ

pañcāhe divase'hni jīvaharaṇaṃ vidyā * taiḥ

nalinīpadmasaṃhatyāḥ sūkṣmasūcyabhivedhane

lavaistruṭiḥ syāttriṃśadbhiḥ kala * vatthitaṃ viduḥ

triṃśatkalāmuhūrtaṃ syādityevaṃ cāpi pañcamam

prāyo vināśa ityuktaścittaṃ sāndhāryate tu yat

prātaḥ kāle tu samaye śaucamācamanaṃ tathā

samāhitamanā bhūtvā śraddhābhakti samanvitaḥ

samyak pratyagrabuddhistu prāyaścittaṃ samācaret

vastrāṇāṃ śukla viṇmūtradigdhānāṃ mṛjjalairapi

lepagandhau ca na syātāṃ ktoyena śodhayet

āvikakṣaumakauśeyaṃ saṃśuddhaṃ gaurasarṣapaiḥ

ariṣṭaiḥ kuntapānāntaiḥ celavaccarmaṇāmapi

pūrveṣāṃ vaḥ kalādīnāṃ śodhanaṃ ca bhavettadā

govālagharṣaṇaṃ cāpi viśeṣātparikīrtitam

kumbhasthena jalenaiva śuddhiḥ syānnandikeśvara !

māṇikyaṃ mauktikaṃ śaṃkhaṃ prabālaṃ mauktikaṃ tathā

sūtipātre pikaṃ spṛṣṭe viṣṭhāmūtrādidūṣite

tṛṇapāṣāṇa kāṣṭhānāṃ valālandalavalliṣu

ulūkhale ca musale bhārave sṛksṛvādike

tadāvedāntave cāpi tatkṣaṇācchodhanaṃ bhavet

dravyāṇāṃ sahitānāṃ tu vrīhīṇāṃ prokṣaṇācchuciḥ

kṛmikīṭapataṃgānāṃ sparśanānnandikeśvara !

śvavarāhakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ spṛṣṭo vai vidhvanāhake

makṣikākeśasaṃmiśre tuṣāṃgārakasaṃyute

tatspṛṣṭadeśamutkṛtya pūrvavacchoṣayejjale

gośakṛnmūtravalmīkamṛttikābhasmavāribhiḥ

adhyāpanādikāle vā vane coktamanāḥ śuciḥ

kutsita dravya saṃyogācchuddhiratraiva cocyate

ālaye sphuṭite bhagne piṇḍikā calite'pi vā

bhittau citre paṭecchinne bhinne vaṃśatṛṇāvṛte

nadīpravāhabhagne vā bhagne vā * * * * *

saṃspṛṣṭe kukkuṭādīnāṃ babhrūṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

kharoṣṭrakolamārjārasparśanaṃ tvaṃganandibhiḥ

vrātyaiśca pretakaiścaivarogajālayutairapi

nityapūjāvihīne ca āvṛtārcā vivarjite

puṣpagandhavihīne ca dhūpapādyaiśca varjite

kālātīte ca mantrāṇāṃ vyatyaye ca vyatikrame

pavitrāropaṇe hīne damanāropaṇe tathā

ātmārthaṃ yajane hīne tasya bhedavihīnake

mahāpātaka saṃyukte pañcapātakasaṃbhave

mantrāṇāṃ saṃkare caiva cātyaiṣṭyādi vivarjite

sphuṭite bhinne prāsāde bhavet kartṛvināśanam

mantranyāsādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvavatpūjayedguruḥ

susthitaṃ pūrvavatkuryāt sandhayedaṣṭabandhanaiḥ

pīṭhe tu bhagne sphuṭite kārayedakṣataṃ punaḥ

bhagne ca sphuṭite liṃge rāṣṭra nāśo na saṃśayaḥ

liṃgaṃ tu tavakaṃ kṛtvā pratiṣṭhāṃ samyagācaret

pūrvavatsnapanaṃ kuryāt pañcagavyena deśikaḥ

saṃprokṣaṇādi sarvaṃ ca pūrvavannandikeśvara !

yajamānasya nāśaḥ syād durbhikṣaṃ ca bhavettataḥ

liṃgārcanāntarasyāntaṃ yatnena ca parigrahet

aṣṭabandhanahīne tu anāvṛṣṭirna saṃśayaḥ

prāsādaliṃgapratimā * * carūpagādiṣu

pūrvavatsnapanaṃ kuryālliṃgādiṣu viśeṣataḥ

citraṃ rūpavihīnaṃ cet punaścitraṃ samālikhet

caṇḍālādyaistu saṃspṛṣṭe vairibhirbhayamucyate

śatamāvartayedāśu mantraṃ pāśupataṃ guruḥ

ekasandhivihīnena dviguṇaṃ cārcayetpunaḥ

sandhitrayavihīne ca pañcagavyena secayet

pañcāṃhe snapanaṃ kṛtvā brahmāṃgairhomamācaret

māsaikaṃ pūjanāhīnaṃ yadi gavyābhiṣecanam

pañcabrahma ṣaḍaṃgaiśca juhuyānnandikeśvara

āvṛtārcanahīne syāt bhṛtyavargasya nāśanam

balidānavihīne tu na prītāḥ sarvadevatāḥ

snānato yavihīnaṃ ced anāvṛṣṭirbhavetkṣaṇāt

gandhadravyavihīne tu bālānāṃ nāśanaṃ bhavet

pādyādi dravyahīne tu mahāmārī pravartate

sandhyādīpavihīne tu dṛṣṭirogaśca jāyate

havirhīne tu durbhikṣaṃ dviguṇaṃ tattu dāpayet

saṃdhyātikramayuktaṃ ced vairibhirbhayamāviśet

snapanāṃgavihīne tu mahāvātaśca jāyate

adīkṣitānāṃ saṃsparśa liṃgaṃ vā pratimāṃ tu vā

pavitrāropaṇe hīne paracakrādbhayaṃ bhavet

uktamāse tu tadṛkṣe cotsavaṃ na kṛtaṃ yadi

parārthamevaṃ yuktaṃ tad ātmārthe śuddhirucyate

rājacorabhayādau ca dehe vyādhyādi bādhite

viraktasya gṛhasthasya prāyaścittaṃ dvidhā bhavet

āṣoḍaśāntaṃ bālaḥ syād aśītyādiśatāntakam

rogārtāśca striyaścāpi prāyaścittārdhayogyakāḥ

svayaṃ kartumaśaktaśced anyena dviguṇaṃ bhavet

puṇyakṣetrasya deśaṃ syāt gurudīkṣārṇadeśakāḥ

rāṣṭradoṣabhayaṃ caiva riktavyādhyādireva ca

deśikastu vicāryaiva kārayettadanantaram

gurubhiḥ kalpitaṃ yattu tadvatiścātra doṣakṛt

candroparāgaviṣuvapuṇyakālāyaneṣu ca

anyakāle gṛhasthasya rātryarcā doṣamāvahet

rātrau pūjādinā caiva naiṣṭhikasyāpi sarvadā

dvisandhyālopake caiva triśataṃ japamucyate

ekakālārcane hīne japedghorasahasrakam

ekāhamevaṃ saṃjapya dvitīye vāsare'pi ca

evaṃ tu vardhayeddhīmān dinaṃ prati dinaṃ prati

vardhayetpratimāsaṃ tu dviśatottaravṛddhitaḥ

sarpamatsyādibhiścaiva grahe naṣṭe tathaiva ca

ekāhamupavāsaṃ ca paścānnaktāśanī bhavet

guruṇādattaṃ liṃgaṃ ca jyeṣṭhenāpi tathaiva ca

evaṃ ca piṇḍikānāṃ tu pratiṣṭhāpyātha pūjayet

lākṣānbandhucyate śuddhistrīcaturluṭhite yutāt

ayutaṃ madapāte tu cyutamānamanantaram

acchinne ca nirādhāre dviguṇaṃ bahurūpakam

kāmāttu vedhane cāpi prāyaścittaṃ tato nahi

mahāpāpavināśe tu bahurūpaṃ praśasyate

na tasya prāyaścittaṃ vai mahāpātakinastu vai

aghoradvyayutaṃ caiva tryayutaṃ ca japetpunaḥ

bāhyasparśe tu sāhasraṃ pratilome tu lakṣakam

kapālairbharannaiścaiva paramārgasamanvitaiḥ

spṛṣṭe pāśupataiścāpi catuśśatamatho japet

lakṣaṃ tu bahurūpeṇa japaṃ kuryādviśuddhaye

rājādibhiścatuṣpādairghoraiścaivādivātame

anyaliṃgaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya pūjayennandikeśvara

nirvāṇamupagaccheta ekarātramupoṣitaḥ

trirātraṃ daśasāhasraṃ japācchuddhiśca jāyate

viśuddhiṃ tena cāpnoti snānahīnatāṃ japet

balidānavihīne'pi mūlamaṣṭaśataṃ japet

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japyaṃ gāyatrīṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

catuguṇaṃ bhavettantu prāyaścittādviśudhyati

aghoramayutadvandvaṃ japettasya viśuddhaye

taddine sandhihīne tu cāṣṭakṛtvo japaṃ bhavet

aghoraṃ laghuyugmaṃ ca japtvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ japet

devasvaharaṇe yukto gurudravyavināśanaḥ

kṛtaghno jyeṣṭhabādhā vā daśaite'tyantapāpinaḥ

śivanindāratasyaiva cāndrāyaṇayugaṃ caret

gurunindārato vāpi gurvanugraha pūrvataḥ

śivajñānasya nindāyāṃ pūrvavannandikeśvara

cāndrāyaṇajapādīni pūrvavatkārayetkramāt

cāndrāyaṇadvayaṃ kuryāt pūrvavajjapamucyate

surāpasya viśuddhistu kālāntena na vidyate

tena śuddhistu vihitā guṇatattara vidyate

ācaredyena śuddhiḥ syāt pramādādyadi tasya tu

gurutalpagatasyāpi guroragre priye rataḥ

ete vai pañcakāste'pi mahāpāpasamanvitāḥ

tālapatrayuto gacched dakṣiṇāṃ kakubhaṃ prati

purastāddeśikasyāpi tathaivāpriyavādakam

athavānugrahāttasya gurośśuddhirvidhīyate

enamāmaraṇātkuryāt tena tasyāpi śuddhibhāk

maunī dehaṃ tyajetpaścāt tena śuddhiśca jāyate

śivanindāsamaṃ jñeyamaghoraṃ lakṣakaṃ japet

tadvāṇava māyotthatatharmādyaśupāsanāt

śivānubhūtādhyayanādānandācchuddhimāpnuyāt

sumahatpātukādyante copapātā va sānikam

agre'pi cogratapasā cālpe cālpe ca vaibhavam

atikṛcchraṃ tṛtīyaṃ syātparā kaścitsvarūpakam

trirātramekabhuktistu tridinaṃ vopavāsanam

uṣṇāmbu tridinaṃ pītvā kṣīrabhojī jitendriyaḥ

tapta kṛcchra iti khyātamatikṛcchramathocyate

atikṛcchrassamākhyātaḥ parākastviha kathyate

māsārdhaṃ grāsavṛddhiḥ syācchukle kṛṣṇe'vabhāsayet

māsārdhaṃ grāsamāsesyādhrāsaṃśukle ca vardhayet

sāyamādyantayorahvaḥ sāyaṃ prātaśca madhyame

madhyamastvitayaṃ vātha tadardhaṃ nādameva vā

vrataṃ vai brahmacaryādi bhojanaṃ tatra kathyate

śakaṃ yāvakapiṇyākaṃ kandamūlapalāśanam

yasminvidhiniṣedhovā nāsti karmaṇyanuṣṭhite

prāyaścitte hutādīnāṃ śāntiṃ sadyassamācaret

sadyaḥ kṛtaṃ ratnagarbhaprāyaścittaṃ na śasyate

māsāvasānakaṃ yattu hīnaṃ tathaiścara

ātmārthe ca guṇaṃ proktaṃ prāyaścittavidhau tadā

svāhānte bhagavat kṣobhavaṃśapadaṃ punaḥ

paścādanantaraṃ pāpī śataṃmantrāntaraṃ tu vā

pūrṇāhutiṃ ca juhuyād vidhinā nandikeśvara

pakṣatrisaptanakṣatre avicchinnā yatastataḥ

ātmārthaliṃgamanyena pūjitaṃ vā svayaṃ punaḥ

naiṣṭhikopi haridrāṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ yadi sevate

saptāhādūrdhvamevaṃ syātkṛcchramekaṃ caretsudhīḥ

aghoraṃ tu sahasraṃ syāddinaṃ pratidinaṃ prati

śivadrohe gurudrohe jñāninindādini vrate

aṣṭāviṃśati koṭīnāṃ narakepyatidāruṇe

sumahatpātaṃśakṛthame tatkālārdhaṃ tu pīḍyate

tatsame tu tadardhaṃ syānniraye vasatissadā

* * * * * * * * sahasraṃ vasati dhruvam

tasmātsarvaprayatnena pātakādīnvisarjayet

pāpānnarakasaṃprāptistasmātpāpaṃ na kārayet

vaśyaṃ pūrvamukhaṃ caiva dakṣiṇaṃ cābhicārakam

uṣṇīṣaṃ kañcukīnāśo muktakeśodalāvṛtaḥ

jṛmbhāniṣṭhīvanaṃ caiva kṣutamardana tantrikā

ācamanaṃ bhavetteṣāṃ smaredvāmāmumānvitam

* * * naśśayānovā gacchedutthita eva vā

pādau prasārya na japet kukkuṭāsanako navaḥ

suśaktaścetsarvameva śaktayaśśaktito japet

grathanontaritaḥ proktā vidarbho dvyantarīkṛtaḥ

tambhanaṃ rodhane vidyādākṛṣṭau syāddvidarbhanam

anirdiṣṭamasaṃjñaṃ ca yatkṛtaṃ tadvṛthā bhavet

mokṣe prāyaścitteṣu saptattuṣu japaṃ bhavet

māraṇaṃ śiśire kuryāt tathānyāni vasantake

uccāṭanaṃ parāhne syān madhyāhne śaiśiraṃ tu vā

ardhayāṃmottare kāle hemante karma śasyate

cāradaṃ cārdharātre syād vāsantaṃ sārdhavādhake

sarvadoṣanivṛttyarthaṃ sarvaloka sukhāvaham

rāṣṭrasya sasyaphaladaṃ bhuktimukti phalapradam

utsavaṃ kḷptabhedaṃ ca puṣkalaṃ vijayānvitam

udbhūtasṛṣṭikāritvād utsavaḥ kathitaḥ kramāt

amāvāsyāṃ samārabhya māsotsavaṃ tathaiva ca

caitramāse trayodaśyāṃ māsanakṣatrameva ca

sarvamāse tu kartavyaṃ mahotsavavidhikramaḥ

ṣoḍaśaṃ sarvarakṣārthaṃ caitraṃ dvādaśakaṃ tathā

pārthivaṃ pañcadinaṃ caiva dvyahaṃ vṛddhikaraṃ tathā

saptaviṃśati nakṣatraṃ dhvajārohaṇa pūrvakam

aṃkurārpaṇakaṃ pūrvaṃ dhvajaṃ paścātsamarcayet

sarveṣāmutsavaṃ kuryāt pūrvaṃ vṛṣabhamarcayet

pratiṣṭhādikrameṇaiva maṇḍapaṃ tu prakalpayet

dvādaśaṃ daśahastaṃ syānnavahastamathāpi vā

hastārdhaṃ pucchamevoktaṃ tripucchaṃ tu dvipucchakam

ūrdhve tasya lakṣmīṃ caiva adho bhāge tu kumbhakaiḥ

pārśve tu dīpa evokta athavā kurameva vā

śvetaṃ raktaṃ tathā puṣpaṃ sarvālaṃkārameva ca

kāmyāddharahavarṇena ābhicāraṃ tu * * kam

kṛte taduktavarṇānāṃ vālāpītena śobhitam

sarvābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ sarvapuṣpairalaṃkṛtam

darbhamālāṃ samāveṣṭya muktādāmapralambitam

tadardhaṃ taṇḍulaiḥ kuryāt tadardhaṃ tu tilairapi

tadagre sthaṇḍilaṃ kuryāt netra bhaṃtu sthāpayet

madhye vṛṣabhamāvāhya gandhapuṣpaissamarcitam

ikṣuṃ ca gopatiṃ caiva śaṃkukarṇaṃ tathaiva ca

mahodaroṣṭamaścaiva svasvanāmnā prapūjayet

tadagre sthaṇḍilaṃ kuryātsitaissikatameva ca

prācya sūtraṃ trisūtraṃ syādudaksūtraṃ tathaiva ca

nāke suparṇamantreṇa dakṣiṇeṣu likhedbudhaḥ

yorudra iti mantreṇa udak sūtraṃ tathaiva ca

indraṃ viśveti mantreṇa paścime tu samālikhet

vāgīśvaraṃ tu vāgīśīṃ āsanaṃ tu prapūjayet

pūrvavaccāgni saṃskāraṃ pūjayedvṛṣabhaṃ guruḥ

paristaraṇe dadyād darbhādīni paristaret

ājyasthālīṃ tathā sthāpya carusthālīṃ tu sthāpayet

sṛksṛvābhimukhe sthāpya sthāpayettu adhomukham

samidhādvayapūrve tu * * * * * * * *

āhuti tritayaṃ kuryād vyāhṛtyā juhuyātkramāt

vṛṣagāyatrimantreṇa tatra homatrayaṃ hunet

caruṃtvaghoramantreṇa lājaṃ tatpuruṣeṇa tu

āḍhakaṃ mudgamāṣāṇāṃ tathaiva juhuyātkramāt

homānte vṛṣakumbhaḥ syādvṛṣakumbhastu mūrtimān

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca adhidevāḥ prakīrtitāḥ

arcayedbheridevaṃ syād ācāryastāḍayetkramāt

aṃgapūrṇa samāyuktaṃ śaktyā sahitameva ca

nandikeśvaramāvāhya vādyānhṛdaya saṃpadam

tatpuṣpaṃ nikṣipedbheryāṃ samatālaṃ tu vādyakān

astradevaṃ samabhyarcya madhyapatre tu rudrakam

gaurīṃ ca madhyakūle tu sarasvatīṃ dakṣiṇe tathā

gaṇeśaṃ pālikāmadhye skandaṃ daṇḍāgra eva ca

kṣetrapālaṃ tathaiśānyāṃ daṇḍamadhye tu śāstu ca

caṇḍeśaṃ daṇḍamūle tu sarvadeve ca saṃgrahe

dhūpadīpa samāyuktaṃ sarvadeva nivedyakam

pratiścandradharaṃ caiva vṛṣadhvajasahaiva ca

dinadaivatamāvāhya pīṭhāgre vā dhvajāgrake

tṛtīyaṃ brahmarātraṃ syād gandharvaṃ tu caturthakam

saptamaṃ ṛṣirātraṃ syād aṣṭamaṃ nāgarātrakam

ekādaśaṃ rudrarātraṃ dvādaśaṃ śivarātrakam

pañcādaśaṃ śāsturātraṃ śaktirātraṃ ca ṣoḍaśa

vighnādi cendraparyantaṃ saukhyamutsavameva ca

vighnādi bhūtaparyantaṃ pārthivotsavameva ca

vighnādi bhūtaparyantaṃ pārthivotsavameva ca

prathamaṃ gaṇapate rātramutsavāditi saṃbhavet

śuddhānnaṃ tu nivedyaṃ syādvighnarātrau baliṃ kṣipet

dvitīye'hani dātavyaṃ paiśācaṃ mantramuccaran

tṛtīye'hani dātavyaṃ brahmamantramanusmaran

caturthe'hani dātavyaṃ gāndharvaṃ mantramuccaran

pañcame'hani dātavyaṃ bhūtamantramanusmaran

ṣaṣṭhe'hani pradātavyaṃ skandamantramanusmaran

saptame'hani dātavyaṃ ṛṣimantramanusmaran

aṣṭame'hani dātavyaṃ nāgamantramanusmaran

navame'hani dātavyaṃ manumantramanusmaran

kadalīpanasaṃ cāmraṃ nālikeraphalānvitam

māṣānnaṃ ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ kṣīraṃ ca gulakhaṇḍakam

śuddhānnaṃ dadhisaṃyuktaṃ bṛhatī mūlasaṃyutam

kṛsarānnaṃ ghṛtaṃ sarpira madhukṣīraphalaṃ tathā

mudgānnaṃ māṣasaṃyuktaṃ gulaṃ kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ tathā

pāyasaṃ ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ gulakhaṇḍasamanvitam

śuddhānnaṃ laḍḍukaṃ sarpiḥ kadalīkāravallikā

ṣoḍaśe'hani dātavyaṃ śaktimantramanusmaran

brahmādyaiśānaparyantaṃ lokapālabaliṃ kṣipet

paścimadvāraharmye tu vāstubhūtabaliḥ kramāt

evaṃ balikramaṃ kuryān nityameva baliṃ kṣipet

jyeṣṭhādi vighnaparyantaṃ svasvanāmnā baliṃ kṣipet

dhvajādibalipīṭhāntaṃ sarvameva baliṃ kṣipet

* * * * * * lakṣmīṃ gosthāne ca sarasvatīm

śmaśāne sundarīṃ caiva devakoṣṭhaṃ baliṃ kṣipet

grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryānmahāpīṭhe baliṃ kṣipet

jale tu bhūtakaṃ kuryād visṛjedanna liṃgakam

triṃśadbalikramaṃ kuryāt ṣoḍaśaṃ dinameva ca

aṣṭādaśabaliṃ kuryāt saumyamevaṃ vidhīyate

balisaṃkhyā iti proktā utsavasya vidhiṃ śṛṇu

pratiṣṭhādyutsave hīne yatheṣṭaṃ māsamānayet

dvāhāpīṭhaṃ * * * * prasādurdhūḍhameva ca

pratiṣṭhāśikharaṃ hīnaṃ utsavaṃ varjayettataḥ

prāsādadvāra * * * * * mevamihocyate

prārabhyamutsave hīne yatheṣṭaṃ māsi mānayet

kālātītādidoṣeṇa śāntihomaṃ samācaret

rājarāṣṭre sukhaṃ prāpte punarutsavamācaret

* * * te kāle * * * naṣṭe utsavameva ca

māsanakṣatra tīrthāntaṃ ārdrāntamutsavaṃ param

rājajanmanyanakṣatre yajamānavaśādbudhaḥ

tatkāle tīrthamārabhya saṃkramaṃ saṃbhavetsudhīḥ

pratimāṃ varjanātpaścād ṛjyavaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ

nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyamutsavaṃ trividhaṃ bhavet

yatheṣṭhaṃ kāmyamityuktaṃ utsavaṃ trividhaṃ bhavet

iṣṭamāseṣu kāmyārthamutsavaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ

dhvajārohaṇa * * tu varjayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ

dhvaje dhvajaṃ na kartavyaṃ kartavyaṃ vṛṣabhadhvajam

devayāgaṃ ca kurvīta viṣṇuyāgaṃ vināśayet

grāme ca nagare caiva pattane rājadhānike

hari * * * * * syāt haripṛṣṭhaṃ tu śobhanam

utsavaṃ kārayeddhīmān puṇyamāse tu pūrvakam

caitramāse tu citrāntaṃ viśākhe vaiśākha eva ca

āṣāḍhe pūrvanakṣatre śrāvaṇe vaiṣṇavāntakam

pūrṇe tu māsanakṣatre samudrasnānamuttamam

kārtike kṛttikāntaṃ vā ārdrāntamutsavaṃ param

sarvotsavaṃ ca ṛkṣe tu sauraṃ saukhyāntameva ca

bherītāḍanapūrvaṃ ca aṃkurārpaṇapūrvakam

utsavāṃkuraṃ dvitīyaṃ tu tīrthāṃkuraṃ tṛtīyakam

nayanaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ caiva jalādhivāsanaṃ vinā

śālitaṇḍula tanmadhye vṛṣakumbhāntameva ca

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra homaṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ

madhusarpistathā kuryān netraṃ tu sparśanaṃ tathā

darpaṇe abhiṣekaṃ tu mudgānnaṃ tu nivedayet

maṇḍape samalaṃ kṛtya madhye sthaṇḍilameva ca

abhitaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau ukṣādīnkramaśo nyaset

homānte nayanonmīlaṃ madhusarpiśca locanam

darpaṇaṃ jalādhivāsaṃ ca pratiṣṭhāṃ kārayedbudhaḥ

maṇṭape sthaṇḍilaṃ kuryādaṣṭadroṇaiśca śālibhiḥ

kautukaṃ bandhayeddhīmānsvarṇaṃ rajatasūtrakam

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra kautukaṃ bandhayetkramāt

śayanaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ kuryāt tadagre vṛṣakumbhake

tadagre agniṃ saṃsthāpya homayetpūrvavadguruḥ

paścime astradevaṃ tu śālibhiḥ sthaṇḍilaṃ kramāt

agnikāryoktamārgeṇa homayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ

uttare āsanaṃ kalpya adhomukhaṃ tu sādhayet

prādeśakūrcasahitaṃ triḥsparśanajalaṃ tathā

prokṣayetprokṣaṇīṃ caiva prokṣaṇīsthajalaṃ tathā

janārdanaṃ samāvāhya uttare sthāpyameva ca

agnisaṃskāra mevaṃ tu ājyasaṃskārameva ca

prādeśe sparśanaṃ kuryādvisarjayedagnimeva ca

evaṃ saṃskāramevaṃ tu yathoktaṃ pūrvameva ca

tilasarṣapamudgāni gulakhaṇḍaṃ daśa smṛtāḥ

ūrdhvaṃ tu samidhā kṛtvā pariṣecamanantaram

samidhā sadyamantreṇa ājyaṃ vāmena hūyate

tilamīśānamantreṇa sarṣapaṃ kavacena tu

vṛṣamūlaṃ ca gāyatrīṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ śatam

jayādi rabhyādhānaṃ ca rāṣṭrabhṛcca hunetkramāt

tāmbūlaṃ dāpayetkuryān mūrtiṃ vṛṣabha kumbhake

bherīghoṣaṃ tataḥ kuryādāvāhanamathocyate

dīkṣitānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ ācāryastāḍayetkramāt

sarvābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ sarvapuṣpairalaṃkṛtam

tāḍayennavatālaṃ syādācāryaḥ śiṣya eva ca

śivādi caṇḍaparyantaṃ āvāhanavidhiṃ kuru

balipātra samāyuktamannaliṃgāstrameva ca

vṛṣayantraṃ samādāya dhvajapaṃ bandhayetkramāt

antarbalikramaṃ pūrvayāgānte vā baliṃ kṣipet

sarvamabhyarcayeddhīmān paścādvāstu baliṃ kṣipet

athavā dārujaṃ pīṭhaṃ tālamāna pramāṇataḥ

grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā mahāpīṭhabaliṃ kṣipet

dhvajapaṭaṃ sthālikāṃ caiva daṇḍamūle tu saṃsthitam

āsanaṃ khādiraṃ caiva bilvacampakameva ca

madhūkamarjunaṃ caiva kṛṣṇavṛkṣa samanvitam

vakraṃ bhinnaṃ ca suṣiraṃ kṛmikoṭaravarjitam

sthūpyantaṃ vāgalāntaṃ vā vṛṣasthala samaṃ tu vā

trayastriṃśati vā hastaṃ pañcaviṃśati hastakam

kṣīravṛkṣaṃ tu saṃgrāhya yaṣṭiṃ kṛtvā vicakṣaṇaḥ

agnyaṃgulaghanaṃ proktaṃ caturākṛtyalaṃ kṛtim

upadaṇḍapramāṇena veṇudaṇḍaṃ vidhīyate

ṣaṭtālaṃ pañcatālaṃ ca kanyasaṃ upadaṇḍakam

kārpāsasūtraṃ rajjuṃ ca valayadviguṇaṃ bhavet

bhūmi saṃprokṣaṇaṃ kuryād yaṣṭibandhana saṃyutam

ratnajaṃ rajataṃ caiva kūrmāsanaṃ tu lohajam

trihastaṃ tu pramāṇena dvihastaṃ vā nayetkramāt

vedikālakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ * * bhedanikā tathā

hastadvayaṃ tu vistāraṃ utsedhaṃ hastamātrakam

vistārāttusamutsedhaṃ hastamātramathottamaḥ

vedikālakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ daṇḍamūle vicakṣaṇaḥ

kautukaṃ bandhayeddhīmān daṇḍamadhye subandhanam

āyasaṃ valayaṃ rajjudviguṇaṃ mūlamāśritā

dhvajaṃ ca bandhayeddhīmānkiṃkiṇī puṣpa śobhitam

sumuhūrte tu yaḥ kuryād dhvajārohaṇameva ca

viparītaṃ ca rohaṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate

sarvadevānsamāvāhya daṇḍamūle tu yojayet

nṛttagītasamāyuktaṃ sarvaghoṣa samanvitam

balyannaṃ tu tataḥ kuryāt digvidikṣu ca eva hi

amaṃgalya kanyakā caiva apasmāro dṛḍhaṃ budhaḥ

śivāgre maṇṭapaṃ kuryād gomayenopalepayet

suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vātha atha kārpāsameva ca

sthalike sthaṇḍilaṃ caiva tanmadhye tanturakṣakam

navavastra samāyuktaṃ tūryaghoṣa samanvitam

triyambakena mantreṇa rakṣāsūtraṃ subandhayet

puṣpamālāṃ tataḥ kuryāt kūṭaṃ vā maṇṭapaṃ tu vā

vāyau some tathaiśānyāṃ yāgamaṇṭapameva ca

ratnimātra samutsedhaṃ darpaṇodarasannibham

navāgniruttamaścaiva pañcāgnirmadhyamo bhavet

ekāgniṃ vātha kurvīta pūrvapaścimameva vā

tadagre agnikuṇḍaṃ vā homayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ

catustambhasamāyuktaṃ caturdvārasamāyutam

gātraveṣṭana saṃyuktaṃ patākā dhvajameva ca

tadardhaṃ taṇḍulaiḥ proktaṃ tadardhaṃ tu tilairapi

madhye sadāśivaṃ kumbhaṃ vardhanīṃ tu tathottare

dvārakumbhaṃ samādāya pūrvādīśāntamarcayet

vāruṇyabhūte tathaiva vāyusome yugāntaṃ ca trikumbhamīśe

sarvadevaṃ samāvāhya gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra kautukaṃ bandhayetkramāt

kuṇḍasaṃskāramevoktaṃ agniṃ prajvālyameva ca

ācāryaḥ śiṣyasaṃyukto homaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ

bilvaṃ ca khadiraṃ caiva nyagrodhaṃ vahnimeva ca

paristaraṇadarbhāṇāṃ paridhitrayameva ca

brahmāṇaṃ dakṣiṇe'bhyaryca uttare tu janārdanam

praṇītā pātramādāya pūjayecca janārdanam

homānte tadbaliṃ kuryān mekhalopari vinyaset

bahirbalividhiṃ kuryād antarbalimathācaret

prathamamaṣṭanāgaṃ ca dvitīyaṃ dvādaśādikam

agnimaṃtramanusmṛtya svāheti ca niyojayet

havirnivedayettatra sarvadevaṃ nivedyakam

āhūyavādyakānpaścād ācāryaḥ śiṣya eva ca

prathamamantarbaliṃ kuryād dvitīyaṃ dinadevatā

umāskandeśvaraṃ devaṃ grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ tathā

śaṃkhaghoṣa samāyuktaṃ brahmaghoṣa samanvitam

chatraṃ ca cāmaraṃ caiva vyajanaṃ dhvajameva ca

sarvabhaktajanānāṃ tu nṛpatibrahmameva ca

rudrakanyakaya yuktaṃ gāndharvaṃ tu śivāgrake

pādyamācamanaṃ cārdhyaṃ dhūpadīpa samanvitam

darpaṇaṃ cāmaraṃ caiva vyajanaṃ chatrameva ca

nālikera samāyuktaṃ kadalīphala saṃyutam

taddinaṃ tu samārabhya yajñaṃ saptadinaṃ bhavet

āsthāna maṇḍapaṃ kuryād gomayālepite śubhe

tanmadhye tu ghṛtaṃ sthāpya dūrvākṣata samāyutam

dūrvākṣata samāyuktaṃ ūddhū * * * * budhaḥ

snapanaṃ kārayitvā tu pañcāśatpañcaviṃśatiḥ

muktābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ śvetacandanalepitam

tāmbūlaṃ tu nivedyaṃ syāddhūpadīpa samanvitam

grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt praveśe dīpadāpanam

paścāddhomabaliṃ kuryāt astrarāja samanvitam

rātrau baliṃ tataḥ kuryāt sarveṣāṃ balimācaret

aṣṭame vā navāhe vā gandhotsava vidhikramam

taddinātpūrvarātrau tu kautukaṃ maṃgalaṃ kuru

ekanṛttasamāyuktaṃ snapanaṃ tatra kārayet

gomayālepite śuddhe sthaṇḍilaṃ parikalpayet

tadardhaṃ tilasaṃyuktaṃ devāgre sthaṇḍilaṃ tathā

tanmadhye tu tripādīnāṃ brahmajajñānamantrataḥ

āpyāya sveti mantreṇa uttare rekhayetkramāt

idaṃ viṣṇvitimantreṇa pratīcyāṃ rekhayetkramāt

tanmadhye sthāpitaṃ kuryāt pradakṣiṇa mathācaret

tatpuro hyāsanaṃ kalpya praṇītāṃ prokṣaṇīṃ tathā

samittraya samāyuktaṃ paridhitrayameva ca

kuśapuṣpākṣatairyuktaṃ prokṣaṇīṃ sādhayettataḥ

uttare caiva saṃkalpya janārdanaṃ samarcayet

kūrcagranthiṃ visṛjyātha agnimadhye sthitaṃ tāḥ

vyāhṛtiṃ juhuyātpaścād brahmajajñānamantrakaiḥ

pūrve viṣṭharaṃ saṃkalpya samidāhutimācaret

aghoreṇa caruṃ hutvā lājaṃ tatpuruṣeṇa tu

mudgamāṣaṃ ca śālīnāṃ netramantreṇa hūyate

prati prati tathā kuryāt pratyekaṃ tu daśāhutīḥ

dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā daśahutvā samācaret

homānte kautukaṃ kuryāt punaḥ puṇyāhamācaret

śaktestu vāmahaste tu kautukaṃ bandhayetsudhīḥ

dhūpadīpasamāyuktamārātrimarcayedbudhaḥ

brāhmaṇānbhojayettatra caturvedasamanvitān

apūpaṃ nivedayeddhīmān bahunaivedyasaṃyutam

dhūpadīpasamāyuktamārātrikamathācaret

bhasmaśeṣaṃ ca nṛpatirbhaktānāṃ dāpayetsudhīḥ

sarvābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ muktābharaṇabhūṣitam

nīlotpalasamaṃ puṣpaṃ na bhavettu bhaviṣyati

harmyapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryād āsthānaṃ maṇṭapaṃ sthitam

bhasmaṃ tu dāpayetpaścāc chatracāmaradarśanam

prātaḥkāle samārabhya gandhotsava vidhikramam

citravastrairalaṃkṛtya sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam

śaṃkhadundubhinirghoṣaiḥ stotramaṃgalavācakaiḥ

devasannidhisaṃprāpte homaṃ kṛtvā viśeṣataḥ

puṇyāhaṃ kārayeddhīmān homaṃ kṛtvā vicakṣaṇaḥ

homānte kṛṣṇagandhena lepayettu naṭeśvaram

brāhmaṇānbhojayettatra svastisūktasamanvitam

mūlaberaṃ sugandhaṃ syāt parivāra samanvitam

caturveda pravaktṝṇāṃ dvijānāṃ bhojayetkramāt

godarśamaśubhaṃ prāpya kanyādarśana saṃbhavam

gandhadvāreti mantreṇa tāḍayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ

puṣpāñjalirnamaskṛtvā vādyakāntāḍayetkramāt

nandimantraṃ samuccāryaṃ mṛdavādyaṃ tu tāḍayet

jhallarīmukhaṃ caiva paṭahaṃ mardalaṃ tathā

saptatālaṃ tataḥ kuryān mahānāṭyaṃ tathaiva ca

dhūpadīpa samāyuktaṃ nīrājanaṃ viśeṣataḥ

chatraṃ ca cāmaraṃ caiva vyajanaṃ ca kramātsudhīḥ

sarvabhaktajanānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ gandhameva ca

dhūpadīpa samāyuktamārātrimarcayetkramāt

śaṃkhanāda samāyuktaṃ dundubhīmardalānvitam

rathe vā śibikāyāṃ vā citradīpa samanvitam

pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ dhūpadīpa samanvitam

nālikerasamāyuktaṃ kadalīpanasaṃ tathā

chatraṃ ca cāmaraṃ kuryāt gītavādya samanvitam

snapanaṃ kārayitvā tu pūrvavatparikalpayet

abhiṣekaṃ prakurvīta snapanaṃ kārayetkramāt

śuklavastraparīdhānaṃ śvetamālyānulepanam

śvetayajñopavītaṃ ca śvetavastrottarīyakam

śibikāyāṃ tataḥ kuryāt prākāre tu pradakṣiṇam

dhūpadīpa samāyuktaṃ ārātrika samanvitam

prātaḥkāle samārabhya citravastrottarīyakam

sarvamaṃgalavādyaṃ ca śaṃkhakāhalameva ca

āsthāna maṇṭape sthitvā dhūpadīpasamanvitam

darpaṇaṃ cāmaraṃ caiva vyajanaṃ ca nayetkramāt

sarvabhaktajanānāṃ ca bhasma datvā ca dāpayet

snānaṃ kṛtvā yathānyāyaṃ havirnivedayettataḥ

tripuraghnapratimāṃ kuryāt kalyāṇasundaraṃ tathā

kārukairvidyadṛcchaṃ vā kecitkāle śivotsavam

paridhāya yayussarve dhanurdharāstathaiva ca

mṛgapakṣivarāhāśca mayūrāḥ kukkuṭāstathā

helāvilāsakathitaṃ gacchedbhaktajanairyutam

grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt praviśedālayaṃ saha

śivāgre sthaṇḍilaṃ kuryādaṣṭadroṇaiśca śālibhiḥ

śālikātaṇḍulaiḥ pūrya tanmadhye kumbhakaṃ hṛdā

bherīdevaṃ samabhyarcya caṇḍeśapratimāṃ tathā

puṇyāhaṃ pūrvavatkuryāt kautukaṃ bandhayeddhṛdā

astraṃ tu kautukaṃ kuryādbheryāṃ kautukabandhanam

sarvadevaṃ samāvāhya saptatantuṃ samāpayet

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra gandhapuṣpa samanvitam

snānaṃ kṛtvāstra tatvajñaḥ sarvabhaktajanaissaha

tīrthādhivāsanaṃ kṛtvā mahātīrthaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

prātassaṃgamakāle tu yāgānte vā balikramam

āsthānamaṇṭape sthitvā dhūpadīpa samanvitam

gomayālepite śuddhe śvetavarṇairalaṃkṛtam

lūkhalaṃ brahmadaivatyaṃ musalaṃ viṣṇudaivatam

rajanīdroṇa saṃyuktaṃ lūkhalaṃ pūrṇameva ca

tripuṇṭrabhasmasaṃyuktaṃ śvetavastrairalaṃkṛtam

lūkhalaṃ tu namaskṛtya rajanīcūrṇameva ca

rajanīṃ lūkhalaṃ pūrya devasannidhi saṃyutam

maṃgalāṃkuramāvṛtya sumuhūrte vicakṣaṇaḥ

mūlaberaṃ ca parito tailābhyañjana karmaṇi

dūrvāṃkurākṣatairmūrdhni arcayitvā viśeṣataḥ

bhakṣyabhojyaviśeṣeṇa tāmbūlaṃ tatra kārayet

brāhmaṇānbhojayettatra caturvedasamanvitān

tailārambhe tu sahasā cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā vicakṣaṇaḥ

śaṃkhaghoṣa samāyuktaṃ vāstudevabalikramaḥ

bhūpatiṃ tailasaṃyuktaṃ mahāsenā samanvitam

tadītīre taṭāke vā tīrthaṃ ca sa maheśvaram

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra gandhapuṣpasamanvitam

sarvadevaṃ samāvāhya sarvatīrtha samanvitam

astreṇa sarvabhaktānāṃ sahasnānaṃ vidhīyate

mūladevasya snapanaṃ pratimā snapanaṃ tathā

śivakumbhaṃ tatoddhṛtya ācāryaḥ paricārakaiḥ

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra abhiṣekaṃ ca kārayet

snapanaṃ tatra kurvīta vastreṇālaṃkṛtaṃ śubham

aṣṭottara śataṃ śreṣṭhaṃ paṃcāśatpaṃcaviṃśatiḥ

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra snapanaṃ snānamācaret

havirnivedya saṃyuktaṃ tāmbūlaṃ tatra kārayet

maunavratadharāssarve grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ tathā

ālayaṃ tu praviśyātha ācāryaḥ pūjayettataḥ

brāhmaṇānbhojayettatra bhaktānāṃ paricārakaiḥ

avarohaṃ tu kurvīta śaṃkhakāhalamardalaiḥ

prākāraṃ tu pradakṣiṇya caṇḍayāgaṃ samācaret

caṇḍāgre homasaṃyuktaṃ agnikāryaṃ samācaret

dhūpadīpasamāyuktaṃ arcayitvā viśeṣataḥ

astradevaṃ samādāya maunaṃ baliṃ samācaret

dīpahīnaṃ tathā kuryā prabhānāthaṃ tu ghoṣayet

pādaprakṣālanaṃ kuryāt praviśedālayaṃ tathā

sāyecotsavamārabhya umāskandeśvaraṃ tathā

sarvalokasukhaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvarāṣṭravivardhanam

bhogamokṣaphalaṃ caiva utsavaḥ kathitaḥ kramāt

tathā ca liṃgenaikena utsavo jāyate kramāt

patraṃ caiva maheśādi kumbhādi kusumaṃ smṛtam

maheśādiṣu kumbheṣu dhvajeṣu ca viśeṣataḥ

mantraiḥ kumbhaiḥ śivaṃ pūjya liṃge tṛptaṃ sadāśivam

abuddhi pūrvasaṃskāradīkṣābhedaṃ catuṣṭayam

arcanādutsavāntaṃ tu prāyaścittaṃ catuṣṭayam

viśeṣamarcanaṃ kāmyaṃ nityaṃ naimittikaṃ viduḥ

tadaṃgakarmahīnaṃ tu prāyaścittamiti smṛtam

dhvajārohāditīrthāntaṃ daśakāryamidaṃ bhavet

bījāṃkurārpaṇaṃ caiva dhvajārohaṇameva ca

yāgadīkṣādhvaśuddhiḥsyādvṛṣārūḍhātmarūpakam

dīpāvalokanaṃ caiva śivarūpamiti smṛtam

śaktiyātrā maheśasya śivabhogamiti smṛtam

dhvajadaṇḍaḥ patirjñeyo dhvajasthitavṛṣaḥ paśuḥ

icchājñānakriyāśaktiryaṣṭitrayamidaṃ bhavet

utsavasya tu kartṛtvaṃ dhvajārohaṇamucyate

tannaḥ śivaḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno agniḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno rudraḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno viṣṇuḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno brahmā pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno devī pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno dantiḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno dharā pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno gaurī pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno vṛṣabhaḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno mātṝḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tannaḥ skandaḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno jyeṣṭhā pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno durgi pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tannaḥ sūryaḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno vīraḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno brāhmī pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tannaśśaṃkari pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tannaḥ kaumārī pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno vaiṣṇavī pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno vārāhī pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno indrāṇī pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tannaścāmuṇḍī pracodayet * * * * * * * *

tanno bhairavaḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno mayūraḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tannaḥ skandaḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno gajendraḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno nandī pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tannaścaṇḍaḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tannaścaṇḍī pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno haṃsaḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno'nantaḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tanno rudraḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

tannaḥ śivaḥ pracodayāt * * * * * * * *

* * * * * * * * * iti pāśupatāstram

oṃ prasphura prasphura ghoraghorataratanurūpa caṭacaṭa

ghātaya ghātaya | ityaghorāstram

iti pāśupatāstram * * * * * * * * *

kaṭirudravacanavidhāri rakṣamāṃ pratyaṃgiredhāma

svāhā | iti pratyaṃgirāstram * * * * * * *

oṃ hāṃ maheśvarāyanamaḥ | oṃ hlīṃ haṃ viṣṇave namaḥ

namaḥ

oṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ vīrabhadrāya namaḥ | oṃ juṃsaḥ oṃ

oṃ haṃsaḥ śivāya namaḥ | ityātmapañcākṣaram

phaṭ hlāṃ hlāṃ hlāṃ tiśaraṇaṃ

huṃphaṭ | iti dvādaśākṣaram

hrīṃ vidyāṃ gāstrāya namaḥ

oṃ hrīṃ bhuvaneśvaryai namaḥ

oṃ śrīṃ mahāgauryai namaḥ

oṃ aiṃ klīṃ sarasvatyai namaḥ

oṃ raṃ vaṃ yaṃ saṃ traṃ haṃ amṛteśvarāya namaḥ

hrīṃ kamalākṛta prāsādamāha | hāṃ kṣamaṇa prāsādamāha

hṛṃ sṛṃ kṣmṛṃ ḷṃ vṛṃ śraṃ traṃ ūṃ kaṇṭherṇya prāsādamāha

auṃ āditya prāsādamāha * * * * * * *

oṃ hāṃ hauṃ śivāyanamaḥ | prāsādapañcākṣaram

sthūlaṃ namaśśivāya sūkṣmaṃ namo'ntaṃ śivāya iti siddhaḥ
